*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*10*

Koda dare yayi suka shiga bacci dukansu tunani d'aya ne a cikin zuciyoyinsu, waton tunanin  Aliyu haidar mai lak'abi da sunan  BASARAKE,  domin kuwa Munara tunanin had'uwar da sukayi kawai take yi tana ta wassafo siffarshi a zuciyarta,a cikin shauk'in sonshi da ke ta mamaye dukkanin zuciyarta, saboda jin takeyi kamar tafi kowace mace sa'a kaf a duniya,   tunda har tayi gamon katar da samun irin shi. 

Munawwa ko da ta tuno fuskarshi musamman  pic's d'in da taga yayi da Munara, sai taji kirjinta ya buga dam! dam! sannan abu na biyu tunanin muryar sabon d'an aikinsu da ya addabeta a zuciya, musamman idan ta tuno k'wayar idaniyarshi da idan suka had'a idanuwa take jinta a cikin wani yanayi, wanda kai tsaye bazata iya fassara abunda take ji ba, uwa uba muryarshi mai dad'i da take ji har cikin kwakwalwarta kanta. 

B'angaren Basarakenma hakan ce ta kasance dashi a lokacin,  domin kuwa da tunanin 'yan biyun ya kwana a zuciyarshi wanda ya kasa tantance me ya dace ya aikata?, saboda tabaas yana son Munara amman tun ranar da ta fara wulak'antashi, tun a ranar yaji ta fita kanshi saboda halinta na rashin girmama d'an adam da yagani, yayinda a gefe d'aya kuma yake ganin girman Munawwara saboda irin tsantsar mutuncin da ya hango take dashi, sannan uwa uba tausayin da ta nuna masa ya saka shi jin wani abu a zuciyarshi dangane da ita, tsawon ranar a duk lokacin da ya tuno ta sai ya tsinci kanshi a cikin mutuwar jiki,  tareda wata kasala da take lullub'e masa dukkan sassan jikinshi, a daddafe yaga safiya ta waye bayan ya yi breakfast  ne yayi shigarshi ta kullum ya yi tsinke sai inda yake ajiye motarshi, sannan ya hau acab'a yayi  unguwarsu Munara, tun a bakin get d'in gidan ya jiyo sautin fad'anta, a gurguje ya biya d'an acabar ya tura 'yar k'aramar k'ofar a hankali sannan ya shige, Munara tana kallonshi ta yo kanshi tana fad'a kamar wadda zata dake shi tace,    "Wai kai wani irin kidahumin mutum ne kai?, da rana d'aya da fara aiki zaka fara baiwa mutanen gida matsala?,to bari kaji idan ka sake ka sake yin latti to ka d'auka ma an sallameka kawai daga aikin, don ba gidan ubanka bane ballantana ka dinga yiwa mutane iko" tana huci ta k'ara cewa " ka iya driving ? "Basarake yayi saurin d'aga kanshi ba tareda yayi magana ba, a cikin d'aga murya ta k'ara cewa,    "To k'adangare Magana zaka yi ba d'aga wa mutane kai ba, karb'i jawo motar mu gani don kar muje ka watsar da mu a saman titi"

Ta k'are maganar tareda wurgo masa key d'in motar, wanda yayi daidai da fitowar Munawwa rungume da littafanta, tana ganin abunda Munara tayi masa a lokaci d'aya taji ranta ya b'aci, da sauri ta k'arasa wurin ta duk'a da niyyar d'auko masa key d'in ne, aka yi dace shima ya duk'a hannunsu ya had'u wuri d'aya, duk da a cikin safa hannunshi yake,  amman sai da yaji wani yarr a jikinshi ba shiri ya kai kallonshi gareta, wanda itama ta d'ago ta kai kallonta gareshi a tare zuciyarsu ta buga dam! dam!, da sauri Munawwa ta zare hannunta tareda d'auke idanuwanta akanshi, sannan ta tashi tsaye tana yi masa ina kwana, ya amsa fuskarshi cike da murmushinshin yak'e, sannan ta nufi motarsu inda Munara take tsaye jikin motar tana faman kiran wayar Basarake, Munawwa ta bud'e murfin gaba ta shiga ranta a b'ace tace da Munara ,   "Yakamata ki rage abunda kike yiwa wannan bawan ALLAH"

Ta k'are maganar  ba tareda ta juya ta kalleta ba, Munara tana shirin bud'a baki ne sai gashi ya shigo ya tada motar, yana murmushi yace da Munara "shigo muje ko" tayi masa wani kallon renin wayo sannan ta matsa gefe d'aya tace,    "Je dai ka gwado mu gani in ka k'ware wallahi ba zan shiga ka halaka ni ba." 

Basarake yayi murmushi kamar baiji zafi ba yace da Munawwa,   "Kema ki fita in jaraba inga ko zan iya."

Munawwa tayi saurin fad'in,    "Muje kawai."

Basarake yayi saurin kallonta yace,   "Ba kya tsoron in watsar dake?kin fa san tuk'in d'an koyo sai a hankali fa."

Munawwa tayi murmushi mai sauti sannan tace   "Ai kaima ran ne da kai kamar kowa, naji na amince duk abunda zai sameka ya same mu a tare, bare ma jikina yana bani kai gwani ne a wurin driving d'in mota."

Basarake yayi saurin kallonta zuciyarshi ta buga amman ya dake abunshi,  ya tada motar a hankali yayi ribas sannan ya fito get d'in gidan, wanda tun kafin su k'araso tuni Isah ya wangaleshi, saboda jin tsoron cin mutuncin 'yar gaban goshin Daddy wato Munara, bayan ya fito ne ya d'an fara tafiya kan titi Munara tana biye dasu tayi tsaye k'ofar gidan tana kallon yanda yake tuk'a motar, aranta sai jinjina shishigi irin na Munawwa take yi don bala'in haushinta take ji, idan taga tana nuna damuwarta akan wannan bak'in mutumin mai fuskar shanu. 

Tana tsaye tana dannawa honey'nta  kira har suka zagayo suka dawo amma wayar tak'i shiga, BASARAKE ya tsaya da motar a bakin titin yana jiran ta k'araso, a hankali ta tako har zuwa wurin idanuwanta akan wayar ta shiga motar, ba shiri ta zabga tsaki saboda jin haushin rashin samun honey'n da bata yi ba, tsakin da tayi yasa dukansu suka waiwayo suka kalleta,  Basarake ya janye idonshi daga kanta ya fara tafiya a cikin k'warewa, ai kuwa Munara ta d'aga murya cikin jin haushin rashin samun wayar da bata yi ba tace,     "Wayyo honey rashin jinka yana sani a damuwa, bansan me yasa kake jin dad'in rufe waya ba kwanannan."

Munara ta tab'e baki ta juya tana kallon gefen titi tareda cewa,   "ISKA yana wahalar da mai kayan kara." 

Basarake yayi murmushi yaci gaba da tuk'insa hankali kwance, Munara kuwa da daman cen jiraye take da Munawwa ta zak'alk'alo mata da fad'a tace,    "Shi dai wanda ALLAH ya d'orawa shishigi baiji dad'in rayuwarsa ba wallahi, ba'a kasa da mutum ba yace zai kwasa, ai kece iska ke wahalarwa keda kika rasa wanda kike so har yanzu, tun zamanin k'urciyarki har izuwa yanzu mutum biyar bai bud'a baki yace dake yana sonki ba, ni ko fa ke sheda ce masoya har sai na ture saboda farinjinin da ALLAH ya yo mani, don ni ko yanzu kasuwa ta watse naci ribar had'uwa da honeyna, d'aya tamkar da million a cikin mazaje idan kuma kinyi zuciya ki samo irinshi in kin isa."

Munawwa tayi dariya mai sauti tace,   "Ai ba'a zuciya a cikin lamarin Ubangiji, ki sani abun rabo ne idan ya raba ya baka kaf duniya ba mai k'watar  maka, don haka ki kiyayi yin gori ga abunda kika san ALLAH ne ke badawa, don zai iya baiwa kowa kuma a lokacin da yaga dama." 
Ta k'are maganar a cikin muryar kuka saboda taji haushin gorin da Munara tayi mata, Munara ta buga wani uban tsaki tace,    "Kanki ake ji." 

  Basarake dai tuk'inshi kawai yake yi ba tareda ya ko kallesu ba, amman fa zuciyarshi cike take fam da tausayin Munawwa, saboda ganin tana sharar hawaye a fakaice saboda bata son yaga tana kuka, har suka je makarantar in banda kwatancen da Munawwa take yi masa na hanyar da zai bi ba wanda ya sake magana. 

Suna zuwa Munara ta b'alle murfin ta fito ko kallonsu bata sake yi ba ta tafiyarta, Munawwa ta fito itama ranta a jagule tareda rungumo littafanta tace dashi "mungode fa"

Basarake yayi murmushi yace "Nine da godiya zan bar maku motar ne anan ko kuwa da ita za'a koma sai kun tashi in dawo in d'auke ku.?"

Munawwa ta kawar da kanta a gefe tace,   "Ka tafi abunka kawai idan mun tashi zamu dawo da kanmu."

Ta fara taku a hankali zata bar wajen Ya d'aga murya ya kira sunanta, cak ta tsaya saboda jin sunanta a bakinshi wanda ya fito rad'am tamkar a bakinshi aka halicci sunan, saboda yanda taji sunan yayi dad'in fad'a a bakin nashi, a cikin takunshi na k'asaita ya isa gabanta yace,    "Kin manta da makullin ko duk saurin ne.?" 

Munawwa ta d'ago fuskarta ta kalleshi da  idanuwanta da suka yi ja saboda kukan da take tayi, gaban Basarake ya fad'i saboda har cikin ranshi yaji zafin yanayin da ya ganta a ciki, a hankali yace da ita,    "Kiyi hak'uri kinji da sannu alkhairinki zai zo har gida ya sameki."

Munara ta d'aga kai tana yi masa kallon mamaki, ya d'aga mata kai alamar tabbatarwa sannan ya mik'a mata key d'in ya wuce, Munawwa ta bi bayanshi da kallo kamar wata sakarya,  har sai da idanuwanta suka daina ganinshi sannan taja k'afafunta ta shiga class zuciyarta sai maimaita kalamanshi takeyi tamkar  bitar karatu . 

Da k'arfe biyu dam Basarake da Malam Isah suna zaune suna fira, ne suka jiyo horn d'in motar su Munara Malam isah jikinshi yana rawa har yana tuntub'e yaje ya wangale get din, abun Mamaki sai ga Munara ita kad'ai ta dawo, tana ajiye motar ta yi fuuuu ta shige cikin gidan ranta a b'ace, Basarake yayi mamakin rashin dawowar Munawwa tareda Munara, zuciyarshi tace wata k'ila ita bata k'are abunda take yi bane shiyasa, Malam isa ya dawo saman bencin da suke a zaune suka ci gaba da firarsu, sai kawai ga Munawwa ta shigo tana sauri tayi cikin gida, Malam isah ya lek'a  wajen gidan yace,   "Ita kuma wannan yarinya me ya kaita dawowa cikin adaidaita?, bayan yanzu 'yar uwarta ta dawo itama, ko dai fad'an ne aka k'ara yi acen makarantar?tunda ita wannan masifaffar bata bari a zauna lafiya." 

Basarake ya lek'o shima yana ganin Napep d'in a waje tana jira,  ya fito yace da mai napep d'in "nawa ne kud'in?"

Yana fad'i Basarake ya ciro 1k ya bashi yace dashi yaje kawai, yana k'ok'arin tashi ne sai ga Munawwa ta fito da kud'i a hannunta, kafin ta k'araso ya cilla kan napep d'inshi ya tafiyarshi, ta kalli Basarake a cikin jin nauyi tace,    "Ga kud'in ka da ka bada ina ta sauri kar yaga na tsaida shi."

Basarake yayi murmushi yace,    "Kije kawai bakomi."

Munawwa ta tsareshi da idanuwa tace,    "Karb'i kawai wallahi ba komi, bana so in d'ora maka nauyi kai ma kana fama da kanka." 

Basarake ya b'ata fuska yace,   "Ba kya son in sami ladar ne?"

A cikin sanyi jiki tace

"Ina so amman bana son in takuraka don ALLAH kayi hak'uri ka karb'a." 

Basarake ya rungume hannuwanshi a k'irji yace,    "Ya aka yi baku dawo tare ba?"

Nan take fuskarta ta canza zuwa b'acin rai tace,    "'yar matsala ce a tsakaninmu amman komi ya wuce."

Ya tsareta da idanuwanshi masu saka ta shiga yanayi yace,     "Ki dad'a hak'uri kinji insha ALLAHU zaki ci ribar kykkyawan halayenki."

Munawwa tace,   "Nagode da fatan alkhairin da kake ta yimani." 

Ta fara tafiya zata shiga get d'in gidansu ya d'aga murya ya kira sunanta, ba shiri ta waigo saboda kiran sunanta da yayi  ya ratsa dukkan gangar  jikinta, a hankali yace "zan iya samun number wayarki?" 

Munawwa tayi saurin kallonshi a cike da mamaki tace,    "Me zaka yi da munber ta.?" 

Ya tako a hankali har ya kai kusa da ita sannan ya tsura mata kallonshi mai sata birkita lissafinta yace,   "Me ake yi da number idan an karb'a? " 

Munawwa a tak'aice tace,   "kira ko sak'o"

Yayi murmushi yace da ita "to aciki ke wanne kika fi so nayi maki? ko bakya son mu k'ulla zumunci ne?"

Munawwa tayi murmushi tace,   "Me zai hana?"

Yace   "OK bani number d'in."

Munawwaa ta fara karanto masa duka layinta biyu yana sakawa a k'aramar wayarshi da ko marfi babu, tana k'are fad'a masa tace    "zan iya shiga gida ko akwai wani abun kuma?" 

Yayi murmushinshi yace,    "Nagode sosai da lokacinki da kika bani, ALLAH ya hutar da gajiya."

Munara ta shige ya bita da kallo ranshi fari k'al saboda samun number'rta da yayi, sannan ya shigo ya iske Isah yana gyangyad'i yana tsaye yana kallonshi sai yayi luu ya dawo, tausayinshi ya kamashi matuk'a ya fito gidan ya zauna bisa kan wani dutsin, sannan ya danna kiran wayar Kakale suka sha firarsu, bayan sun k'are  ya kira Abbu yana sanar dashi  halin da ake ciki akan business d'in da yake k'ullawa da wani company anan kadunar. 
 
Munawwa kuwa koda ta shiga babban falonsu ta tarar da Mummy tana ta yiwa Munara fad'a, akan baro ta da tayi a makaranta ta tafowarta, wadda ta cika tayi fam saboda jin haushin fad'an da aka yimata akan Munawwa, a cikin muryar kuka tace,   "Sai da fa nace tazo muje ta tsaya fira da k'awayenta niko naji haushi na fisge key d'in na tafowata, tunda naga bata tashi zuwa gudan ba ita."

Munawwa ta katse ta tace,    "Ke dai kinji haushin sallamar bawanki da nayi, tunda bai zauna zaman jiranmu har mu tashi dawowa ba,don nace ya dawo za mu zo da kanmu shine ta dinga ciman zarafi a gaban mutane."

Mummy ta nunata da  yatsa tace,    "Don ubanki nawa kika saka kika sawo shi ne da zai ta zaman jiranki tun safe har biyu a makaranta?wallahi Munara ki canza hali saboda ko kad'an bakya kyautawa kanki da 'yar uwarki, ke kullum abu kad'an idan tayi sai ki zuk'ule shin waye baya da zuciya ne?, ke bakya ganin abunda kike yimata kuma a kullum tana hak'uri da halinki?, to idan kika kawo iskanci wallahi sai a bar mata motar ita kad'ai, ke kije kiji da bak'in halinki da ke kenan kullum sai an tada jijiyoyi akanki."

 Munara ta tashi fuu ta shige d'akinsu tana gunguni Mummy ta bita da kallo rik'e da baki tace,    "Ubangiji ya shiryeki Munara"


Nice taku🤝🏻

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*11*

Tana shiga d'aki ta fad'a kan gadonsu ta fashe da kuka,  saboda jin haushin fad'an da Mummy tayi mata akan wannan shegen kuturun. 

Basarake kuwa yana gidan har marece sannan yayi sallama dasu yayi masaukinshi, bayan ya k'are kimtsa kanshi ne ya fara turawa Munawwa text mai zafi a cikin harshen turanci, da wani layinshi daban na musamman ba tareda ya bayyana mata kansa ba, sannan ya jawo wayarshi ya kira Ummi har suka k'are gaisawa Munawwa bata mayar masa da reply ba, ya yi wani kayataccen murmushi sannan ya d'auko 'yar makararriyar  wayar shi da ko murfi babu ya danna mata kira da d'ayan layinta, ba wanda ya tura mata text da shi ba saboda yasa a ranshi zai yita wasa da hankalinta har sai ya kama ta a hannu sannan ya bayyana mata kanshi, aikuwa kira d'aya biyu ta d'aga saboda text d'in da ta gani ya bata mamaki sosai, don kalaman soyayya masu zafi aka yi amfani dasu, duk da batasan mai text d'in ba amman hak'ik'a ya burgeta sosai, a zuciyarta tana ta wasiwasin anya wannan text d'an aikinsu ne ya turo shi kuwa?, shiyasa da taga kira da new number tayi saurin d'auka domin ta tabbatar, a cikin sanyin jiki ta yi masa sallama shima ya amsa a cikin daddad'an muryarshi mai sakata nutsuwa, bayan sun gaisa Munawwa tace,   "ban fa gane ba."

Yayi murmushi mai sauti sannan yace, 

"Kina magana da sabon d'an aikin gidanku, da fatar dai baki manta shi ba?"

Munawwa tayi murmushi tareda tashi daga kwancen da take a kan kujera ta zauna,  sannan tace,   "taya zan manta ka ko?"

Basarake yace,   "Daman na kira ki ne kawai mu gaisa nagode sosai da lokacinki da kika bani." 

Tace "nima nagode"

 Sannan suka yi sallama, sai bayan sun k'are wayar ne Munawwa ta tuno da text d'in, aikuwa ta sake danna masa kira caraf ya d'auka yayi saurin fad'in,    "Ranki ya dad'e lafiya dai ko?."

Munawwa tayi k'asa da muryarta tace masa,   "Kayi hak'uri don ALLAH wani text ne na gani na zaci kai ka turo shi, amman kuma naga number ba d'aya ba shiyasa na shiga kokwanto."

Yayi murmushi sannan yace,    "text kuma?" 

Yayi maganar a cikin nuna alamun mamaki munawwa tayi murmushi tace,   "Eh shi ko ba kai ka turo ba?"

Yayi saurin fad'in "ba ni bane ranki ya dad'e ni wannan layin ma da kika gani shine kad'ai  dani."

Munawwa tace,   "OK ba damuwa shikenan nagode"

Kafin yace wani Abu ta datse kiran, a cikin mamakin to wanene mamallakin wannan daddad'an  text d'in ?. 

Bata koma bi ta kan text d'in ba taci gaba da harkokinta har zuwa dare, lokacin da tazo kwanciya ne tana duba wayarta ta k'ara ganin wani sabon text ya shigo, wanda yake d'auke da kalaman soyayya masu shiga jiki ana yi mata  sai da safe,  zuciyarta ta k'ara shiga rud'u duk da taji dad'in kalaman ba kad'an ba, shiyasa ma taso ta mayarda reply amman ta share kawai aranta tace,   "Sai ka gaji ka bayyana kanka." 

Ta ajiye wayar ta gyara kwanciyarta idonta ya kai gefen Munara, inda  take ta zuba chatting da honey'nta sai k'yalk'yala dariya ta keyi a cikin jin dad'in firar da suke yi. 

Munawwa ta juya sannan ta yi addu'ar bacci tana jiran bacci ya d'auketa, amman tunanin sabon mai aikinsu da wannan text's  d'in sun sakata a gaba, abun mamaki kuma da ta runtse idanuwanta fuskar honey'n Munara take gani a cikinsu, wanda ba kullum ne fuskar bata yi mata gizo ba tayi ta  fad'o mata a rai,wanda abun yana damunta matuk'a saboda abun kunyar da zuciyarta take so ta jawo mata, tareda hargitsin da take so ta jefata a ciki. 

Koda safiya ta waye tun da safe BASARAKE ya zo gidan, Munara ta saka shi ya wanke mata motarsu bayan ya k'are ne ya zauna yana hutawa, sai gata ta fito da shirinta na zuwa makaranta, tana zuwa ta jefa masa key d'in ba tareda tace da shi komi ba, ya d'ago fuskarshi yana kallonta har ta shige motar ta hakimce tana jiransa,k'aton glass d'inta bak'i make akan kyakkyawar  fuskarta sai taunar cingom take yi tana hurar hanci, ya jima yana jiran Munawwa ta fito amman shiru bata fito   ba, zuciyarshi ta shiga nazarin  me ya tsaidata har ne da bata fito ba,a ranshi yace,   "Watakil ita ba zata je makarantar ba" yana k'ok'arin tashi ne yaji muryar Munara akanshi tana fad'in, 

"Idan aikin ne ka gaji dashi ai sai kayi bayani a sallameka mallam,  don ni bana d'aukar k'ananan iskancin masu zuwa cin arzik'i gidannan ." 

Basarake ya tsura mata idanuwanshi sosai ranshi a b'ace,  tana had'a  ido dashi taji gabanta ya k'ire ya fad'i ba shiri, saboda tunda take bata tab'a had'a idanuwanta dashi ba sai ranar, ta waske tareda buga masa wani k'aton tsaki ta shige mota ta zauna,  domin bata son ya gano taji tsoronshi ya ce zai  rena ta. 

Basarake ya k'araso idonshi akanta yace,   "D'ayar hajiyar fa ko bada ita zamu je ba?"

Munara tana latsar waya ba taresa ta d'ago ba tace dashi,   "Ka shiga ka fito da ita to tunda har rashin ganinta ya dameka."

Yayi murmushi kamar baiji zafin maganarta ba ya shiga ya tada motar, yazo zasu fita get d'in ne isa ya duk'o yace,     "Adawo lafiya hajiya."

Munara ko kallo bai isheta ba balle ta amsa masa, dole Basaraken ne ya mayar masa da amsarsa izuwa murmushi sannnan suka fice, ya hau kan titi ne suka fara tafiya yace da ita,    "Ranki ya dad'e ya maganarmu ta kwana ne?, nifa har yanzu ina kan bakana fa, ko aikinnan ina yi  ne kawai dalilinki, don ALLAH ki daure ko yaya ne ki bani lokacinki."

Munara taji shi sarai amma tayi banza da shi sai latsar wayarta take yi, a cikin sanyin murya yace da ita,   "hak'ik'a So masifa ne wanda idan ya sako mutum a gaba baya ji kuma baya gani, ni mai sonki ne na gaske ki daure ki soni koda kad'an ne, idan kinyiman haka zan ta dawwama a matsayin bawanki,  tareda dukkan ahalin gidanku gaba d'aya."

Munara ta d'ago tareda watsa masa wani kallon wulak'anci tace,    "Wai kai da wa kake wad'anan surutan?, to bari kaji in gaya maka kuskuren da kake so kayi na k'arshe a wurina shine, ka sake bud'a k'azamin bakinka kace kana sona, wai kai me yasa bazaka banbance tazarar dake tsakaninmu ba?, nidai wallahi ka gama cutata a kalleka a kalleni ace wai kana sona it's ashame wallahi."

Ta k'are maganar da wata  murya kamar tayi kuka saboda ita a ganinta ya mata k'arshen reni da wulak'anci , Basarake baice da ita k'anzil ba har suka kai makarantar, ta b'alle murfin motar ta fita har ta fara tafiya ya kira sunanta, ba shiri ta waigo a take zucitarta ta buga dam! dam! , ya fito ya tako har kusa da inda take tsaye  yace da ita,  

"Tunda ke ba kya sona shin kin amince da in nemi soyayyata a wurin 'yar uwarki Munawwara?"

Munara  tayi 'yar k'aramar  dariya mai sauti  sannan tace,   "Meye nawa a ciki kaje kawai idan kun sasanta kanku shikenan zan taya ka murna." 

Basarake yana murmushinsa mai kama da yak'e yace,   "ba fushi?"

Munara tana dariya tace,   "fushin me kaje kawai daman da ita ka dace."

Ya saka hannu a aljihu yana dariyar mugunta yace,   "yanzu kina nufin ke bandace da ke ba kenan?"

Ta fara taku zata bar wajen tace dashi,   "ido mudu ne ai kaima kasani  kaje wurinta kawai zaku YI matuk'ar dacewa da juna."

Ta k'are maganar tana dariya yana kallonta a cike da mamakin halinta yace,   "Kin bani ita kenan?"

Tace, "Kyauta ma kuwa."

Zuwan wasu abokanta a wurin shi ya katse masu firar inda wata d'ayar abokiyarta tace,   "Shegiya muny wannan ko dashi za'ayi ne?"

Munara ta kai kallonta a kan bak'ar fuskarshi tayi mere tace,    "Direban gidanmu ne ko baku da idanuwa ne, ku kalleshi dakyau fa  ku kalleni ai ku kunsan cewa ruwa ba sa'ar kwando bane, ku dube shi fa da kyau dangin kutaren kuke so ku manna man indinga...... ."

Ta kwatanta yanda kutare suke yi da hannunsu tareda yin maganarsu ta hantahanta tace,  "a bani na Annabi" Gaba d'aya abokan suka sa dariya, wata daga cikinsu tana dariya tace,   "Munny baki da kyau fa. "

Munara tana dariya tace,   "Sai akan fuskata"Suka bar wurin suna tafe  tana  kwatanta yanda kutaren suke yi  tana kwaikwayon maganarsu,  suko sai dariya suke kwasa har suka  k'ule wa ganinshi yana tsaye a wurin yana kallon hanyar da suka bi.

*🔥BAKON YANAYA🔥*

*12*

A cikin sanyi jiki yaja k'afafunshi ya shiga motar tareda fisgarta da k'arfi ya bar makarantar, koda yaje gidan ya tarar da Malam Isah sai sauraren radio'nshi yake yi yana gyangyad'i,har saida yayi horn da k'arfi sannan yayi zumbur ya tashi ya bud'e masa gate ya shigo, bayan yayi parking yazo ya zauna saman bencin Malam Isah tareda janyo wayarshi yana danna kiran wayar Munawwaa, saboda rashin ganinta ya dameshi sosai har SAI da wayar ta kusa tsinkewa sannan ta d'aga acikin wata 'yar siririyar murya tace, 

"Hello" ba shiri tsikarshi tayi yarr! A cikin tattausan lafazi yace,     "Ranki ya dad'e barka da safiya dafatar dai lafiya ce ta b'oye ki.". 

Munawwa ta lumshe idanuwanta saboda jin dad'in maganarshi tace    "Kaina ne yake ciyo jiya ko baccin kirki bansamu nayi ba shiyasa yau banje makarantar ba"

Basarake har cikin ranshi yaji zafin jin da yayi bata da lafiya a cikin muryar damuwa yace,     "Subhanalllah Uwar d'akina ALLAH ya baki lafiya, bansan ya zanyi in ganki ba da nazo na duba ki."

Munara ta k'ara lumshe idanuwanta tace,    "Ayya sorry ba damuwa ai ko hakan ma nagode sosai."

Yana murmushi yace,   "Naso ace na ganki ko don inga ya yanayin jikin naki yake, amman ba komi ALLAH ya baki lafiya."

Itama tayi murmushi tace,   "Kar ka damu ai nama ji sauk'i yanzu zanje inyi wanka insha ALLAH da naji k'arfin jikina zan fito sai mu gaisa." yana murmushin jin dad'i yace,   "ALLAH ya k'aro afuwa Uwar d'akina."

Idonta a lumshe tace,   "Nagode sosai da adduarka."

Yace   "Ba komi yiwa kai ne."

Sannan suka yi sallama ta ajiye wayar tareda yin shiru tana nazarin kirki irin na sabon d'an aikinsu, har ga ALLAH taji dad'in damuwar da taga yayi da ita sannan uwa uba muryarshi da take ji har tsakiyar zuciyarta,a cikin wani Kuzarin da ya ziyarceta saboda wayar da suka yi taji kwata kwata ma ciyon ya tafiyarshi, don daman cen damuwar da ta saka aranta ne yasa ta rashin bacci da wuri, wanda yayi sanadin ciyon kanta saboda bashin baccin da taci ne yake tambayarta, domin kuwa raba dare tayi tana tunanin abunda yake shirin faruwa da ita waton soyayyar honey'n Munara da ta dabaibaye mata zuciya, wanda ko rufe idanuwa tayi shi ne take kallo yana yi mata Murmushi, a gefe d'aya kuma wannan d'an aikin nasu da Muryarshi tareda k'wayar idanuwanshi da suke sakata shiga a cikin wani yanayi, wanda ita kanta bazata iya fassara shi ba, sannan wannan texts da aka yo mata jiya ya tsaya mata arai abun mamaki yau ma tun da sassafe an k'ara turo mata wani ana yimata ina kwana, wanda ta maida reply da cewa,    "Plss wanene?" amman har zuwa time d'in ba reply, ta yunk'ura ta tashi tareda jan k'afa ta shige toilet minti biyar a tsakani sai gata ta fito tana tsane  ruwa da towel d'in dake d'aure a jikinta, ta zauna a kan gadonsu tareda dafe kanta da taji ciyon ya dawo mata, ta sanadiyyar tuno Fuskar HAIDAR d'in Munara da a kullum ta ga fuskarshi a cikin kwayar idanuwanta sai taji kirjinta ya buga dam! dam!, ta lallab'a ta shafa mai tareda shafa hoda kad'an sai lips, ta bud'e ma'ajiyar kayansu ta saka wata doguwar rigar material wadda ake yiwa suna da bubu, ta d'auko k'aramin gyale ta d'ora akanta bayan ta shafa d'an turare samasama sannan ta janyo plat shoes d'inta ta saka, sannan ta fito babban falonsu inda ta tarar da Mummy tana kallon Arewa, tana zuwa Mummy ta washe fuskarta tace da ita,   "Jikin ya saki kenan?" 

Munawwa tana murmushi tace  "Eh na d'an ji k'arfin jikina yanzu shiyasa ma nace, bari na fito na d'an yawata koda jikin nawa zai saki bari inje in d'an zagayo in dawo Mummy."

Mummy taji dad'in ganin 'yarta ta washe saboda tun d'azu da  taji shiru bata je gaidata ba, sannan taga Munara ita kad'ai ta tafi Makarantar shiyasa ta je ta dubota, jikinta da d'an zafi ta matsa mata sai da tasha d'an tea sannan tasha magani, ko zaman da take YI anan falon idonta kawai ne ga Tv'n amman zuciyarta tana cen tunanin 'yarta, sai gashi taganta ta fito shiyasa taji dad'in ganinta, har ta fice idonta yana kanta saboda farin cikin da tayi na ganin ta warware, domin tsananin son da take yimata saboda biyayyar da take yimata sau da k'afa ya sa nata son daban yake a cikin zuciyarta, sab'anin Munara da a kullum sai tayi abunda ba daidai ba,wanda dole har sai ta b'ata ran Mummy tayi ta  fad'a,  saboda Mummy bata wasa amman duk da haka sai da Munara taso ta gagareta, saboda d'aurin gindin da take samu a wurin Daddynsu. 

 Basarake yana k'are wayar da ita ne ya kalli Malam isah da tun d'azu yake ta kallonshi, saboda jin firar da sukeyi ne yasa ya gano da Munawwa yake yi, a cike da mamaki ya kalleshi yace,    "Anya Ali baka yi tsaga da fad'i ba?, naga fa kana son ka cusa kanka wurin yarinyar nan, baka gudun abunda zai je ya dawo ne?, ni dai shawarar da zan baka don ALLAH ka rufawa kanka asiri ka tsaya a matsayinka, bana son kayi abunda zai sa a kore ka daga aikin ka ."

Basarake yayi murmushi mai sauti yace,    "Baba fa kasan bawa baya ci sai rabonshi, ka sani ko rabon ne yasa na zo gidannan."

Malam isah ya zaro idanuwa yace,   "Rabo fa kace?"

Basarake ya gyad'a masa kai yana 'yar dariya yace,   "Shifa Baba."

Yace "cabd'i jam to gamu gani idan tusa zata hura wuta."

Basarake baisan lokacin da dariya tazo masa ba, yana cikin dariyar ne sai ga Munawwa ta fito a hankali take taku har ta k'araso wurinsu ta fara gayar da Malam Isah sannan ta kai kallonta ga Basarake tace,   "Sannunka"

Basarake da tun da ya tsinkayota yaji kirjinshi ya buga da k'arfi saboda kyawon da yaga tayi masa sosai duk da ba wata kwalliya ce tayi ba, a ranshi ya furta cewa "har ga ALLAH Munawwa kina burgeni takunki da komai naki yayi man"

Wannan nazarin da ya lula shi ya hana shi jin abunda tace dashi har sai da ta k'ara kai kallonta a kanshi tace,   

"Ni zan koma sai anjima." 

Sannan yayi firgigit ya wayance ya tashi tsaye yana kakkab'e rigarshi tareda fad'in, 

"ya jikinki?"

Tana wasa da yatsunta tace da "sauk'i" don bata son su had'a idanuwa, saboda gudun ya gano yanayin da take shiga idan sun had'a idanuwan, har ta fara tafiya zata bar wajen ne ya d'an d'aga murya yace,    "akwai maganar da nike son muyi dake fa idan ba damuwa", Munawwa tayi saurin kallonshi saboda fad'uwar gaban da ta sameta a lokaci d'aya, batareda tace dashi komi ba ta fara tafiya zuwa wajen rumfar motar su wajen wasu bulo bulo da aka zagaye sabuwar shukar wata flower  da su, ta sami d'aya daga cikin bulon ta zauna tareda duk'ar da kanta tana wasa da yatsunta tana tunani aranta, akan ALLAH yasa dai ba ganota yayi ba,  don ita kam wannan abin kunya ne agareta ace ya gano halin da take ciki game dashi. 

Shima ya sami d'aya daga cikin buloluwan ya zauna, sannan ya sauke ajiyar zuciya yace,    "Munawwara kinsan tun farkon zuwana  gidannan saboda Munara ne, amman hankalinki da nutsuwarki da na hango yasa naji kamar saboda ke nazo gidan,shiyasa naji zan iya fad'a mk damuwata ko ALLAH zaisa ki taimakeni,  har ga ALLAH ina son Munara amman ita  naga sam bata d'auki hakan da muhimmanci ba,  gashi ni kuma ina  sonta sosai na rasa ta hanyar da zanbi ta fuskance ni, don ALLAH idan ba damuwa ina son ki shawo man kanta duk da nasan hakan ba mai sauk'i bane, amman ina son kiyi man iya k'ok'arinki koda ALLAH zai sa ta fuskanceni WALLAHI ni da gaske nike sonta."

K'irjin Munawwa ya buga dam! dam! a take ta mik'e tsaye tareda sauke ajiyar zuciya tace,   "Me zai hana ka tareta da kanka ka sanar da ita, ina jin hakan zai fi sauk'i akan ka aika d'an sak'o."

Ta k'are maganar a cikin b'acin rai wanda ya kasa b'oyuwa a akan fuskarta, Basarake ya kalleta da kyau na tsawon minti biyu sannan yace  "kiyi hak'uri naga kamar ranki ya b'ace, kinga abunda nike jiwa tsoro tun a yanzu gashi na fara fuskantar matsala daga gareki ballantana ita da nasan har marina tayi akan son da na furta mata tun a ranar da muka fara had'uwa da ita, Munawwara ki taimakeni ki shawo man kanta saboda  ALLAH ne ya jarabeni da sonta ba yin kaina bane"

Da yasan yanda maganganunshi suke zafi a zuciyarta da bai k'ara  fad'ar wad'annan maganganun ba,ba shiri hawayen da take mak'alewa suka sauko akan fuskarta, wad'anda ita kanta idan za'a titsiyeta batasan ko na minene ba. 

Hankalin Basarake ya k'ara tashi saboda ganin saukar ruwan hawaye  akan fuskarta, da sauri ya zo gabanta ya tsaya tareda rungume hannunwanshi yana k'are mata kallo, a cikin sanyin muryarshi yace da ita,   "Nasan ni bankai matsayin da zance ina sonta ba, shiyasa saboda tausayina kike kuka ko? to naji idan har bazata soni ba shin ke zaki iya taimako na ki share mani hawayena?????"

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*13*

Munawwa tayi saurin kallonshi ido cikin ido zuciyarta  sai aikin gudu take yi da sauri da sauri, abunda ta hango cikin k'wayar idonsh yasa tayi saurin janye nata idanuwan, hawayenta basu daina sauka ba ta saka hannu ta share fuskarta,  sannan ta aro jarumta ta kalleshi ba tareda ta bari sun had'a idanuwa ba tace,   "Aliyu ko? meyasa zaka yi saurin yanke jink'ai har ka furta wannan magana?, anya ko da gaske kake sonta kuwa?, nidai nasan shi so idan ya shiga zuciyar mutum ba zai yi saurin fita cikin k'ank'anin lokaci ba, sai dai in daman cen son bai kai cikakken so ba, ni ka ganni nan bantab'a so ba amman jikina yana bani matuk'ar na dulmiya a cikinshi,  to abune mai wahala in cireshi ta farad d'aya domin ba  cirar k'aya bane balle ace da ancire shikenan an daina jin zafin, saboda shi cirar so a zuciya dafi ne dashi haka yake tamkar k'unar wuta, da idan tsautsayi yasa  ta k'ona mutum to tabbas zai dad'e yana fama da jinya, kuma koda ya warke daga baya to shi tambon konuwar yana nan daram ajikii, don wata k'unar ma duk tsawon shekarun da mutum zai yi a duniya tambon yana nan ba zai tab'a gogewa ba."

Basarake ya tsura mata idanuwa tamkar bai tab'a ganinta ba, saboda maganganunta sun ratsa shi sosai haka sun k'ara mata girma da matsayi a cikin zuciyarshi, Munawwa ta d'ago ido cikin ido tace dashi,   "Me yasa a matsayinka na ma'abocin son ka ke ik'irarin wata ta taimake ka ta karb'i sonka bacin kuma ga wadda kake bala'in son a kusa da ita?, anya bazaka hargitsa d'an zaman lafiyan da ya yi saura tsakanina da 'yar uwata ba?"

Ga baki d'aya kan Basaraken ya k'ulle ya rasa me ya dace yayi kuma a wannan lokaci, yana k'ok'arin yin magana ne sai ga kiran Ummi ya shigo a wayarshi, jikinshi yana rawa ya fiddo 'yar wayarshi da ko marfi babu don yasan duk mai kiran bai wuce Ummin ko Abbu ko kuma kakale sai Abokinshi don su kawai suke da wannan Number, sai kuma ita kanta Munawwara da ya kirata da ita. 

Yana duba wayar yaga Ummi ce sai ga wani farin ciki ya lullub'e shi ya kara wayar a kunnenshi yana gaidata, Munawwa ta fara taku zata bar rumfar ya yi saurin janye wayar a kunnenshi yace,   "Zo ki gaisa da Ummi."

Cak! ta tsaya ba tareda ta k'ara wani taku ba da kanshi ya k'araso wurinta yana fad'in,   "Ummi ga 'yar ki nan ku gaisa."

Ya mik'a wa Munawwa  wayar ba tareda ta kalleshi ba ta karb'i wayar ta fara gaida ta a cikin ladabi wanda daman cen d'abi'arta ce girmama babba, Ummi bayan ta amsa gaisuwar neta yi ta sanyawa Munawwara Albarka, saboda jikinta ya bata surukarta ce kuma ga dukkan alamu ba k'aramin so Basaraken yake yimata ba tunda har baiji shayin had'a su ba, don a halayyarshi da ta sani shi ba ma'abocin kula mata bane balle har ya so budurwar tasan da ahalinshi, don randa take dashi bai tab'a bata wata budurwarshi suka gaisa ba, shiyasa ta saki jiki a cikin farin cikin ganin nesa ta  kusu zuwa kusa addu'ar da sukeyi a kullum safiya  ta karb'u. 

 Munawwa ko a cikin jin dad'in addu'ar da Ummin ke yimata take ta fad'in    "Ameen Ameen Ummi."

A cikin tsantsar farin ciki Ummi tace da ita,  

"Miye sunan 'yar tawa ne?."

Ta sunne kai kamar tana ganinta tace da ita,    "Sunana Munawwarah."

Ummi tace,   "Sannu 'yata kinji ALLAH yayi maki albarka ya k'ara had'a kawunanku, kuma ya nuna muna lokacin."

Ba shiri Munawwa ta kai kallonta gareshi sannan cikin sanyin murya tace,   "Ameen" saboda jin nauyin fad'ar Ameen d'in domin a take taji kunyarta Ummin ta lullub'eta, da sauri ta mik'a masa wayar taje jikin wata mota da ke rumfar ta jingina bayanta, tana jiran ya k'are wayar taji me yake nufi da abunda yayi yanzu?,  Ummi ko a cikin tsantsar farin ciki wanda ya kasa b'oyuwa akan harshenta tace,   "Ka kira hajiya ka had'a ta  ita su gaisa, ko don a sami natsuwarta don yau tun da safe ta tub'ure muna sai ankawota wurinka,dak'yar aka shawo kanta gashi duka layukanka ankira bata shiga  wannan ce kawai aka yi sa'a."

Yana murmushin jin dad'i yace,   "Ai yanzu zan kirata Ummi."

Sannan suka yi sallama  ya danna kiran wayar Kakale kira d'aya tayi caraf ta d'auka tareda cewa, 

"Tunda ba zaka dawo ba ai ni sai a kawoni wajenka mu zauna tare."

Basarake ya fara dariya mai sauti yace,  

"Haba kakalenah yanzu dai kwantar da hankalinki ga Matata nan ku gaisa." 

Ba shiri Munawwa ta kalleshi shima ya k'ura mata idanuwanshi ya mik'a mata wayar tareda yi mata wani kallo wanda ya saka tsigarta tashi yarrrr!, maganar da taji a cikin wayar ne yasa ta karb'i wayar jikinta a sanyaye tayi sallama, 
Cike da farin ciki kakale tace,   "Waton ke kika rik'e man shi a kaduna ko?,  idona idonki tunda naga k'wace kike son kiyi man na maigida, to kisani aro ne zan baki nice ta k'arfen koda auren aka yi, kar ki d'auka ko yafi sonki dani ni ne a'a na bashi damar ya k'aro aure ne kawai don in tausaya maki  ba don na kasa ba ko yanzu da k'arfi na, amman duk da haka ga Amanarshi  nan na bar maki ALLAH ya nuna muna lokacin, kuma yasa ina da rabon ganin 'ya'yan Aliyu a duniya." 

Munawwa cike da mamaki tace
 "Ameen" kakale tana dariya  tace 
"mik'awa Basaraken wayar inji me nayi mashi da zai yiman kishiya.?"

Ta k'are maganar a cikin farin ciki Munawwa ta cire wayar daga kunnenta ta mik'a masa,  amman fa ta kasa d'auke idanuwanta daga kanshi tana yi masa kallon  tuhuma, saboda tsananin mamakin da ya cika mata zuciya sai maimaita sunan BASARAKE take tayi a zuciyarta, a cikin ko in kula ya karb'i wayar suka yi ta barkwancinsu da suka saba sannan ya kashe wayar, don baya son ya fad'i wani abunda zai sa Munawwa ta  gano wani abu dangane da shi, har sai lokacin da yayi mata kamu dumu dumu sannan ya bayyana mata kanshi. 

Ya soke wayar a cikin aljihunshi sannan ya kalleta wanda itama kanta idonta akanshi suke, saboda gubar mamakin da ya shayar  da ita a lokaci d'aya, a cikin sanyin muryarta tace dashi,   "Me kake nufi da wannan abun da kayi?, idan daga baya suka gano gaskiya fa me zaka ce dasu?, sannan wane AUREn ne naji ana fad'a? bayan kasan ni da kai ba wata alak'a makamanciyar wannan." 

Basarake yayi wani murmushin rashin damuwa yace da ita,   

"Duk hakan zasu faru ne idan har kin bani dama ranki ya dad'e."

Munawwa tayi mashi wani kallon kama rena ni tace,    "Ita Munara kayi yaya da sonta?, kana nufin idan bata amince ba sai kayi auren huce haushi dani kenan?"

Basarake ya daburce a cikin rud'ewa da tambayoyinta yace,   "Matsayinki a wurina daban yake  Ranki ya dad'e da zaki bani dama da kin gano gaskiyar abunda na  fad'a maki."

Munawwa ta harareshi tace,   "Idan har ace na baka dama to ya zaka yi da son bala'i da kake yiwa  'yar uwata a cikin zuciyarka?"

Yayi murmushinsa mai kama da yak'e ba tareda yace da ita k'anzil ba, Munawwa har ta fara taku a hankali sannan ta waigo tace,   "Basarake ko? to kaje kayi nazari da kyau idan har zaka iya had'a taura biyu a baki kuma ace ka cisu a lokaci d'aya."

Basarake yayi murmushi yace,   "Ranki ya dad'e takunki daban yake da na saura, hak'ik'a jikina yana bani cewa ke alkhairi ce a wurina, alfarmar da na rok'a a wurinki tun  farko ki k'ok'arta man na baki lokaci kije kiyi nazari da kyau". 

Munawwa ta maka masa wata harara sannan ta juya ta fara tafiya, amman zuciyarta cike da mamakin maganganunshi take yi , gefe d'aya kuma zuciyarta cike take fam da jin zafin aikin janyo masa  soyayyar Munara da ya d'ora mata nauyin nemowa, har taci karo da Mummy bata ma sani ba saboda yanda ta lula cikin sak'e sak'en zuci. 

 Tayi saurin fad'in,   "Sorry Mummy"

Mummy ta kalleta da kyau tana karantar yanayinta sannan tace,   "Yanzu wajen ma zanje domin in ga me ya tsayar dake tun d'azu?."

Munawwa ta saki ranta tana 'yar dariya tace,    " Aliyu ne ya kira 'yan gidansu ya bani su muka gaisa."

Mummy ta kalleta da kyau tace,   "anya Munawwa wannan kula bata yi yawa ba?, sau biyu naga kinyi  fad'a da 'yar uwarki  akanshi, ko dai akwai wani abu ne dake tsakaninku bansani ba?" 

Munawwa ta zaro idanuwa tana dariya tace,   "Ba fa wani Abu Mummy, ai kinsan bazan b'oye maki ba idan akwai kece zaki riga kowa sani."

Tana k'are maganar ta shige d'akinsu tana murmushi, Mummy ta bita da kallo tareda yin tagumi amman fa zuciyarta ta tabbatar mata da akwai wani abun da Munawwa ta ke b'oye mata, duk da tasan halin 'yarta da kyakkyawan halayenta na  kirkin amman wannan kulawar da take baiwa d'an aikin nan yasha bambam da na saura. 

Ta sauke tagumin tareda cewa,   "ALLAH ya shige muna a gaba"saboda jikinta yayi sanyi sosai saboda tana jin tsoron ace son shi take yi. 

Har Munara ta dawo daga makaranta Munawwa bata saki jikinta ba, zuciyarta sai kai da komo take yi don har ga ALLAH tana jin wani abunda ta kasa gano menene game da wannan d'an aikin nasu, ita dai tasan tana son ganinshi da sauraren muryarshi, uwa uba k'wayar idanuwanshi da ko tuno yanda yake kallonta tayi sai taji tsikarta ta tashi, shin me hakan yake nufi wai?, ta tambayi kanta hakan yafi shurin masaki saboda ita dai tasan ba sonshi take ba, to amma meyasa fatan auren da Umminshi da Kakalenshi suka yi masu, taji shi har cikin b'argon jikinta saboda jin dad'in fatan da suka yi mata akan aurenta dashi, shin me hakan yake nufi wai????. 

Da marece sakaliya Munara tana kwance tana waya da honey'nta Munawwa ta shigo d'akin ta sami wuri ta zauna, da taga dai bata da niyyar k'are wayar ne tace da ita,    "Magana nazo muyi ni da ke fa."

Munara ta kalleta tareda maka mata wata uwar harara taci gaba da wayarta, Munawwa tayi saurin zuwa zata fisge wayar saboda jin zafin sauraren firar da sukeyi, Munara tayi saurin tashi zaune ta kalli fuskar Munawwa yanda take huci sannan tace,   "Honey two minutes"
  Sai da ya bata dama sannan ta kashe wayar ta kalleta ranta a hade tace,   "Sannu Uwata ina jinki Tsugudidin ne ya motsa ko to fad'i meke tafe da......?"

Kafin ta kai k'arshen maganar sai kiran Basarake ya sake shigowa ga wayar Munawwara, ta kai dubanta ga screen d'in wayar taga  sunan ALIYU b'aro b'aro ya fito tayi saurin amsa danne wayar da niyyar ta datse kiran, sai gashi tayi kuskure ta latsa receiving kiran    sannan tayi cooling temper d'inta ta zauna idanunta akan fuskar Munara ta fara magana tace,   "Magana ce nike son muyi akan sabon mai aikinmu, wanda ke kinsani soyayyar da yake yimaki ce ta janyo yasa yake  zama a gidannan, ki daure ki bashi dama saboda WALLAHI yana tsananin k'aunarki tsakaninshi da ALLAH gashi iyayenshi sun matsa masa akan yayi aure, tunda shi wannan HAIDAR d'in ya kasa zuwa gidannan, to me zai hana ki amincewa wanda ya sadaukar da lokacinshi ya ajiye komi ya dawo gidanku, ya zauna a k'ark'ashin ikon ki kina bautar dashi kuma duk yaji ya gani saboda soyayyarki, ki daure ki bashi dama ko yaya ne saboda mai sonka har abada bazai zo d'aya da mak'iyinka ba, don bakisan inda rana zata fad'uwa ba watak'ila ma shine mijin amman kike ta faman wulak'antashi........."

Tassssss! taji saukar Mari akan fuskarta d'aga idon da zatayi sai ga Munara a tsaye tana huci, a cikin b'acin rai tace da ita "yanzu Munara ni kika mara?" Munara ta nunata da d'an yatsa tace,     "Ladar b'ata man lokaci da kika yi na tsaya sauraren wad'annan banzayen maganganu naki ne, kinci daraja ma da ban had'a maki da duka ba WALLAHI , to bari kiji kije ki fad'a masa bana sonshi mai sonshi bana k'aunarshi duniya da lahira, ni Munara bazan tab'a sonshi ba kije na bar maki shi tunda kina sonshi sai ki aureshi ai, tunda dani dake  komi namu iri d'aya ne ki bashi farin cikin da ni na kasa bashi ya daina zama k'ark'ashina ya dawo naki, sannan kice bansan inda rana take fad'uwa ba nasani mana, ranar zata fad'i akanki ne sai ki aureshi amman nikam bazan tab'a auren Matsiyacin da bai aje ba bai baiwa wani ajiya ba, ko a kyawon halittar ma ba abun kai gida gashi kuturu ga bak'ar fuska, ke Uwar da ta haifesa ma WALLAHI ALLAH ya tsine mata,  tunda har tayi sanadin   zuwanshi duniya ta kawo muna mugun iri a cikin doron k'asa. "

Basarake ya YI saurin katse wayar sannan ya danna Recording d'in maganar da yayi , sannan ya runtse idonshi tareda dafe kanshi idanuwanshi suka yi masa jawur, a cikin k'ank'anin lokaci saboda tsananin  b'acin rai.

Munara

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*14*


Mummy dake k'ok'arin shigowa d'akin wannan maganar ta Munara ta shiga kunnuwanta, fuu tayi saurin shigowa a zafafe sai kawai Munara taji saukar Mari akan fuskarta,  zumbur ta juya idanuwanta suka   had'u da na Mummy, yanayin da taga fuskar Mummyn ne yasa tasha jinin jikinta, domin zuciyarta ta tabbatar mata da taji lafuzzanta, a cikin fushi muryarta a shak'e saboda tsananin b'acin rai tace,     "Wannan tarbiyya ta zagin manya mu ba'a gidannan kika sameta ba, don ni kam har yanzu ina kaffa kaffa da wanda nasan ya haife ni don samun tubarraki, zaki ga hukuncin da zan d'auka a kanki matuk'ar gigi ya sake janki na koma jin irin wad'anan maganganun a bakinki, ki bi duniya a sannu saboda shi d'an hakin da ka rena shine yake tsole ma idanu, ke kuma zo ki bani maburkin miya nayi ta nema banganshi ba."

Tana k'are maganar ta fita da sauri a cikin fushin da ya kasa b'oyuwa akan fuskarta, Munawwa ta bi Munara da da wani kallon takaici saboda jin zafin zagin Ummin Basarake da tayi, a cikin d'aga Murya tace 

"Kinyi na farko kinyi na k'arshe muddin kika sake zagar masa Uwa a gabana sai na baki Mamaki wallahi." 

Tazo zata fita d'akin ne Munara ta yi saurin shak'o wuyan rigarta ta baya a cikin masifa tace,   "Idan ansake d'in me zakiyi?"

Cikin fushi Munawwara tace "dukan bala'i zan yi maki a cikin gidannan wallahi."

Munara tayi saurin sakin rigarta tana wata irin dariya tana nunata da hannu, saboda tsananin dariyar da Munawwa ta bata ba shiri, cen ta d'ago da niyyar ta k'ara yin zagin taga wayam ta ficewarta, a ranta tace,      "Wai duka abun dariya ai WALLAHI da hannunki bazai sake dukan kowa ba, don sai na kakkaryasu yanda ba zasu sake yi maki amfani ba."

Ta kwanta kan gadon tareda shafo gefen da Mummy ta mareta tace,    "dad'in abun dai NI na fara yiwa 'yar son taki marin."

Ta yi maganar tareda murgud'a baki ta janyo wayarta tareda dannawa honey'nta kira har ta tsinke bai d'auka ba, tayi kira ya fi a kirga kuma tana ringing bai d'auka ba har ta gaji ta koma tura masa texts nan d'inma ba alamun an duba bale taga reply, ta k'ara gwada kiran aikuwa sai gashi carab ya d'auka yanayin da taji muryarshi ne yasa taji gabanta ya fad'i ba shiri, a cikin rawar murya tace   "Honey lafiya.?"

A cikin murya mai cike da tsananin b'acin rai yace     "Baby yanzu Abbuna ya kirani yana sanar dani ya nema min Mata, don haka kiyi hak'uri ki nemi wani ni kam bazan aure ki ba  sannan ya zama dole in rabu dake ko ina so ko bana so, zan saka hannu biyu in rungumi matar da ALLAH ya zab'a man wadda itace ta dace dani ba ke ba, daga yau na yanke duk wata alak'a da ke tsakaninmu ko a waya kar kiyi tunanin sake jina ballantana ki ganni a fili.......... ."

Diffff! Ya kashe wayar bakinta yana rawa saboda tashin hankali ta kurma wata irin k'atuwar k'ara, ta fara birgima akan gadon a cikin rasa hankalinta da tayi na wuccin gadi, sai ga Mummy da Daddy da yake shigowa gidan a lokacin Munawwa tana bayansu, kamar an jefo su haka suka fad'o d'akin zuciyoyinsu sai bugu suke yi da sauri da sauri, saboda tashin hankalin da suka shiga ganin yanayin da take ciki ne yasa suka rufo kanta kowa yana rige rigen kamota domin aji meke faruwa?, Daddy yana rik'ota ta yi cikinshi kamar wata Kura ta gartsa masa cizo, duk da zafin ya ratsa shi amman bai bi ta kai ba sai k'ok'arin janyota yake yi, Mummy tana ganin inda ta cije Daddyn ya fasa har da jini tayi saurin d'auketa da wani shahararren Mari har sai da ta k'ara gigicewa, ta tashi sama ta fad'o kan gadon ta tareda k'wallah wata k'arar sannan ta sume,sai kumfa yake fita a bakinta da k'arfi Daddy yace,      "Baby ki tashi kar ki mutu ki barni." 

Sai hawaye shaaaaa akan fuskarshi ya kinkimota ajikinshi yazo zai fita Mummy ta biyo bayanshi ko mayafi babu saboda rud'ewar da tayi, yayi saurin kallonta yace 

"Taku d'aya idan kin k'ara a nan da niyyar biyomu  a bakin aurenki, kuma ki sani Wallahi muddin 'yata ta mutu sai nayi shari'a dake."

Yana k'are fad'ar maganar ya fice da sauri ya sakata mota Munawwa tayi saurin shiga kafin ma ta rufe k'ofar  ya fisgi motar da k'arfi ya fice, Isah maigadi ya bisu da ALLAH ya kiyaye da mamakin  yanayin da yaga Munara a ciki, a ranshi kuma yace "ita kuma Uwar tsiwar me ya sameta ne?."

A rud'e yake tuk'in wanda ALLAH ne kawai ya kaishi asibitin, yanayinta da aka gani yasa aka shiga emergency room da ita gaggauce, likitoci suka rufu kanta domin k'ok'arinsu na son ceto rayuwarta da take barazanar rabuwa da gangar jikinta. 

Daddy sai safa da Marwa yake yi yayin da Munawwa take ta wani kuka a cikin tashin hankali, domin itama taji tsoron ganin wannan yanayi da taga 'yar uwarta a ciki, don iya saninta da wayonsu bata tab'a shiga makamancin wannan hali ba duk ko artabun da suke sha da Mummy kullum safiya.

Har magriba tayi amman ba'a fito da Munara ba dak'yar Daddy yaje yayo sallah a masallacin da ke asibitin ya dawo, inda ya tarar da Munawwa a zaune saman d'an gyalen da ta yafo ko  wanda da alama itama sallahr ta k'are yi, sannan ta shi tsaye tareda kakkab'e gyalen ta yafa tareda ciro hijabin da ta ara ta baiwa maishi, sannan ta zauna suka City gaba da zaman jiran tsammani, aiko sai ga ta anfito da ita akan gado an shiga da ita wani d'aki na musamman wanda ita kad'ai ce a cikinshi, su Daddy suka biyo bayan nurses d'in da hanzari, Daddy sai tambayarsu yake yi a rud'e   " Ya jikinnata da sauk'i ko?,  ta farfad'o dai ko? numfashinta ya daidaita?"

Wata daga cikinsu tace,   "Kayi hak'uri zata sami sauk'i da ta farfad'o insha ALLAH, amman kaje Dr yana son ganinka."

Daddy sai da yaje jikin gadon ya ri'ko hannunta tareda fad'in,    "Sannu baby."

Duk da yasan ba amsa masa zatayi ba don bata ma san inda kanta yake ba har a lokacin, sannan ya fice yana ta waigenta ya nufi d'akin likitan wanda da daman cen sun saba da juna, bayan sun d'an fara gaisawa sama sama ne likitan ya fara yimasa bayanin abunda bincikensu ya bayar dallah dallah, inda ya sanar dashi akwai abunda taji ko ta gani wanda ya d'aga mata hankali sosai, wanda ya kasa d'aukar kwakwalwarta har  yayi sanadin da ta rasa hankalinta na wuccin gadi, sannan shine ya janyo mata gushewar numfashinta ta sume. 

Daddy hankalinshi ya k'ara tashi a cikin rawar baki yace,   "Yanzu dai komi normal ko? Don bana son inji wata mummunar magana daga bakinka wadda ta k'unshi wata matsala ga Baby."

Likitan yayi murmushi yace,    "Lafiya k'alau zata falka sai dai......  "

Daddy ya YI saurin katse shi da fad'in    "sai dai mi?"

Likitan yace   "Rashin k'arfin jikinta da shi sai a hankali zata warware, amman zamu rik'eta tsawon kwana biyu anan kafin lokacin muna bata kulawa." 

Daddy ya sauke ajiyar zuciya yace,    "Alhmdllh nagodewa Allah."

Likitan yana dariya yace,    "Yau an tab'o file d'in 'yar gaban goshi."

Daddy yana dariyar yace,     "Ciyon Baby ai ciyona ne."

A tare suka fito suka shiga d'akin da aka kwantar da Munara, koda suka je har Nurse's d'in sun d'aura mata gorar k'arin ruwa  sun fice, Munawwa ce kawai zaune akan kujerar roba tayi tagumi tana kallonta,tareda shiga nazarin son sanin abunda ya janyowa Mata  shiga cikin wannan hali. 
 
Shigowarsu ne yasa ta daina tunanin da take yi Daddy yana kallonta yaji ta bashi tausayi duk da yafi tausayin Munara sau dubu akanta, ta tashi ta bashi kujerar da nufin ya zauna a cikin ladabi, yayi saurin  yace da ita   "Aa zo muje in kai kgida yanzu in je in dawo saboda sai ta kwana biyu a nan, kafin nan ansamu jikinta ya warware."

Munawwa tace,   "Ni zan kwana da ita kaje gidan ka kwana da safe sai ka dawo."

Ya maka mata wata harara yace,   "Baby asibiti ai bazan iya runtsawa ba, in haka ne dai bari inje in d'auko maku abun buk'ata sai in dawo, ai ni jinya ta sameni nida kwana gida sai an sallameta  sannan."

Aiko ba ita  ba har likitan sai da suka yi 'yar dariya saboda yanayin yanda yayi maganar, ya fice likitan ya bishi munawwa ta maida kallonta akan fuskar Munara da a lokaci d'aya duk ta rame, a ranta tayi mata addu'ar ALLAH ya bata lafiya kuma ya sauya mata wasu halaye nata marasa kyau ko don zaman lafiyar iyayensu, saboda taji tsoron gargad'iin da Daddy yayiwa Mummy kafin su fito. 



Koda ya koma ko kallon Mummy dake falon zaune bai yi ba yana hawa sama yace da ita   "ki had'o man kayan Baby kala biyu da abincin da zan kai masu yanzu."

Mummy ta kalleshi jikinta sanyi k'alau ta tashi tayi abunda yace da ita, bayan ta had'o kayan ne ta ajiye a falon tana jiran ya sauko, minti biyu a tsakani sai gashi ya fito da alama ma wanka yayi saboda kayan da ya shiga dasu ba dasu ya fito ba, yana zuwa jikinta yana rawa ta mik'a masa kayan sannan tace da shi    "Ya jikin nata?"

Daddy ya karb'e kayan ya fara tafiya yace,   "Wannan ba abunda ya shafeki bane" sannan ya fice cikin sauri, ta bishi da kallo har ya fice tamkar ta kurma kuwwa saboda ta matsu tasan a wane hali 'yarta take ciki ne?. 

Daddy yana fitowa da kayan a hannu Malam Isah ya k'araso da sauri ya rik'o masa kayan, bayan ya saka masa a bayan motar ne yace dashi,   "Maigida lafiya dai ko?"

Daddy ya fad'a motarshi ya tada sannan yace dashi,   
"Baby ce ba lafiya ka rufe gida saboda a cen zan kwana."

Malam Isah ya b'ata fuska alamun jimami sannan yace,   "ALLAH ya bata lafiya Ubangiji ya tashi kafad'unta."

Daddy yaji dad'in addu'ar shi ya amsa masa faranfaram sannan ya ja motarshi ya fice, yana tafe yana jinjina abunda yayi sanadin jefa Baby cikin wannan mawuyacin hali. 

Har yaje asibitin bai tantance ba, koda ya shiga yanda ya barta haka ya dawo ya tarar da ita, da sauri Munawwara ta karb'i kayan ta ajiye sannan ta fara duba kayan abincin tana ganin hadda abincin Daddy ta samo plate ta zuba masa sannan ta kai masa, ya kai kallonshi ga abincin ya b'ata fuska yace    "bana ci"
Munawwa ta yi magana a cikin sanyin Muryarta cike da ladabi tace    "Ka dauri kaci wani abin ko da kad'an ne Daddy tunda ma bayan tafiyarka ta falko sannan ta koma."

Da sauri Daddy yace,   "da gaske?"

Tace   "Eh har ma sai da ta tambayeka."

Yana jin haka yaji wani dad'i ya ziyarceshi yana murmushin jin dad'i  yace,   "Alhmdllh bani abincin yanzi kam hankalina ya d'an kwanta."

Ta YI saurin mik'a masa abincin zuciyarta cike da jin dad'in dubarar da tayi, don in ba haka ba tasan Daddy shawo kanshi abu ne mai matuk'ar  wahala, itama ta zuba abincin ta fara ci har ta k'are taje ta wanko hannunta a toilet d'in da ke d'akin, shima kad'an yaci yaji ya k'oshi ya ajiye sannan ya fita, koda Munawwa ta fito bata tarar dashi ba sai Munara da ta hango tana motsa k'afarta tareda yamutsa fuska alamar tana so ta falka, a razane ta bud'e idanuwanta sannan ta zabura tareda k'wallah k'ara tana fad'in sunan honey'nta na ainahi waton HAIDAR. 

Munawwa tayi saurin zuwa ta rik'eta saboda k'arin ruwan da take k'ok'arin fisgewa, shima 
Daddy aguje ya shigo d'akin yayo kanta yana kiran sunanta, Munara ta saka hannu cisge k'arin ruwan tana fad'in,    "Wayyo honeyna kar ka gujeni wallahi ni ina sonka, idan ma wasa kake to ka daina wallahi zan iya rasa rayuwata akan hakan, HAIDAR kazo kace mani wasa kake ba zaka rabu dani ba."

Daddy rungumota jikinshi a cikin rud'ewa yake bata hak'uri, Munawwa ta fita da sauri taje ta kira Nurse's suka zo suka danna mata allurar bacci, a cikin mayen baccin da ya sake d'ibarta tace,   Daddy ka ce ya dawo gareni wallahi ni ina so.... n.... Sh....i "

Ta k'are maganar a cikin slow saboda baccin da yaci karfinta, Daddy ya gyara mata kwanciya sannan aka sake mak'ala mata ruwan, sannan hankalinsu ya kwanta dukansu Daddy ya kai kallonshi ga Munawwa yace "wanene shi?"

Tace "WALLAHI nima Daddy bansanshi ba ban masan me ke faruwa ba muna kitchen nida Mummy muka jiyo k'ararta." 

 Daddy ya sauke numfashi da k'arfi yace "ina wayarta?" 

Munawwa tace,   "Tana gida."

Daddy ya fice harabar asibitin sai kai da komo yake yi, Munawwa taja kujera ta zauna tana kallonta tareda nazarin maganganunta, a take zuciyarta ta bata honey'n nata ne ya yaudareta, don daman ita zuciyarta tasha bata ba son tsakani da ALLAH yake yimata ba, tunda har duk tsawon had'uwarsu ya kasa zuwa gidansu ya nuna kanshi ga iyayensu, a take taji wani irin haushinshi ya kamata tana ta Alla Alla safiya ta waye taje gida. 

Cikin dare ma Munara ta falka tana ta kiran sunanshi sai magiya take yi akan don ALLAH kar ya gujeta, haushi ya k'ara turnuke Munawwa 
Aiko safiya tana wayewa tace da Daddy zata je gida tayi wanka sannan ta karb'o masu abun break fast, Daddy ya bata key d'in motarshi taja tayi gida yanayin da ta iske Mummy a ciki ne yasata jin k'afafuwanta sunyi sanyi, saboda ramar da tayi idanuwanta sunyi zuru zuru saboda kukan da tasha tareda fargabar da take ciki na rashin sanin halin da Munara take, da sauri Munawwa taje wurinta tana bata hak'uri hawaye suna sauka akan fuskarta, bayan tayi mata bayanin taji sauk'i anjima ma za'a sallamosu tareda kwantar mata da hankali a cikin dad'ad'an kalamai, sai a lokacin hankalin Mummy ya kwanta ta dinga yi mata addu'ar k'arin samun sauk'i. 

Sannan munawwa ta nufi d'akinsu ta d'auko wayar Munara da ta jefar a k'asan carpet d'insu, ALLAH yasa bata yi komi ba ta kunna da ya yake tasan security d'in da tasa a wayar, sannan ta shiga Gallery ta goge duk photunan Basarake da ke cikin wayar,har wanda suka yi da Munara duk ta goge kuma ta d'auki number shi ta saka a wayarta bayan ta goge ta cikin wayar Munara tareda saka shi a black list, sannan ta zauna ta fara kiranshi wayar har tayi ringing ta k'are ba'a d'agawa, k'arshe ma sai taji wayar a switch up ta buga uban tsaki tace, 

"Bak'in mayaudari ALLAH sai ya saka mata, naso ace ka d'auka da na shikka maka rashin mutuncin da ban tab'a yiwa kowa ba a duniya bak'in mugu." 

Ta k'are maganar a cikin fushi itama ta goge number'r a wayarta, sannan ta fad'a toilet  tana wanka tana k'ara tsine mashi a ranta.

*🔥BAK'ON TANAYI🔥*

*15*

Basarake kuwa yana shirin zuwa gidansu Munara ne yaga kiran Munawwa ya shigo a wayarshi, kasancewar yayi saving d'in numbobinta a duka layukanshi shiyasa yak'i d'aukar wayar, don yasan bata sanshi da numbar ba a take zuciyarshi ta bashi cewa akan case d'insu da Munara ne ta kira shi, yana kallo har wayar tayi ta ringing har ta katse  bai d'aga ba,  saboda har izuwa lokacin ranshi a b'ace yake saboda tsinuwar da yaji  Munara tayi wa Umminshi, shiyasa Zuciyarshi ta k'ara k'arfafa masa gwiwa a kan ya cusawa Munawwa soyayyarshi ta k'arfi, saboda d'aukar fansar da ya d'auki  niyyar yi akan Munara ko don ta rage hayak'in da ke fita saman kanta, wanda a d'an zaman da yayi dasu ya fahimci Daddynsu ne duk yayi silar b'ata mata tarbiyya, saboda yanda a fili yake fifita sonta akan na kowa haka ma  ya bata dama akan tayi duk abunda take so ba kyara ba hantara. 

Munawwa tana k'are wankan a gurguje ta shirya ta kwashi wayoyinsu ta zuba a jaka, sannan ta fito ta karb'i abin karin nasu da Mummy tun asuba ta kammala, ta zuba food flak's ta ajiye saboda rashin baccin da idanuwanta suka kasa yi. 

Bayan ta karb'a ne tayi sallama da Mummy ta fito tana sauri ta nufi motar Daddy da tazo da ita, ta bud'a sit bayan
  ta saka kayan d'agowar da zata yi tayi arba da Basarake dake k'ok'arin zuwa wurinta, wanda shigowarshi kenan shima suna gaisawa da Malam Isah ya hango ta, da fara'arshi ya nufo ta yayin da ita kuma tata fuskar ba yabo ba fallasa duk da taji gabanta ya fad'i lokacin da idanuwanta suka hango shi yana zuwa yace,   "Ranki ya dad'e barka da safiya, yau lecturer d'in ba ya wasa kenan tunda naga wannan fita tun da sassafe."

Ya k'are maganar da Murmushinshi na yak'e, itama tayi d'an murmushi tace,   "Ba makaranta zan je ba ai,  Munara ce ba lafiya tana asibiti an kwantar da ita tun jiya."

Basarake yaji k'irjinshi ya buga dam! dam! A cikin jimami kamar gaske yace,    "Subhanallah ALLAH ya bata lafiya" 

Munawwa tace "Ameen." sannan ta shiga motar ta bata wuta da sauri BASARAKE ya duk'o cikin ladabi yace,    "Ranki ya dad'e zan iya zuwa muje in duba ta?."

Munawwa a cikin ko in kula tace dashi   "ba damuwa shigo muje."

Ya bud'e sit d'in gaba ya zauna sannan  ya kira sunanta a cikin wata siririyar murya, ba shiri ta kai kallonta gareshi idanuwansu suna had'uwa taji k'irjinta ya buga, da sauri ta janye nata idanun ba tareda tace dashi k'anzil ba, shima yayi kamar bai gano yanayin da ta shiga ba yace da ita,   "idan ba damuwa ki bani nayi driving d'in naga kamar kema ba kya da lafiyar."

Munawwa ta sauke ajiyar zuciya sannan ta yi ribas ta doso kan motar zuwa bakin get wadda tuni Malam Isah ya wangale mata shi , tana zuwa daidai wurinshi ta tsaya sannan ta d'an rusuna tace   "Mun tafi Baba sai mun dawo."

Malam Isah bakinshi a washe yace   "ALLAH ya tsare hanya 'yar albarka muna gaidata da jiki kinji? ALLAH ya bata lafiya."

Munawwa tace "Ameeñ."

Basarake ma ya d'aga masa hannu yace,   "Sai mun dawo Baba."

Malam Isah ya kalleshi a cike da mamakin wannan zak'ewa tashi da yaga tayi yawa, sannan yace   "To Ali sauka lafiya."

Bayan ta fito ne ta hau kan titin  sannan ya k'ara cewa da ita "Me ke damunta?"

A cikin sanyin Muryarshi ya yi maganar ba tareda ta kalleshi  ba tace   "D'an zazzab'i ne ya buge ta."

Aiko zumbur ta mik'e ta shige ko waiwaye babu jikinta yana rawa ta shigo cikin gidansu. Yayinda ta bar Basarake anan Zuciyarshi cunkushe da takaicin rashin jin amsar da zata bashi. 

Malam isah kuwa da gangan yayi hakan don baya son alak'arsu domin gudun wani abu yaje ya faru ace da hannunshi a ciki, duk da ya yaba da hankalin Alin, amman dai  ai ance ba'a yabon d'an kuturu har sai ya girma da yatsunshi.

Basarake ya shigo harabar gidan ya zauna bisa dogon bencin da Malam Isah yake zama, tared dafe kanshi da hannu biyu saboda tsananin haushin da yaji na rashin son jin amsar da Munawwa zata bashi, Malam Isah ya k'ura masa kallo tsawon minti biyu yana karantarshi,zuciyarshi dai ta tabbatar  da zarginshi ya zamo gaskiya waton dai Ali soyayya yakeyi da Munawwara,  a hankali ya kira sunanshi  saboda tsananin tausayin da ya bashi na d'ebowa kanshi rigimar da tafi k'arfinshi, ya k'ara maimaita kiran sunanshi har sau biyu sannan Basarake ya d'ago fuskarshi tareda yin tagumi da hannunshi duka biyu yana kallonshi ba tareda yace da shi k'anzil ba, Malam Isa ya zauna gefenshi a cikin tausayawa yace "Ali shawara ce zan baka wadda nake muradin ka d'auketa kayi amfani da ita ko don tsira da mutuncinka, a matsayinka na d'ana da nike ganin tamkar nine na haifeka, saboda tun zuwanka a gidannan naji hankalina ya kwanta da kai sosai, amman duk da haka dole ne na fad'a maka gaskiya don bana son abunda zai cutar da kai nima ya jawo man fitina ina zaman zamana, nasan kana son yarinyar cen amman da zaka daure ka cire sonta a zuciyarka da ka huta akan rigimar da zaka d'ebowa kanka,  saboda ina jin tsoron ka jawo wa kanka matsala, saboda Maigida wallahi komi zai iya aikatawa akan yaranshi, bana son ka kad'a kanka a cikin matsala, ka tsaya matsayinka in da ALLAH ya ajiyeka ka shafawa kanka lafiya ka rabu da yarinyar nan,  tun kafin mahaifinta ya gano abunda ke tsakaninku ya kore ka daga aikinka da kake rufawa iyayenka asiri da shi."

Basarake ya lumshe ido kamar yana ganinta yace,   "Sai zuwa wane lokaci kike ganin zaki san minene Son? Pls ina son in tambaye ki amman sai in kinyi man alk'awali zaki fad'a man gaskiya."

Ta sulale kan gadon ta kwanta tareda bajewa sosai saboda taji dad'in firar, don gaba ki d'aya jikinta ya mutu murus saboda yanda Voice d'inshi ke ratsa duk wata gaba dake jikinta, a lokaci d'aya wata kasala ta sauko mata tayi jarumtar furta cewa    " idan na sani me zai hana in fad'a maka?kawai ka fad'a matukar kasan ina da masaniya akan abunda zaka yi tambayar."

Munawwa ta tashi tsaye ta d'auko maganin ta b'alla sannan ta bud'e d'an  karamin firjin dinsu da ke d'akin, ta janyo ruwa ta jefa maganin a baki sannan ta kurb'a ruwan maganin ya wuce sannan ta sha ruwan sosai ta ajiye saura, sannan ta ciro wayar da ta mak'ale tsakanin wuyanta da kafad'a tana murmushi tace,   "Lalle baka jin garin."

Ba shiri Munawaa ta mik'e zaune tareda dafe k'irji saboda sunan da taji ya kira ya tuno mata da fuskar HAIDAR d'in munara, a cikin rawar murya a rud'e tace   "Wane HAIDAR kuma?ka mance saurayin Munara ne ba ni ba?kuma sannan ni fa ba wata alak'a tsakanina dashi kai atak'aice ma ni ko ganinshi ban tab'a yi ba sai a photo, to taya zaka ce man Matarshi?"

Munawwa duk da tsananin dad'in da taji saboda yabonta da taji  yayi, amman nan take ranta ya b'aci saboda jin zafin sukar 'yar uwarta da yayi, a take b'acin rai ya ziyarci fuskarta da muryarta cikin jin haushinshi tace,    "Ai gari da yawa maye baya cin kansa, don wani yak'ita ai wani da gudu zai sota mazan har wuya ma ne da su? gasunan kamar jamfa a Jos."

Munawwa tayi shiru saboda gaba ki d'aya ya kwance mata notukan kai, haka suka ci gaba da fira ya saki jiki sai zuba mata fira yake yi mai cike tsantsar so da kauna, ba shiri ta ware suka ci gaba da soyewarsu a fakaice, don ita Munawwa har a lokacin bata yarda da cewa wai sonshi take yi ba, saboda ita ganin take yi da nauyi ace tana soyayya da d'an aikin gidansu, amman kuma bata jin nauyin sakin jiki suyita soyewarsu ba.  

 Bayan sun k'are wayar ne mummy ta turo k'ofar ta shigo wadda wannan shigowa tata itace ta uku tana tarar da ita  suna wayar, ba tareda ita Munawwarar tasan da zuwanta ba saboda shauk'in soyayya da ya kwasheta, Mummy a ranta taji dad'in ganin 'yarta ta sami wanda take so saboda yanda taga annushuwa a fuskarta  ya tabbatar mata da cewa ba k'aramin so take yi masa ba.

Yayi shiru na minti biyu kamar bazai ce komi ba, sai cen taga yana typing ta kur'e kallonta akan screen d'in wayar saboda ta k'agu da taji amsar da zai bata, aikuwa sai ga shi ya saki ya shigo d'irrrrr! _"A auren wasa irin na d'iyar 'yar tsana ko?"_

_"Shekaruna basu kai matsayin da zan aureki bane halan?."_

_"Sorry ba abunda nike nufi kenan ba kawai dai naga daga ganin sarkin fawa shikenan sai miya tayi zak'i?daga had'uwar yau kawai shikenan sai zancen aure nan da sati biyu?sai kace wadda aka ce maka an gaji da zamanta gidansu ana neman hanyar da za'a rabu da ita?ai koda kazo gidan namu kamar yanda kace to ba haka kawai daga zuwan farko za'a ce dakai kaje ka shiryo kazo anbaka ni nan da sati biyu aure, cab aure sai kace irin na wasar yara?."_

Ta k'are maganar tareda sako d'an dariya kamar haka😆.

A B'angaren Basarake kuwa mai sabon suna Muhammadu Nuradden,wanda sunan abokinshi ne na k'ud da k'ud yayi amfani dashi, har pic d'in ma nashi ne ya saka akan dp don kawai ya ji dad'in shigar da ita, ganin pictures d'in da ta turo masa yasa ya k'ara dulmiya a cikin kogin k'aunarta, saboda tsananin kyaun da tayi a photunan duk da kowane pic ba wanda ta bayyana tsiraici, guda d'aya ma sai da tayi rolling d'in gyalen ya zagaye 'yar faffad'ar fuskarta, sauran biyun kuma ta aza d'an karamin gyalen kawai ta sako lebunan gyalen a gaban doguwar rigar da take sanye a jikinta, sai dai ta yi kyau matuk'a fuskarta ta fito rad'am a jikin photon, ya kurewa photon kallo babu banbanci da picture's d'in munara da ke wayarshi, in banda yanayin shigarsu da rashin ji da Munara ta ninka Munawwa da shi ko a cikin picture's d'in, domin ita har wad'anda daga ita sai kayan bacci duk tura masa takeyi ba ruwanta, ba shiri ya sauke ajiyar zuciya ya nace ala dole sai ya gano abunda ya banbanta su amman ya kasa, saboda Munara da Munawwara identical twins ne komai nasu iri d'aya ne banbancinsu kawai ta b'angaren shiga da halayyarsu, wadda ko a minti biyu zaka iya banbance su saboda Munara mutunci yayi mata k'aranci, yayin da Munawwara kirki in yana yawa  sai ace nata ma yayi yawa.
