A cikin matuk'ar mamaki ya dawo da kallonshi ga ledodi biyu da ke jiye sit d'in gaba, a hanakali ya duk'a tareda janyo ledar ya fara dubawa jikinshi a mace ya d'auko ledar tareda rufo motar, key a hannunshi don ya mance shaf bai bata ba, don itama kanta saboda saurin da take yi ta shiga gida bata tsaya ta karb'a ba. 

 A cikin rashin kuzari ya nufi bencin malam Isah ya zauna, tareda Duk'e kanshi zuciyarshi ta shiga zurfin tunani, saboda abunda Munawwa tayi masa a yau ya k'ara tabbatar masa da cewa ita ta daban ce a cikin jerin mata, haka zalika halinta yayi wa Munara fintinkau ta ko'ina, don samun ire irenta masu kyakkyawar zuciya a wannan marra abu ne mawuyaci, yana nan zaune sai ga Malam Isah ya zo buta rik'e a hannunshi idonsh na kanshi har ya ajiye butar ya zauna ba tareda ya sani ba, saboda ya nutsa sosai da tunanin nemo wa kanshi mafita wajen zab'in macen da ta dace da rayuwar duk wani mutumin kirki. 

Baba Isah ya zauna idonshi akan ledar da ke ajiye  gaban Basarake, ya kira sunanshi har sau biyu sai a na uku ya d'ago tareda bud'e  idanuwanshi da suka yi jawur Malam isah yace   "Lafiyarka kuwa yau Ali?
Naga tun d'azu kake shiru ko 'yar firar da muke yi yau baka saki jiki munyi ba". 

Basarake ya sauke k'atuwar ajiyar zuciya sannan ya jawo ledar ya fara nunawa Malam Isah kayan da ke ciki, a cikin d'aurewar kai malam Isah yake bin kayan da kallo, har sai da ya k'are nuna masa sannan yace   "Eh nagani ka tashi ka kai masu kayansu idan har aikenka akayi."

Basarake yayi tagumi da hannunshi biyu yace,     "'yarka ce ta bada akaiwa su Ummi, saboda gobe insha ALLAH zan je k'auyenmu in dubo su, saboda rikicin tsufa da kakalena take yi akan dole sai na dawo ta ganni ko ita ta zo."

Malam isah yana jin haka ya fad'ad'a fuskarshi yana 'yar dariya yace

"Lalle ka garzaya kaje ka gano Uwargida tunda sarautar ce ta motsa, Ubangiji ALLAH ya kaika lafiya ya dawo mana da kai lafiya, eh lalle wannan 'ya tawa tayi k'ok'ari sosai  ALLAH ya bata lada, sannan ya cika maku gurinku na alkhairi."

Basarake yana murmushin jin dad'i yace    "Ameen Baba nagode sosai da addu'arku."

Baba yana wasar baki  yace   'Addu'a kam ai kullum bamu fashin yiwa 'ya'yanmu ita a kullum safiya, fatar mu kawai shine ALLAH ya karb'a."

Basarake yayi murmushi fuskarshi cike da jin dad'i wani gefen zuciyarshi kuma cike take fam da tausayin Baba Isah, saboda ganin yanda yayi tsaye kai da fata zuciya d'aya yana aikinshi, saboda kawai ya tallafi iyalinshi da 'yan uwanshi da nauyinsu ya rataya a kanshi, ya zab'i ya sai da ranshi domin su ya faranta masu yasa a ranshi da gurinshi ya cika zai tallafa masa ya daina wannan sana'ar da yakeyi, shima yaje gida ya zauna cikin iyalinshi. 

Munawwa kuwa tana shiga agajiye tib'is ta fad'a gidan Mummy ma gaida ta kawai tayi ta shige d'akinsu, tareda jefar da jaka ta fad'a toilet ta d'auki tsawon minti goma kafin ta fito, sai gata ta fito towel d'aure a jikinta tayo wanka a gurguje ta shirya tayi SALLAH sannan ta fito falo, saboda yunwar da ke ta k'wak'walar cikinta. 

Aiko Mummy ta baje mata abincin tana shirin kiranta tazo sai gata ta fito, jikinta yana rawa ta fara zuba abincin Mummy tana kallonta tana yi mata dariya, sai bayan ta fara cin abincin ne tace    "Kai yau naji yunwa sosai WALLAHI saboda tests d'in da nayi muna, saukinta ma ba a lokaci d'aya aka yi su ba, amman duk da haka naci kwakwata sasai Mummy, saboda kwana biyun nan da bamu je ba anyi abubuwa da yawa bama nan ALLAH ya taimaka Nusy tana zuwa, don shiyasa ma test d'in munara tayi man sauk'i, amman fa wallahi wani k'unataccen lecturer d'insu yaso ya gano ba Munara bace, saboda kallona yayi tayi yana jefo man tambayoyi ai kuwa nayi ta acting irin nata sai gashi duk k'wak'warshi dole ya kyaleni ."

Mummy tayi ta dariya tace    "ki ce yau 'yata dakyar tasha naso inga idonki a lokacin."

Munawwa sai da ta guma loma a baki sannan tace     " ai kuwa baki gani ba ido sun rena fata, kamar an kama sarki yayi k'arya  sai bayan an K'are ne nayi ta dariya." 

A tare suka yi ta dariya aikuwa tsidik sai ga Munara ta shigo  Daddy biye da ita a baya, yanayin da suka ga Munawwa da Mummy a ciki ya sake tunzura su, musamman Daddy da yaga wato ma ko a jikinsu, shi yana cen yana fama da baby a asibiti su suna nan suna jin dad'i abunsu,ya haye sama ba tareda ma ya kalli gefen da suke ba, Mummy jikinta yana rawa ta bi bayanshi zuciyarta cike da fargabar abunda zai biyo baya. 

 Munara kam har sai da ta k'arewa Munawwa kallo sama da k'asa sannan ta watsar tareda buga mata wani k'aton tsaki  ta shige d'akinsu, Munawwa jiki a sanyaye ta ajiye plate d'in abincin ta bi ta da kallo, ALLAH ma ya taimaketa taci abincin farko K'ari ne tayi da shikenan ba zai shiga cikinta ba, a cikin sanyin jiki ta kwashe kayan abincin takai kitchen ta ajiye sannan ta nufi d'akinsu, wanda koda taje bata tarar da ita a ciki ba sai k'arar ruwan da ta jiyo a toilet d'in nasu wanda alamu suka nuna wanka take yi, ai kuwa ta zauna kan gadonsu kenan tana tattara littafansu da suka zube sai ga Munara ta fito, fuskarta murtuk'e ba alamun sassauci a cikinta, Munawwa ta kalleta tace   "Ya jikin?" Munara ta watsa mata wani kallo mai cike da harara sannan tace,    "Ina ruwanki dani balle lafiyata?, wallahi ki kiyayeni don yanzu a shirye nake da duk wani munafuccinki, kuma wallahi tsaf zan gyara maki saiti a cikin gidannan, tunda na gano ba k'aunata ake ba,  wai ace Uwar da ta haifeka ma bata sonka to sai kuma wa yayi saura wanda ya dace ace ya soka kuma?"    

Munawwa ta bita da kallo cike mamakin kalamanta a cikin dakiya tace,     "Kin tab'a ganin Uwar da ta haifi d'a tace bata sonshi?"  

Munara ta d'aga matab hannu tace   " dakata malama ba wani kilbibi da zaki yiman yayi tasiri a yanzu, domin da idanuwa na nagani ba fad'a man aka yi ba, ke d'in da take so taje ta jik'a ki tasha ni kuma asaka NI a turmi a dake har sai na mutu sannan a fidda ni." 

Ta k'are maganar tareda murgud'a mata baki saboda jin take yi kamar da Mummyn ce take magana, Munawwa tayi shiru ba tareda ta sake cewa da ita komi ba, don ta fahimci a wuya take k'iris take jira a yi uwar watsiya da ita, shiyasa tayi gum ta ci gaba da gyaran littafanta amman a zuciyarta sai fatan shiriya take yimata, saboda ganin yau abun bai tsaya iya kanta ba har kan Mummy  bai tsallake ba. 

 Sai dai a bayyane tace   "Ai ba mune muka kar zomon ba, balle a sauke fushin a kanmu."

Munara da ke kallon kanta a madubi tana ganin yanda ta rame a cikin kwana biyu, maganganun Munawwa yasa ta juyo ba shiri tace   

"Aike ce babbar wadda ta kare zomon domin kece kika k'ara haddasa man duk wani bala'in da nike ciki a yanzu, saboda bak'ar muguntarki da mugun nufinki akaina nida HAIDAR, ko kin aza bansan kece babbar  mai adawa da soyayyarmu ba?, shiyasa kika YI amfani da damarki kika rusa man farin cikina, to inma bak'in ciki kike yi kinga na sami d'aya tamkar da dubu a cikin mazaje,  to kisani ke har ki mutu bazaki tab'a kamo k'afata ba, balle har ki sami irin honey na."

Munawwa ta fashe da dariyar tura haushi sannan tace   "Honey'n mai rabo dai amman ke kam ya riga da ya fita hannunki sai dai hak'uri."

Munara kamar wata zakanya ta shak'o wuyan rigar munawwa ta baya jikinta yana rawa tace,     "WALLAHI ki kiyayi sake fad'in wannan magana a bakinki, in ba haka ba zan watsar maki da hak'ora, akan hakan zan rufe ido in karta maki rashin mutuncin da sai kinyi danasanin sanina WALLAHI, don haka takie care of your self if not you will regret."

Ta k'are maganar tana huci tamkar wata kububuwa, Munawwa ta fincike rik'on da tayi wa wuyan rigarta tareda gyara zaman rigar jikinta tace, 

 "Ba laifinki bane wallahi saboda ni nasan baki cikin hayyacinki, ki nemi tsari da sharrinshi tun kafin ya kaiki a tarkon danasanin da bazaki iya fitar da kanki ba."

Munara bakinta yana rawa saboda masifa tace     "Wa kike nufi?"

Munawwa tace    "Shed'an saboda shine yaje ingizaki kike duk abubuwan da kike ganin kina aikatawa, idan baki tashi kika yak'e shi ba to shi da rundunarshi zasu yi nasara akanki, don haka ko yanzu akwai dama kiyi gaggawar koma wa  ALLAH tun kafin lokaci ya kur'e maki, don yana gaf da sanya ki tashi a tutar babu saboda a kullum shikam baya gajiya wajen farautar mabiyanshi."

Maganganun munawwa sun so suyi tasiri a zuciyar Munara sai dai a take shed'an ya canza mata tunani, ta sheke da wata arniyar dariya sannan tayi d'if kamar ba itace tayi ba tareda ri'ko k'ugunta tace,    

 "Sannu alarammiya mai mai kashin aya tareda aman hadissai ni nafi k'arfin kiyi man dad'in baki, kunne dai na ja maki ki kiyayi danganta HAIDAR d'ina da wata mace in ba ni ba, domin har duniya ta tashi bazan daina sonshi ba kuma nawa ne ni kadai iyayenshi suka haifa ma shi, kuma duk wadda tayi saken da ta shiga gona ta sai na maida ta mujiyar da ko 'yan uwanta mujiyoyi sai sun yi gudunta ballanta wani ya zo kusa da ita, saboda haka ki sani ki k'ara sani HAIDAR nawa neeeeee niii ka daiiiii, Haidaaaarr nawaaaaaa neeeeeee niiiiii Munaraaaaa babuuuuu wata macennnnn da ta isaaaaa ta rab'e jikinshiiiiiiii a matsayin matarrrrrrr shi alhali  alhali ina numfashiiiiiiii a doron k'asaaaaaaa."

Ta k'are maganar a cikin karaji kamar wata mai aljannu ta fad'i kan gado tana k'yalk'yala dariya. 

 Munawwa ko saboda tsananin tsoron da taji kirjinta sai bugawa yake yi faattt! fat!! Saboda tashin hankali, haka kawai taji wani kuka yazo mata saboda ganin yanda yar uwarta ta zauce akan namijin da bai ma san tana yi ba, aranta sai nanata hasbunallahu wani'imal wakil kawai take yi, saboda ganin take yi anya ma wannan HAIDAR d'in mutum ne kuwa?, don tabbas ya cika cikakken mayaudari tunda har yayi nasarar  cusa mata sonshi sannan daga baya yazo ya gujeta. 

Jiki a sanyaye ta fito falo ta baro mata d'akin inda anan d'inma zamanta keda wuya ta jiyo sautin Daddy yana ta zuba ruwan masifa, wanda ko tantama babu akan cewa da Mummy yake yi, saboda yanda ta ke jiyo sautinta tana bashi hak'uri, haka kawai taji gidan ya gundureta, lokacin marece sakaliya ya gabato ta fito compound d'in gidan, kai tsaye ta nufi wurin malam Isah zuciyarta cike da begen ganin Basarake, aikuwa tana zuwa ta ce da Malam Isah, "Baba barka da yamma." 

Ya d'ago daga gyaran tsohon radionshi da yake yi fuska ba walwala yace,    "Yawwa barka dai 'yata." 

A cikin jajjefa idonta inda zata yi nasarar hango shi tace 

"Yana ina baba ko ya tafi gida?"

Malam isah ya wayance tareda fad'in 

"Shi wa fa?"

Munawwa taji nauyin fad'in sunan sosai amman ta dake tace  

"Aliyu nake nufi baba ko ya wuce."

Ran baba isah a jagule yace da ita    "Eh ya tafi tun lokacin da Maigida ya dawo saboda 'yar uwarki da ta nuna bata son ganinshi, shine maigida yace yaje ya sallameshi daga aikin har ma ya biyashi kud'in aikin da yayi, kingansu nan ma ya barman  saboda ranshi da ya b'aci sosai, ba yanda banyi da shi ba akan ya tafi da kud'inshi yak'i kinga makullin motarku ma ya bani in ajiye maku, saboda yace gobe tun da sassafe zai kama hanyar zuwa k'auyensu". 

Ba shiri Munawwa ta fad'i tareda k'walla k'ara tana birgima saboda tsananin tashin hankalin da ta shiga, Daddy da mummy da Munarar kanta sai gasu sun fito da gudunsu saboda dukansu sunji k'arar da Munawwa tayi, Kafin su je har Baba isah ya zo kanta yana bata hak'uri, aikuwa sai ga Mummy da Munara sun yo kanta suna tambayar me aka yi mata?, baba isah ya tashi tsaye jikinshi yana rawa yace,   "Don kawai ta tambayi ina Ali nayi mata bayani maigida ya sallameshi daga aiki shikenan naga ta fad'i tana birgima."

 Daddy ko tunda ya gano ba Munara bace tayi kuwwar tashin hankalinshi ya ragu a cikin kashi d'ari saba'in ya tafiyarshi, saboda ganinta ta fito suna rigengen zuwa wajen domin su ga meke faruwa, aikuwa sai gashi ya koma tafiya maimakon gudun da yake yi, tun daga nesa ya jiyo sautin  muryar Baba Isah yana bayanin abunda ya faru, aikuwa ba tareda jin ba'asin komi ba ya wankawa Baba Isah mari har sai da ya wuntsila hular kanshi ta fad'i, saboda jin haushin wai akan wancen matsiyacin mutumin da babynshi bata son ganin  fuskarshi ake wannan tarzoma. 

Munara ma tana jin haka ta makawa Munawwa da ke ta kuka kashirb'in harara, sannan ta sake ta tareda buga tsaki mai d'auke da yi mata harara ta bar wajen, Mummy kam tana Jim ba'asin maganar ta gano akwai matsala saboda munawwa tayi zurfi sosai da soyayyarshi, Daddy ma yabi bayan yana banbamin fad'a wai haka kawai ta tadawa mutane hankali akan wani bakin talaka. 

Mummy dai bata bi ta kan zantukanshi ba ta ri'ko munawwa da take ta cin uban kuka tareda tayar da ita tsaye, ta kalli Baba Isah da tunda Daddy ya mareshi bai cire hannunshi daga kan fuskarshi ba, saboda tsananin mamakin da ya kama shi wai ace shine yau aka mara da girmanshi har da furfura, saboda kawai yazo neman abin rufawa kai asiri saboda bazai iya tuno ranar k'arshe da aka mareshi ba tun yana d'an karami HAR kawo wa yanzu da manyanta ta kamashi. 

A cikin jin nauyin abunda Daddy yayi masa Mummy ta fara bashi hak'uri kamar tayi kuka, sannan ta Jawo munawwa suka shige cikin gida inda kai tsaye d'akinta ta nufa da ita, saboda zuciyarta da ta cunkushe da bak'in cikin halayen Daddy, don wannan shi ake kira ga mari ga tsinke jaka,  bata gama wartsakewa da gumaguma bak'ak'en maganganun da ya jefeta da su BA akan Munara, sai ga kuwwar 'yarta wadda tafi soyuwa a cikin zuciyarta, k'arin takaicin kuma marin da yayiwa Baba Isah wanda ko a ido ka kalleshi kasan zai girmi Daddy sosai,don ko yayanshi Alhaji Musa da wuya in Malam Isah bai girmeshi ba,  amman baiji kunyar cira hannu ya zabga masa mari ba. 

A cikin kwantar da murya ta dinga baiwa Munawwa baki amma ina Munawwa ta rikicewa Mummy sosai tana fad'in    "Don ALLAH mummy ki barni inje in bashi hak'uri saboda gobe tun da safe yace zai je garinsu,gashi Daddy ya koreshi watak'ila ma idan yaje bazai dawo ba, don ALLAH Mummy ki baiwa Daddy hak'uri ya dawo dashi, ya hanashi tafiya k'auyen ma duka don ALLAH mummy."

Mummy kam tashin hankalinta ya k'aru don yanzu ta k'ara tabbatar da son da take yi mashi sosai,ganin Mummy tayi shiru ko motsi ta kasa yi yasa ta fita da gudu ta nufi sama d'akin Daddy, kamar an jefota ya ganta tsidik cikin falonshi nan take ya had'e fuska gane itace ba Munara ba, aiko tana zuwa ta durk'usa k'asa tana wani irin kuka gwanin ban tausayi tace,    "Don ALLAH Daddy kayi hak'uri ka dawo dashi don ALLAH wallahi yana da kirki Daddy."

Tsawar da ya daka mata ce tasa ta kai bakin k'ofa ba shiri hannunta biyu had'e wuri d'aya hawaye suna kwaranya  akan fuskarta tace  "Don ALLAH Daddy don ALLAH Daddy."

Ya taso kamar zai daketa da sauri ta sauko k'asa ya rufo k'ofar falonnashi garam! Tareda buga tsaki yace   "Useless girl haka kawai zan dawo dashi alhali fuskarshi tana tsorata baby?yaje cen ya k'arara da shegiyar bak'ar fuskarshi.". 

Aiko tana saukowa ta fita aguje ta d'auki key d'in motarsu da ke ajiye saman teburin malam Isah sannan ta bud'e gate da kanta ta je da sauri ta fad'a mota, ta jawota da k'arfi ta fice sai jin k'arar tashin motarta suka yi, Mummy ta k'ara sautin Kukanta, saboda ta kasa jure abunda yake damun zuciyarta, don tun fitar Munawwa zuwa wurin Daddy ta fara kuka saboda tsananin tausayin rayuwarsu dukansu,  tun daga kan Daddyn har Munara da ita kanta Mummyn tareda Munawwarar, wanda tafi alak'anta komi akan Daddy don a ganinta shine silar duk wani rikicin da ke faruwa a gidan. 

Munawwa ko unguwa unguwa tayi ta bi inda tasan nanne unguwannin marasa shi, saboda tsammaninta duk acen ne zata iya cin karo da shi, tayi ta yawo har ta gaji dole don kanta ta dawo gida saboda tuno ta baro wayarta a gida balle ta kirashi taji unguwar  da yake,  amma har lokacin bata daina kuka ba saboda takaicin abunda aka yi masa. 

Bayan ta dawo gida ne ta nufi d'akinsu ta janyo wayarta da ta bari, ta fito falo don ma bata son wata magana ta had'ata da Munara, ta dinga buga wayarshi tak'i shiga ana cewa line busy ta kira har sau biyu sai a na uku ta samu ta shiga, yana d'auka ko sallama bata yi masa ba tace     "A Wace unguwa kake yanzu zan zo na same ka." 

Basarake da yanzu Malam Isah ya gama yi mashi bayanin duk abunda ya faru, a cikin damuwa yace     "Ki jira ni zan zo k'ofar gidanku a yanzu, amman fa in kinsan da matsala mu hak'ura kawai muyi zancen a waya."

Munawwa tayi shiru na d'an lokaci sannan tace   "Ka zo kawai ba wani abu."

Don ta k'uduri koda Daddy zai kasheta sai taje ta bashi hak'uri, sannan ta sanar dashi ya dawo bakin aikinshi idan ya dawo, a ganinta kafin lokacin da zai dawo zata shawo kan lamarin, sannan suka yi sallama ta tashi taje d'akin mummy tayi ramakon sallahr magriba da bata yi ba sannan tayi isha,tareda addu'o'inta tana k'arewa  ta aza kanta saman gadon Mummy hawaye suna zuba a gefen idonta, ta tsurawa  wayarta ido tana tsumayen kiranshi kamar yanda suka yi akan da yazo k'ofar gidan zai kirata. 

Aiko mintuna biyar a tsakani sai ga wayar tana haske alamun kiranshi ya shigo saboda silent d'in da ta saka wayar, a zabure ta mik'e jikinta yana rawa zata fito d'akin ta ci karo da Mummy zata shigowa,mummy tace 

"Ina zaki je kike sauri haka?" a cikin fad'uwar gaba tace 

"Mummy Aliyu ne yazo yana k'ofar gida shine zan je in bashi hak'uri."

Mummy tayi shiru tana kallonta tareda karantar yanayinta taga idan ta hanata zuwa tabbas zata k'arawa y'arta damuwa, idan kuma ta barta batasan abunda zai je ya dawo ba tsakaninta da Daddy, Munawwa tace 

"Plss Mummy wallahi bazan wuce minti biyu biyar ba."

Ta k'are maganar tareda zubowar wasu hawaye akan fuskarta, Mummy tayi k'arfin halin ce wa,    "Kinsan halin Daddynku fa." 

Munawwa ta fara tafiya da sauri tace 

"just five minutes Mummy."

Ta wuce da sauri Mummy ta bita da kallo har ta k'ulewa ganinta, sannan ta zauna tana nazarin wannan rikici da yake shirin tunkaro su, saboda jikinta ya bata tabbas sai anyi d'an k'aramin yak'i kafin daddy ya amince da wannan had'in, duk da ko ita abun bai kwanta mata a rai ba don dai kawai yanda taga ita Munawwar ta damu dashi ne. 


 Koda tazo fita Malam Isah yana d'akinshi har lokacin yana jimamin abunda Daddy yayi masa, don ya k'uduri niyyar barin aikin ko don tsira da mutuncinshi, duk da Aliyu yayi ta bashi hak'uri akan ya zauna ya k'ara basu lokaci kafin ya bar aikin, saboda shi Basaraken baiso Baba ya ajiye aikin a lokacin don tafiyar da zai yi yana son ya zamo idanuwanshi akan duk abunda za'ayi baya nan, yafi son har sai maganarsh da Munawwa ta kankama sannan yabashi abunda zai tallabi kanshi. 

Sai da ta d'an lek'o sannan ta hango shi a cen nesa da gidan, da taimakon hasken  nefar da ya haskake k'ofar gidan, sannan ta fito ta tsaya shima ya tako a hankali zuwa daidai inda take sannan ya tsaya idonshi akanta yace

"ALLAH yasa lafiya ranki ya dad'e wannan nema cikin dare?. "

Munawaa ta sauke ajiyar zuciya tace 

"hak'uri nazo baka akan abunda Daddy yayi maka don ALLAH kayi hak'uri, kuma idan kaje ganin gidan ka dawo aikinka yana jiranka."

Basarake ya fara girgizar kai alamun bazai yiyu ba a cikin sarkewar murya tayi saurin fad'in 

"For my sake."

 Ya yi murmushinshi na yake sannan ya magantu yace   "Zan jure komai ma in dai akanki ne amman kinga Daddy da kanshi ya sallameni, idan na dawo with out par mission  zai ga kamar na rena shi ne kiyi hak'uri insha ALLAH zan dinga lek'owa muna gaisawa aduk lokacin da na sami lokaci."

Wasu hawaye masu zafi suka gangaro mata a cikin sauk'in son da ya taso mata wanda ta kasa controlling d'in kanta, a cikin dusashiyar muryar da tasha kuka tace  "idan na rasa ka bansan ina zan saka rayuwata ba, kayi hak'uri ka dawo aikinka wallahi zan shiga damuwa sosai idan na bud'e ido ban ganka cikin gidannan ba." 

Basarake wani dad'i ya lullubeshi a cikin wata muryar da ba ko yaushe yake yi mata magana da ita yace 

"Munawy base on yanda ki ke treating d'ina sai in ga kamar nafi kowa sa'a, amman idan na tuna har yanzu ban tab'a jin kalmar ina sonka Aliyu ta fito a bakinki ba, sai inyi ta ganin kamar ban kai matsayin ba,  saboda haka bazan juri zama a hakan ba gasky tunda har yanzu ina under probability saboda haka kiyi hak'uri kawai idan na dawo zan dinga samun lokaci ina zuwa muna gaisawa bye sai watarana."

Yana k'are maganar ya juya zai fara tafiya Jikinta ya d'auki rawa kirjinta ya fara dakan lugude sai  bugawa yakeyi fat! fat! fat! A hankali ta furta, 

"Aliyu I love u morethan anything in dis word, I don't have any word to describe how i feel when I see u, i love u so much my one and only ."

Basarake ya yi wani juyi kamar wani zautacce a zabure ya isa wurinta tareda ri'ko hannunta kamar wani zautacce yace 

"Da gaske?"

Ta d'aga masa kai kamar wata k'adangaruwa a hankali ya rungumota jikinshi tareda sauke ajiyar zuciya yace, 

"Am so happy Munawy  much luv u my beautiful baby  I luv u so so much my angel."

Itama bata san lokacin da tace 

"me too my HAIDAR."

Munara da ke bayansu tsaye zuciyarta cike da tsanarsu tana jin munawwa ta ambaci HAIDAR tayi saurin fitowa ta tsaya kansu k'erere tana yi masu wani kallo.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*21*

Tana k'are masu kallo tun daga sama har k'asa zuciyarta cike fam da tsanarsu a cikin tsawa tace   "Waton daman cen kun saba tanbad'ewarku ashe?" 

Munawwa da Aliyu kam sai k'ara nanukar juna suke yi a cikin wani shauk'in so sai furtawa juna kalamai masu k'ara tabbatar da son da suke yiwa juna suke,  sun manta da kowa da komai sai kansu kawai basu ma san tana wurin ba, har sai da ta saka hannu ta fara k'ok'arin raba su tana zazzaga ruwan masifa, sannan ne hankalinsu ya dawo a jikinsu da sauri Basarake ya sake Munawwa, yayin da itama ta shiga taitayinta ba shiri ta gayyato natsuwa tareda jin kunyar abunda suka yi, jikinta sai rawa yake yi saboda ganin Munara a wurin. 

 shiko yana ganin Munara akansu k'irjinshi ya buga saboda yana jin tsoron ta sake k'ulla masa wani sharri  wurin Daddy, don baya son duk wani cikas da zai kawo masa ziyara wurin neman auren heart beat d'inshi Munawy, a hankali ya juyo yace da Munawwa "ki shiga gida nagode sosai zan tafi  sai munyi waya i love u bye"

Yana tafiya yana d'aga mata hannu itama hawaye suna kwaranya akan fuskarta tana d'aga masa hannu, saboda bakinta ya kasa furta wata kalma saboda tsananin kukan da take yi. 

Tana tsaye har ya kulewa ganinta, yayin da Munara itama ta kafe nata idanuwan akan Munawwara, saboda mamakin da ya cika mata ciki wanda saboda mamakin ma ne ta kasa cewa da Basarake k'anzil har ya bar wajen, sai bayan ya tafi ne taji haushin da bata yagalgala shi ba ta tara masu mutane, Munawwa ta saka gefen hijabin dake jikinta ta goge ruwan hawayen da suka b'ata mata fuska, sannan ta juya da nufin ta shiga gida sai kawai Munara ta jawo hijabinta ta baya, ba shiri Munawwa ta tsaya cak har Munara ta zagayo ta gabanta,  idanunta basu daina mamakin abunda ta gani ba, sannan ta kwashe da wata arniyar dariya tace,    "Gaske kike damuwa akan an koreshi ashe ke kinsan me kuke yi shiyasa, yanzu saboda ALLAH don kawai kina son ki zubar muna da mutuncin gidanmu kike soyayya da wancen bak'in bakauye matsiyaci d'an talakawa?WALLAHI mu dai kin cucemu, yanzu ace gida irin wannan ki rasa wa zakiyi soyayya dashi sai irin wancen bak'in  kuturun cab wallahi dai anji kunya."

    Munawwara ta maka mata wata shahararriyar harara da jajayen idanuwanta, a cikin fushi ta fisge hijabinta daga rik'on da tayi mata tareda nunata da d'an yatsa bakinta yana rawa tace    "Ki kiyayi fad'in miyagun kalamai ga Aliyunah idan ba haka ba wallahi yanzu zan had'a maki jini da majina, rayuwata ce ba taki ba ni shi na gani kuma shi nike so haka zalika shi na mallakawa zuciyata, kuma shine mijina uban 'ya'yan da zan haifa halittarshi da komai nashi ba damuwata bane, ni shi d'in nike so ba wani abu nashi da zai mallaka ba, kud'i kyau ko dukiya basu isa sukai darajar son da nike yi masa ba don haka ki rubuta ki ajiye ALIYU  HAIDAR BASARAKE mijin Munawwara ne wanda duk baiso sai dai bak'in ciki ya kashe shi, no going backward ever ki bud'e kunnuwanki da kyau kiji kuturun nan da kike fad'a  i luv him, duk da bak'ar fuskarshi da talaucin duk naji na gani ina son abuna  kuma zan aureshi a haka, saboda haka wannan ba damuwarki bace tawa ce ni da HAIDAR d'ina."

Tana k'are fad'ar abunda ke zuciyarta ta  ture Munara ta shigo gidan, Mummy da ke tsaye tana saurarensu tashin hankalinta ya karu saboda lafuzzan da taji Munawwa take fad'a, wanda ko tantama babu daga cikin zuciyarta suke fitowa. 

  Dalilin zuwanta shine saboda ita ta turo Munara ta kirata da tagaji da jiran tsammani ne ta biyo bayansu, saboda tana jin tsoron Daddynsu yasan ta fito ashe da rabon taji abunda zai k'ara d'aga mata hankali, don kam Munawwa ta tabbatar mata da yanda take son wannan mutume, kafin ta tantance abinyi sai ga Munara ta k'ara jawo mata hijabi ta shaketa iya k'arfinta ta matse ta jikin k'ofar gate tareda nunata da d'an yatsa kamar zata kai mata naushi  tace    "Bakinki ya kiyayi fad'in sunan HAIDAR d'innan tun kafin in sauya maki kamannu wallahi."

Da sauri Mumny ta k'araso wurinsu tareda katsa masu tsawa tana baiwa Munara umurnin ta sake ta, ganin yanda ran Mummy yake a b'ace yasa ta saketa tana huci, Munawwara ko duk da taji zafin shak'ar amma hakan bai hanata yin murmushi mai sauti ba tace       "HAIDAR is my own not u because naki na bugi ne nawa ne real don gashi kin gani da idonki." 

Munara tayi cikin Munawwa kamar wata zakanya ta kai mata duka, Mummy ta d'aga hannu ta d'auke Munara da wani bahahon mari a cikin tsananin fushi tace

"Ke wace irin kangararriyar yarinya ce ke da bakya jin magana?shikenan ni bazaki tab'a barina in huta ba masifar safe daban ta dare daban to wallahi na fara gajiya sai in bar maku gidanku tunda ba kwa son ganin farin ciki na."

Ta k'are maganar tareda fashewa da wani kuka mai tsanani, Munawwa  tayi saurin zuwa wurinta ta rungumeta itama sai kukan take yi a cikin tashin hankali, saboda kukan da taga mummy tana yi ya bala'in d'aga mata hankali matuk'a. 

Munara kam tana jin zafin marin ba shiri ta k'walla k'ara tareda kiran sunan Daddy, tana fad'in    "Daddy kazo zasu kashe ni tunda kai kad'ai ne mai sona a gidan wayyo Daddy kazo ka gani saboda Munafukar 'yarta zata cire mani kunne."

 Aikuwa sai ga Daddy afujajan ya katse kallon news d'in da yake yi ya fito saboda jin k'arar  muryar shalelenshi da ya jiyo, yanayin da ya fito kamar wani wanda zai yi fad'a da k'atti goma, amma yana yin arba da Mummy dake ta kuka gwanin ban tausayi dole jikinshi yayi sanyi, duk da kai tsaye wajen 'yarshi ya nufa amman yaji zafin kukan da yaga tana YI a ranshi, shiyasa bai ce k'anzil ba yaja 'yarshi suka shige cikin gida yana lallashinta, amman fa zuciyarshi a cike take fam da jin zafin kukan da Mummy take yi. 

Bayan shigewarsu ne Baba Isah da ke tsaye k'ofar d'akinshi ya k'araso wurinsu Mummy ya fara basu hak'uri a cikin lafazi mai dad'i, Mummy ta tsayar da kukanta ta koma lallashin Munara,sannan  taja ta suka shiga cikin gida, Malam Isah ya bisu da kallo gwanin ban tausayi saboda duk abunda aka yi kuma aka fad'a a gaban idonshi.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*22*

B'angare Basarake kuwa yana tafe iska yana kwasar shi, saboda yanda jikinshi yayi sanyi k'alau saboda soyayyar Munawwa ta saka shi kasala sosai, musamman idan ya tuno da kalamanta inda take cewa, 

_Aliyu i love u morethan anything in dis world, i don't have any word to describe how i feel when i see u, I love you so much my only._

Yana tafe muryarta tana yi masa amsa amo a kunne har yaje inda ya ajiye motarshi, da sauri ya tayar da ita  ya bar wajen bayan yayiwa d'an liti mai shago ihsani, d'an liti ya bishi da kallo zuciyarshi cike da tunanin yau meke damun mutumin nashi ne?, saboda ad'an fahimtar da yayi masa ya gano mutum ne shi mai matuk'ar kirki, duk da bai cika yawan surutu ba tun  da marece lokacin da  yazo d'aukar motarshi ya fahimci akwai abunda yake damunshi a cikin ranshi yace 

_ALLAH ya kawo maka mafita a cikin lamurranka kuma ya yaye maka abunda yake damunka gurinka na alkhairi kuma Ubangiji ya cika maka shi koma menene tunda kace alkhairi ne._

Saboda bai b'oye masa ba tun ranar da ya fara bashi ajiyar motar,  ya yi  masa bayanin akwai aikin da yake zuwa yi wanda yake fatar ALLAH yasa yayi nasara akan lamarin, d'an liti ya nuna masa in har yasan abun bana gaskiya ba ne to don ALLAH ya fidda shi a ciki, Basarake ya tabbatar masa da ba komai sai alkhairi shiyasa ya saki jiki da shi har suka fara sabo da juna, don wani zubin ma idan ya fito  gidan sai  ya tsaya nan sun d'an tab'a fira har ma yayiwa 'yan matasan da ke zama wurin alkhairi, wani zubin ma sai yayi SALLAH wurinsu kafin ya wuce gida, shiyasa d'anliti zuciyarshi ta natsu da shi sosai. 

Munawwa ko kai tsaye Mummy ta wuce da ita d'akinta saboda bata son su had'e su k'ulla fad'a cikin dare ba wanda ya sani, sai da ta tabbatar ta daina kukan sannan ta fito ta nufi d'akinsu inda Munara take a kwance tayi shiru tana nazarin abunda zata yiwa Munawwa ta huce da marin da Mummy tayi mata, shigowar Mummy yasa tayi saurin tashi zaune don ta zaci Munawwa ce, saboda taci alwashin sai ta rama marin da Mummy tayi mata akanta, tana ganin Mummy ce ta k'ara had'e fuska tana gunguni, Mummy bata damu da yanayinta ba ta zauna akan bed side d'in gadonsu, idonta akan Munara da ta fara zunbure zunburen baki tana muimui, a hankali ta kira sunanta tace, 

"Munara me yasa a kullum ba kya son ganin farin cikina ne?, me nayi maki Munara?ki fad'a mani me 'yar uwarki ta tare maki ne da a kullum masifarki akanta take k'arewa?idan har wani abu take yi maki ki sanar dani bazan ji nauyin rok'arki ki yafe mata ba, saboda nidai na gaji da wannan tashin hankali, 'yan biyu ne ku fa wad'anda kuka fito ciki d'aya kuka sha nono d'aya kuka tashi gida d'aya, me yasa bazaku had'a kanku ku k'aunaci junanku ba? amman ace kullum kuna cikin rikici da juna, wanda tun yana tsakaninku har yanzu yazo ya koma tsakani na da mahaifinku, don ALLAH munara ki natsu ki gyara halayyarki ku zauna lafiya da 'yar uwarki kinji?"

Munara ta tashi tsaye tana ta kunburi ta nufi hanyar toilet sannan ta tsaya tace    "Bayan duk kece kike haddasa komi sannan yanzu ki dawo kina d'ora man laifi, duk kin manta banbancin da kike nunawa tsakaninmu? kin nuna kinfi sonta dani to ai ba komi tunda nima Daddyna yana sona."

Ta shige toilet tareda rufo k'ofar, Mummy ta yunk'ura jiki ba kuzari taje k'ofar toilet d'in ta tsaya  a cikin muryar Kuka tace  "Da ke da ita duk 'ya'yana ne wad'anda nike so fiye da komi a duniya, yanda kike ganin ina sonta haka kema nike sonki, banbancinku d'aya a wurina ita tana jin maganata duk abunda na haneta bata yi kuma duk abunda nace tayi shi take yi, sab'anin ke da a kullum bakya shayin sab'a mani akan koma minene, shin taya ni da na haifeki baki yi man biyayya sannan ace ni zan faranta maki?, kiyiwa kanki karatun ta nutsu munara ki sani akwai rayuwa akwai Mutuwa, kuma idan zata zo ba k'wank'wasa kofa take yi ba, balle kiyi sauri ki kimtsa kafin ta shigo ta d'auke ki, ki gyara halayyarki ki zauna da 'yar uwarki lafiya ku kaunaci junanku, saboda ba tabbas d'in zamu yi ta kasancewa tare da ku har k'arshen rayuwa  balle muyi ta zaman yi maku shari'a, a kullum fatanmu ku k'ara had'a kanku musamman ku da kuke mata don shi yayanku namiji ne ba ruwanshi da wannan, tunda ko yanzu kina gani tun da yayi aure ya tafi da matarshi har yanzu ko ganin gida bai zo ba, shin idan ku kun ware wurin wa zamu ji sanyi?."

Mummy tana k'are maganar a cikin tuk'owar wani sabon Kuka da yazo mata, da sauri ta fice d'akin tayi kitchen saboda bata son Munawwa ta gani ta k'ara d'aga hankalinta. 
Munara kam daga toilet d'in  duk abunda Mummy ta fad'a tana jinta, amman sai mere take yi tana wani abu da baki alamar duk abunda ma Mummyn ke fad'i ba ta cikin kunnuwanta maganganun suke shiga ba, har sai da taji shiru sannan ta d'an bud'e k'ofar ta lek'o tana ganin bata wurin ta fito sadaf sadaf ta sakawa k'ofar d'akin key, sannan ta fad'a kan gadon tana tunanin ta wace hanya zata rama muguntar da Munawwara tayi mata, tunda itama yanzu ta kamu da son wancen bagidajen tasan zafin so, shiyasa ta yanke shawarar idan har taga za'a aura mata shi to ko sai tayi ta zuga Daddy ya raba su dole don tasan  muddin aka raba su to tabbas za'a k'unsa masu bak'in ciki ba kad'an ba, to da hakan zata rama duk munafuccin da Munawwa ta dad'e tana k'ulla mata wurin Mummy, wanda har yayi sanadin da bata sonta kuma wanda  yasa  tafi sonta akanta.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*23*

A daren ranar babu wanda yayi bacci mai dad'i kowa da abunda yake sak'awa a ranshi daga Basaraken har Munawynshi, Daddy da Mummy tareda Munara kai har baba Isah shima kanshi kwana yayi yana juyi zuciyarshi cike da tunanin rikicin da ya b'allo cikin gidan, tareda tausayin Aliyu da ya cika masa ciki saboda rashin aikinshi da yayi, amman da ya k'ara nazari sai yaga gara ma hakan da cin zarafin da Munara take yi masa, sannan uwa uba rashin hankalin da Maigidan yake yi duk don ya farantawa shalelenshi. 

Koda safiya ta waye Basarake bai bar garin Kaduna ba har sai da sak'on da yake jira ya iso, sannan ya bada umurnin akai wa Munawynshi kai tsaye tareda uwar siyayyar da ya narko mata, wadda duk yayi ne da sunan Muhammadu zaharadden waton abokinshi, sannan ya hau tashi motar direba ya ja shi suka bar kuduna cikin kewar Masoyiyarsa ya nufi birnin tarayya Abuja. 

Da misalin k'arfe d'aya da rabi Munawwa tana zaune akan sallaya ta k'are SALLAH tana jera addu'o'i, idanunta har lokacin basu daina zubar da hawaye ba, saboda hargitsin da zuciyarta take ciki, saboda  damuwa goma da ashirin d'in da suka cika mata zuciya, duk da dai damuwar ta rage kad'an saboda Mummy da Daddy da ta hango kamar sun d'an shirya, saboda tun da ta tashi da safe yanayin da taga suna tarairayar juna ya tabbatar mata da ansamu ci gaba, duk da har lokacin fuskar Mummy cike take taf da damuwa. 

Bayan ta k'are addu'o'in ne ta mik'e tareda cire hijabinta tana cikin linkewa ne taji shigowar text a wayarta, da sauri ta d'auki wayar tana dubawa saboda tsammanin ko Aliyunta ne, don tayi ta kiran number'shi bata samu ba ta zaci ko rashin netwk d'in k'auyensu ne yasa wayar tak'i shiga, don ta matsu sosai taji ya sauka lafiya, aikuwa sai gashi taga sab'anin tunaninta, domin kuwa text ne na mutuminta Nuradden yana sanar da ita gift d'in da yace zai bata zai zo yanzu d'an aiken zai kawo mata, a cikin jin haushinshi ta buga wani uban tsaki ta ajiye wayar  cikin ranta tace,   

_"rashin aikin yi. "_ 

Ta d'an gincira akan gadon Mummy tana k'ara gwada kiran Number'r Aliyu, aiko ta kira har ta katse batareda ya d'aga ba saboda hankalinshi ya d'auku sosai wajen tunani, har sai da direbanshi da ke ta sharara gudu a cikin daji hankalinshi ya kai in da wayar ke ta fidda haske sannan yace 

"'yar lab'ai anata nemanka a waya fa"

Sannan hankalinshi ya dawo jjikinshi daga zuzzurfan tunanin da ya lula, jiki a sanyaye ya fiddo wayar da ke aljihunshi sai ga number'r Munawynshi ta fito b'aro b'aro akan screen d'in wayar, wadda ya sakawa suna da heartbeats a take wani farin ciki ya lullbeshi, fuskarshi cike da yalwataccen murmushi ya d'auka kenan yaji Muryar Mummy tana cewa 

"Kije Baba Isah yace ana nemanki a waje."
 
Munawwa bata sami damar cewa dashi komi ba ta datse kiran saboda bin Umurnin Mahaifiyarta dake tsaye akanta, ta yunk'ura ta tashi jiki ba kuzari ta saka hijabin da tayi SALLAH da shi ta fice, Mummy ta bita da kallo zuciyarta cike da tausayin 'yarta saboda yanda ta zama so cool a lokaci d'aya. 

Tun daga nesa ta hango fuskar Baba isah cike da annushuwa sai da ta k'araso jikin gate tace, 

"Barka da rana Baba naji ance ana nemana wake nema na halan?"

Baba Isah yana murmushi yace 

"Je ki gani wani aka aiko da sak'o" 

A cikin kokwanto tace a ranta  _anya ba wannan d'an renin hankalin bane kuwa?_

Ta tsaya bakin k'ofar gate d'in ta lek'a kanta aikuwa idanunta suka hango mata wata dank'areriyar mota light pink mai shegen kyau sai k'yallin sabunta take yi, da wani mutum tsaye a k'ofar gidan wanda yana hangota yayi saurin k'arasowa yace,   

"Ranki ya dad'e sak'o ne aka ce in kawo maki in har ba damuwa a bud'e gate d'in sai in shigo maki da ita a ciki."

Munawwa a cikin   mamaki tareda rud'ewa ta gwalalo ido a waje tace,    "wane sak'o ne wannan da har sai an bud'e gate?." 

Mutumin yayi d'an murmushi saboda yanda yaga ta rud'e yace,    " wannan motar da kika gani taki ce ke aka ce akawo Ma wa inji Muhammadu Nuradden  kisa a bud'e in shigar maki da ita sauri nikeyi jirana ake yi."

Kafin tace wani abu
Sai ga Baba isah ya wangale gate d'in yana baiwa mutumin umurnin ya shigo da motar,kamar wata sakarya haka ta rab'e tareda bin mutumin da kallo har ya shigo da motar  yayi parking,  ya ciro k'attin ledodin da ke cikin motar ya ajiye a gabanta, sannan ya rusuna ya mik'a mata key d'in motar ta zura hannu ta karb'a ba tareda tamasan ta karb'a ba, saboda hargitsin da kwakwalwarta ta shiga ta kasa gano me ya dace tayi , har  mutumin ya wuce baba isah ya rufe gate d'in ya isa wurinta  yana murmushi yace da ita,   "Kwantar da hankalinki 'yata kin isa ne shiyasa kuma kinfi k'arfin kowace kyauta a duniya saboda kyawawan halayenki, ALLAH ya sanya alkhairi kuma ya kareki da sharrin k'arfe, bari in kai maki kayan daga ciki."

 Baba Isah ya jidi ledodin ya shigar mata dasu ya ajiye cikin corridon da zai kaika  k'aton falonsu, sannan ya dawo inda ya barta anan ya taradda ita, a cikin mamakin ganin yanayinta yace   

'yata gasu cen na ajiye maki sai ki k'arasa shigar dasu daga ciki,  Munawwa  kam sai a lokacin ta dawo daga suman tsaye na wuccin gadin da tayi, idanunta suka sake sauka akan tsadaddar motar wadda take ta so ta tuno inda ta tab'a ganin irinta, aiko  take  yanke zuciyarta ta tuno mata da motar da tasha gani ajiye bakin gindin wani iccen dalbejiya idan zasu je makaranta ko in sun dawo, wadda har ta tab'a yiwa ALIYU korafi akan ajiyetan da ake yi, ba shiri kirjinta ya buga dam! dam! A ranta tace, 

_"Na shigesu ko dai mai motar shine Nuradden d'in?"_

A rikice ta bar wajen ta nufi k'ofar shiga cikin gidan idanuwanta suka yi tozali da ledodin da ke ajiye, da sauri taci birki hankalinta ya k'ara tashi  cikin tsananin rud'ewa ta rab'a gefen ledodin,  ta fad'a babban falonsu tana k'wallawa Mummy kira 

"Mummy! Mummy! Munara ku fito ku ga wani abun al'mara."

Saboda tama manta da wai fad'a suke da Munarar, 
Aikuwa sai ga Mummy a d'ari ta fito da ludayin miyar da ta saukar  tana zubawa a kula, Munara ma dake bacci a zabure ta fito d'akinsu domin taji kiran har tsakiyar kanta, suna fitowa jikinta yana rawa tace 

"Ku zo muje waje ku ga abun Mamaki." 

Aikuwa suka yi dii suka bi bayanta da sauri.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*24*

Fitowarsu keda wuya idanuwansu suka yi tozali wad'annan manyan ledodin, don Munara har sai da tayi tuntub'e dasu lokacin da ta fito  saboda gaf suke da shiga falon,  a cikin takaici tareda mamaki tace 

"Wa ya ajiye muna kaya anan?don kawai asa mutane suje su karya k'afafuwansu."

Ta k'are maganar tareda buga wani uban tsaki,  Mummy ma ido bud'e tabi kayan da kallo, taja ta tsaya tana k'ok'arin yin magana ne Munawwa ta jawo hannunta tace, 

"Zo muje ku gani."

Aikuwa Mummy tabi bayan Munawwa Munara ta k'ara buga wani uban tsaki ta juya zata koma cikin gidan, mai ta tuna kuma sai gata ta dawo tabi bayansu. 

 Aiko Sai ga idanuwansu sun hango masu dank'areriyar motar sai k'yallin sabunta take yi, jikin Munara har tsuma yake yi yayinda taje take  shafa motar tareda fad'in

" wowwww!"

Ta k'are maganar tana bud'e motar jikinta har rawa yakeyi ta shiga ta zauna, farin ciki bayyane akan fuskarta saboda yanda kyawon motar ya tafi da ita da yawa. 
Mummy kanta motar ta burgeta sosai amma zuciyarta cike take fam da tunanin shin wai ta ina motar ta fito, don ko fuskarta bata b'oye mamakin da take yi ba, har ta isa wurin motar sannan ta kai dubanta ga Munawwa tace, 

"Nifa kinsani a duhu Munawwara daga ina wannan motar ta fito tareda wad'ancen tulin kayan da na gani?"

Munawwa kam  a tsule take da fargaba tareda tsoron abunda zata fad'a,  hannunta dafe da bakinta tana nazarin bayanin da zata yi kai tsaye su fahimceta, hannunta duka biyu dafe da kumatunta idonta akan motar tace, 

Wallahi nima Mummy abun ya bani mamaki sosai, wani fa ne daga had'uwarmu a chat shikenan yace zai zo gidanmu amma kafin yazo sai gift d'inshi ya riga zuwa, shine fa yanzu wannan kiran da kika ce Baba isah yazo ana yiman, wai shine fa  sai d'an sak'on ya kawo motar da kayan yace inji mutumin, kuma fa NI wallahi Mummy ban ma sanshi ba fa, kuma NI banma san a ina yake ba amma kinga wannan haukar da ya yi sai kace an saka shi."

Mummy ta zaro ido hannunta a gaba bata masan lokacin da ludayin miyar ya sub'uce a hanunta ba, sai k'arar faduwarshi suka ji, Munawwa ta duk'a ta d'auki ludayin yayin da Munara ta fito da hanzari tana karewa Munawwa kallo sama da k'asa tana girgiza kai sannan tace, 

"had'uwar chat kuma baki sanshi ba ko ganinsa baki tab'a yi ba amma yayi maki wannan kyautar cab! to ai hankali ma bazai tab'a d'aukar wannan zance ba, don ko a garin mahaukata ba'a tab'a yin irin haka ba, balle mu kizo ki rena muna wayo, WALLAHI da sake 'yanmata Mummy ki binciki 'yar son ki da kyau, don kar taje ta jawo muna bala'in da zai sa mu k'are rayuwarmu a prison, don wannan mota wallahi ko Daddy bai kai matsayin da zai hau irnta ba balle har a bada ita a matsayin kyauta, kyautar ma wai irin wannan ta renin hankali da naji tana fad'a saboda ta maida mu wasu jahilai."

Tana k'are fad'ar maganganun ta yi wa Munawwa wani matsaiyacin kallo sama da k'asa ta buga wani k'aton tsaki sannan ta bar wajen, ta nufi cikin gidan tana ta sambatun cin mutunci da cin fuska ga Munawwara, tana zuwa wurin kayan dake ajiye cikin corridon ta saka k'afa ta shure ledar da ke gabanta, ba shiri tace 

"Wash! Allah wad'annan shegun kaya zasu kassarani."

Mummy kam shiru tayi tana nazarin abun sannan ta cire tagumin da tayi tace 

"Mu shiga daga ciki kafin Daddyn naku ya dawo, amman kafin nann ina son ki fad'a man gaskiya Munawwara don bana son abunda zai zo daga baya ya d'aga muna hankali muna zaman zamanmu."

Tana maganar tana tafiya munawwa biye da ita a baya har suka kai wajen kayan Mummy ta duk'a ta d'auki leda d'aya jin da tayi bazata iya d'aukarta ita kad'ai ba saboda nauyinta,yasa tace da Munawwar ta rik'a su shiga da ita, aiko nik'i nik'i suka kama ledar suka kai d'akin Mummy, sannan suka dawo suka k'ara d'aukar d'aya. 

Bayan sun ajiye ledodin ne Mummy ta koma kitchen  ta zuzzuba abincin,  sannan ta dawo d'akin ta zauna kan gadon inda Munawwa take zaune tayi tagumi da hannunta biyu ta fad'a zuzzurfan tunani. 

Mummy ta kalleta taga yanda duk jikinta yayi sanyi, ta kira sunanta a hankali da wata muryar tausayi a cikin nutsuwa tace, 

"Munawwara"

Munawwa ta d'ago kai ta kalleta tareda cire tagumin da tayi tace, 

"N'aam Mummy." 

Mummy ta sake kiranta tace

"Munawwara ina son kiyi  man bayani tsakaninki da ALLAH me kika sani dangane da wannan kayan da wanda ya turo da su?"

Munawwa kam jikinta ya k'ara yin sanyi k'alau a cikin sassanya murya tace

"Mummy kinsan bazan maki karya ba koda ace ina yinta, wallahi abunda kika ji na fad'a shi d'in dai ne,sai dai k'arin tambihin da zan yimaki akan wannan lamari." 

Daga nan ta fara yi mata bayanin duk abunda tasani tun daga kan text's d'in da yake turo mata har chat d'in da suka YI  dashi, sannan ta k'ara yimata bayanin abunda zuciyarta take zargi, waton mai motar da take gani a kan hanya wanda zuciyarta take ta kai komo akan zaton shine Nuradden d'in. 

Mummy ta sauke k'atuwar ajiyar zuciya tsawon d'an lokaci tana tunani sannan tace 

"Dank'ari lalle wannan al'amari dole ne ayi bincike yanzu ai kina da number'shi ko? "

Munawwa ta gyad'a kai, Mummy tace to kirashi yanzu ki sanar dashi kinga sak'o kin gode amman Iyayenki sunce baza'a karb'a ba har sai ya fad'a maki wanene shi? a ina ya sanki?"

Munawwa ta jawo wayarta dake kan gadon ta danna masa kira, don wannan shine karon farko da ta tab'a kiranshi, don ko muryarshi bata tab'a Ji a kunnenta ba, har ta katse bai d'auka ba sai a na biyu ne ya d'auka a cikin murmushin jin dad'i yace

"Ina kan hanya idan na sauka zamuyi magana"

Kit ya kashe wayar Munawwa ta kalli Mummy da yake a hand's free ta saka, Mummy tace 

"To mu jira shi anjiman ai kafin Daddynki ya dawo don musan me zamu fad'a masa idan mun tashi."

Munawwa tace   "To Mummy"

Munara da ke lab'e tana saurarensu ta yi tsul ta shigo da 'yar fara'arta wadda iya kacinta a fuskar ta nufi kayan tana cewa, 

"Bari mu zo mu bud'a kayan mu kam mu d'ebe k'eyar idanuwa."

 D'ayar ledar kuma turame ne manya manya da lesussuka tareda shaddodi gizner gizner har wani kyalli sukeyi  da kamfaloli manyan masu wani maik'o kamar an shafa   masu mai. 

A cikin tantama Mummy ta d'auki wani set d'in sark'a ta duba bakinta har rawa yake yi tace, 

"Gwal!!!!"

Daga Munara har Munawwara da sauri suka yo kan sark'ar suna rige rigen karb'a su gani, a cikin Mamaki Munara tace 

"Wai shi wannan mutumin ko baya da hankali ne ni kam, anya ma wai ko ba d'anfashi ba ne?"

Mummy ta yi mata wani mugun kallo sannan tace, 

"Idan kinsan bakinki ba zai dinga fad'ar alkhairi ba don ALLAH ki fita kawai, ke wai shikenan baki da wata magana sai wadda zaki b'atawa mutane rai?"

Tana maganar tana tafiya har ta fice ta bugo d'akin da k'arfi, Mumny da Munawwa suka bita da kallon takaici, Munawwa tayi saurin cewa 

"Don ALLAH Mummy kada ki b'ata ranki akanta don ALLAH kici gaba da yimata addu'a kawai koda za'a sami sassaucin abun."

Mummy ta sauke ajiyar zuciya tace

"Ni na rasa me ke damun Kwakwalwarta shin ita wai bata tunanin abunda take bai dace ba?kullum jiya iyau, nifa lamarin Munara fa ya fara bani tsoro fa."

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*27*

Nuradden ya zaro ido a waje yana dariya yace 

"Rufani ka saya kar kasa masifaffiyar budurwarka ta tsokale man idanuwa saboda shegen kushinta, don wannan jarababbiyar yarinya wallahi komi zata iya aikatawa akan ka, in ba so kake yi ka yiwa Aysha asara ba."

Basarake yana 'yar dariya yace 

"Matsoraci fa baya zama gwani saboda haka ka saurareni da kyau kaji bayanin da zanyi, don ya zama dole fa kaine zaka YI aikin saboda amfani da nayi da sunanka."

Nuradden ya gimtse dariyar da yake yi saboda yanda yaji k'irjinshi ya buga,  Basarake yaso yayi dariya amma ya basar, kai tsaye ya fiddo wayarshi aljihu ya shiga nema chat d'in da yayi da Munawwa ya mik'a masa wayar, jiki a sanyaye Nuradden ya zura hannu ya karb'i wayar, sannan Basarake ya ce dashi, 

"Karanta chat d'in zaka gano abunda nike nufi"

Nuradden ya mayar da kallonshi akan wayar sannan ya fara karanta chat d'in, yana kawowa a dai dai inda Basarake yake sanar da ita  sunanshi Muhammadu Nuradden ya d'ago kanshi da sauri ya kalle shi, Basarake yayi wani munafukin murnushi tareda gyara zamanshi ya aza k'afa d'aya kan d'aya ya fara girgizar k'afa, alamun ba sauk'i a cikin duk wata magana da zata fito a bakinshi, Nuradden bakinshi yana rawa yace 

"Miye manufar saka sunana a cikin wannan issue?."

Basarake a take ya had'iye murmushinshi ya zama so serious ya fara magana kamar haka, 

"Nayi saka sunanka ne saboda ina son inyi amfani da kai in cimma manufata, wadda da ita ne nike son in k'ara gano gaskiyar soyayyar da Munawy take yiman, yanzu haka na aika mata da sako  amma da sunanka, saboda haka yanzu zan kirata sai kuyi waya sai naji abunda kuka tattauna sannan in had'a target d'ina  na gaba, saboda haka don ALLAH abokina ka bani had'in kai kada ka bari koda wasa a gane ba ni bane kai plss and plss, na zab'e ka ne saboda nasan kaine kafi cancanta da hakan dafatar ba zaka watsa man k'asa a ido ba."

Nuradden ya sauke ajiyar zuciya sannan yace, 

"Nasan wane ne kai Aliyu kuma kasan duk abunda ka nema aguna bazan tab'a k'in  yi maka ba matuk'ar abun ba na sab'awa Mahaliccinmu bane, wanda nasan ko kusa kai ma ba zaka aikata hakan da gangan ba, saboda haka naji na amince zan baka gudunmuwata dai dai yanda kake buk'ata, yanzu fayyace man komai nayi alk'awali zan yi maka koma menene."

Basarake yayi murmushin jin dad'i domin yasan daman cen ba zai k'i ya  masa ba, a tak'aice ya fayyace masa duk abunda yake nufi da abunda yasa agaba,da matakin da zai d'auka a gaba idan gaskiya ta bayyana, Nuradden ya jinjina masa tareda yimasa kirari, 

"Aliyu Haidar basaraken K'asar agadas Ali mai aikin gidan Daddy honey'n Munara angon Munawwara kai a gaisheka Muhammadu Nuradden."

Gaba d'aya suka kwashe da dariya suna cikin dariyar ne kiran Munawwa ya shigo wayarshi, amma ita Nuradden ne ta kira saboda Mummy ta matsa mata  akan ta k'ara kiranshi tunda shi har lokacin bai kira ba. 

Aiko suna ganin kiran gaba d'aya suka tsayar da dariyarsu,saboda daman wayar tana hannun Nuradden shi yasa yana ganin ansa heartbeat ya gano Munawyn Basarake ce, kuma da layin ne wanda yake a matsayin na Nuradden, aikuwa yana nuna masa yayi masa umurni da hannu akan ya d'auka, aiko ba b'ata lokaci ya d'auka zazzakar muryarta da ya jiyo ya sa shi natsuwa ba shiri, a cen b'angarenta kuma wani dad'i ya ziyarceta sosai don gaba d'aya bata cikin nutsuwarta daga ita har Mummy, saboda banbamin bala'in da Daddy ya rufe ido yana ta yi har lokacin, shiyasa Mummy ta matsa sai an kirashi, 
a hankali tace, 

D'azu sak'onka ya zo kamar yanda ka fad'a, sai dai Daddy yayi fad'a sosai da ganin sak'on, shiyasa yace ka zo yana son magana da kai, kasan tun farko sai da na sanar da kai akwai matsala amman kayi biris da zancena, saboda haka kazo yana nemanka domin kayi masa bayanin komai da bakinka." 

Nuradden yana murmushin da iya kacinshi a baki yace 

"OK ba damuwa ki dube ni nan da kwana biyu, insha ALLAH zan zo kada ki sami damuwa, komai kika ga nayi maki nayi ne kawai don son da nike maki, saboda kinji hausawa sunce tukuicin so kyauta ne, nagode sosai da kulanin da kike yi."

Kit yaji ta kashe wayar saboda ita gaba ki d'aya kanta ya k'ulle saboda rigimar da take ganin ya k'ara jefasu a tsakaninsu ita da Mummy, da kuma Daddy da Munara don tun lokacin Munara sai jifanta da jafa'i kala kala take yi har sai da Mummy ta so dukanta, itace ta hana don gudun tashin wata sabuwar  tarzoma tsakaninsu da Daddy. 

Nuradden da Basarake suka kalli juna suka yi murmushi, Basarake yace 

Shikenan komai yayi dai dai nima a ranar ce nike so in koma ALLAH ya shige muna a gaba, daga nan suka ci gaba da tattaunawa akan matsalar sannan sukayi sallama, Nuradden ya tafi zuciyarshi tana ta kai da komo akan wannan badak'alar. 

Basarake kam bajewa yayi ya kira Munawynshi suka ci gaba da fira duk da ya fahimci damuwar da take ciki a sautin muryarta. 

Koda safiya ta waye da sak'on munawwa ya tafi yi wa su Ummi ina kwana, sai da ya fara zuwa wurin Ummi bayan yayi mata barka da safiya ne sannan ya mik'a mata ledar, tareda yi mata bayanin sak'o ne daga surukarta, duk da kayan ba wasu bane amman taji dad'i sosai kuma ta yaba, ganin yanda taji dadi ne yasa ya kira Number'r Munawynshi ya had'a ta da Ummi, a cikin farin ciki ta fara gayar da Ummi cikin murya mai cike da ladabi, Ummi taji dad'i har a ranta ganin a waya ma yanda take girmamata ina ga fili?, tayi mata  godiyar sak'on sannan suka YI sallama, a cikin tsantsar farin ciki Ummi ta dinga yi masu addu'a tareda fatan alkhairi, Basarake zuciyarshi cike da jin dad'i ya nufi wurin Kakale. 

Kakale ma da taga sak'on taji dad'i sosai amman fa zuciyarta tak'i kwantawa da budurwar da yake son, saboda ita dai da zai bi ta tata da ya rabu da tagwayen duka yaje ya nemi wata ya aura, saboda samun nutsuwar aurenshi don ita kam ta hango akwai matsala a cikin wannan tafiya, duk da bai fayyace mata komai ba, don hausawa sunce abunda babba ya hango yaro ko ya hau sama bazai iya hango shi ba, Itama ya had'ata da Munawwa tayi mata godiya tareda d'an barkwanci sannan suka yi sallama. 

Sannan ya fito ya je wajen Abbanshi sun dad'e suna tattauna matsalolin kasuwancinsu da ci gabanshi, tareda shawarwarin hanyoyin da zasu bi su k'ara fad'ad'a shi a fad'in Nigeria da k'asashen ke'tare, sannan Basaraken ya fara sanar da shi akwai yarinyar da yake so amman dai basu kai k'arshe ba, ALHAJI Dikko yayi murna sosai kuma yayi masa fatar alkhairi. 

Munawwa da Mummy kam suna jin yace zai zo amma sai nan da kwana biyu, hankalinsu ya k'ara tashi saboda su a sonsu yazo yau d'innan don kawai ya walwalewa Daddy komai da bakinshi a huta, Mummy tace da Munawwa taje inda tace tana ganin motarshi ta tambayi mai shagon taji gaskiyar komi tunda bazai rasa sanin wanene shi ba, aikuwa tana k'are shirin makaranta ta barwa Munara key d'in motarsu ta hau napep ta nufi wurin d'an liti mai shago, bayan sun gaisa ne take tambayarshi 

"Don ALLAH malam wani bawan ALLAH nike tambaya"

D'anliti yana aunawa wani yaro sugar yace 

"ina saurarenki 'yanmata ALLAH yasa dai na sanshi"

Munawwa tace    "ka sanshi ma ai ko domin anan unguwar taku yake"

D'anliti ya mik'awa yaron sugan 'ya maida hankalinshi akanta yace 

"To wanene a cikin unguwar" 

Munawwa ta gyara tsayuwarta tace 

"Ina wata mota da ake ajiyewa a gindin wannan dalbejiya?"

D'an liti ya d'aga kai alamar ya gano tace 

"Yawwa to don Allah kasan mai motar?"

Gaban D'anliti ya fad'i saboda yaji tsoron kada ace wani abu Aliyu yayi, a cikin sanyin jiki yace   "Eh nasanshi sosai meke faruwa."

Munawwa tayi saurin fad'in    "Eh to ba abunda yayi mummuna gaskiya kawai dai ina son in san miye sunanshi? kuma miye sunan mahaifinshi?kuma sannan miye sana'arshi?"

D'anliti yace   "Rigiji gabji to a gaskiya ni sunanshi kawai nasani amman bansan sunan Babanshi ba, don saboda yace mani shi ba d'an garinnan ne ba wani aiki ne ya kawo shi garinnan,haka ma ni bansan sana'arshi ba abu d'aya ne nasani yana da kirki sosai, sannan a fahimtarshi da nayi shi mutum ne mai son ibada, don watarana ma shi yake ja muna jam'in SALLAH a wannan masallacin"

Munawwa ta kai kallonta ga wani d'an fili da aka zagaye da duwarwatsu, inda yake yi mata nuni da hannu sannan ta dawo da kallonta gareshi tace, 

"Miye sunanshi?"

Kai tsaye d'anliti yace  "Aliyu."

Kirjin munawwa ya buga dam! Dam! Saboda sunan Aliyunta da taji ya kira a hankali tace,    "Anya ba Nuradden sunanshi ba ko?"

D'anliti ya shiga tunani na d'an lokaci sannan yace,     "Aliyu ne sunanshi gaskiya sai dai in ba wanda kike nufi bane."

Munawwa ta fara tafiya sannan  tace "OK nagode sosai da lokacinka da ka bani."

Ta juya ta fara tafiya har takai wurin napep d'in da ke tsaye tana jiranta d'anliti ya d'aga murya yace,     "Miye sunan Hajiyar?"

Tayi murmushi mai sauti kai tsaye tace,     "Munawwara."

 Ta k'are fad'a ta shige napep d'in suka bar wajen zuciyarta tana ta sak'e  sak'e akan cewa 

_waton dai ba shi bane Nuradden d'in Oh ALLAH shi wannan mutumi wai a'ina ma ya ganni nikam?._ 

 Sukuku take har aka tashi School ta dawo gida, Mummy ma da taji ba shi bane ta shiga rud'u tareda zullumin zuwan ranar, aiko tun daren ranar suke ta addu'ar ALLAH yasa yazo kar ya ce kuma wani uzuri ya rik'e shi, aiko ranar tun da safe Nuradden ta turo mata text akan ta dubi zuwanshi. 

**

Basarake kam a daddafe yayi kwana uku Abuja ya baro garin tareda Abokinshi Nuradden suka yo Kaduna,  kai tsaye suka nufi hotel d'in da yake sauka, bayan sunci sunsha ne Nuradden yayi shigar manyan alhazawa, domin ko shaddar dake jikinshi ka kalla kasan ba k'aramin mai kud'i bane, don agogonshi ma kawai zai yi 50k balle takalman da ke k'afarshi da hula, Nuradden fa ya fito tamkar wata d'an daren goma sha biyu fuskarshi sai shek'i take yi, don ko fatarshi ka kalla a take zaka gano hutun da ya ratsa jikinshi, duk da shi ba fari bane kamar Basarake haka kuma bai kai basarake kyau ba amman ko kad'an babu marena a halittarshi. 

Sab'anin Basarake da yayi shigarshi da ya saba idan zai je gidansu Munara,waton dai wani k'aramin yadi ne mai sauk'in kud'i amman yasha guga sosai kuma yayi masa kyau,  saboda ire iren kayan da yake sakawa kenan in zaije gidan, don takanas yasa aka d'inka masa su don kawai ya cika manufarshi. 

A cikin wata arniyar mota sabuwa suka shiga direba ya jasu suka tafi, Basarake kam ana kaiwa wurin d'an liti aka ajiye shi, sannan suka wuce kai tsaye sai gidan su Daddy.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*28*

Munawwa kam tun lokacin da yace zai zo madadin taji farin ciki sai kuma ta koma fad'uwar gaba, saboda ita dai har zuciyarta bata jin wata murna balle d'okin ganinshi, fatarta kawai yazo ya tafi da kayanshi koda zata sami natsuwa da kwanciyar hankali. 

Mummy kawai ce keta farin ciki da zuwanshi, yayin da Munara take ta yi masu kallon uku saura kwabo, tana wani mere idan taga Mummy ta wuce tana ta kiciniyar had'a abun tarbon bak'in, Munawwa kam ita har da guzurin tsaki take samu gareta idan sun had'a ido  ko sunyi clashing. 

Koda k'arfe d'aya na rana tayi suna kitchen suna aiki Mummy tace da Munawwra taje tayi wanka, sannan ta tafi zuciya ba dad'i tayi wanka tayi SALLAH tareda yin shirinta normal kamar yanda ta saba, Mummy tana ganin shirin da tayi ta bata umirnin taje ta sake kaya,  sannan taje ta saka wani boil wanda aka yiwa aikin fonis yasha dinkin doguwar riga, tana cikin shirin ne wani text ya shigo yana sanar da ita nan da minti biyar ta zuba idon ganinshi, aikuwa gabanta ya k'ire ya fad'i dam! Dam! ta fito daga d'akinnasu ta nufi kitchen inda take tsammanin Mummy tana cen, Munara da ke falo kwance tana kallo da remote a hannunta tana canza tashoshi, tabi bayanta da kallo sannan ta buga wani  uban tsaki,Munawwa tayi kamar bata ji ta ba sai ga Mummy tana ganinta ta fara murmushin jin dad'i tace,      "Ko ke fa yanzu ba gashi kin k'ara fitowa ba."  

Munawwa tayi murmushi tana wasa da 'yan yatsunta da suka sha jan lalle, saboda Mummy ta matsa dole sai da taje aka yi mata shi tareda zuwa saloon aka wanko mata kanta, Munawwa tana k'ok'arin yin magana ne sai ga sallamar Malam Isah a bakin k'ofar falon, yana sanar da su bak'on ya iso kirjinta ya k'ara bugawa da sauri da sauri, Mummy tace da shi ya kaishi d'akin bak'i dake cikin compound d'in gidan,   sannan ta kalli Munawwa tace jeki ki k'ara shiryawa bari inje in sanar da Daddynku, ta haye sama da sauri zuciyarta cike da farin ciki da  jin dad'in,  bakinta a washe take sanar da Daddy zuwanshi, sai da yayi banza da ita kamar ba zaice komi ba sannan yace,       "To naji ko kuma sai kin saka ni agaba kin kaini wurinshi?"

Mummy ranta a jagule tace,     "ALLAH ya baka hak'uri." sannan ta fito zuciyarta cike da jin zafin bak'ank'anun maganar da yake sakar mata tun ranar da yaga wannan motar, kamar su suka rok'a aka basu. 

Sai da ya kwashi minti biyar a zaune bai motsa ba sannan ya tashi ya sauko ya nufi wani d'aki da zai kaishi kai tsaye cikin d'akin bak'in, tun daga k'amshin turaren Nuradden da ya jiyo ya san lalle wannan ba k'aramin mutum bane, tunaninshi ya kwance ne a lokacin da idanuwanshi suka yi tozali da Nuradden da ya hakimce akan kujera sai shan k'amshi yake yi yana latsa tsadaddar wayarshi sabuwa dal, Daddy ya shigo da sallama Nuradden ya amsa a cikin wani gadara gadara, Daddy ya zauna ba tareda yaga aibin abunda Nuradden yayi ba saboda yanda yayi masa kwarjini a ido sosai, bayan sun gaisa ne Nuradden ya fara magana a cikin wata isa isa yace,      "Ni sunana Muhamnadu  Nuradden Alhaji Mustapha mai gwal, da sauri Daddy yakai kallonshi ga Nuradden a cikin d'inbin mamaki yace,        " kana nufin d'an gidan Mustapha mai gwal ne, Nuradden ya k'ara tabbatar masa a cikin rawar baki mai cike tantama Daddy yace, 

"Na hannun daman Alhaji Dikko mai Gwal fa?"

Nuradden ya d'aga masa kai fuskarshi cike da wata izza izza yace,    "Naga 'yarka Munawwara naji ina sonta da aure naso nazo kafin zuwan sak'ona, sai gashi hidima tasha kaina ina jin sak'on neman da kake yi mani shine na katse komai nazo, dafatar dai ba wata matsala ce ta cisgo ba."

Daddy jikinshi yana tsumar farin ciki yace, 

"Ba wata matsala kawai dai ina son in san wanene yake son 'yata, don wannan kyauta da kayi mata ta bani tsoro sosai shiyasa na ce kazo in ji ta bakinka, saboda mu fita daga kokwanton da muke yi don da nasan kai ne da bazan tada hankalina ba, amman yanzu shikenan hankalina ya kwanta indai Munawwara ce ka d'auka ma kamar anyi aurenku saboda na baka ita kyauta duk lokacin da kake so ka zo kawai a d'aura maku aure."

Nuradden yayi murmushin gefen baki yace,     "Da sauri haka?"

Daddy ya gyara zama sannan yace,     "Waye bai san ALHAJI Dikko da Alhaji Mustapha a fad'in Nigeria ba?, ai Mahaifinka sananne ne a duk wannan nahiya, kowa ya shede su mutane ne na kirki to me za'a jira ai burin kowane Uba ya aurawa 'yarshi miji nagari d'an gidan Mutunci." 

Nuradden yaji dad'in yabon iyayensu da yaji Daddy yayi har cikin ranshi, a cikin muryarshi mai cike da izza yace,      "Eh duk haka ne zancenka amman ina son a fara bincikar yarinya in har tana sona, na amince abata dama a hannunta ba tareda an tursasa mata aure na ba." 

Daddy yace,    "ko a musulunci nine yafi dacewa da in samo mata mijin aure a matsayinta na budurwa, kuma ai itace ta kawo ka ba mu muka kawo ka muka ce dole sai kai zata aura ba, don haka ina ganin wannan ba matsala  ba ce kaje kawai ka shiryo a duk lokacin da ka tashi ni zan aura maka ita."

Nuradden ya gyara zamanshi sannan yace,       "Nagode sosai da wannan karamci ALLAH yasanya alkhairi a cikin lamarin."

Daddy yace "Ameeñ" tareda mik'ewa tsaye yace,      "bari inje in turo maka ita "     yana k'ok'arin fita d'akin ne Nuradden yace  "Ranka ya dad'e account d'inka nike so ka bani, aiko bakin daddy har rawa yake yi wajen fad'o masa sannan ya fita, farinciki kamar ya take kan jariri jinkinshi har rawa yakeyi ya isa wurinsu Mummy da ke falo, ya kalli Munawwa a cikin wani jin dad'i da ya mamaye zuciyarshi yace,    "Tashi kije ke yake jira ALLAH yayi maki albarka..... ."

 Kafin ya iyar da maganarshi ne yaji shigowar alert da sauri ya fiddo wayar da ke aljihunshi yana dubawa ya zaro ido, su Mummy suka bishi da Kallo Munara tayi saurin fad'in     "Daddy lafiya?"

Daddy ya d'ago fuska cike da murna yace   

"200k mai neman auren Munawwara ya turo man yanzu."

Ga baki d'aya suka zaro ido Munawwa da ke k'ok'arin barin wajen tana jin maganar k'irjinta ya tsananta bugawar da yake yi, Munara dafe da gaba tace,     "200k fa Daddy kace cab ah lalle Munawwa kura ta sami nama." 

   Mummy ta maka mata harara Daddy yace,      "D'an gidan Mustapha mai Gwal ne ashe, ai ba banza ba kai da ganin wata kyauta sai d'an gidan girma."

Mummy da Munara suka k'ara zaro ido tareda had'a baki wajen fad'in     "Mustapha mai Gwal fa?"       Yana girgizar Kafafuwa yace     "Shifa na hannun daman ALHAJI Dikko. Mai gwal, wanda a tarihi ma ance shine silar arzik'inshi abokai ne na k'ud da k'ud, shifa Dikkon ance shi kanshi baisan iya adadin dukiyarshi ba, wanda a tarihin masu kud'in Nigeria in baizo na biyu ba to dole ne ya zama na Uku."

Mummy tayi shiru kamar ruwa sun cita Munara kam sai fad'i take ga zuciyarta 

_Ina ma nice don ita wannan sokuwar ba sanin kan duniya tayi ba._

Munawwara kam tana k'ara kusantar d'akin bak'in  gabanta yana k'ara gudun fad'uwar da yake yi, ta d'an ja lokaci a k'ofar d'akin sannan ta yaye labulen d'akin ta shiga da makalalliyar  sallamarta a baki, kanta a k'asa ta sami kujera ta zauna ranta a had'e ta fara gayar dashi kamar bata so, Nuradden kam yana d'ora idonshi akanta yaji k'irjinshi ya buga dam! dam!, yanda take motsa lab'b'anta wajen gayar dashi ya burgeshi matuk'a, a hankali yace, 

"Munawwara laifin me nayi ne da ba'a son had'a idanuwa dani?"

Ta k'ara sunne kanta tareda zubowar wasu hawayen da batasan ko na minene ba, a cikin tashin hankali yace,     "Subhanallah me aka yi ne da zafi da har ya saka ki kuka?,idan har ni nayi laifin ina neman yafiya kaina a bisa wuya."

Munawwa ta share hawayenta kanta a sunkuye tace,     "Ba komi"   sannan ya sauke ajiyar zuciya yace,      "Yau gani a gabanki Munawwara nazo da kokon barana akan ki bani soyayyarki."

Munawwara ta d'ago fuskarta suka kalli juna ido cikin ido tace da shi,       "Kayi hak'uri an riga da anyi man miji, ka yafe man nayi laifi da tun farko ban sanar da kai ba, abunda duk ka bani komi yana nan yanda ka kawo idan ka tashi ka tafi da abunka nagode."

Ta yunk'ura zata tashi ne sai ga sallamar Munara da k'aton tire sai wani shu'umin murmushi take yi, saboda ta jiyo abunda Munawwara ta fad'a har a ranta taji dad'in hakan, Musamman da ta d'ora idonta akan Nuradden ta yaba sosai da tsarinshi, tana ajiye tiren ta zauna tana gayar dashi tareda fara zuzzuba masa abincin, Nuradden yana murmushi tareda k'ara k'ule kallonshi ga Munara saboda yana son ya gano banbancin da suke dashi, amman duk iya nacinshi ya kasa gano banbancin don shi in banda ya gansu su biyu da sai yaga kamar ma duk mutum d'aya ce, banbancinsu kawai suturar da ke sanye a jikinsu, Munawwa da ke tsaye bakin k'ofa rik'e da labule fuska ba walwala tace,       "Ni zan shiga daga ciki ALLAH ya sauke ka lafiya." 

Tana k'are fad'ar haka ta fice fitt Munara ta bita da kallon takaici sannan ta dawo da kallonta akanshi tace 

"Don ALLAH kayi hak'uri wani matsiyaci ne take so, wanda ko cikin matsiyatan shine number one."  

Nuradden yaji zafin zagin da tayiwa abokinshi har tsakiyar ranshi, amman yayi k'ok'arin  dannewa ya zaro kud'i a aljihunshi ya ajiye a gabanta sannan yace,      "Na saka ki ki zamo sarkin yak'i na ina son ki janyo man ra'ayinta"


Munara ta kai kallonta ga kud'in sannan tace

"Da ka bar abunka don wannan kangararriyar tunda kaji ta fad'a, to da wuya a iya tankwara ta tunda ta nuna bata sonka sai dai ka yi hak'uri kaje ka nemi wata, saboda son wancen mutumin ya riga da ya rufe mata ido."

Nuradden ya mik'e tsaye saboda jin yake yi kamar ya maketa, a cikin damuwar da ta bayyana akan fuskarshi yace,     "Ni zan tafi sai kuma wani lokacin idan na dawo."

Har ya kai bakin k'ofa ya juyo yace     "Bani account d'inta idan kin sani"

Munara ta dinga karanto masa yana lodawa, sannan ya mayar da wayar aljihunshi ya fita yana gyara zaman malunmalunshi, Munara tabi shi da kallo aranta tace,    

_"gentleman"_

Saboda ya burgeta sosai ta tashi tsam ta koma cikin gida inda ta tarar da Daddy da Mummy kad'ai a falo, jikinta yana rawa tace, 

"Daddy yace zai tafi"

Daddy ya kwalawa Munawwa kira dake d'akinsu sai gata ta fito afujajan, tana zuwa Daddy yace 

"Jeki ki rakashi."

Munawwa ta fito  ranta a jagule ta nufi k'ofar gidansu, tana lek'awa sai gata da fitowa gaba d'aya  farin ciki da ya lullebeta na ganin sahibul k'albinta Basarake, da ke zaune akan wani dutsi yana k'ok'arin kiranta a waya.
