*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*29*

Kai tsaye ta nufi wurinshi fuskarta a washe saboda farin cikin da ya lullub'e mata zuciya, yana duk'e yaji alamun tsayuwar mutum akanshi, da sauri ya d'ago idonshi suka yi tozali da kyakkyawar fuskarta tana murmushi, a take shima farin ciki ya lullub'e tashi zuciyar a cikin natsuwa ya Mik'e tsaye yana k'are mata kallo, saboda shigar da tayi ta burgeshi sosai a hankali ya bud'a bakinshi yace, 

"My beautiful."

Munawwa ta rufe fuskarta da tafukan hannayenta, yabi yatsunta da kallo jan lallen ya k'ayatar da shi matuk'a, a hankali ya sauke ajiyar zuciya sannan yace, 

"Yau kunyata ake Ji kenan bazan sami a saki jiki ayiman sannu da zuwan ba, balle in samu ace mani ya hanya."

Munawwa ta sauke hannuwanta tana murmushi tareda wasa da 'yan yatsunta tace, 

"ba haka bane ba."

Yayi saurin fad'in,    "To menene?"

Zata yi magana kenan suka tsinkayo Muryar Munara tana fad'in, 

"Amman dai wannan mutumin anyi k'unatacce, wallahi ALLAH wadaran wata rashin zuciya, yanzu duk korar da Daddy yayi maka bai isa yasa ka daina zuwa gidannan ba,  har sai ka dinga kwaso kutaren kafafuwanka ka dawo, ALLAH ya kyauta wallahi anyi asarar kud'in tara an yada zuciya kare ya cenye."

Ta k'are maganar tareda buga tsaki ta nufi Nuradden inda yake har lokacin jingine da motar, sai dai sab'anin d'azu ba wayar yake latsa ba rungume yake da hannuwansa biyu yana kallonsu Basarake, maganganun da yaji Munara ta fad'i sun b'ata masa rai sosai, wanda har sai da b'acin ran ya bayyana akan fuskarshi, yanayin da taga fuskarshi ne yasa ta shiga taitayinta tareda k'ara jin haushin Munara da ta biye Basaraken, don ita a tsammaninta ganin da yayi masu ne yasa shi b'acin rai, a cikin muryar lallashi ta fara magana, 

"Kayi hak'uri don ALLAH WALLAHI haka muke fama da ita, narasa me ta gani a jikin wancen matsiyacin har take kula sa."

Nuradden ranshi ya k'ara baci ya furzar da iska a bakinshi yace, 

"Wanene shi?"

Munara ta kai kallonta garesu tace,    "Waye shi fa banda k'aton matsiyacin da a duba d'aya zaka hango tsabar talaucin da ya gada iyaye da kakanni."

Nuradden yayi mata wani kallo har sai da hantar cikinta ta kad'a  fuskarshi a tsuke yace,      "Miye dangantakar su?"

Munara ta yatsina fuska sannan tayi mere tace,      "shine dai wanda nike fad'a maka tana so, ita ko kunya ma bata ji."

Ta k'are maganar tareda yin tsaki, Nuradden cikin fushi ya gyara zaman rigarshi, yayo cikinta kamar zai daketa da sauri taja baya kai tsaye ya wuceta ya nufi inda suke tsaye, hankalinsu ya d'auku sosai sun nutsa cikin nunawa juna k'auna, sai kawai ganin mutum suka yi a kansu. 

A cikin kakkausar murya mai cike da fushi Nuradden ya kalleta yace,     "Wane ne shi da har kika bashi fuskar dani kika kasa bani a zuwana na farko a gidanku?ki ka zab'i ki wulakantani a kanshi"

Munawwa tayi wani Murmushin tura haushi tace,    "Shine mijin da zan aura ko ka manta na sanar da kai an tsayar man da miji?"

Nuradden ya zaro ido a cikin rawar baki yace 

"Miji fa kika ce wannan shine mijin?ai ni na zata mai wanki da gugar gidanku ne, to ke Munawwara in dai wannan ne me na sama yaci bale jefo man?kiyiwa kanki karatun ta natsu, kizo inda zaki huta kiji dad'i iyayenki da 'yan uwanki suji dad'i suma, kici arzik'i har kiyiwa wani hanyar samu, idan kin Amince dani zan jiyar dake dad'i, domin zan ajiye ki cikin k'aton gida hajji duk shekara umrah duk bayan wata d'aya, sutura sai kin zab'a mota sai wadda kika ga damar hawa,ki amince dani ki mori kurciyarki 'yanmata."

Ya k'are maganar yana wata dariyar shakiyanci, Munawwa tayi d'an murmushi sannan ta kalli Basarake ta kalli Nuradden sannan tace,       "Ni kud'i ba sune a gabana ba kaga wannan to shine cikar muradin Zuciyata idan har na aure shi, ina maka fatar samun mace tagari amman dai nikam na riga da na yiwa kaina zab'i ruwa da iska bazasu cenza NI akan ra'ayina ba."

Nuradden ya gyara tsayuwarshi tareda gyara zaman babbar rigarshi yace,      "Mahaifina siyar da gwalagwalai ne sana'arshi, nima itace tawa sana'ar zanyi ta baki su kiyi ta ado har sai kince abarsu hakanan, kar ki bari kurciya ta cuce ki yanzu an daina aure don soyayya, kedai kawai ki sami inda hankalinki zai kwanta zaki ji dad'i iyayenki suji."

Munawwa ta b'ata fuska sannan tace,   "Ni dai hak'uri kawai zan baka saboda na riga da nayiwa zuciyata zab'in mijin da zai zamo abokin rayuwata."

Nuradden cikin fushi ya fara magana a sama yace,    "Yanzu akan wannan k'aramin mutum zaki wulakantani?, to kisani ko kina so ko bakya so sai na aure ki, kai kuma daga yau na sake ganin k'afarka a k'ofar gidannan sai nasa an batar man da kai."

Yana k'are bubutan ya saka hannu aljihu ya ciro bandur d'in 1k ya cilla masa a jiki sannan yace,    "Kaje kaji da kanka tun kafin fushi na ya sauka akanka."

Yana k'are fadar maganar ya wuce fuuu ya fad'a mota Munara tayi saurin bin bayanshi ta fara bashi hak'uri ko kallo bata ishe shi ba yasa direba yaja motar su bar k'ofar gidan tareda bad'a mata k'ura. 

A cikin zafin nama ta shige gida da guda Munawwa tana ganin haka sanin Daddy yana gida yasa tace da Basarake,     "kayi hak'uri kaje zamuyi waya, saboda Daddy yana gida bana son yazo ya same ka anan, ka d'auki  kudinka kaje zasu yi maka amfani."

Basarake ya saka hannu a aljihu ya k'arewa kud'in  kallon sannan ya duk'a ya tattara kud'in ya taso ya mik'a mata tak'i karb'a, ya janyo gefen gyalen dake sanye a jikinta ya zuba kud'in ya k'ulle, sannan yace

"idan yazo ki maida masa da abunsa kice masa nagode."

Ya juya zai fara tafiya ta kira sunanshi a hankali tace, 

"Aliyu"

Ya tsaya ba tareda ya waigo ba a cikin sanyayyiyar muryarta tace,     "Ka turo iyayenka so zo nema maka aure na, ni na amince zan aureka a duk yanayin da zan same ka."

Basarake yayi saurin juyowa a hankali ya dawo kusa da ita ya tsaya, ido cikin ido yace da ita,      "Zaki iya auren talakan da bai ajiye ba bai ba wani ajiya ba?, zaki iya auren maraya wanda iyayenshi daman cen ba wasu bane balle yanzu?zaki iya auren wanda akullum safiya sai ya tafi nema sannnan ya samo abincin da zai baki a kullum."

A cikin dakiya tayi kundunbalar fad'in 

"Arzik'i da rashi duk na ALLAH ne, don haha  idan har ka shirya aurena to ka turo iyayenka."

Basarake wani farin ciki lullub'e masa zuciya,yana k'ok'arin yin magana sai ga Daddy ya fito kamar anjefo shi, yana k'yalla ido ya hango Munawwa tsaye da Basarake a cikin sauri ya isa garesu, ba b'ata lokaci ya d'aga hannu ya zabga mata mari sannan ya bata umurni akan ta shiga gida. 

Sai da Munawwa ta shige gida sannan ya dawo da kallinshi ga Basarake bakinshi yana kumfa sabida bala'i yace,      "da na k'ara ganin k'afarka anan sai na had'a ka da 'yan sanda sun lallasa man kai wallahi."

Basarake yayi saurin dafa k'afarshi yace

"Don ALLAH maigida kayi hak'uri wallahi da gaske nike sonta, itama tana sona don ALLAH ka barmu muyi aure."

A cikin zafin nama Daddy ya fincike k'afarshi tareda ja baya kad'an, yana yi masa kallon wulak'anci yace

"Aure? Aure fa kace duk ta rasa wa zata aura sai kai, to bari kaji wannan yaron da kagani shine na baiwa ita, kuma shine wanda za'a d'aurawa aure da ita nan da d'an lokaci, saboda haka ka taimaki kanka ka d'auke k'afarka daga gidannan, tun kafin ka tsokalo fushin masu kud'i su batar da kai a banza ni ba ruwana."

Basarake ya mik'e tsaye tareda kakkab'e k'asar da ke hannunshi, sannan ya fara tafiya zai bar wajen  ba tareda yace da Daddy komi ba, har yayi taku d'aya biyu zuwa uku Daddy ya d'aga murya yace,   

"ka tallafi talaucinka kada ka bari zuciyarka ta yaudareka kayi jayayya da masu kud'i, don wannan yaro da zan aurawa Munawwara d'an gidan Mustapha mai Gwal ne, saboda haka kaje ka nemo dai dai kai k'warya ta bi k'warya kawai, don in ta hau akushi kam bazata moru ba."

Yana k'are fad'ar maganganun ya yi fuuu ya shige gida tareda bugo 'yar k'aramar k'ofar da k'arfi, Baba Isah yana ganin Daddy ya shige ya  lek'o ga mamakinshi ya hango Ali tsaye d'an nesa da gidan kad'an, da sauri ya fito fuskarshi a washe yace,       "A'a Ali saukar yaushe?."

Basarake ya shanye damuwarshi ya fad'ad'a fuskarshi da fara'a yace,     "yau na diro baba ban dad'e da shigowa garin ba, shine nace bari nazo na gaisa da ku." 

Baba isah ya k'araso wurinshi yana ta farincikin ganinshi Aliyu ya rusuna ya gayar dashi Baban yace da shi,    "Ya ka baro mutanen garin naku? Da fatar dai kowa lafiya?."

Basarake yana 'yar dariya yace,     "Kowa lafiya baba duk suna gaida ku."

Yana maganar ya saka hannunshi aljihu ya ciro d'an konkon 'yan hannu da zobensu ya mik'awa baban, a cikin mamaki baba ya saka hannu ya karb'a sannan yace, 

"sak'on wa ye?"

Basarake yana Murmushi yace,    "Munawwara na siyowa Baba sai gashi Daddy bai bari na bata ba, don ALLAH ka bata tsarabar ta ce."

Sannan ya saka  hannu aljihu ya mik'awa Baba Isah yace,    "Gashi Baba kaima ka siye goro."

Baba isah ya karb'i kud'in zuciyarshi cike da mamaki ya kidanya kud'in, sannan yace

"Dubu biyar fa Ali?ai baza'a yi haka ba kaima kana ta fama da kanka."

Baba ya cire dubu biyu ya mayar masa da Uku yace,     "Ka rik'e sauran nagode ALLAH ya biyaka ya saka maka da gidan Aljannah Ubangiji ya cika maka gurinka na ALKHAIRI."

Basarake yaji dad'in addu'ar Baba Isah baki a washe yace,     "Ka barsu kawai Baba ka dai ci gaba da yiman addu'ar ALLAH ya shige muna a gaba."

Sannan suka yi sallama baba Isah sai zuba masa ruwan albarka yake yi, tareda yi masa addu'ar fatan nasara a rayuwarshi tareda gamawa da duniya lafiya. 

Munawwara kam da gudu ta shiga cikin gidan tana kuka,kai tsaye d'akin Mummy ta nufa Mummy tayi saurin bin bayanta, sai da ta k'ule Munara ta tashi tana rawar Murna zuciyarta cike da jin dad'i tace,     "Ai sai nayi ta saka Daddy yana kuntata maku tunda har kin kasa gano abunda ya dace dake, in banda dakikanci taya zaki had'a wannan had'ad'd'en guy da wancen bak'in matsiyaci dangin matsiyata."

Daddy ya shigo ranshi a b'ace ya fara hawa sama sannan ya juyo yace da Munara,    "Duk lokacin da kika ga ya k'ara zuwa gidannan ko bana gida ki kirani a waya ki sanar dani."

A cikin jin dad'i tace,   "To Daddy."



D/Auta novel🤝🏻

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*30*

Munawwa kam ta dunk'ule a kan gadon Mummy sai kuka take yi, Mummy ta shigo jiki a sanyaye ta zauna bakin gadon tareda kiran sunanta, Munawwa tayi k'ok'arin tsayar da kukanta tareda yunk'urawa ta tashi zaune,  sai sharar hawayen da suke sauka akan fuskarta takeyi, Mummy ta rik'o hannunta a cikin sigar lallashi tace,      "Duk da bansan abunda ya saka ki kuka ba amman nasan abun ba mai dad'i bane, tunda daga yanayin da naga Daddynku ya fita, Munawwara inda sabo ya kamata ace kin saba da halin Mahaifinku, ki zama jaruma akan duk wata matsala da zata fuskanto ki, ki daina bari zuciyarki tana rauni har ki zauna kina tsiyayar da hawayenki, shawara d'aya zan baki a duk lokacin da kika tsinci kanki cikin b'acin rai, kiyi k'ok'ari ki dinga nanacin fad'ar INNALILLAHI WA INNA ilaihirrajiuna, da sannu zaki tsinci kan ki cikin nutsuwa tareda rage k'uncin damuwar da take damunki, sannan ki kiyaye a duk lokacin da kika ji damuwa ta dameki to ki d'auko al Qur'ani ki karanta, insha ALLAHU zaki ji zuciyarki tayi sanyi har ma ta gushe maki damuwar da kike ciki, kiyi hak'uri kinji kar ki gaji wurin yiwa Mahaifinku addu...... ."

Sai ga wayarta tayi k'ara Mummy dake kusa da wayar ta jawo ta mik'a mata, tana duba mai kiran ta b'ata fuska tareda buga tsaki ta ajiye wayar, a cikin Mamaki Mummy tace,     "Ki d'auki wayar mana."

Munawa ta zunb'uro baki a waje wasu sababbin hawaye suka gangaro akan fuskarta ta saka hannu ta share  sannan tace,       "Nifa WALLAHI Mummy bana son shi."

Mummy tayi saurin fad'in,     "Wanene?"

Munawwa ta share hawayenta da suke ta fareti akan fuskarta  tareda gyara zamanta tace,       "Wannan da yazo d'azu amman naga Daddy da Munara sai rawar jiki suke yi da shi, don yanzu haka ma Munara ce taje tayi man gami wurin Daddy yazo ya mareni ya koroni ina da tabbacin bazai yi k'asa a gwiwa ba wajen ciwa Aliyu zarafi, narasa me Aliyu ya tarewa Munara da take ta son wulak'anta shi."

Mummy ta sauke ajiyar zuciya sannan tace,      "Yaushe Aliyun yazo? ko Daddynku ya maida shi kan aikinshi ne?."

Munawwa ta b'ata fuska sannan tace 

"A'a Mummy ya dai zo mu gaisa ne."

Mummy tayi tagumi idonta akan fuskar Munawwara tace,    "Yanzu kina nufin bakya son D'an gidan Mustapha mai Gwal d'in?"

Munawwa tayi saurin d'aga kai sannan ta k'ara da cewa,    "Ni fa wallahi ba kud'i ne agaba na ba, don sam ni kud'insu basa rud'ani idan dai Munara tana sonshi shikenan, nidai abarni da zab'ina don ALLAH Mummy."

Mummy ta sauke k'atuwar ajiyar zuciya sannan ta cire tagumin tace,    "Ki rufawa kanki asiri ki bar wannan magana a tsakaninmu, saboda muddin kikace bakya sonshi daga ni har ke mun shiga uku ga Daddynku, don zai ce nice nike zuga ki kuma bayan ni kaina wannan Aliyun fa bai kwanta man arai ba, ki natsu ki fahimci abunda ya dace ba wai ina rena matsayinshi bane a'a  kawai dai hankalina ne kawai bai kwanta da shi ba"      Munawwa ta fashe da kuka gwanin ban tausayi sannan tace,    "Don ALLAH Mummy ni dai ki taimakeni ki  fad'awa Daddy bana son Nuradden d'in a aurawa Munara shi don Allah." 

Mummy ranta a jagule tace,    "wai ke kuka baya yi maki wuya ne?nifa bana son ganin kukan nan, ki tanadi Hawayenki zuwa lokacin da zasu yi maki amfani, don ina da tabbacin Daddynku ba zai taba lamunta akan ki auri Aliyun ba,  amman idan kina da jarumtar tunkarar shi da zance, kije da kanki ki sanar dashi don nikam wallahi babu ruwana."

Tana k'are maganar ta tashi tsam ranta a b'ace ta fita, Munawwara ta k'ara sautin kukanta saboda ita dai so take kawai a yarda da zab'inta, duk ita kanta tasan abu ne mai matuk'ar wahala zuciyarta ta natsu da tunanin mafita, sai kawai Alhaji Musa ya fad'o mata a rai wato yayan Daddynsu Uwa d'aya uba d'aya, aiko ta jawo wayarta ta fara dealing d'in number'r Yusuf, kira d'aya biyu ya d'auka a cikin barkwancin da suka saba yace,     "Student ya aka yi ne?."       a cikin damuwa ta shanye kukanta tareda k'ok'arin saita muryarta tace,     "Yusuf ALHAJI Baba yana gari? gobe da safe ina son inzo wurinshi idan zan je makaranta."

A cikin damuwa da yanayin muryarta da ya jiyo yace,     "Lafiya me aka yi maki kike kuka Munawwara?" 

Munawwara ta shanye Kukakanta sannan tace,     "Bana son tambaya ni dai ka gaya man yana gida gobe zan zo wurinshi,  don ALLAH ka fad'a masa don yasan da zuwana."

Kit ta kashe wayar  yusuf yayi sororo rik'e da waya a hannu zuciyarshi cike da mamakin abunda ya b'atawa Munawwara rai har haka, saboda sanin da yayi mata mace ce mai sanyin hali da hak'uri  duk da yasanta gwana ce a wurin kuka abu kad'an ne zai saka ta kuka, sab'anin Munara da abu ne mawuyaci aga kukanta, duk ko da jibgar da Mummy tasha yimata lokacin k'urciyarsu, saboda d'an banzan rashin jinta don ta k'ware da muguntarsu ta k'ofi, don Mummy Tasha wuyar Munara sosai lokacin da tana 'yar k'arama, saboda duk lokacin da taci zalin Munawwara Mummy ta dake ta ita ko ranar baza'a dafa abinci a gidan ba sai dai asiyo na waje aci, don ko ruwa ko zafi ba zasu yi ba balle har ta kai ga adafa abinci dasu aci, yanda ta zubasu haka zata zo ta tarar da abunta, in tazo dukanta Daddy ya hana ya sakata mota suje su siyo abunda za'a ci, tareda yi mata gargad'i akan kada ta kuskura ta doketa idan bata son hakan tana faruwa to ta daina dukanta, dole ba don taso ba take hak'ura sai dai idan ta cikata watarana ta sakata d'aki ta rufe mata baki da k'yalle tayi ta jibgarta, shiyasa ta kangare tun tana 'yar k'arama don ma Mummyn a kullum tana tofeta da addu'ar shiriya, da ALLAH kad'ai yasan inda bala'inta zai kai don wani zubin mummy sai taje bud'a sanwarta taga macizai suna yawo sun cika tukunya fam, ta fito  kitchen d'in da gudu a firgice Munara ta lab'e tayi ta cin dariya abunta, hankalinta a kwance tunda ta rama dukan da Mummy tayi mata, don ko Munawwara sai da ta shekara biyu bata iya tafiya ba, sai daga baya aka gano itace tayi mata k'ofi saboda ita tun tana wata takwas ta iya tafiya, Munawwara fa bala'in da tayi ba kad'an ba don ma an samu abun ya rage ba kamar lokacin da suna k'ananu ba. 

Munawwa tana k'are wayar da yusuf taga text akan wayarta tana dubawa taga alert d'in kud'i ne da Nuradden ya turo mata 100k, ba shiri ta zaro ido a cikin tsoro tace,     "Shi wai wannan me yake nufi ne?ni zai saya da kud'i koko ya abun yake ne?ni fa wallahi bana son hak....... ."

Maganarta ta katse ne saboda shigowar Mummy idonta akanta tace,     "Ke da waye kike fad'a kuma?"

Munawwara ta yi saurin fad'in "Nuradden wai ya turo man dubu d'ari abu sai kace hauka, bancin wad'anda ya baiwa Daddy kuma fa ko ALIYU sai da ya watsa masa kud'i wai yaje ya rik'i kansa dasu kada ya koma ganinshi a k'ofar gidanmu, nifa WALLAHI Mummy wannan b'arin kud'i da yake yi tsoro yake bani, kofa Munara na ganta da kud'i a hannunta kuma na tabbata shine ya bata, sannan ga wannan motar da ya bada ga uwar siyayyar cen shin Mummy ina hankalinmu ya shige ne?, da ba zamu  zauna mu fahimci abunda ya dace muyi ba , yanzu fa idan za'a lissafa kud'in da ya kashe akanmu wallahi Mummy zasu kai kusan Miliyan uku daga had'uwarmu da ko wata ba'a haura ba shin siyata zai YI ne ko kuwa?."

Mummy ta kai zaune ba shiri jikinta a sanyaye tace,      "In don wannan da wannan gaskiya, amman kuma ai kinji Daddynku yace masu kud'in gaske ne fa, duk da ni ba kud'in ne abun ji ba nafi so kije inda hankalinki zai kwanta, da zaki bi shawarata ki daina zancen Aliyunnan mu duk'ufa rok'on Unbangiji akan ALLAH ya kawo maki wani nagari da yafi tunda dai wannan bai kwanta maki a rai ba."

Munawwa ta sauko k'asa tareda kai gwiwoyinta k'asa ta ri'ko hannun Mummy, hawaye suna zuba akan fuskarta tace

"Wallahi Mummy ina son ALIYU kema ki so shi don ALLAH jikina yana bani ALIYU shine kad'ai wanda zan zauna dashi inji dad'in aure, Plsss Mummy don ALLAH kema ki so abunda nike so, koda zan rage mizanin tashin hankalin da nike ciki, ni nasan ALIYU ba kowa bane kuma ba d'an kowa bane amman ni dai zuciyata ta natsu da shi, saboda samu da rashi duk na ALLAH ne, idan ya so zai azurtashi ta hanyar da bai yi tsammani ba, saboda haka ni dai naji na amince zan zauna dashi a hakan, fatana kawai kiyi ta yiman addu'a ALLAH ya sanya alkhairi a cikin lamarin."

Kalaman Munawwara sun dabaibaye zuciyar Mummy, wanda har ta kasa samun abun fad'a saboda duk abunda Munawwa ta fad'a gaskiya ne, sai dai ita tana hango rikicin da za'a yi da Mahaifinsu, don tasan koda yasha giyar wake ba zai tab'a amincewa da Munawwara ta auri Aliyu ba, koda ko babu Nuradden balle yanda taga ya rud'e da wuya in zai yarda da zab'inta. 

Mummy ta fara sharewa Munawwara hawayen da ke ta sauka akan fuskarta, a cikin wata kasala da ta sauko mata tayi jarumtar fad'in,    "Ki kwantar da hankalinki kici gaba da addu'a insha ALLAHU komi zai zo da sauk'i."

A cikin sanyin jiki Munawwa ta d'ora kanta akan cinyar Mummy tace,     "Kema ki na son shi ko Mummy?"

Mummy ta dafa kanta wasu hawayen tausayinta suka sirnano mata, da sauri ta saka hannu ta share don bata son ta gani, a cikin wata murya mai cike da tausayi tace,     "nima Ina tare dake kuma ina goyon bayan duk abunda kike so, matukar babu sab'on ALLAH a ciki."

Munawwara ta tashi zumbur ta rungumeta a cikin tsantsar farin ciki tace,      "Nagode Mummy ALLAH ya saka maki da gidan aljannah."

Mummy tace,    "Ameen kuma ALLAH yayi maku albarka  kuma ya baku mazaje nagari ya k'ara shirya man ku duka har d'an uwanku, shima ALLAH ya karkato da hankalinshi ya waiwayo gida hakanan."

Munawwa tace, "Ameeñ Mummy ai insha ALLAHU zai dawo ne ki dai ci gaba da yi masa addu'a don ba abunda yafi k'arfin ALLAH,saboda  duk tsanani yana tare da sauki."

Da dare misalin k'arfe tara Munawwa ta nufi d'akin Daddy, har ya fara Murna ya zaci Munara ce, ganin sanyinta da yanda take ta d'ari d'ari dashi yasa ya gano Munawwa ce, nan take ya had'e fuska ko gaisuwarta a ciki ya amsa mata, ta sami jikin kujera ta rab'e gabanta yana fad'uwa ta fara magana,      "Don ALLAH Daddy kayi hak'uri nidai wallahi bana son mutumin nan, in Munara tana sonsa don ALLAH  a aura mata shi a matsayin ni don ALLAH Daddy."

Ba shiri ya kai kallonshi gareta a cikin tsananin fushi yace,       "Wai ke dak'ik'iyar ina ce da kwatakwata k'wak'walwarki ta dusa ce, don ma kin samu yace yana sonki maimakon kiji dad'i ki godewa ALLAH sai ki koma yiwa ALLAH butulci, a zatonki kinfishi kenan? To bari kiji ko a k'asar waje yaso yaje ya auro mace WALLAHI da gudu za'a bashi balle ke don kin samu zaki zowa da mutane iya shege da iskanci, WALLAHI ki kiyayeni tun kafin in canza maki kamannu a gidannan, aure keda shi ba fashi matuk'ar ba shine yace ya fasa ba, amman kuma kada kiji nace haka ki dinga wulak'antashi har yayi zuciya yace ya fasa d'in, WALLAHI ki sani koda wasa naji kin yi abunda zai b'ata masa rai sai na illataki a cikin gidannan, bak'ar munafuka mai k'ashin tsiya wadda arzik'i yana kiranta tana gudu, muguwa wadda bata son ganin ci gaban kanta balle na wani, Wallahi idan kika yi wasa sai na tsine maki akan aurennan."

 Munawwa ta fara kuka tana rok'arshi akan ita dai bata sonshi ya dawo ga Munara, aiko cikin zafin nama yayi cikinta da duka dak'yar ta samu ta fito d'akin ido rufe ta fara saukowa daga saman amman Daddy bai daina biyarta yana dukanta ba, aikuwa ta fad'i ba shiri sai gata tana gangarowa tana kai k'arshen step d'in ta baje plat babu alamun numfashi a tattare da ita. 




D/Auta Novel👍🏻


*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*31*

A d'ari Mummy ta k'araso wurin tareda janyota jikinta ta fashe da kuka tana fad'in,    "Wayyo ALLAH ya kashe mani 'ya."

Munara dake d'akinta sautin dukan da Daddy ke yiwa Munawwa, yasa jikinta har rawa yake yi ta fito domin kawai ta gani tayi dariya, aiko ganin Munawwa  tana gangarowa daga sama yasa ta aza hannu aka tareda gwalalo idanu waje, hankalinta ya tashi sosai Musamman da taga ko motsi bata yi ba bayan ta fad'o, da gudu ta k'araso wurin tareda dafe bakinta zuciyarta cike da fargabar kada ace ta mutu, Daddy kam yana ganin  aika aikar da yayi jiki ba kuzari ya sulale ya koma d'akinshi, zuciyarshi cike da tsananin tsoro da fargaba don baisan a wane hali take ba, amman girman kai ya hana ya je ya gani koda zai samu natsuwa.

Daddy ya bita da kallo sororo ya kasa magana Mummy ta k'ara maimaita masa a cikin d'aga murya tace,      "Munawwara bata da lafiya akaita asibiti yanzu a shawo kan matsalar tun kafin matsalar tayi tsauri"

Daddy yaja key d'in mota ba tareda yace da ita k'anzil ba ya fito ta biyo bayanshi da sauri aikuwa sai ga Munara tana kokawa da ita akan bazata fita ba, da taji haushi ta gartsa mata cizo a hannu, ba shiri ta saketa aiko ta zunduma aguje zata fita a cikin tsawa Mummy  ta d'aga murya tace, 

"Kada ka barta ta fita bata cikin hayyacinta" 

Aiko caraf Daddy ya ri'kota sai fisge fisge take yi akan sai anbarta ta fita,Mummy taje ta sako hijabinta jikinta sai rawa yake yi ta fito, koda tazo ta tarar da Munawwa ta k'ara firgicewa sai kici kicin fisgewa take yi a hannun Daddy tana runtuma kuwwa, tana zuwa ta ri'ko hannunta tana tirjiya suka fito da ita gidan, kai tsaye motar Daddy suka nufa dak'yar suka sakata tareda taimakon Baba Isah, sannan Mummy taja Daddy ya fisgi motar da k'arfi suka nufi wata private hospital, yanayin da suka ganta yasa aka yi saurin karb'arta aka shiga da ita emergency room, sai kurma kuwwa take yi ko'ina ana jin sautin muryarta sai baiwa Daddy hak'uri take yi akan bazata sake ba. 

Mummy takai zaune ba shiri tareda fashewa da wani sabon kuka, Daddy ma jikinshi yayi sanyi k'alau saboda zuciyarshi ta tabbatar masa da fad'uwar da tayi ce sanadi.

Basarake kam yana barin k'ofar gidan ya haye acab'a ya nufi hotel, zuciyarshi cike da tunani ya shiga d'akin da ya sauka inda Nuradden yake zaune yana jiranshi, aiko shigowarshi keda wuya Nuradden ya bashi hannu suka yi musabaha a cikin barkwanci yace,     "Ya dai saurayin budurwata?."

Basarake yayi d'an murmushin yak'e sannan ya kai hannunshi ga clips d'in da ke wuyan rigar Nuradden  ya cire, sannan yayi wani munafukin Murmushi ya d'auko computer tareda ciro wani abu jikin clip d'in ya saka a jikin computer'r, Nuradden yana biye dashi da kallo saboda mamakin Basarake da ya cika masa ciki, saboda ganin plan d'in da ya had'a masa wanda shi kanshi baisan dashi ba, jiki a sanyaye ya zo ya zauna gefen Basaraken idonshi akan fuskar computer'r,  aikuwa sai ga d'akin bak'insu Munara an hasko sannan daga baya sai gata ta shigo d'akin ta zauna, tiryan tiryan photon abunda ya wakana ya yi ta haskawa tareda duk maganganun da aka tattauna, har zuwa k'ofar gida inda yake tsaye da munawwara da barazanar da Nuradden yayi masa duk, har zuwanshi hotel d'in da d'akin da suke a yanzu, sai dai shi Nuradden ba'a ganin fuskarshi sai Muryarshi kawai da ke fita, sannan dukansu suka sauke ajiyar zuciya, Nuradden ya kalleshi yana dariyar yak'e  mai cike da jin haushin abunda Basaraken yayi masa, waton dai abun nufi kamar bai yarda dashi ba ranshi a jagule yace,      "Trust to me my man alkawali na d'auka ko wani bazan tab'a bari ya cutar da kai ba balle ni, amman idan kasan zuciyarka tana kokwanto akaina ka cire ne kawai kaci gaba da fighting d'in kai d'aya." 

Basarake yayi shiru kamar ba zai yi magana ba, sannan ya furzar da iska mai zafi  bakinshi sannan yace, 

"For give me my man nayi hakan ne kawai don bana son a fad'a man nafison inji kuma in gani da idona believed me i trust to you kaima ka sani"

Ya k'are maganar tareda bubbuga kafad'arshi Nuradden ya sauke ajiyar zuciya yace,     "What's our next target?"

Basarake ya rufe computer ya nutsa cikin zurfin tunani sannan yace,    "Tace in turo iyayena kuma ni bana son in sako su Abba a cikin lamarin har sai na tabbatar da ta cenye sauran exam's d'in."

Nuradden ya kai masa duka yana dariya yace

"Baka da kyau mutumina yarinyar nan fa da gaske take sonka don ALLAH ka tausaya mata haka nan plss."

Basarake ya tashi tsam ya fara tafiya hannuwanshi a baya ya fara zirga zirga neman mafita, sannan yace,    "Nasan tana sona amman akwai amfanin cikasa plans d'in ko don lokacin da duk gaskiya ta bayyana, 'yar uwarta ba zata ga naci amanarta ba, don na tabbata ita ko jarabawa d'aya bazata cin marking ko d'aya ba balle ta canye, saboda haka zanyi amfani da damata in damalmala mata tunaninta da na mahaifinta,  don nasan lokaci zai zo da ita da shi duka za su durk'usa gwiwa bibbiyu suna neman alfarma a wurina, ni kuma a lokacin na riga da nayi masu nisa dukansu."

Ya k'are maganar a cikin zafin rai wanda har b'acin ran ya bayyana akan fuskarshi, Nuradden ya taso ya dafa shi a cikin sigar lallashi yace,     "Easy easy abokina yana da kyau kaci gaba da jajircewa amman don ALLAH ka sassafta."

Basarake yana k'ok'arin yin magana ne Malam Isah ya kira number'r Basarake, har ta tsinke bai d'auka ba don ko dubawa ma bai yiba balle ya gano wa yake kiran, aiko aka sake kira na biyu Nuradden yaje ya d'auko a cikin 'yar dariya yace,    "Baba Isah ne fa."

A cikin zafin nama ya karb'a tareda kanga ta akan kunnenshi amman ya kasa cewa komi saboda zuciyarshi da yake ji ba dad'i, baba Isah kam kai tsaye yazo da maganarshi yace,     "'yar gidanka ce aka fito da ita ba lafiya aka nufi asibiti da ita don kamar ma mai shafar aljannu ko tab'in hankali"

Da k'arfi Basarake yace      "What!!!!!!!" ba shiri ya kai zaune tareda maimaita INNALILLAHI wa'inna ilaihirrajiun a cikin zuciyarshi, Nuradden ya karb'i wayar tareda tambayar Baba Isah abunda ke faruwa, ya maimaita masa duk abunda ya faru, jiki ba kuzari ya kashe wayar, sannan ya koma lallashin Basarake da duk yabi ya rud'e sai kiran Numbobin Munawwa yakeyi da layinshi mai sunan Nuradden , suna ta ringin ba'a d'auka saboda wayar tana d'akin Mummy ajiye akan gado, sannan a silent take balle Munara ta jiyo k'ararta ta d'auka.

"Nayi ta kiran Numbar Munawwara bata d'auka ba shine nace bari in kiraka inji ko lafiya."

Daddy jikinshi yana tsuma yace,      "Eh WALLAHI bata da lafiya muna asibitin Daula an shiga da ita emergency."

Nuradden yace,    "Subhanallah gani nan zuwa yanzu tunda ALLAH ya nufa ban riga na bar garin ba."

A cikin jin dad'i Daddy yace,      "OK ALLAH ya taimake ka sai kazo."

Sannan suka yi sallama Mummy kam ko kallonshi bata yi ba balle ta tambayi  wanene, saboda haushinshi da take ji tunda suka zo asibitin bata ce dashi k'anzil ba, sai fatar samun sauk'in 'yarta take yi don tasha alwashin da taji sauk'i, dole ne ta nuna masa b'acin ranta akan cin zarafin da yake yi masu ita da Munawwa, sannan ta nuna masa aibin banbancin da yake nunawa tsakanin 'ya'yanshi. 

Aikuwa ba b'ata lokaci suka k'araso asibitin don shi Basaraken ya rud'e sosai ko fuskar da yake sakawa bai saka ba haka ya fito suka garzayo asibitin, zuwansu yayi daidai da fitowa da Munawwa daga emergency room d'in, aka gangarota akan d'an k'aramin gadon d'aukar  majinyata, sambal tamkar wata matacciya saboda  allurar bacci da aka yi mata, da gudu Mummy tabi gadon sai hasbunallahu wani'imal wakil take fad'a a cikin zuciyarta, saboda firgicin da take a ciki tana fatar zuciyarta ta sami salama, Daddy kam bai bisu ba saboda ganin su Nuradden da yayi ya tsaya suna gaisawa, fuskar  Basarake da ya gani yasa ya k'ara rud'ewa saboda ya shaidar fuskar kasancewarshi maabocin kallon news tareda karatun jaridu, fuskarshi ba b'oyayyiya bace a wurinshi duk da bai tab'a ganinshi a zahiri ba, jikinshi yana rawa ya mik'a masa hannu suka gaisa tareda kallin Nuradden bakinshi yana rawa  yace ,      "Kace ba kai kad'ai kazo garin namu ba ashe har da babban bak'o kuke tare?lalle yau nayi farin gani tunda..... "

Da sauri Basarake ya katse sa zuciyarshi cike da jin haushinshi  yace,     "Ina mara lafiyar ne?"

Sai a sannan ne Daddy ya tuno da abunda ya kawo su a cikin jin kunyar kansa yace,    "Ku zo muje ranka ya dad'e yanzu aka shigar da ita d'aki."

Suka bishi a baya sai surutu yake zuba masu wanda baya da tushe balle makama,  saboda tsananin rud'ewar da yayi na yin ido biyu da, yaro da kud'i abokin manya waton ALIYU HAIDAR BASARAKE. 

Koda suka shiga d'akin kukan Mummy shine ya tarbesu, jiki ba kuzari suka k'arasa jikin gadon saboda tsananin tausayin da ta basu, Basarake ya kur'e kallonshi akan fuskar Munawwa yana jin soyayyarta tana k'ara fisgarshi, tareda tausayin halin da take ciki ba shiri ya dafe goshi saboda sarawar da kanshi yayi, Nuradden ne kawai ya saki jiki yana yi mata addu'ar samun sauk'i, Daddy ya jawo kujerun robar da ke d'akin guda biyu yace dasu su zauna, Nuradden ya zauna amman Basarake kikam yayi ya kasa zama saboda bai maji abunda Daddyn  ya fad'a ba, har sai da yace dashi,   "Ranka ya dad'e ka zauna ga kujera nan."

Sai a lokacin hankalinshi ya dawo ya zauna idonshi akan fuskar Munara, sai ga Dr yazo da wata farar takarda a hannunshi, ya kai gaisuwa a wurin su sannan yace da Daddy akwai maganar da zasu yi akan matsalar Munawwa, idan ba damuwa ya biyo shi office sai su tattauna, da sauri Daddy yace,      "Ai nan duk ba bare fad'a kawai ni Mahaifinta ga Mahaifiyarta sannan  wannan shine Mijin da zata aura."

Ya nuna Nuradden likitan yace,     "OK ba damuwa dama abinciken da muka yi mun gano kanta ya bugu wanda har jini ya tab'i kwakwalwarta shiyasa ta firgice ta fita out of her sense, a dalilin hakan dole sai an yi mata  aiki an goge jinin sannan ne zata dawo cikin hankalinta idan an dace, amman fa gaskiya aikin akwai had'ari sosai don d'ayan biyun ne ko ayi nasara ko akasin hakan wanda bama fata, saboda rashin nasarar zai janyo abu biyu ko tab'in hankalin ya k'aru  ko kuma ma arasa rayuwarta gaba d'aya."

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

Mummy ta k'ara sautin Kukanta, Basarake zuciyarshi ta buga dam! Dam! saboda tsananin tashin hankali har sai da jijiyar kanshi ta fito, a take ya ciro wayarshi ya fara rubuta text, minti biyu a tsakani text d'in ya shigo wayar Nuradden da sauri ya ciro wayar ya duba, saboda ya gano akwai magana tun daga kallon fuskar Abokinshi da tashin hankalin da yake a ciki, aikuwa sai gashi ya tabbatar saboda abunda yaga ya turo masa waton,    _Kayi amfani da damarka saboda ayi gaggawar fitar da ita waje nan da awa biyu masu zuwa._ 

Aikuwa a take Nuradden ya kalli Dr yace,      "Zamu fitar da ita waje cen ayi mata aikin." 

Dr cikin farin ciki yace, 

"Alhmdllh hakan ma shi yafi dacewa bari inje a had'o maku file d'inta"

Dr ya fita da sauri Daddy dake rakub'e jikin bango  jikinshi yayi sanyi k'alau saboda tsoron da ya shige shi, don baya son ace shine yayi sanadin mutuwar 'yarshi da hannunshi, aikuwa yana jin maganar da Nuradden yayi kaso mafi rinjaye na tashin hankalinshi ya ragu, sannan ya k'ara girma a idonshi ninkin wanda yake dashi a baya, kuma yaci alwashin da taji sauk'i ko sati ba za'a YI ba zai d'aura mata aure dashi, koda sonta ko bata so don bazai tab'a bari wannan dama ta sub'uce masa ba. 

Mummy kam hamdala tayi a zuciyarta saboda jin abun alkhairin da Nuradden zai yi masu na ceto rayuwar 'yarta, haka kawai taji tana tausayinshi saboda yanda yake ta d'awainiya da ita kuma ita gashi ba shine a gabanta ba. 

Cikin awa d'aya zuwa biyu Basarake ya yiwa Munawwa cuku cukun zuwa wata babbar asibitin da ke k'asar India, tareda rakiyar Mummy da Munara Nuradden da Basarake da yayi basajar rufe fuskarshi ruf, wajen saka wata jacket ya saka hularta da ta rufe fiye da rabin fuskarshi, sannan ya k'wama k'aton bak'in tabarau ya zauna nesa  da inda suke zaune, sai dai duk wani motsinsu akan idanuwanshi ne. 

Jirgin ya tashi da misalin k'arfe sha biyu da rabi na dare, Koda awa uku da rabi tayi suna filin jirgin babban birnin India waton New delhi, har airport motar asibitin tazo ta d'auki Munawwa da aka yiwa allurar bacci wanda har a lokacin bata falka ba,kasancewar basa buk'atar d'an jinya ita ka d'ai suka d'auka su Mummy suka bi Nuradden inda suma wata motar tazo ta  d'aukesu. 

Abun Mamaki sai ga Basarake nagani a cikin motar asibitin, hannunshi cikin na Munawwa ya k'ura mata ido sosai yana kallon yanda ta rame a cikin k'ank'anin lokaci, a take wasu hawaye suka sirnano masa wad'anda ya manta tsawon lokacin da zai tuna yayi kuka, amman yau sai gashi yana sharb'ar hawayen tausayin abar k'aunarshi, yana rik'e da hannunta har aka je asibitin dashi aka saka ta d'akin da za'a yimata gwajin Kwakwalwa, wanda tun kafin su zo har an turo masu da bayanin komai na file d'inta, aikuwa ba b'ata lokaci suka shiga binciken kwakwalwarta, sai gashi ma sun gano ba jini bane ya tab'i kwakwalwar tsabar firgici ne ya janyo mata shiga wannan hali, aikuwa suka duk'afa wajen bata agajin gaggawa saboda suna hangen matsalar tata mai sauk'i ce a wurinsu, aiko cikin d'an lokaci suka shawo kan matsalar, sannan suka  ajiyeta wani d'aki wanda zata d'auki tsawon awa goma sha biyu kafin ta farfad'o, sannan sunyi hani ga kowa akan ya matsa kusa da ita har sai lokacin awannin sun cika, amman Basarake ya sami damar da zai zauna wurinta shikadai, da sharad'in ko motsi mai k'arfi ba'a son ayi mata balle har ta kai ayi mata hayaniya, aikuwa Nuradden yaje yayiwa su Mummy bayanin ba'a buk'atar d'an jinya akwai masu kula da ita, saboda haka suzo ya kaisu masaukinsu don a lokacin dare ne su a wurinsu, Mummy ta kafe akan ita dai tana son taga 'yarta aiko dole sai da ya kaita ta glass d'in k'ofar ta hango Munawwa dake kwance sambal na'u'rori mak'ale a kanta da hannunta sai sharar bacci take da oxygen a hancinta dake k'ara taimakonta wajen zuko numfashi, aiko numfashinta sai sauka yake a hankali, sannan hankalinsu ya kwanta Munara ma taji tausayin 'yar uwarta sosai, Mummy kam hawayenta basu daina zuba ba har suka je inda aka tanadar masu, waton wata had'ad'd'iyar hotel ce wadda fad'in  kyaunta a baki b'ata lokaci ne, sai da suka yi sallah suka ci abinci duk da Mummy tsakurawa kawai tayi saboda alhinin yarta, sannan suka yi wanka Munara kam bajewa tayi tana ta baccinta bayan taci ta koshi,  Mummy kam dukufa tayi tana ta jero nafiloli tana rok'on ALLAH ya baiwa 'yarta lafiya, ya yaye masu matsalolin da suka kunno masu kai da wad'anda suka dad'e cikin gidansu ba tareda an shawo kansu ba, don tasan ko angama wannan akwai sauran wata rigima a k'asa, tunda dai kam Munawwa bata son Nuradden gashi kuma Daddynsu sam bazai canza ra'ayinshi ba, Musamman yanzu da yaga wannan bajinta da yayi masu. 

A cen asibitin kuwa Basarake yana zaune sai sak'awa da kwancewa yake yi, yana zaune a wurin sallah ce kawai ke tada shi tareda rik'on ALLAH ya baiwa Munawynshi lafiya, abinci ma kwata kwata baya jin sha'awar ci duk da yaji ance ciyon da sauk'i,  amman yafi son sai yaganta ta tashi garas sannan ne hankalinshi zai kwanta. 

 Dak'yar wani likita da kamar akwai sanayya tsakaninsu ya shawo kanshi ya d'an kurb'a tea a office d'inshi, sannan ya dawo ya zauna yana jiran tashinta sai duba agogo yake yi, aiko Awa goma dam ta fara motsa k'afarta a cikin tantama ya k'urawa k'afar kallo, aiko sai gashi ta k'ara motsa hannunta da sauri ya fita ya kira Dr'n suna zuwa suka tarar da ita dafe da kanta da hannunta d'aya da ba'a sakawa komi ba, tana yamutsa fuska alamar tana so ta bud'e idonta, da sauri likitan ya cire mata na'u'orin da aka mak'ala a kanta, aikuwa sai gashi ta bud'e idonta tana k'arewa d'akin kallo, caraf idonta suka sauka akan fuskar honey'n Munara dake tsaye yana kallonta, Murmushi kwance akan fuskarshi ba shiri ta runtse idanuwanta tak'i bud'ewa har sai da taji k'arar rufe k'ofa sannan ta bud'e, sai gashi taga d'akin ba kowa sai ita kad'ai ta nutsa sosai domin ta gano a'ina ne take, aikuwa k'arar wata na'ura dake d'illll! d'illll! Ta d'aga kai ta kai kallonta ga na'urar tareda k'ok'arin tab'o ta da hannunta dake mak'ale da k'arin ruwa, aikuwa zafin da taji ya ziyarceta ne yasa ta d'ago hannun tana  kallon ruwan dake mak'ale dashi sai a lokacin ta tabbatarwa da kanta a Asibiti take, ta tambayi kanta a cikin zuciya tace,,     _"Ta yaya ni me ya kawo ni asibiti kuma?."_

K'arar bud'e k'ofar d'akin ne ya dawo da hankalinta akan k'ofar aiko sai ga shi ya doso gadon da take kwance fuskarshi cike da yaltaccen Murmushi, da sauri ta sake runtse idonta da karfi  saboda tsammaninta ko mafalkin da ta saba yi ne, aikuwa sai gashi taji an rik'o hannunta da sauri ta sake bud'e idonta aikuwa suka sauka akan fuskarshi, ta k'ara bud'e idanuwa ta tsaidasu akanshi mamakinta ya k'aru ne lokacin da taji ya duk'o ya sakar mata kiss a goshi, har lokacin murmushinshi kwance akan fuskar saboda  tsananin farin cikin da yake a ciki, domin Dr'n  ya sanar dashi ta sami lafiya nan da kwana biyar idan sunga brain d'in ta yi normally  sosai  za'a sallameta. 

Munawwa ta d'ago fuskarta daga kwancen tana k'are masa kallo, zuciyarta ta tabbatar mata da cewa  _"lalle shine HAIDAR d'in Munara, to shin ita miye had'inta da shi ne? Kuma ina Mummy da suka barta ita kad'ai ko dai ya yi tunanin Munarar ce?"_

Ta matsu da tasan amsar tambayoyin ta bud'a baki da nufin ta tambayeshi ne sak'o ya shigo wayarshi da sauri ya fiddo wayar a aljihu ya duba, yana ganin abunda ke ciki ya mayar da wayar da sauri, ya k'ara duk'awa ya sakar mata kiss a baki sannan yace, 

"Congrats my heart beat." 

Sannan ya juya ya fice d'akin da sauri ko minti biyar ba'a yi da fitarshi ba,  sai ga Mummy da Munara tareda Nuradden sun shigo, Munawwa ta kasa gano meke shirin faruwa da ita?, saboda wannan Muryar da taji yayi amfani da ita wurin yimata magana, da ita Aliyunta yake yimata  magana wani lokaci shin me hakan yake nufi?, tayi imani ko kad'an ba danganta ko alak'a tsakaninshi da Haidar d'in Munara balle tace ko 'yan uwa ne, to koda 'yan uwane ai bai zama dole sai sunyi murya d'aya ba,ba shiri ta dafe goshinta a fili ta furta,    "Shin mike faruwa dani ne?anya ba aljanu ba ne suke damuna ba kuwa?"

Da sauri Mummy ta k'araso cike da farincikin ganinta tace,     "Ba wasu aljanu ki kwantar da hankalinki aduk inda kike ina tare dake ."

Munawwa ta kai kallonta ga Mummy wasu sababbin hawaye suna zuba akan fuskarta, wad'anda bazata ce ga ko na minene ba Mummy ta rungumeta tana lallashinta, Munara ta fito d'akin ba tareda tace da ita k'anzil ba,  taje kan wata kujerar silba dake ajiye corridon ta zauna tana wulla idanuwa tana kallom mata da mazan indiya dake safa da marwa a cikin asibitin sai kai komo suke a wurin, caraf idanuwanta suka kai kan fuskar wani dake fitowa office d'in dake gabanta tareda wani Dr suna magana, juyowar da zaiyi da nufin ya bar wajen tayi tozali da honey'nta da sauri ta bud'e ido tareda murzasu, kafin ta tantance yayi mata nisa da gudu tabi bayanshi tana kiran sunan HAIDAR, duk da yaganta kuma yaji sunanshi da take kira amman baisa shi ya juyo ba har sai da ya kai inda mutane basa yawan zirga zirga sannan ya tsaya yana latsar waya kamar baisan tana biye dashi ba, tana zuwa taja ta tsaya gabanshi tana haki tareda dafe gaba tace,      "Honey?" 

Yayi saurin kai kallonshi gareta a cikin ko in kula yace,    "HAIDAR dai"

Ta zaro ido a waje tace,       "Kana nufin daman da gaske kake yi zaka iya rabuwa dani?"

A cikin kallo mai cike da reni yace,     "Kinga zahiri ai."

Ba shiri ta cakumoshi tana wani masifaffen kuka tace,     "WALLAHI kayi kad'an kace zaka yaudareni ni zaka guda me nayi maka? Wallahi kasani tunda nike sonka sai ka aureni ko baka so, ko in mayar da kai abun kwatance daga kai har matar da tsautsayi zai hau kanta."

A cikin zafin nama ya fisge daga wawan rik'on da tayi masa tareda mazga mata wani arnen mari sannan ya nuna ta da yatsa yace,     "koda wasa kika sake kika koma kawo kanki inda nake to ki sani zanyi ajalinki."

Yana k'are maganar ya buga mata tsaki tareda yi mata wani kallo mai cike da gargad'i  yayi fuuuu ya bar wajen ta bishi da kallo baki a sake zuciyarta cike da mamakinshi.

*🔥BAK'ON  YANAYI🔥*

*33*

Munara ta tashi a cikin zafin nama ta riskeshi, tareda cakumo rigarshi ta baya a cikin hayagaga tace,    "Idan baka kashe ni ba ALLAH ya tsine maka, gani na dawo don ALLAH kada ka bari in k'ara ko second d'aya a duniya." 

A cikin tsananin b'acin rai ya juyo ya d'aga hannu da nufin ya kai mata wani marin me ya tuna kuma ya sage hannu, jiki a sanyaye ya fisge rigarshi daga rik'on da tayi masa tareda nunata da yatsa yace,     "Ki godewa ALLAH ina kallon darajar irin fuskarki, wallahi da yanzu anan sai na yi maki abunda ko hango ni kika yi sai kinji tsoron k'ara zuwa kusa da inuwata ma balle ni, saboda haka ki kiyayi ranar da zan saki k'wanji na akanki."

Ya fara tafiya ta k'ara biyoshi tasha gabanshi a cikin kuka tace,     "Ka sanar dani me nayi maka ka gujeni, bayan kasan ina sonka don ALLAH Honey ka yafe mani WALLAHI rabuwa da kai abu ne mai wahala a wurina, WALLAHI ina sonka kaima ka sani."

Basarake ya gyara tsayuwarshi sannan ya kalleta ido cikin ido yace,      "Ni kuwa kinga yanzu sam sam bana sonki, kamarki kawai nike so wadda a kullum itace mafalkina, itace wadda nike fad'i tashi akan in tabbatar da gaskiyar sonta a kaina, yanzu haka k'iris ya rage in mallaki abata don jikina yana bani itace Matata ba ke ba,saboda ke kwata kwata bakya da hali mai kyau, wanda har zai sa in inyi sha'awar aurenki, don haka ki sa aranki tamkar ma baki tab'a sani na ba, don nayi maki nisan da ko kin kira bazanji ba balle in waiwayo."

Yana k'are fad'ar maganar ya fara taku zai bar wurin, jikinta yana tsuma ta d'aga murya tace,    "Idan baka aureni ba WALLAHI ka gayyatowa kanka bala'i da musiba da hannunka, kuma ka sani nayi alwashin sai naga bayan duk macen da ka aura matuk'ar ina numfashi a doron k'asa."

Ya waigo yana Murmushin tura haushi yace,     "Try  your best."  

Sannan ya fara taku a cikin k'asaita har ta b'acewa ganinshi, da sauri ta bar wajen ta nufi office d'in da taga ya fito, aikuwa tana shiga ta tarar da Dr'n  da taganshi dashi d'azu, tayi saurin saita nutsuwarta tace,    "plss ser can i come in?"

Yace     "yes you can come in."

Munara taje ta zauna jikinta sai tsuma yake saboda tashin hankalin da take ciki, a cikin mamakin yanayin da ya ganta saboda kamar da yaga tana yi sak da patient d'insu yace,     "What's your problem?." da turancinshi mai kama da indiyanci a cikin jarumta tace,    "The man i saw with you thirty minutes ago, i want know  something about him."

Yace   "which man?"

Tace   "Nigeran Man"

Yace   "OK" tareda gyara zamanshi yana jujjuyawa akan kujerarshi idonshi akanta yana wasa da biron hannunshi, aikuwa ganin ya bata dama ne tace,    "Did You really know him very well?" yace    "yeah!"

Tace,    "His Name plss"

Yace,   "ALIU" ya fad'a a cikin muryarshi ta indiyanci, a cikin zak'uwa tace,     "His father Name."

Yace  "Dukk........ " maganarshi ta katse ne saboda shigowar wata nurse da sauri tana sanar dashi lokacin shiga tiyatar da zai yi ya cika, aiko da sauri ya fara had'a takardun dake gabanshi ya fice da sauri tareda cewa,   "I want close my office"

Ta gyad'a masa kai tareda binshi da kallo har ya fice, jiki a sanyaye ta fito office d'in ya rufe sannan ya wuce yana ta sauri, dole taja k'afafuwanta ta koma bakin k'ofar d'akin tana tunanin mafita, don tasha alwashin sai ta nemo HAIDAR tareda tarihinshi, don ta fara aiwatar da manufofinta akanshi. 

Tana nan zaune tana ta karanta wasikar jaki tareda tuno miyagun lafuzzan Haidar a kunnenta da suke ta yimata amsa amo,kamar a lokacin ne yake fadarsu aiko cen ta tuno da abunda ya ce wanda tayi ta nanata maganar a zuciyarta tana so ta gano ma'anarta, waton cewar da yayi

_Kamarki kawai nike so wadda a kullum itace mafalkina,  itace wadda nike fad'i tashi akan in tabbatar da gaskiyar sonta a kaina, yanzu haka k'iris ya rage in mallaki abata don jikina yana bani itace Matata ba ke ba._

Da sauri k'irjinta ya buga dam! dam! Daram!! dararam!!! Ba shiri ta Mik'e tsaye tareda tambayar kanta 

"Munawwara??????"

A fili ta furta "Impossible" ta k'are maganar tareda cije leb'e ta nutsa dogon tunani, saboda ita dai iya saninta ko ganinshi Munawwara bata tab'a yi ba, haka ma shima bai tab'a ganinta ba sai a pic ne suka san juna, don ko a waya bata tab'a had'a su suka yi magana ba, to tayaya idan har itace? A take zuciyarta ta k'aryata hakan, don tasan Munawwa tsaf banda wannan matsiyacin kuturun ba wani wanda take saurare, sai ko yanzu shigowar Nuradden wanda ko tantama bata yi akan shi zata aura, to ko dai wata ce mai maka da ita d'in ba Munawwa ba?, ta kasa samun wanda zai bata wannan amsar,tasa aranta zata bugi cikin Munawwar domin ta gano gaskiyar zancen, aiko jikinta yana tsuma ta koma d'akin da aka kwantar da Munawwa, abun takaici ta tarar da ita tana bacci, Mummy zaune ita kad'ai tana sharar hawaye, da sauri ta k'arasa wurinta tayi tsaye ba tareda tace da ita k'anzil ba, duk ko da kukan da taga tana yi ita dai gurinta d'aya ne ta sami dubarar da zata tada Munawwa tayi mata tambayar da ke zuciyarta, Mummy ta share hawayenta ta d'ago tana kallonta sannan tace,    "Ina kika je tun d'azu anata nemanki ba'a ganki ba."

Munara ta b'ata fuska tareda zunb'uro baki tace,   " ina waje fa Mummy ba wani nisa nayi ba."

Mummy ta yi mata wani kallo damuwa kwance akan fuskarta tace,     "To ki dai kiyaye don nan ba k'asarmu bace ba inda kika sani bale kice zaki je yawo, kada kije ki b'ata ki saka mu shiga Uku."

Munara tayi mere a fakaice sannan nufi drowar jikin gadon ta d'auko gorar ruwan dake sama, tareda tsiyaya ruwa a kofi ta fara yunk'urin tada Munawwa, Mummy tayi saurin fad'in,      "Lafiya me zata maki ne da zaki tada ta tana barcinta?."

Munara ta k'ara tsuke fuska tace     "ruwa zata sha Mummy ba wani abu ba"

Mummy ta buga mata wata harara tace,     "Ita tace maki tana son shan ruwan? Ko sak'o ta baki tace idan kinzo kin tarar tana bacci ki tada ta tasha ruwa?, WALLAHI Munara ki kiyayeni tun kafin in sab'a maki, ke shikenan ba zaki bar mutane suji da abunda ya dame su ba har sai kin k'arawa mutane damuwa."

Munara ta tashi daga sunkuyon da tayi sannan ta fara tafiya zata bar d'akin tace    

"Ai fa shikenan ni komai nayi bana gwaninta."

Ta fice fuuu Mummy takai kallonta ga k'ofar ta girgiza kai a cikin zubowar wasu hawaye tace,    "Ubangiji ka yafe mani idan laifi nayi maka ka bani Munara." 

Ta fashe da kuka gwanin ban tausayi,  Sautin kukan da take yi ne ta tada Munawwa, a cikin firgici ta kai kallonta ga Mummy ruwa mak'ale a hannunta ta fara yunk'urin tashi, Motsinta da Mummy ta jiyo ne yasa tayi saurin share hawayenta ta isa Wurinta, tareda tambayarta me take so, a cikin damuwa Munawwa tace,     "Me aka yi maki kike kuka Mummy?, in don ciyo na ne kiyi hak'uri ai nama ji sauk'i ni, da zasu sallameni ma mu koma gida  da naji dad'i ni, saboda nagaji da wannan kwanciyar saboda akwai abubuwan da nike so in gudanar idan mun koma gida." 

Mummy ta saita natsuwarta tareda gyara muryarta tace,     

"ALLAH ya k'aro lafiyar saboda muma fatarmu kenan ki mirmije mu koma gida, ko banza  mun baro Daddynku shi kad'ai gashi ko abincinsa sai an dafo masa daga gidan ALHAJI Baba."

Aikuwa anan take ran Munawwa ya b'aci saboda tuno dukan da Daddyn yayi mata wanda shine silar kwanciyar da take, a cikin zafin rai tace   

"Gaskiya Mummy yakamata ki daina jin tsoron Daddy hakanan, ki fito fili ki nuna masa abunda yake yi bai dace ba, don ko ga ALLAH kin fita hak'k'inshi don kin fad'a masa gaskiya, saboda  idan aka ci gaba da tafiya haka gaskiya akwai gagarumar matsala."

Mummy ta sauke ajiyar zuciya tace,   "nafi kowa sanin halin Mahaifinku saboda shi mutum ne mai zafi sosai, idan aka ce da zafin za'a bi dashi abun zai k'ara rincab'ewa gara abi komai a hankali, ni nasan ba abunda ke d'orewa sai gaskiya komai daren dad'ewa zata yi halinta, amman tashin hankali ba shine maslaha ba."

Munawwa tace,     "Kullum maganarki da kike yi kenan Mummy amman kullum baya ganin hak'urin da kike yi dashi, sai k'ara hargitsa komai yake shiyasa zan nemarwa kaina mafita nikam."

Mummy tayi saurin cewa,    "Mafita kamar ya Munawwara kar dai fa ki jefa kanki a matsala saboda halin Mahaifinku"

Munawwa tayi Murmushi sannan tace,     "Kici gaba da yiman addu'a kawai Mummy kamar yanda kika saba, kuma kisa a ranki bazan tab'a yin abunda bai dace ba "

Mummy tace,     "ALLAH ya zama gatanmu nida ku kuma ya shiga cikin lamurranmu na yau da kullum."    

Munawwa tace    "Ameen."  

Sai ga Nuradden ya shigo tareda Munara yayi farin cikin ganin yanayin da ya iskota, don ko ba'a fad'a ba alamun sauk'i sun bayyana don gashi har fira ya isko tana yi, haka kawai Munawwa ta tsinci kanta da ganin girmansa saboda Mummy tayi mata bayanin komi, a hankali ya tako yazo gaban gadon tareda rusunawa ya k'ara gayar da Mummy, a cikin jin nauyi Mummy ta amsa ya kalli Munawwa yace,   "Ya k'arfin jikin?"

Tace,    'Alhmdllh'

Yace,    "ALLAH ya bida lafiya." 

Dukansu suka ce "Ameen"
Sannan yace su zo ya kaisu masaukinsu saboda saura mintuna kad'an a rufe zuwa dubiya, Mummy ta mik'e a cikin shirin tafiya, Munara ko haushi kamar ya kasheta saboda taso ta yiwa Munawwa tambayar da ke cin zuciyarta, amman ba hali dole ta had'iye maganarta suka yi sallama da juna akan sai gobe da safe zasu dawo. 

Munawwa taji haushin wannan doka don bata so aka rabata da Mummynta ba, aiko suna fita aka zo aka bata magunguna tasha sannan aka yi mata wasu allurori, tun tana tunanin zuci har bacci yayi awon gaba da ita.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*34*

A hankali ya turo k'ofar ya k'araso wurinta idonshi akanta, fuskarshi cike da wani murmushi yaje har jikin gadon ya sunkuya, sannan ya gyara mata gashinta da ya zubo ya rufe mata fuska,  ya sakar mata kiss a goshi tareda ri'ko hannunta ya aza  a saitin zuciyarshi ya lumshe ido tareda rufe su gaba d'aya yace,     "I love you Munawwara."

Aikuwa wukil idanunta suka bud'e saboda yanda taji muryar Aliyunta ta sauka cikin kunnuwanta, a firgice ta zaro ido tareda k'wace hannunta idonta akanshi tace,    "Me kake nema waye kai da kake so ka rikitaman kwakwalwa?"

Basarake yayi mata wani kayataccen murmushin da yaso ya sumar da ita, saboda a rayuwarta b'ata tab'a cin karo da mutumin da ya sace zuciyarta ta farad d'aya ba sai Honey'n Munara waton shi kenan, shiyasa a kwanakin baya tayi ta neman tsari da sharrin shed'an da zuciyarta da take ta so ta jefata a had'ari, yanzun ma kai tsaye sai da tayi ta'awizi a cikin zuciyarta, saboda  tsoron kada zuciyarta tayi galaba akanta, jikinta yana tsuma ta fara yunk'urin tashi zaune tace,    "Don ALLAH ka daina shiga shirgi na ba munara bace fa ni Munawwara ce tawainiyarta ce ni kama ce kawai muke yi."

Idonshi akanta ya jawo kujera daf da ita ya zauna,  cikin murmushin da ya kasa gushewa akan fuskarshi ya rik'o hannunta da lallausan hannunshi mai bala'in taushi, tabishi da kallo kamar wata sakarya ya aza hannun a saitin zuciyarshi yace,    "Kinji yanda bugun zuciyata yake tafiya da sonki?"    ya k'are maganar tareda yi mata wani kallo wanda har sai da taji tsikarta tayi yarr!  ba shiri ta janye idanuwanta akanshi ranta a b'ace tace,   "Munawwara ce ba Munara ba ba fa yakamata ka banbance."

Basarake ya k'ara damk'e hannunta da k'arfi saboda jin zafin sunan Munara da take fad'a sannan a hankali yace,   "Ke nike so ba ita idan kin amince a shirye nike da aurenki a kowane lokaci."

Ba shiri ta fisge hannunta saboda yanda zuciyarta  ta buga, jikinta yana rawa tace,    "Ni ina da mijin da zan aura da na koma gida za'a d'aura muna aure."  ta had'e fuska kamar bata tab'a dariya ba, ta bashi dariya sosai amman ya mazge yace,    "Au ko shine wanda ya d'auki nauyin kawo ki nan k'asar aka yi maki aiki?" Ranta ya k'ara baci fuska a cusgune tace,    "Ba shi bane"  Basarake yace,   "Wane mai sa'a ne wannan nikam?, amm ga dukkan alamu babanshi mai kud'in gaske ne don ke ko a kallo d'aya za'a gano ke ba matar k'ananan mutane bace ke."   Munaww ta maka masa harara sannan tace,     "Idan wannan ne tunaninka ka ajiye, domin ni kud'i basu kai matsayin da zasu sayi soyayyata ba, na riga da na mallakawa wani zuciyata shawara d'aya zan baka shine kada ka kayi kuskuren cutar da rayuwar 'yar uwata don kaga tana sonka, idan kayi haka ALLAH zai bi mata hak'k'inta domin a sanadin rashinka da tayi har sai da tayi  kwanciya asibiti, ka tuna yaudara bata da dad'i don nasan kaima bazaka so ayi maka ba ko kuma ayiwa k'anwarka"  Basarake ya sauke ajiyar zuciya saboda duk maganganun da ta fad'a shi d'an mitsitsin bakinta ya k'urawa kallo, saboda yanda  lebunanta suke d'agawa sama da k'asa yayin da take magana sun burgeshi matuk'a, ji yake yi kamar ya cafko su da bakinshi amman gudun tayi masa fassara mummuna yasa ya mazge duk da yanayin da yake jin kanshi game da ita, yayi k'ok'arin controlling kanshi yace,    "Idan fa Halinta bai yi daidai da ra'ayina ba shima ALLAH zai saka mata akaina kenan?, Manzon ALLAH S.  A. W. Yace Mu bar abunda zuciyarmu take kokwanto akanshi, mu aikata wanda zuciyarmu tafi natsuwa dashi, saboda haka ina son kisani abu marar kyau ne kawai idan zaka aikata zuciyarka bata natsuwa akanshi duk ko tsananin soyayyarka da abunnan zaka ji kana k'yamar mutane suga kana aikata shi, amman ina so ki lura abun alkhairi idan zaki aikata kai tsaye kake yin abunka kuma ba zaka yi k'yamar kowa ma ya gani ba, saboda kai kasan akan gaskiyarka kake to haka nima abun yake a wurina, rok'on da nike kawai ki soni muyi aurenmu  ni da ke don kece kika fi dacewa da rayuwata." 

Wasu hawaye masu zafi suka gangaro mata a cikin dakiya tace,     "Maganarka gaskiya ce amman ni ina da mijina sai dai ina yimaka fatar samun mace tagari daidai da ra'ayinka."

A hankali yace,    "Nagode da fatar da kike yimani, amman idan ALLAH ya k'addara kece matar fa ya kenan?"

Jikinta a mace tace,   "Hakan ma bazata faru ba, nidai rok'on da nike so inyi maka don ALLAH kayi nesa nesa da rayuwata kamar yanda kayi wa 'yar uwata, don bana son ma ta zargi akwai wata alak'a a tsakanina dakai."

Basarake wani tsananin sonta ya diro masa tareda  wani girma da yake ganinta dashi, kamar magnetic ya rik'o hannunta ya runtse a cikin hannuwanshi duka biyu, a cikin cool voice yace,  

"I wish you the best life in your world saboda kina da kyakkyawar zuciya sosai, inama ace itama 'yar uwarki haka tata zuciyar take."

Munawwa tsikarta ta tashi yarrrr! Saboda tsananin laushin hannuwanshi da ya ratsa gangar jikinta, a hankali ta lumshe ido ta k'ara bud'e su tareda sauke su a cikin k'wayar idanuwanshi, da bata tantama suna yi mata kama da na Aliyunta banbancinsu  d'aya,  shi Aliyu idan suka had'a ido tana jin wani abu yana ratsata, sab'anin shi da ko kad'an bata jin hakan ko ko dai don shi tana k'ok'arin  haramtawa kanta shi ne oho?. 

A cikin siririyar murya tace,    "da zaka yi jahadi ka aureta a hakan insha ALLAHU tunda tana sonka ni nasan zata gyara wasu halayenta da baka so."

Basarake ya girgiza kai yace,     "Fatana a kullum in mallaki the best star in the world, kuma jikina yana bani insha ALLAHU ni mai sa'a ne"

Munawwa tayi masa wani kallo tareda rausaya idanuwanta tace,      "Zaka samu INSHA ALLAH a wani wuri amman ni kam an riga da an taya ni har na sayar, fatiha kawai ta rage a shafa muna."

A cikin sanyin jiki yace,   "To ya zakiyi da wannan bawan ALLAH da naga yana ta fad'i tashi akanki?baki gudun nashi hak'k'in kema ya kama ki?"

Munawwa ta b'ata fuska sannan tace "Zab'in Mahaifina ne ba zab'ina ba."  Basarake cikin sanyin jikin ya k'ara jefo mata wata tambaya yace, 
"To taya zakiyi k'arfin halin jayayya da ra'ayin Mahaifinki ba kya ganin tafiyar taki akwai matsala?"  tayi saurin girza kai tace   "Ba wata matsala in ma akwai zan saka siminti da yashi  in toshe k'ofar." 

 Ba shiri Basarake yayi murmushin jin dad'i  idonshi akanta yace,  

"Ta wace hanya fa?"  ta rufe fuskarta da hannunta d'aya tace 

"Sirri ne"  yace    "Au bazaki fad'a man ba kenan?" ta d'aga masa kai   ya sauke ajiyar zuciya sannan ya Mik'e tsaye yace   "any way ni zan fece ALLAH ya baki lafiya, sai watarana idan ALLAH ya had'a fuskokinmu da alkhairi" tace  " Ameen thanks a lot"

Har ya fara tafiya yace 

"Dame fa?"  tana 'yar dariya tace.   "Da komai ma."  ya fice yana mata bye bye itama tana yimasa. 

Soyayya dad'i 😅

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*35*

Munara kam duk k'ok'arinta na son ta keb'e da Munawwa ta yimata tambayar da ke cinta a rai bata sami hakan ba, saboda Mummy ta kasa ta tsare a gefe d'aya kuma wannan Ba'indiyen likitan tun daga ranar bata sake ganin fuskarshi ba, don ko sun had'u baya bata damar da zasu yi magana ma balle ta samu ya amsa mata  tambayoyinta, Kwanan Munawwa biyar asibitin aka sallameta suka suka tattaro suka dawo gida Nigeria, cike da d'inbin nasarar da aka samu ta samun lafiyar Munawwa, Daddy kanshi da ya ganta yaji dad'in samun saukinta saboda kawai gurinshi da yake so ya cika. 

Munawwa kam tun daga ranar da suka yi sallama da Basarake tunaninshi bai bar ranta ba, duk da taso sosai ace sun k'ara had'uwa amman ko mai kama dashi bata  gani ba, duk da ko bayan taji sauk'i Mummy tana fitowa da ita suna d'an zagayawa,  don idan ma taga wani ba indiye daga nesa sai ta d'auka shine, tayi ta kallonshi har sai ya matso ta gano ba shi bane, ta shiga damuwa sosai da rashin ganinshi sosai sai da tayi wani nazari ne  kawai ta zubar da lamarinshi taci gaba da sha'aninta,  amma fa yana nan mak'ale a zuciyarta don duk wani motsi nata shi take gani, amman fa a zahirin gaskiya tafison ta auri Aliyunta duk ko da da yanda take jin HAIDAR d'in a ranta, shiyasa ta maida hankalinta ga Aliyunta kullum suna mak'ale a waya, ta gefen Nuradden kuwa tana kulashi ne kawai saboda girmanshi da take gani, amman tasha sanar dashi ita fa tana da wanda zata aura, amman ya kafe a kullum dare suna mak'ale da waya suna chatt, duk da ba wani sakin jiki take yi dashi ba. 

Mutane 'yan uwa da abokan arzik'i tareda k'awayenta na gida da school  sai tururuwar  zuwa suke yi dubata, tareda yi mata barka da arzik'i akan lafiyar da ta samu. 

 Bayan kwana Uku da dawowarta ne Daddy yayi kiran Nuradden a waya akan ya turo iyayenshi, hankalin Munawwa da Mummy ya tashi saboda ita kanta Mummyn duk da mutuncin da Nuradden yake dashi kuma tana yiwa Munawwar sha'awar aurenshi, amman tunda bata so tafi son  ta auri wanda take so d'in ko don gudun abunda zai je ya dawo, shiyasa takanas ta sami Daddy d'akinshi ta nuna masa illar auren da yake so yayiwa Munawwara tunda bata son Nuradden, shi ko ya kafe kai da fata da Nuradden ya turo iyayenshi ba'a wata d'aya bai aura mata shi ba, don baya son damar da ya samu ta sub'uce masa, Mummy ta saki jiki ta nuna masa b'acin ranta akan hakan aiko a ranar sai da suka YI kare jini biri jini har yana k'ok'arin dukanta, itama ta saki jiki ta nuna masa bafa tsoronshi take ji ba, har tana yi masa barazanar barin gida sannan ya d'an sauko amman ba don ya janye ra'ayinshi ba. 

Aikuwa sai gashi Nuradden ya turo wasu Manyan mutane neman auren, Daddy ya rud'e yace nan da sati Biyu suzo a d'aura aure, Hankalin Munawwa yayi k'ololuwar tashi duk da bata sanar da Aliyu abunda ke faruwa ba, don bata son ta d'aga masa hankali. 

Washe garin ranar tayi shirin zuwa School tunda safe ta d'auki motarsu tayi gidan yayan Daddy Alhaji MUSA, kasancewarta 'yar gida ba bak'uwa ba kai tsaye Yusuf yaje yasanar dashi zuwanta, ALHAJI Baba yace dashi ta shigo, aiko da sallamarta ta shiga d'akin kanta a sunkuye ta durk'usa tana gaidashi, sannan ta zauna da kyau a k'asa tana wasa da gefen gyalenta ba tareda tace komai ba, ALHAJI Baba ya kalleta a cikin kula yace,    "Meke tafe dake Munawwara?,Kwanakin baya Yusuf ya sanar dani zaki zo sai gashi rashin lafiyarki ya wargaza shirinki shin meke faruwa ne? ." 

Munawwa ta fara inda inda ALHAJI baba yace da ita    "saki jikinki ki fad'i abunda ya kawo ki ni mai share maki hawaye ne matuk'ar ina da ikon da zan yin hakan."

Hawaye suna zuba akan fuskarta tace,   

"Daddy  ne yake son in auri wannan mutumin da ya turo iyayenshi, amman ni gaskiya ALHAJI Baba ina da wanda nike so." Munawwa ta fara sosa kai tace,    "Ba'a garinnan yake ba wani d'an k'auye ne a nan kusa damu, yana zuwa garinnan ne neman abinci saboda Babanshi ya rasu shi ke hidima da Mahaifiyarshi da kakarshi, WALLAHI  ALHAJI Baba yana da kirki sosai."

Koda aka tashi School Munara ta cika abokanta cikin motar suka tafiyarsu, Munawaa kam dole sai Napep ta tara ta sauketa gida, sai gashi koda ta isa gidan ma ita bata dawo ba, koda Mummy ta tambayeta ina take tace da ita basu gama lecture ba shiyasa ta baro mata motar.

Tana zuwa ta d'an rusuna ta gaidasu Nuradden yace da ita "ALHAJI Baba yana ciki"   tayi saurin d'aga kai saboda satar kallon Basarake da take yi, shikam latsar wayarshi kawai yake yi har lokacin bai kalleta ba,  Nuradden ya kula da ita sosai da kallon da take yiwa Basarake shiyasa yayi murmushi yace "kice dashi Yayi bak'i"  Zulaihatu ta tafi tana waiwayensu minti biyar a tsakani sai gata ta fito hannunta rik'e da waya tace

" yace ku shiga daga ciki ga k'ofar nan"

Ta k'are maganar tareda nuna masu k'ofar sai a lokacin Basarake ya mayar da wayarshi aljihu ya d'ago fuskarshi, abun mamaki sai ga hasken fulasha akan fuskarsu, Zulaihatu ce tayi jarumtar daukarsu photo a zatonta basu gani ba, batasan Basarake smart bane ko kallonshi da take yi yana gani ta gefen ido, amman yayi kamar bai lura ba suka nufi k'ofar da ta nuna masu. 


To fa wata sabuwa

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*36*

Har suka shige Zulaiha tana tsaye tana kallonsu, Da sallama suka shiga falon Alhaji Baba cikin ladabi duk suka zazzauna a k'asa, sannan suka fara gayar dashi Mamaki kwance akan fuskar ALHAJI Baba, saboda ganin mutanen da ko a TV ba k'aramar sa'a ba ce ace angansu, saboda fuskar Basarake ba boyayya bace a wurin duk wani mai karanta jaridu tareda sauraren News, saboda yanda duniyar take yayinsu, amman yau gasu durk'ushe a gabanshi.

"Munawwara ta sanar dani kana nema na."  a cikin d'aure kai Alhaji baba yace,     "Kaine Aliyun?"    Basarake ya d'aga kai yana murmushi yace.  "Nine Aliyu."   Alhaji Baba da mamaki kwance akan fuskarshi yace,    "Kunce kune kuka turo mutanenku jiya akan a nema maku auren Munawwara,  kuma kai tsaye mun amince mun baku to kuma ya aka yi kasan nace da Munawwara ina neman Aliyu?, ko dai itace tayi kuskure wajen isar da sak'o wurin wanda ba shi na aiketa wurinshi ba, saboda shi wannan Aliyun da nace ta kira man shine wanda tace man tana so, kayi hak'uri Aliyu ba kai nace ta kiraman ba kuskure ne aka samu."

Shin idan har kaine da gaske ta yaya zata zo ta sanar dani ba ta sonka tana da wanda take so?"

Basarake ya  zama serious yace,    "A zahirin gaskiya Alhaji da gaske nine Aliyunta, haka zalika kuma mune wad'anda muka turo Mutanenm akan neman aurenta amman da sunan wannan abokina Nuradden."   ALHAJI baba kai tsaye yace     "Miye manufarku ta yin hakan?"  

Basarake ya kwancewa Alhajin komai tun farkon had'uwarshi da Munara har zuwanshi gidansu da yanda SUKA k'ulla soyayya da Munawwara da nauyinta da d'auka da sunan Nuradden, har izuwa turo iyayen da yayi a matsayin Iyayen Nuradden da sak'on nemanshi da Alhajin keyi wanda Munawwa ta sanar dashi,   ALHAJI Baba jib'i ya kare masa tun daga kai har tsintsiyar k'afafu, saboda jin wannan almara da tashi kasa magana,amman fa ya goyi bayanshi akan wannan zurfin tunani da yayi, saboda ba karamar bajinta ce yayi ba don ba kowa ne zai iya yin abunda yayi ba. 

Basarake ya kwantar da murya yana rok'on ALHAJI Baba akan ya bar maganar a tsakaninsu har sai ya gama plan  d'inshi na k'arshe, aiko ALHAJI baba ya jinjina masa sosai sannan ya sanar dashi auren yana nan kamar yanda Daddy yasa rana, yayi masu addu'a  sosai akan fatar ALLAH ya sanya alkhairi a cikin auren nasu, Basarake da Nuradden sunji dad'i sosai da sauk'in halin ALHAJI Baba, saboda ya fahimcesu sosai don har dalilin da yasa  yayi hakan duk ya fad'a masa, shiyasa yaji tsoron auren macen da za'a had'a kai da ita a cutar dashi ko a yi sanadin rayuwarshi. 

Basarake yayiwa ALHAJI Baba alkhairi sosai sannan suka yi sallama dashi,  akan nan da kwana uku za'a kawo sadaki da lefen Munawwara, yaji dad'i sosai suka rabu cikin mutunta juna tareda ganin girman juna. 

Munawwa kam tana gida ko makaranta bata je ba, saboda tsimayen kiran Aliyu da take yi, saboda sunyi aje akan ya fito idan zata je makaranta  sai ta daukeshi su tafi tare ta kaishi gidan, sannan ta wuce School don ta k'uduri aniyar sake d'aukar motar komi ta fanjama fanjam ita da Munara, sai gashi bai kirata ba har Munarar ta d'auke motar ta tafiyarta, Mummy sai tambaya take yimata yau basu da lecture halan?, tace da ita basu da ta safe shiyasa bazata fita da wuri ba, tun tana tsumayen kiranshi har ta sare gashi ta riga da ta cewa ALHAJI Baba zai zo kada yaga kamar bata fad'a masa ba, ya k'ara gano rashin gaskiyarta, gashi tayi ta kiran numbarshi bata samu ba, shiyasa ta shirya tace da Mummy tayi makaranta,  tana fita kai tsaye Napep ta kaita gidan ALHAJI Baba, saukarta keda wuya suka had'u da Zulaiha zata fita, a cikin zumud'i ta rungumeta tace,    "Amaryar jibi ai wallahi ke kin kere sa'a taki tayi kyau saura mu, wannan zuk'ek'en guy duk ke d'aya?, cab wallahi ki godewa ALLAH don wannan guy ko a k'asar hindu ka sami irinshi ka yi matuk'ar dace balle anan k'asarmu gida Nigeria"   Munawwa ta bita da kallo zuciyarta sai bugawa take yi saboda jin zafin bakaken maganganun da take jifarta da su, don ita tsammaninta zolayarta ce take yi watak'ila taganshi ne shine take so ta buga mata gorori, aiko nan take ranta ya b'ace ta saka hannu a jaka ta biya mai Napep d'in sannan ta rataya jakarta ta fara tafiya batareda tace da ita k'anzil ba, Zulaiha ta janyo gyalenta tabaya sannan ta zagaya suka fuskanci juna tace,      "Naga kamar ranki ya b'aci Munawwa idan kishi ne kijeyi don ina kod'a mijin da zaki aura ALLAH ya baki hak'uri, saboda ni bada wata manufa nayi ba murna ce nike taya ki,  don duk macen da ta sameshi a matsayin miji ta haye ga kyau ga kud'i kuma kyakkyawan hali uwa uba ga addini, amman tunda ranki ya b'ace ALLAH ya baki hak'uri."    ta fara tafiya ta nufi titi ta tsaya zata tari Napep, Munawwa tayi shiru tana nazarin Maganganunta masu buk'atar sharhi, da sauri ta koma wurin Zulaiha ta ri'ko hannunta a cikin sigar lallashi tace,    "Kiyi hak'uri zulaiha inda kika nufa ni ba cen na nufa ba, sannan zancen wani mijina mai kyau mai kud'i mai mi mi shin wanene shi?, sannan kuma ke duk a ina kika san hakan ko kin tab'a ganinshi ne?"

Ta k'are maganar tareda k'ume dariyarta 
Zulaiha ta gyara tsayuwarta sannan ta kalli Munawwa ido cikin ido tace,    "Me yasa kike so ki maida ni k'aramar yarinya ne?, nasan kin girmeni amman kuma ai ko shekara baki bani ba, shin me yasa kike so kiyi wasa da tunanina don kawai kina son ki kare kanki?"   Munawwa ta gimtse dariyarta saboda kanta da ya k'ara k'ullewa a cikin mamaki tace,      "In kare kaina kamar yaya Zulaiha? wai wannan soki burutsun zancen duk na menene?"     Zulaiha ranta a b'ace tace,      "Ban fa ga laifinki  don kinyi kishin abunki ba wallahi, amman ta sigar da kika biyo ne kawai naga kamar baidace ba, amman tunda haka ne sai mu kama kanmu tun yanzu don kada watarana muje gidanki kiga kamar son mijinki mukeyi."  

Munawwa ta gimtse dariyarta saboda yanda taga fuskar Zulaiha, don ta tabbata maganganunta kai tsaye take yinsu har cikin zuciyarta, Munawwa ta dafa kafad'ar Zulaiha tace, 
 "ALLAH ya baki hak'uri Zulaiha ke kanki kinsan bak'in hali ba halina bane ballantana yanzu in fara, abunda yasa kika ga na yi fushi d'azu na zaci watak'ila kinga Aliyuna ne shiyasa kike zolayataa, to ina son abuna a yanda kika ganshi kuma bana son inji wani ya aibata man shi."

Ta k'are manganar da d'an murmushi, Zulaiha tayi mere sannan tace,     "In dai ba'a godewa ALLAH ba ai sai a godewa azabarshi, wannan guy ko sarkin mahaukata bazai kushe shi ba balle ni da hankalina, asali ma ni burgeni yayi shiyasa ma nayi masa pic afakaice."

Munawwa ta zaro ido tareda dafe k'irji  duk da tasan renin sense ne take yiwa Aliyunta, amman ta dake kamar bata ji zafi ba tace

"Ina kika ganshi ko har yazo wurin Alhaji Baban ne bansani ba?."

Zulaiha tace,    "Yazo kam d'azu safe tareda abokinshi naganshi ya had'u iya had'uwa bari ma kiga pic d'in da nayi masu."

Ta jawo wayarta ta kamo photon ta nunawa Munawwa, aiko tana ganin fuskar honey'n Munara ta zaro ido k'irjinta ya buga dam! dam! Tace

"Na shigesu  wa ya fad'a maki cewa mijina ne?" 

Zulaiha ta harareta tace,     "Oho duk boyonki ai rana dai bata k'arya."  zuciyarta cike da tashin hankali ta nuna Nuradden da d'an yatsa tace,     "Kina nufin shi wannan tare suka zo ko dai kowa da tashi tafiya?"     Zulaiha tace 

"Tare suka zo k'afarshi k'afarshi mota d'aya suka shigo, amman kuma ance farin ne mjinki ko?."

Munawwa ta dafe kai tace,      "Waya fad'a maki?"   Zuwan wata Napep ne yasa Zulaiha ta mayarda wayarta jaka ta tsayarda Napep d'in ta shige, saboda ganin take yi kamar renin wayo ne Munawwa take yimata, Munawwa tayi saurin rik'o hannunta a cikin tashin hankali tace,   

"Don girman ALLAH ki fad'a man wa kika ji yace shine xan aura?"

Zulaiha cikin mamakinta tace    "Yaya Yusuf"   Munawwa ta dafe kai kamar wadda zata fasa kuwwa, Zulaihat ta bita da kallo kamar wata sakarya,  saboda haushin da ta bata tace da mai napep su tafi kawai, aiko ya hau titi suka bar Munawwa tsaye dafe da kai a cikin d'aga murya tace,     "Me hakan yake nufi ne me ya had'a HAIDAR da Nuradden?"
Tayi saurin d'auko wayarta ta kira Yusuf tun kafin tayi magana cikin d'oki  yace,     "Amarya ya dai?, ai d'azu naga angonki da kike ta b'oye muna"    hankalin  Munawwa ya k'ara tashi,   kai tsaye ta  tambaye shi wayace masa wannan da yazo d'azu shine mijinta?, Yusuf yana dariya yace,    "Shi da bakinshi ya sanar dani bayan sun fito wurin Alahji Baba, da na gansu nazo muka gaisa."    ba shiri Munawwa ta kashe wayar tana k'ok'arin mayar da wayar ne kiran Aliyunta ya shigo, har taso kar ta d'auka saboda bata son ya gano tana cikin damuwa,  amman taji bata iya k'in d'auka tayi caraf ta d'auka,  cikin zumud'i yace. "Amaryatah"   tana d'an murmushin k'arfin hali tace   "Nayi fushi ni."   yace kaina bisa wuya Amaryata kiyi hak'uri naso mu tafi tare dake amman naji tsoron wani ya ganmu ya fad'awa Daddy, shine da tambaya na gano gidan"    bakinta yana rawa tace    "Kana nufin kaje gidan?"   yana 'yar dariyar jin dad'i yace    "Naje kuma Alhaji Baba ya tareni hannu biyu, har ma ya bani ke sukutum yace in turo iyayena nan da sati biyu za'a d'aura muna aure."      Munawwa ta gwalalo ido nan take farin ciki ya ziyarci zuciyarta, a cikin jin dad'i tareda tuno maganganun Zulaiha da na Yusuf tace      "Lokacin da kazo ka had'u da wasu mutane su biyu?"     Basarake yayi shiru yana nazari sai cen kuma yace, 

"Eh nagansu"    cikin sabon tashin hankali tace,      "Ka rigasu zuwa ko sune suka riga ka?"    Yayi d'an murmushi sannan yace     "ina tsaye suka fito gidan da motarsu sannan ni na shiga, menene halan?"     Munawwa ta share gumin fuskarta tace,  

"Alhmdllh"    yana 'yar dariya yace,     "Kodai wata rigima kika had'o muna?"    tayi murmushin k'arfin hali tace,      "A'a kawai dai tambaya ce nayi, saboda  wani mai son Munara ne yazo wata k'ila shima tare za'a had'ewa dasu."    Basarake yayi 'yar dariya yace,      "Au itama ta sami mijin kenan?"   tace     "Eh tsohon saurinta ne"   

Yace     "Ok to ni yanzu ya kenan in na tashi turo lefena gidan Alhaji baban zan kai ko?"   tayi saurin fad'in   

"Na yafe kai dai ka tanadi Muhallin da zamu zauna."    ta k'are maganar tareda zuwa gindin bishiyar k'ofar gidan ta jingina, Basarake yace.     "Haba dai taya za'a yi auren budurwa sukutum ba lefe?"  kai tsaye tace,     "a fara kaina nidai na yafe kaji da 'yan guntayen matsalolin."   Ya sauke ajiyar zuciya sannan yace      "in ba damuwa gobe ki shirya muje kiga d'akinki a k'auyenmu idan yayi maki shikenan idan kuma bakya ra'ayinshi sai mu dawo nan in kama maki haya."

Munawwa tausayinshi ya kamata har da zubowar wata guntuwar k'walla sannan tace,    "Ko ya wurin yake naji na amince ina sonshi hakan, ba sai naje na gani ba."

Ya kafe akan sai sunje tace masa to kawai amman ba don tana da niyyar zuwan ba.
