*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*27*

Nuradden ya zaro ido a waje yana dariya yace 

"Rufani ka saya kar kasa masifaffiyar budurwarka ta tsokale man idanuwa saboda shegen kushinta, don wannan jarababbiyar yarinya wallahi komi zata iya aikatawa akan ka, in ba so kake yi ka yiwa Aysha asara ba."

Basarake yana 'yar dariya yace 

"Matsoraci fa baya zama gwani saboda haka ka saurareni da kyau kaji bayanin da zanyi, don ya zama dole fa kaine zaka YI aikin saboda amfani da nayi da sunanka."

Nuradden ya gimtse dariyar da yake yi saboda yanda yaji k'irjinshi ya buga,  Basarake yaso yayi dariya amma ya basar, kai tsaye ya fiddo wayarshi aljihu ya shiga nema chat d'in da yayi da Munawwa ya mik'a masa wayar, jiki a sanyaye Nuradden ya zura hannu ya karb'i wayar, sannan Basarake ya ce dashi, 

"Karanta chat d'in zaka gano abunda nike nufi"

Nuradden ya mayar da kallonshi akan wayar sannan ya fara karanta chat d'in, yana kawowa a dai dai inda Basarake yake sanar da ita  sunanshi Muhammadu Nuradden ya d'ago kanshi da sauri ya kalle shi, Basarake yayi wani munafukin murnushi tareda gyara zamanshi ya aza k'afa d'aya kan d'aya ya fara girgizar k'afa, alamun ba sauk'i a cikin duk wata magana da zata fito a bakinshi, Nuradden bakinshi yana rawa yace 

"Miye manufar saka sunana a cikin wannan issue?."

Basarake a take ya had'iye murmushinshi ya zama so serious ya fara magana kamar haka, 

"Nayi saka sunanka ne saboda ina son inyi amfani da kai in cimma manufata, wadda da ita ne nike son in k'ara gano gaskiyar soyayyar da Munawy take yiman, yanzu haka na aika mata da sako  amma da sunanka, saboda haka yanzu zan kirata sai kuyi waya sai naji abunda kuka tattauna sannan in had'a target d'ina  na gaba, saboda haka don ALLAH abokina ka bani had'in kai kada ka bari koda wasa a gane ba ni bane kai plss and plss, na zab'e ka ne saboda nasan kaine kafi cancanta da hakan dafatar ba zaka watsa man k'asa a ido ba."

Nuradden ya sauke ajiyar zuciya sannan yace, 

"Nasan wane ne kai Aliyu kuma kasan duk abunda ka nema aguna bazan tab'a k'in  yi maka ba matuk'ar abun ba na sab'awa Mahaliccinmu bane, wanda nasan ko kusa kai ma ba zaka aikata hakan da gangan ba, saboda haka naji na amince zan baka gudunmuwata dai dai yanda kake buk'ata, yanzu fayyace man komai nayi alk'awali zan yi maka koma menene."

Basarake yayi murmushin jin dad'i domin yasan daman cen ba zai k'i ya  masa ba, a tak'aice ya fayyace masa duk abunda yake nufi da abunda yasa agaba,da matakin da zai d'auka a gaba idan gaskiya ta bayyana, Nuradden ya jinjina masa tareda yimasa kirari, 

"Aliyu Haidar basaraken K'asar agadas Ali mai aikin gidan Daddy honey'n Munara angon Munawwara kai a gaisheka Muhammadu Nuradden."

Gaba d'aya suka kwashe da dariya suna cikin dariyar ne kiran Munawwa ya shigo wayarshi, amma ita Nuradden ne ta kira saboda Mummy ta matsa mata  akan ta k'ara kiranshi tunda shi har lokacin bai kira ba. 

Aiko suna ganin kiran gaba d'aya suka tsayar da dariyarsu,saboda daman wayar tana hannun Nuradden shi yasa yana ganin ansa heartbeat ya gano Munawyn Basarake ce, kuma da layin ne wanda yake a matsayin na Nuradden, aikuwa yana nuna masa yayi masa umurni da hannu akan ya d'auka, aiko ba b'ata lokaci ya d'auka zazzakar muryarta da ya jiyo ya sa shi natsuwa ba shiri, a cen b'angarenta kuma wani dad'i ya ziyarceta sosai don gaba d'aya bata cikin nutsuwarta daga ita har Mummy, saboda banbamin bala'in da Daddy ya rufe ido yana ta yi har lokacin, shiyasa Mummy ta matsa sai an kirashi, 
a hankali tace, 

D'azu sak'onka ya zo kamar yanda ka fad'a, sai dai Daddy yayi fad'a sosai da ganin sak'on, shiyasa yace ka zo yana son magana da kai, kasan tun farko sai da na sanar da kai akwai matsala amman kayi biris da zancena, saboda haka kazo yana nemanka domin kayi masa bayanin komai da bakinka." 

Nuradden yana murmushin da iya kacinshi a baki yace 

"OK ba damuwa ki dube ni nan da kwana biyu, insha ALLAH zan zo kada ki sami damuwa, komai kika ga nayi maki nayi ne kawai don son da nike maki, saboda kinji hausawa sunce tukuicin so kyauta ne, nagode sosai da kulanin da kike yi."

Kit yaji ta kashe wayar saboda ita gaba ki d'aya kanta ya k'ulle saboda rigimar da take ganin ya k'ara jefasu a tsakaninsu ita da Mummy, da kuma Daddy da Munara don tun lokacin Munara sai jifanta da jafa'i kala kala take yi har sai da Mummy ta so dukanta, itace ta hana don gudun tashin wata sabuwar  tarzoma tsakaninsu da Daddy. 

Nuradden da Basarake suka kalli juna suka yi murmushi, Basarake yace 

Shikenan komai yayi dai dai nima a ranar ce nike so in koma ALLAH ya shige muna a gaba, daga nan suka ci gaba da tattaunawa akan matsalar sannan sukayi sallama, Nuradden ya tafi zuciyarshi tana ta kai da komo akan wannan badak'alar. 

Basarake kam bajewa yayi ya kira Munawynshi suka ci gaba da fira duk da ya fahimci damuwar da take ciki a sautin muryarta. 

Koda safiya ta waye da sak'on munawwa ya tafi yi wa su Ummi ina kwana, sai da ya fara zuwa wurin Ummi bayan yayi mata barka da safiya ne sannan ya mik'a mata ledar, tareda yi mata bayanin sak'o ne daga surukarta, duk da kayan ba wasu bane amman taji dad'i sosai kuma ta yaba, ganin yanda taji dadi ne yasa ya kira Number'r Munawynshi ya had'a ta da Ummi, a cikin farin ciki ta fara gayar da Ummi cikin murya mai cike da ladabi, Ummi taji dad'i har a ranta ganin a waya ma yanda take girmamata ina ga fili?, tayi mata  godiyar sak'on sannan suka YI sallama, a cikin tsantsar farin ciki Ummi ta dinga yi masu addu'a tareda fatan alkhairi, Basarake zuciyarshi cike da jin dad'i ya nufi wurin Kakale. 

Kakale ma da taga sak'on taji dad'i sosai amman fa zuciyarta tak'i kwantawa da budurwar da yake son, saboda ita dai da zai bi ta tata da ya rabu da tagwayen duka yaje ya nemi wata ya aura, saboda samun nutsuwar aurenshi don ita kam ta hango akwai matsala a cikin wannan tafiya, duk da bai fayyace mata komai ba, don hausawa sunce abunda babba ya hango yaro ko ya hau sama bazai iya hango shi ba, Itama ya had'ata da Munawwa tayi mata godiya tareda d'an barkwanci sannan suka yi sallama. 

Sannan ya fito ya je wajen Abbanshi sun dad'e suna tattauna matsalolin kasuwancinsu da ci gabanshi, tareda shawarwarin hanyoyin da zasu bi su k'ara fad'ad'a shi a fad'in Nigeria da k'asashen ke'tare, sannan Basaraken ya fara sanar da shi akwai yarinyar da yake so amman dai basu kai k'arshe ba, ALHAJI Dikko yayi murna sosai kuma yayi masa fatar alkhairi. 

Munawwa da Mummy kam suna jin yace zai zo amma sai nan da kwana biyu, hankalinsu ya k'ara tashi saboda su a sonsu yazo yau d'innan don kawai ya walwalewa Daddy komai da bakinshi a huta, Mummy tace da Munawwa taje inda tace tana ganin motarshi ta tambayi mai shagon taji gaskiyar komi tunda bazai rasa sanin wanene shi ba, aikuwa tana k'are shirin makaranta ta barwa Munara key d'in motarsu ta hau napep ta nufi wurin d'an liti mai shago, bayan sun gaisa ne take tambayarshi 

"Don ALLAH malam wani bawan ALLAH nike tambaya"

D'anliti yana aunawa wani yaro sugar yace 

"ina saurarenki 'yanmata ALLAH yasa dai na sanshi"

Munawwa tace    "ka sanshi ma ai ko domin anan unguwar taku yake"

D'anliti ya mik'awa yaron sugan 'ya maida hankalinshi akanta yace 

"To wanene a cikin unguwar" 

Munawwa ta gyara tsayuwarta tace 

"Ina wata mota da ake ajiyewa a gindin wannan dalbejiya?"

D'an liti ya d'aga kai alamar ya gano tace 

"Yawwa to don Allah kasan mai motar?"

Gaban D'anliti ya fad'i saboda yaji tsoron kada ace wani abu Aliyu yayi, a cikin sanyin jiki yace   "Eh nasanshi sosai meke faruwa."

Munawwa tayi saurin fad'in    "Eh to ba abunda yayi mummuna gaskiya kawai dai ina son in san miye sunanshi? kuma miye sunan mahaifinshi?kuma sannan miye sana'arshi?"

D'anliti yace   "Rigiji gabji to a gaskiya ni sunanshi kawai nasani amman bansan sunan Babanshi ba, don saboda yace mani shi ba d'an garinnan ne ba wani aiki ne ya kawo shi garinnan,haka ma ni bansan sana'arshi ba abu d'aya ne nasani yana da kirki sosai, sannan a fahimtarshi da nayi shi mutum ne mai son ibada, don watarana ma shi yake ja muna jam'in SALLAH a wannan masallacin"

Munawwa ta kai kallonta ga wani d'an fili da aka zagaye da duwarwatsu, inda yake yi mata nuni da hannu sannan ta dawo da kallonta gareshi tace, 

"Miye sunanshi?"

Kai tsaye d'anliti yace  "Aliyu."

Kirjin munawwa ya buga dam! Dam! Saboda sunan Aliyunta da taji ya kira a hankali tace,    "Anya ba Nuradden sunanshi ba ko?"

D'anliti ya shiga tunani na d'an lokaci sannan yace,     "Aliyu ne sunanshi gaskiya sai dai in ba wanda kike nufi bane."

Munawwa ta fara tafiya sannan  tace "OK nagode sosai da lokacinka da ka bani."

Ta juya ta fara tafiya har takai wurin napep d'in da ke tsaye tana jiranta d'anliti ya d'aga murya yace,     "Miye sunan Hajiyar?"

Tayi murmushi mai sauti kai tsaye tace,     "Munawwara."

 Ta k'are fad'a ta shige napep d'in suka bar wajen zuciyarta tana ta sak'e  sak'e akan cewa 

_waton dai ba shi bane Nuradden d'in Oh ALLAH shi wannan mutumi wai a'ina ma ya ganni nikam?._ 

 Sukuku take har aka tashi School ta dawo gida, Mummy ma da taji ba shi bane ta shiga rud'u tareda zullumin zuwan ranar, aiko tun daren ranar suke ta addu'ar ALLAH yasa yazo kar ya ce kuma wani uzuri ya rik'e shi, aiko ranar tun da safe Nuradden ta turo mata text akan ta dubi zuwanshi. 

**

Basarake kam a daddafe yayi kwana uku Abuja ya baro garin tareda Abokinshi Nuradden suka yo Kaduna,  kai tsaye suka nufi hotel d'in da yake sauka, bayan sunci sunsha ne Nuradden yayi shigar manyan alhazawa, domin ko shaddar dake jikinshi ka kalla kasan ba k'aramin mai kud'i bane, don agogonshi ma kawai zai yi 50k balle takalman da ke k'afarshi da hula, Nuradden fa ya fito tamkar wata d'an daren goma sha biyu fuskarshi sai shek'i take yi, don ko fatarshi ka kalla a take zaka gano hutun da ya ratsa jikinshi, duk da shi ba fari bane kamar Basarake haka kuma bai kai basarake kyau ba amman ko kad'an babu marena a halittarshi. 

Sab'anin Basarake da yayi shigarshi da ya saba idan zai je gidansu Munara,waton dai wani k'aramin yadi ne mai sauk'in kud'i amman yasha guga sosai kuma yayi masa kyau,  saboda ire iren kayan da yake sakawa kenan in zaije gidan, don takanas yasa aka d'inka masa su don kawai ya cika manufarshi. 

A cikin wata arniyar mota sabuwa suka shiga direba ya jasu suka tafi, Basarake kam ana kaiwa wurin d'an liti aka ajiye shi, sannan suka wuce kai tsaye sai gidan su Daddy.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*38*

Kai tsaye ta nufo d'akinsu  ta tarar da Munawwa ta k'are SALLAH kenan ta d'aga hannu tana addu'o'i, ba shiri ta buga wani uban tsaki ta ajiye jakarta tayi toilet, zuciyarta cike da tsanar 'yar uwartata saboda yanda take nuna a kullum ita mai ibada ce, waton ita zata dinga yiwa kuri da nafilfili da karatun alk'ur'ani, ita a ganinta da gangan take yi don kawai ta nuna mata ita mai addini ce, a komi ita sai ta nuna tafi kowa iyawa. 

Munawwa kam tana k'are addu'o'inta ta ajiye hijabinta ta fito, kai tsaye kitchen ta nufa ta zubo abinci saboda yunwar da keta kwakular cikinta, bayan ta zuba ne ta dawo falon ta zauna tana ci tana d'an kallon wakokin da ake yi tauraruwa, sai kawai taji saukar dirin motar Daddy alamun ya dawo kenan, cikin fad'uwar gaba ta yi saurin had'iye lomar dake bakinta, kafin ta tantance sai gashi tsidik ya shigo falon har lokacin ranshi a had'e yake, da sauri munawwa ta nufi inda yake jikinta yana kyarma tace,    "Daddy sannu da dawowa."   yakai  kallonshi kanta atake ya gano ita ce a harzuk'e ya taso mata yace,       "Don Ubanki had'o kayanki yanzu yanzunnan kibar man gidana, aure ne kije gaki gashi ku zama tantabaru ku tashi sama saboda soyayya amman WALLAHI ba a gidana ba."

Munawwa ta fashe da kuka tareda fara rok'arshi akan yayi hak'uri, amman ina zuciyarshi a wuya take ko d'ar baiji na tausayinta ba, hasali ma wata uwar tsawa ya daka mata akan ta tashi ta d'ebo kayanta tabar masa gida, jikinta yana rawa ta nufi d'akinsu tana kuka tana had'a kayanta a wata jaka, Munawwa da masifar Daddy tasa ta fito bayin ba shiri tana kallonta cike da mamakin ganin kayanta da take had'awa, bata ce da ita k'anzil ba ta saka dogon hijabinta na sallah ta fito falo, saboda har lokacin Daddy bai daina balbalin bala'in ba, inda ya tunzura  Mummy ta biyeshi suna ta sa'insa a tsakaninsu, Munawwa ta d'an d'ebo kayanta da basu wuce kala goma ba sai wasu 'yan abun buk'ata da dole ne sai ta nemesu, sannan ta saka hijabinta ta fito jaye da jakarta tana kuka, tana yin arba da Mummy ta ajiye jakar aguje tayi wurinta tareda rungumeta tana kuka mai tsanani Mummy ta fara lallashinta, A fusace Daddy yace, 

"WALLAHI kizo ki fice man daga gida  tun kafin in fitar dake ta k'arfin tsiya." Yana k'are fad'in maganganun ya juya ya ga Baba Isah tsaye fuskarshi cike da tausayin Munawwa, a cikin masifa yace dashi,     "Muddin ka sake ta k'ara shigowa gidannan to kasani a bakin aikinka WALLAHI."   sannan ya wuce fuuuu yayi cikin gida sai gashi yaci karo da Mummy sanye da hijabinta itama da jakar kayanta, kai tsaye yace     "da kin k'ara taku biyu a wurin da nufin fita gidannan to kema a bakin igiyoyinki duka uku WALLAHI"

Tayi taku d'aya ga na biyu Munara ta yi saurin zuwa ta rukunkumeta tana kuka tareda fad'ar,    "Don ALLAH Mummy kiyi hak'uri WALLAHI idan kika bar gidannan nima barinshi zanyi in shiga duniya."      Mummy ta fashe da kuka mai tsanani tace,      "WALLAHI bazan iya jurar zama gidan da aka kori 'yata ba."

"Aje asaka credit ayi waya da Princess" 

A rud'e mai Napep d'in ya fara godiya zuciyarshi cike da farinciki suka rabu. 




D/Auta Novel

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*39*

Tun daga harabar gidan yaran gidan Munawwa ta fara sabon kuka saboda tuno shikenan ita da nasu gidan, da gatanta za'a maida rab'e rab'e gidan wasu, koda ta shiga gidan ta iske duka matan gidan suna babban falon suna  kallon wani series da ake yi arewa 24 mai suna labarina, shigarta yayi dai dai da kammala shirin, dukansu suka kai kallonsu gareta cike da mamakin ganin jakar da suka yi a hannunta, ga kuma idanuwanta da suka sha kuka har suka kumbure, da sauri Zulaiha ta taso tareda rik'o jakar hannunta tace,    "Munawwa lafiya? Me aka yi maki?"  tambayar ta k'ara tunzura zuciyar Munawwara ta fashe da wani sabon kuka mai sauti, Matar ALHAJI baba ta farko wato mamar Yusuf ta taso da sauri ta ri'ko hannunta, kai tsaye ta nufi d'akinta da ita ta zaunar da ita tana ta lallashinta duk da batasan me aka yimata ba, Zulaiha tazo ta ajiye mata jakar sannan ta zauna itama suka ci gaba da bata baki akan tayi hak'uri, cikin kuka Munawwa tace "ALHAJI Baba yana nan?"    Matar tace    "Baya gida amman duk inda yake yana kan dawowa, tashi kiyi SALLAH kafin ya shigo lokacin SALLAH yayi."   sannan ta mik'e tana sharar hawaye ta shige toilet d'in dake d'akin, Zulaiha ma ta fice domin tayi sallah'r Mamar YUSUF mai suna Hajiya Hindu, ta d'aga waya ta kira Mummy zuciyarta cike da mamakin ganin Munawwa a wannan yanayin, wayar tayi ta ringin ba'a d'auka ba sai cen taji an d'auka, bayan sun gaisa da Mummy ne take tambayarta lafiya taga Munawwa da jaka, Mummy ta fashe da kuka tareda yimata bayani fillah fillah, sannan cikin muryar da tasha kuka Mummy tace,     "Don ALLAH Hajiya Hindu ki kula man da Munawwara sosai na baki amanarta, duk da nasan daman cen bazaku yi mata mummunan rik'o ba"

"Hajiya Hindu tace kar kiji komai Hajiya insha ALLAH ki d'auka tamkar cewa Munawwara tana hannunki ne, kada ki damu ai ALLAH yana bayan masu gaskiya, shi kuma ALLAH ya ganar dashi."

Sannan suka yi sallama wanda yayi daidai da gama sallah'r Munawwa ta baje kan carpet d'in ta kwanta hawaye sunata sauka ta gefen fuskarta, Hajiya Hindu taje tayo arwallar itama tayi sallahr,  sannan ta fara baiwa Munawwa hak'uri akan ta zama jaruma ta ajiye komi aranta ta d'auka cewa ma kamar da Mummynta take zaune, dole Munawwa ta hak'ura saboda kada taga bata ji maganarta ba, suna nan Hajiyar tana ta kwantar mata da hankali sai ga yara suna Murna ALHAJI Baba ya dawo, bayan d'an lokaci ne Hajiya Hindu ta nufi d'akin tsaf tayi masa bayanin komi da Mummy tayi mata, tareda sanar dashi Munawwara tana gidan ya d'aga waya ya kira Daddy amman tayi ringing har ta katse ba'a d'auka ba, da ya matsa da kiran ma sai yaji wayar akashe ranshi ya k'ara b'aci yace,    "Lalle Zakari ni zai yiwa haka akan na nayiwa 'yarshi miji?to WALLAHI sai yayi nadamar abunda ya aikata,  domin kuwa wanda yake tsammanin yarshi zata aura bai da masaniya akan cewa yafi ma wanda shi yake hasashe, hasali ma plan ne suka had'a masa baida masaniya sun rufe shi a baibai shi ko yana ta hauka."

Daga nan ya kwashe komi ya sanar da Hajiya hindu, cikin tsananin mamaki tace

"ikon ALLAH yafi gaban mamaki to ya kagani Alhaji a sanarwa da Mahaifiyarta komi,  don a kwantar mata da hankali koko a k'yaleta itama"   yace 

"A'a ki bar maganar a tsakaninmu ai komi ya kusa zuwa k'arshe kwana nawa ne ya rage?abu d'aya nike so wurinki ki kama bakinki ki tsuke sannan akwai kud'in da ya bani ranar da yazo dubu d'ari biyar zan baki dubu d'ari a aciki ki nemo wa yarinyar 'yar tsarabarku ta mata kiyi ta bata ki kula da ita sosai insha ALLAHU ba zamu in kunyar wannan ja gaba da zamuyi ba, fatanmu kawai ALLAH ya bamu lada." 

Aiko haka akayi cikin kwana uku Hajiya Hindu ta saka Munawwa a gaba da sha wancen turara wannan, dolenta ta saki jiki da ita kamar daman cen a hannunta take, Kwananta hud'u da zuwa gidan ALHAJI Baba yasa ta shirya cikin wata arniyar doguwar riga ja mai had'e da d'an kwalinta, yace da ita taje Yusuf zai kaita k'auyensu Aliyunta ta gayar da  Mahaifiyarshi, zuciyarta cike da mamakin hakan Munawwa ta fito tazo zata shiga gaban motar ne Yusuf yayi saurin fad'in      "Lala koma baya ki zauna akwai mai wurin"  cikin jin haushinshi tayi baya ta zauna, ganin yanda duk tabi ta rud'e ne yasa YUSUF ya fara yimata dariya yace,    

"K'auyen naku ne ba kya son zuwa ko kuwa?, don na fahimci kamar an tilastaki zuwan."    ta maka masa harara sannan suka fice daga gidan inda suka hango ALIYU bakin titi yana jiransu,  Munawwa ta k'ara cika da mamakin yanda Aliyu ya shawo kan Alhaji Baba da har ya amince da tafiyar, tayi kicinkicin da fuska tak'i kulashi har ya shigo bayan shima ya zauna, fuskarshi cike murmushi sannan suka fara tafiya abun mamaki kuma sai Munawwa taga an nufi hanyar makarantarsu, cike da tsiwa tace da YUSUF idan kasan zaka b'ata man lokaci ka ajiyeni in shiga ta haya"   Basarake yayi saurin ri'ko hannunta ya matse har sai da tayi 'yar k'ara tareda k'ok'arin k'wace hannun amman ta kasa, Yusuf dai sai dariyarshi yake yi har suka kai makarantar, sai ga Munara jikinta yana rawa ta bud'e gaban motar ta shiga, duk da taga Munawwa da Basarake amman KO kallo basu isheta ba balle ta nuna ma tasansu, cikin k'unar rai Munawwa tace

_Bak'in Munafuki_   

Maimakon Munara taga anyi hanyar gidan ALHAJI Baba sai kawai taga Yusuf ya saki hanyar barin gari sai sharara gudunshi yake yi, cike da Mamaki tace

_Yau dai komi yazo k'arshe duk wani b'oye b'oye ya kau zaki banbance tsakanin aya da tsakuwa_  

Aiko sai gasu sun kai wani d'an madaidaicin k'auye, inda motar ta gangara sai k'ofar wani gida dake farko farkon garin, sannan suka yayi parking suka firfito, Itakam k'ara zama kawai tayi and motar tana yatsinar fuska, Yusuf ya sunkuyo yana dariya yace

"Lale marhabin da zuwan Kishiyata"

"Sannu 'yata ALLAH yayi maki albarka ALLAH ya baku zaman lafiya"   Basarake da Aliyu suke ta fad'in Ameeñ a fili,  Munawwa kam sai dai ga zuciya saboda tsananin kunyarsu da take ji. 

Suna cikin gaisuwar ne sai ga Munara afujajan ta shigo gidan, saboda wani fitsari da ya cika mata mara har yana k'ok'arin zubowa, Inna da A'i suka bita da kallo cike da mamakin kamar da suke yi da amaryar Ali, ko gayar dasu bata yi ba tayi kan butar da ta hango ta bakin bayi ta rida tareda fad'awa bayin, dukansu suka bita da kallo cike da mamakin abunda tayi, kowa da abunda ke ranshi har ta fito zuciyar Munawwa cike da jin kunyar abunda tayi, yayin da Basarake yake jin kamar yaje ya shak'o shegiya yayi ta jibga, saboda bala'in takaicinta da yake ji, ta tako a hankali tana yatsine yatsine tayi hanyar waje, Inna ta d'aga murya tace 

"Ke ko 'yarnan bazaki zo ki gayar da 'yar tsohuwa ba?"  sannan ta dawo a tsaye k'erere ta gayar da ita, A'i dai ko kallo bata samu ba balle gaisuwar, itakam ko a jikinta taje ta nufi inda ta fito daga farko sai gata ta fito da kwanon shan ruwa sabo cike da fura, ta ajiye gaban Munawwa tace    "'yata ga fura kisha"  Munawwa ta na sunne ta girgiza kai 'yar tsohuwar tace, 

"Aiko baki isa ba ace kizo ganinmu ki kasa shan komi."

Munawwa ta d'auki kwanon furar ta d'an kurb'a sannan ta ajiye,'yar tsohuwar ta kalli Munara dake tsaye k'erere akanta tace,     "Ke tsaida rage tsawonki kisha kema."   

Munawwaa ta maka mata harara a fakaice sannan tayi yak'e tace, 

"Idan na shata a'i kafin inje gida cikina ya lalace, idan ma anyi sa'a ban amayar da 'yan hanjina ba"

Tsohuwar cike da mamaki tace. 

"Ba shakka d'iyar santanbul shafaffiya da mai"   Basarake duk da yake ranshi yake a b'ace yanayin yanda tayi maganar tareda mayar da baki gefe ita ala dole an b'ata mata abun ya bashi dariya, Yusuf ma sai d'a ya k'yalk'yala Munawwa kam shiru tayi saboda ranta a b'ace yake da abunda Munarar tayiwa gidan surukanta, Basarake ya mik'e yace da Munawwara    "taso muje kiga d'akinki"  

Munawwa ta noke 'yar tsohuwar tazo ta tada ita tareda cewa,     "Yau naga budurwa irinta k'auyenmu."

Duk suka saka dariya Munawwa tabi 'yar tsohuwar Aliyu a gaba har wani d'an  k'aramin b'angare, inda yasha sabon fenti wanda da alama d'aki d'aya ne tal sai bayi dake gefen wurin ko kitchen babu. 

Suka shiga d'akin dukansu yayinda Munara take biye dasu tana yamutsar  fuska, zuciyarta cike da jin dad'in inda Munawwa ta kawo kanta saboda soyayyar banza. 

Suna cikin d'akin Basarake yace da Munawwa,    "To hajiyata ga dakinki ya kika ganshi?"  

Munawwa ta rufe fuska tana dariya tace 

"Yayi man"

Sai kawai suka ji Munara tace,    "ALLAH ya wadaran naka ya lalace, yanzu ke miye abun yi da wannan kangon d'akin?da ko maigadin gudanmu baya kwana a cikinshi, cab WALLAHI anyi asarar kud'in tara anan."

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

'yar tsohuwar tayi zumbur tace,      "Kedai kam anyi ja'irar yarinya don nikam da in haife irinki gara b'arin cikin, kinsan ALLAH da ace anan kike da tuni na zauneki sai kin kasa shed'awa."        ta k'are maganar tana kwatanta yanda  zata zauneta harda SU cije baki, dukansu sai da suka murmusa Munara kam bakinta toshe tareda  dafe ciki,  sai cin dariya take saboda ganin yanda tsohuwar take 'yar firit da ita,  amman kuma wai itace zata taushe wani tana dariyar duk ta d'ago ta kalli tsohuwar ta k'ara fashewa da wata sabuwar dariya, cikin tsarguwa da dariyar y'ar tsohuwar tace,      "Wallahi ke kam anyi 'yar tselan uwa,  kinci darajar Matar Ali da wallahi sai nayi maki wanka da kashin shanu, amman ba komi zaku had'u da gamonki." "To ALLAH ya tsare hanya ya kaiku lafiya " 

Sannan suka rako su HAR gida Inna ta shiga d'aki ta d'auko wata 'yar leda ta mik'awa Munawwa tak'i karb'a, dole Basaraken ne ya karb'a suka shiga mota tareda yiwa juna sai anjima, Yusuf yaja motar a cikin k'warewa suka KAMA hanya, sun fito titi kenan motar ta tsaya,Yusuf ya fito ya fara kwalularta amman tak'i tashi, Basarake ma yayi iya k'ok'arinshi amman ko gezau, suna nan tsaye suna jimamin yanda zasu sami bakaniken da zai gyara masu ita  gashi sun fito garin, sai ga wata arniyar mota zata wuce har ta tafi sannan ta dawo a hankali tareda zuge glass d'in, ya lek'o da kanshi wazan gani kamar Nuradden, Basarake yayi saurin zuwa suna magana na dan lokaci sannan ya dawo yace masu su zo za'a rage masu hanya,  Munara da Munawwa suka fito  Munara tana yin arba da Nuradden ta washe baki cikin farin cikin ganinshi, ta isa wajen motar tareda bud'a  gaba ta shiga ta zauna, Munawwaa ta bud'e baya ta zauna shima Basaraken ya zauna d'ayan b'angaren,  sannan suka tafi aka bar Yusuf da zummar za'a kawo masa mai gyara.

fad'a masa, sai gashi hakan ya zamo man alkhairi, yanda na ganshi dattijon kirki dai dai da yanda nasa aka bincikoman halayenshi shiyasa ban b'oye masa komi ba na fayyace masa gaskiyar al'amari, ya k'ara bani k'arfin gwiwa na cika plan d'ina na k'arshe, wajen kaiki k'auyen da nayi a yanzu kika gano d'aki, hmmmm wanda har 'yar uwarki ta shikka rashin mutunci a tsammaninta iyayena ne da gaske, to bari kuji wannan tsohuwar da kuka gani Mahaifiyar Baba Isah ce maigadin gidanku Inna A'i kuma matarshi ce, wannan d'akin da na kaiku d'akin yaronshi na biyu ne da zaiyi aure kwanannan."     

Munawwa jikinta ya mutu murus amman sai da tayi jarumtar furta,    "To meye faidar d'aukar Munara mu tafi tare!, haka ma naji Yusuf yana wani zance wanda bangano kanshi ba"    yayi murmushi mai sauti sannan ya jawota duka ta fad'a jikinshi sannan ya zagayo hannunshi ta bayanta ya kalli Munara ido cikin ido yace,     "Amfanin zuwa da ita shine kawai domin in k'ara auna dusar kwakwalwarta sannan in sami damar sanar da ita yanzu abunda taji da kunnuwanta, nayi amfani da yusuf ya kirata yana sanar da ita cewa ga honey'nta nan yazo wajen ALHAJI Baba domin a had'a aurensu tare da namu ni dake, amman yace sai tazo yaji ra'ayinta, da wannan ne muka hilato ta tayi ta tsumayin zuwan Yusuf ya kaita gidan dafatar kin gamsu."

Ya k'are maganar tareda kashe mata ido d'aya ba shiri ta ture shi cikin fushi    sannan tace,     "Ka natsu dakyau ka dawo da soyayyarka ga masoyiyarka ta farko don ni sam bazan tab'a amincewa da wannan cin amana ba, saboda nasan irin son da take maka wanda har yanzu duk da bakwa tare bata kula kowa saboda soyayyar da take maka, na amince a d'aura maku auren tare da ita ranar da aka saka za'a yi namu ni dakai, naji na amince zan sadaukar da soyayyata ga 'yar uwata in hak'ura in bar mata kai."

Basarake yayi saurin saka hannu ya buge mata bakin, sannan yace      "Zaki fad'a a baki amman ba har cikin zuci kike fad'ar  maganar ba, saboda nasan yanda kike sona kuma so wanda ba algus, ko kinmanta kin tab'a fad'a man duk da a lokacin bakiyi zurfi ason ba, amman kince cire so ba abune mai sauk'i ba da za'a cireshi tamkar cirar kaya?, kince fa dafi ne dashi ko ka warke tambon bazai barka ba, to nikam taya zansa macen da nafi so a cikin mataye jinya kuma akan soyayyata? ever my golden girl to bari kiji ni nan ko da  giyar wake nasha  WALLAHI bazan taba daina sonki ba, haka ma bazan tab'a aurenta ba koda ko ita kad'ai ce ta rage sauran mace a duniya,  ki ji da kyau babu sauran soyayyarta koda 'yar k'ire a zuciyata hasalima ni in banda fuskarta da ta zama d'aya da taki WALLAHI da sai ince ko lahira bana fatar ALLAH ya had'ani da mai irin fuskarta ma balle ita, ina da ke nikam mezanyi da jaka wawuya dak'ik'iya mara hankali irinta?"

Munara zata yo kanshi Sojojin suka sake d'ora mata bindigogin dole taja burki tareda fashewa da wani masifaffen kuka ta fad'i k'asa tana turje turje, Nuradden dake tsaye yana kallon abunda yake faruwa yayi saurin tsugunnawa wurinta cikin muryar tausayi yace,      "Da ace kina da halin kirki  da na tambayi budurwar da zan aura idan ta  amince sai na hadaku tare na aura, amman gaskiya halayenki ko k'ofar gidana bazan so ki taka  ba balle har in saka ki ciki ki zamo mani bomb ki tashi dani in tarwatse."   ya k'are maganar cikin kyalkyalewa da dariya sannan ya d'ago yana kwaikwayon maganarta yace     

 "Kayi hak'uri don ALLAH WALLAHI haka muke fama da ita na rasa ne tagani a jikin wancen matsiyacin da har take kulasa."  

Gaba d'aya sojojin suka barke da dariya wasu har da rik'e ciki saboda yanda Nuradden ya k'arye murya tareda lankwashewa irinta ta 'yan daudu yana yatsinar fuska, Munawwa ma taso tayi dariya amman ta waske, saboda abunda ke gabanta yafi k'arfin dariya har ga ALLAH bata jin dad'in abunda ake yiwa 'yar uwarta ba, shiyasa cikin fushi ta nufi k'ofar fita basarake yabita ya cafkota tareda had'ata da jikinshi murya cikin rad'a saiti da kunnenta yace,     "WALLAHI kika sake yunkurin fita sai na tsotse bakinki tass agaban kowa wurinnan"  

Ba shiri ta durk'ushe tareda  fashewa da kuka shima ya duk'a murya k'asa k'asa yace,     "Ki natsu dakyau bana son shirme duk abunda nace kiyi kawai WALLAHI ko in aiwatar da abunda na fad'a    tashi tsaye"     jikinta yana rawa ta mik'e ya ri'ko hannunta har inda Munara take a durk'ushe tana kuka,  yayiwa wani soja magana da ido ba shiri ya katsawa Munara tsawa yace   

"Stand Up!!!!"   ta mik'e a zabure idonta a runtse tana kuka ya sake buga mata wata tsawa tareda kara mata bindiga yace    "Open your eyes."

Ba shiri ta bud'e idonta sai gasu sun sauka akan Basarake da Munawwara da suke tsaye gabanta rungume da juna,  tayi saurin kawar da idonta tareda zubowar wasu hawayen, cikin murmushi Basarake yace       "ya kika ga wannan couple? Ki kallemu dakyau ki gani cikin idonki zaki hango dacewar da muka yi da juna"  ya kalli Munawwa ya k'ara kashe mata idanuw sannan yace

"Kaf Matan duniya kece wadda nafi k'auna a birnin masoya,  banida wani buri a yanzu da ya wuce muyi aure ni da ke, ke d'aya tal nike so kuma ke zan rik'e gam gam don kin isheni gaba da bayana, gaki 'yar  fara kyakkyawata wadda ko talkaminki yafi gold daraja a wurina komai tsadarshi, ke daban ce a gareni kyawon halinki ne ya janyo nake dad'a kaunarki safe da rana, saboda tarbiyyarki da girmama matsayin kowa, nagode da ALLAH da ya had'a ni dake a rayuwa burina d'aya ne a mallaka mani ke a matsayin matata" 

Ya saka hannu aljihu ya ciro wani d'an box na zoben gold mai masifar kyau ya janyo hannunta ya saka mata sannan ya saita bakinshi saiti da zoben yayi mata kiss wanda har sai da ta d'an zabura saboda yanda taji tsikarta ta tashi saboda saukar tattausan lips d'inshi ga fatar hannunta, sannan ya saki hannun ya saita bakinshi a goshinta ya yi mata wani kiss, sannan ya dawo saman karen hancinta yayi mata wani, sai gashi ya gangaro saiti da bakinta ta fara girgiza kai tana yi masa kashedi da ido amman kamar tana ingizashi, caraf ya cafko halshenta ya shiga juya nashi cikin bakinta, duk da mutsu mutsun da take sai da ya shanye ruwan bakinta KAF sannan ya saketa,   Munara BA shiri ta saka hannu zata fisge  bindigar sojan dake tsaye kanta da nufin ta halbesu duka, amma ina bata sami wannan damar ba, don sojan yasan aikinshi sai ma ball da yayi da ita ta fad'i k'asa kamar wata mai aljannu tana mulumula. 

Munawwa ta k'ara  fashewa da kuka Basarake ya ja hannunta suka fito tana tirjiya ya sakata motar da suka je ya nufi gidan ALHAJI Baba da ita, inda anan ya tsaya bakin gate d'in saboda motocin kawo lefen da suka cika harabar gidan har wasu a waje,  ta sauka da gudu tayi cikin gidan kamar wata zararra ya bita da kallo yana dariya . 

Munara kam sai da suka lallasata da kyau sannan suka ja kunnenta sosai tareda gargad'i mai tsanani, sannan suka mik'a mata wayarta da ALLAH yasa bata ci screen ba amman gaba daya daga glass d'in wayar har an tub break d'in duk sun fashe, suka korata ta fito gidan tana layi saboda azabar da tasha uwa uba ga bak'in cikin dake dank'are cikin zuciyarta, tana tsaye kan titi tana neman napep sai ga kiran Zulaiha a wayarta ya shigo, kamar kar ta d'auka kuma dai cen ta d'auka ba tareda tace k'anzil ba, aiko taji Zulaiha ta fasa kuwwa tace, 

"Wayyo ALLAH Munara zo kiga surprises an kawo lefen Munawwara akwaiti goma sha takwas kala kala d'ari da hamsin, gwalagwalai kamar a shago"    ba shiri Munara ta rotsa wayar a saman titi tana huci  wanda yayi daidai da zuwan mai napep, yana tsayawa ta fad'a kamar wata zakanya tareda fad'ar sunan unguwarsu Alhaji Baba,   Mai napep yace wayarki fa da ta fad'i? Anan zamu barinta ba tareda tace dashi komi ba ta fito ta d'auki layukanta sannan ta koma, ya tada agwagwar suka fara tafiya tana hango wani d'an Black market tace dashi ya tsaya yana tsayawa ta ridi galam d'aya na fetur ta ciro agogon dake d'aure ga tsintsiyar hannunta ta ajiye masa sannan ta cire takalminta da d'an kunnenta duka ta ajiye masa, jikinta yana rawa tace ka ajiye man zan dawo in karb'i kayana in baka kudinka, idan ma baka ganni ba ka siyar dasu kawai kazauna wa kudinka tana k'are fad'ar maganar ta fad'a napep d'in, mai petur kam galala yayi da baki yana kallonta har suka bar wurin. 

Ana ajiyeta k'ofar gidan taga ashana gaban napep d'in ta sauko da sauri ta rida  cikin zafin nama tayi cikin gidan, duk da kiran da Mai Napep d'in  yake mata akan ta bashi kud'inshi tak'i juyowa, shikam yana ganin haka yabar k'ofar gidan a bala'e, tana shiga gidan taga 'yan gidan gaba d'aya sun baje  k'aton falon suna ta kallon lefe, saboda 'yan kawo lefen sun tafi ba wanda ya lura da zuwanta hankalinsu ya d'auku sosai sai jin tashin warin petur suka yi, kafin su tantance sai ga wuta tana ci balbal tsakaninsu aguje kowa ya tashi ana rige rigen barin wurin, kafin kace me gaba d'aya falon ya kama da wuta.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*41*

Aiko matan gidan yara da manya kaf suka fito gidan aguje, Munawwa tana d'akin hajiya Hindu kwance tana kukan takaicin abunda aka yiwa munara, duk da a ranta taji dad'in bayyyanar ganin HAIDAR a matsayin Aliyunta, a gefe d'aya kuma cike take da murnar lefen da take taji anata zuzutawa, don daga nan d'akin tana jiyo tashin muryoyinsu suna ta shewa,  tayi zurfi sosai da tunanin abunda ya faru yanzu da abunda yayita  faruwa abaya, zuciyarta cike da farin ciki ta kira Mummy suna magana duk da kunyar da take ji amman bai hanata bayyanawa Mummy cewa Aliyunta shine HAIDAR d'in Munara ba, tareda fayyace mata komi, Mummy taji dad'i sosai kuma tayiwa ALLAH godiya sai dai a ranta tasan ko yanzu akwai sauran rina a kaba, saboda ba k'aramin  rikici ne Aliyun ya had'a ba tsakaninsu, Munawwa tana cikin kwantarwa da Mummy hankali ne akan ba abunda zai faru, kawai taji sautin muryoyi kala kala sai kuwwa suke tareda  fad'in         "wuta wuta jama'a ayo agaji"   aiko a zabure ta fito koda tazo k'ofar babban falon da niyyar fita halshen wutar ya bugota, dole ta dawo baya ba shiri a firgice gashi hanyar ce kad'ai  ake bi a fita, ta yi kitchen da gudu cikin tsananin tashin hankali,  ta bud'e windown cikin zafin nama ta dira ta baya. 

 Mutanen sun taru sosai matan gidan da mak'ota tareda wasu masu wucewa da abun hawa, duk sun cika k'ofar gidan ana kallon halshen wutar da yayi tsinke ana jimami, kowa ya zubawa sarautar ALLAH ido, Hajiya hindu da sauran yaran gidan sai kuka suke yi suna fad'in    "Wayyo ALLAH Munawwara tana ciki a taimaka a fiddota kar ta k'one"   d'aya daga cikin Matan Alhaji Baba ta fara bayanin cewa,    "Munawwara fa ta fito tare muka fito gidan da ita."     Zulaiha tana Kuka tace   "WALLAHI ba Munawwara bace Munara ce don itace ke yawan saka k'ananun kaya kuma ni na kirata tazo taga lefe, ai ita Munawwa jar doguwar riga ce a jikinta kuma tana d'akin Hajiya Mama tun da ta dawo"   aiko gaba d'aya wurin ya rud'e da salati Sai ga Alhaji Baba aguje da mota, saboda kiran da aka yi masa ya dawo wuta gidanshi, yana ajiye Motar ya fara duba kowa na gidan bai ga Munawwa ba, a cikin fad'uwar gaba yace     "Ina Munawwara?"   Duk suka had'a baki wajen fad'in,     "muma ita muke tambaya"  d'aya daga cikin yaran gidan da bai wuce shekara biyar ba yace,     "WALLAHI tana ciki ta kone"    a zabure ALHAJI Baba yayi kukan kura zai fad'a gidan Mutane suka rirrik'e shi, hawaye suka gangaro masa cikin kuka  yace    "ku barni in je in fito da Amanatah, bansan da wane ido zan kalli Iyayenta da Mijin da zata aura b..... ."     kafin ya k'arasa zancen sai gata aguje cikin firgici ko d'ankwali babu, ba SHIRI ALHAJI Baba ya kufce daga rik'on da aka yi masa ya je ya  rungumeta tareda fitowa da ita gidan a ranshi yana ta hamdala, saboda ganin ba abunda ya sameta fitowarsu yayi daidai da zuwan motar 'yan kwanakwana, kafin kace me sun fara aikinsu, amman duk da k'ok'arinsu kasancewar wutar ta d'an d'auki  lokaci tana ci, d'aki daddaya ne a gidan bata laso ba, bayan an kashe Wutar ne hankalin kowa ya d'an kwanta wasu ma har sun fara watsewa bayan sunyiwa su Alhajin ALLAH ya tsare gaba, kasancewar Yusuf yana tareda su Basarake har lokacin, suna tattauna yanda za'ayi tsare tsaren bikin, sai ga kira daga wani abokinshi yana sanar dashi abunda yake faruwa a gidansu, hankali tashe ya sanar dasu Basarake abunda aka fad'a masa, dukansu hankalinsu ya tashi sosai Musamman basarake, da gudu ya fad'a motar  yaja kamar zai tashi sama, saboda tunanin ko wani abu ya sami Amaryarshi,. 

Cikin minti biyar kacal suka k'araso gidan inda har lokacin mutanen gidan suna tsaye cirko cirko sun kasa shiga, Alhajin ne kawai da sauran wasu mak'otanshi da samarin da suka yi saura suka shiga ana dudduba inda wutar taci. 

Tun daga nesa ya hango Munawwa daga ita sai doguwar rigar dake jikinta  ba talkami ba d'ankwali, ba shiri yace      "Ya salam"   wani bala'in kishinta ya ziyarceshi, da sauri ya fito motar ya nufota kamar ya daketa ita bata ma san zuwansu ba, saboda labarin da Zulaiha take fad'a mata, wanda ya sakata kukan zuciya dana fili ta durk'ushe a wurin tana kuka, Matan gidan sunata bata hak'uri amman ina hawayenta sun kasa tsayawa,  Zulaiha sai lallashinta take akan tayi hak'uri haka ALLAH ya kaddaro daman cen lefen ba rabonta bane, ita kam ba wannan ne damuwarta ba damuwarta d'aya Zulaiha da ta sanar da ita Munara tazo gidan, da ta tambayeta waya fad'a mata ankawo lefen tace,   itace ta kirata domin tazo su had'u su taya juna Murna, don ita batasan dawan garin ba shiyasa ta zaci abun duk d'aya ne, batasan ta jangwalo bala'i da hannunta ba don gashi yayi sanadin kone gidansu, Munara tana durk'ushe tana kukan Zulaiha ta duk'o tace 

"Ga angonki nan zuwa ashe shima yaji"  ba shiri  ta tashi tsaye aiko ta hangoshi ya nufosu, Basarake kam ganin idanuwan Matan da yaran gidan duka akanshi yasa ya wayance tareda gayar dasu  kamar daman cen wurinsu yaje, sannan ya tambayi Alhajin akace yana ciki, Yusuf ya k'araso wurinsu a rud'e yana tambayar meye mafarin wutar,    Hajiya hindu tace 

"WALLAhi ba wanda yasan mafarinta muna tsaka da kallo sai kawai muka fara jin warin fetur, sai tashin wuta butt ba shiri muka fito dukanmu."

Aiko sai ga yaron d'azu yace    "WALLAHI Aunty 'yan biyu ce fa ta zuba kalanzir ina kallonta  ta kunna ashana." kasancewar duk yaran gidan haka suke cewa Munawwa. 

Aiko ba shiri Munawwa ta buga k'irji tace    "Ni!?  Kai ni ka gani mujahit ?"  ya d'aga kai yace      "Kece fa  har kika saka dogon wando ai na gane ji"   a take wurin suka fara fad'in,    "WALLAHI Munara ce, itace ta saka wando Jean's da wata half gwn d'in rigar roba."    Munawwa da Basarake kai har ma da Nuradden da Yusuf duka sai da kirjinsu ya buga,    aiko d'aya daga cikin matan Alhajin tace,    "Tabbas WALLAHI naga shigowarta da galam a hannu, amman banzaci abunda tazo yi kenan ba" 

Hajiya Hindu da tasan komi tace,   "Amman wagga yarinya Ubangiji ya shiryeta ya ganar da ita gaskiya, amman WALLAHI dole ne ad'auki mataki idan ba haka ba ALLAH ka d'ai yasan abunda zata yi agaba."

Sai Alhaji baba ya fito saboda kiran da Yusuf yayi masa akan yazo gasu BASARAKE, yana zuwa ya basu hannu suka gaisa, bayan sun yi masa jaje  ne Alhaji yace su shigo su gani suna shiga falon suka yi turus saboda tsibin lefen da suka yi gashi a narke, gaba d'aya komi ya kone sai toka sai abunda yake k'arfe da yayi bak'i k'irin, Basarake yayi murmushin takaici a ranshi yace,    

_"Wannan yarinya sai na koya mata hankali"_

Bayan sun fito ne Basarake yace da ALHAJI baba akwai gida da ya saya a garin, yazo suje yanzu akaisu gidan saboda wannan dole sai an gyarashi kafin su shiga ciki, ALHAJI ya nuna masa ba komi yanzu ma za'a iya gyara inda zasu kwanta kafin safe, shiko ya kafe akan don ALLAH ya amince kawai ba wani abu, Alhajin ya amince ba don yaso ba saboda nauyin surukuta dake tsakaninsu amman yaji dad'in wannan albishir d'in, yayi farin ciki sosai yana ta yi masa godiya BASARAKE yace yiwa kai ne,  ALHAJI yayiwa matanshi bayanin abun alkhairin, cike da farin ciki aka fara kwasarsu zuwa gidan, tareda jagorancin Yusuf da Nuradden aka kwashi wasu aka tafi, aka bar Basarake da Munawwa da Zulaiha da sauran matasan 'yanmatan, yana ganin ba idon babba, sai ji Munawwa tayyi yaja mata kunne da k'arfi tareda jawo dogowar kalabar dake kanta da k'arfi, ba shiri tace    "Wash don ALLAH kayi hak'uri WALLAHI bazan sake ba"    cikin fushi yace,    "Ashe kinsan laifinki?"  ya k'are maganar tareda k'ara jawo gashin, da magiya da lallami da ban hak'urin da su Zulaiha suke badawa ya saketa, amman ya b'uge da jifarta da harara don Ji yake kamar ya cire rigar jikinshi ya bata ta rufe nata jikin to amman ganin gidan sarakai ne yasa ya mazge amman fa zuciyarshi bata daina zugi ba, saboda ganin yake Allah kad'ai yasan iya wanda ya ganta a haka.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*42*

Suna nan tsaye sai ga Nuradden ya dawo ya kwashi sauran matasan 'yan matan da su Munawwara,  Basarake ya zauna a gaba suna tafe yana ta sak'a abubuwa da yawa aranshi,  har suka kai wannan katafaren gidan da aka caskala Munara,  koda suka je suka tarar da ALHAJI baba yana magana da Yusuf ranshi a b'ace, wanda ga dukkan alama Yusuf ya gaya masa cewa Munara ce tayi sanadin wutar, bayan sun fiffito ne Basarake ya kira waya a wani company,  aiko minti goma a tsakani sai gasu sun bayyana da manya manyan motocinsu, tareda ma'aikatansu d'auke da had'ad'd'un   furnitures da kujeru da SU labulai da komai ma na b'angaren k'awata gida, kafin kace Me lungu da sak'o na falunan dake gidan tareda  d'akunansu duk sunyi sharr komi yaji, har sai k'arfe tara na dare sannan suka k'are duk da k'warewarsu ga aikin,  saboda yanda gidan ya kasance k'atoto guda, domin wannan kantamemen falon akwai k'ofa shida a cikinshi, wadda idan an shiga kowace k'ofar zata sadaka da wani falo mai d'auke da d'akuna uku d'aya shi kad'ai biyu duka ciki da falo ne sai kitchen, atak'aice dai kowane wuri ya zama wurin zaman mace d'aya har da yaranta in tana da su da d'akin bak'i ba takura, kuma a hakan duk sai da aka zuba furnitures kowane d'aki, wad'anda SUKA lak'ume kud'i ba y'an kad'an ba, don ko k'aton  main falon set d'in kujeru hud'u aka saka duk iri d'aya, kasancewar gidan sama da k'asa ne shima cen saman kowace mace tana da d'akinta, Matan ALHAJI Baba Uku kowace ta zab'i inda take so d'aya wurin aka barwa su Munawwara waton yanmatan gidan nasu d'aki  daban mai k'unshe da ciki da falo, haka ma 'yan matasan 'yan matan suma da d'akinsu, sai d'ayan sashen wanda yake da k'ofa a wajen gidan mai d'auke da k'aton seat room wanda ya zama mallakin Alhaji Baba, sai d'ayan sashen na Yusuf d'aya kuma na matasan mazan gidan, kowa cike yake da murna da farin cikin wannan ci gaba da suka samu, wanda suka ce k'ara da aka yi wannan gobara don ta zame masu ALKHAIRI, wanda koda mafalki basu tab'a tunanin zasu mallaki irin wannan gida ad'an k'aramin lokaci ba, sai addu'a da fatar Alkhairi suke yiwa Basarake, don ita kanta Munawwara ba k'aramin dadi taji ba, don wannan ya tabbatar mata da lalle ba k'aramin so Aliyunta yake yimata ba, sannan wannan rashin hankali da Munara tayi yasa taji ko kad'an bazata iya sadaukar da son Basarake  gareta ba, taci alwashin sai dai suyi fito na fito da ita in dai akan Aliyunta ne tunda har ta nuna ita kanta kawai ta sani. 

Cikin daren Basarake da Nuradden da Yusuf suka je suka lodo uwar siyayya ta readymade din suturar da zasu nema da safe, kasancewar dukansu ba mai kayan sakawa duk wuta ta lashe, wasu sun fara konewa wasu sun k'one ma gaba d'aya, sannan b'angaren abincin ma duk sun sissiyo waton abin karin safe da NA girkawa buhu buhu kwali kwali, don harta ruwa da lemuka DUK sai da aka cika firij firij d'in dake gidan tantsan da lemuka kala kala. 

***

ALHAJI Baba bayan komi ya natsa ne yace da Basarake gobe idan ALLAH ya kai mu yazo akwai maganar da zasuyi, kasancewar  lokacin goman dare tayi gashi kowa ya gaji neman inda za'a yada k'afad'u  ake yi shima gangara jiki ya sami huta. 

Yazo zai tafi ne ya ce da yusuf ya nemo masa Amaryarshi suyi sallama, aiko cikin minti biyu ya rakota har gindin motarsu Basarake, sai sunne sunne take saboda rashin sabo da sabuwar fuskarshi da yanzu ta zame mata tamkar bak'uwa, cikin nauyin baki tace

"Mun gode sosai da wad'anan  abubuwan alkhairi da kayi muna, ALLAH ya saka da ALKHAIRI ya biyaka da gidan aljannah, ko aljannar ma mafificiya mai k'ololuwar lada."

 Basarake ya bita da kallo mai cike da tsantsar k'auna, zuciyarshi cike da farin cikin addu'ar da tayi masa ranshi fes yace da ita

Ta rufe fuskarta cikin jin kunya tace,     "ka cancanta fiye da hska "

Ya b'ata fuska sannan yace,      "Kina nufin baki san wata addu'ar da zaki yimani ba bayan wannnan?"

Cikin murmushi tace. 
"nidai Iya sanina wannan itace k'arshen addu'a."      cikin zafin nama ya ri'ko hannunta ya matse gam da nashi ba shiri tace,    "Da zafi fa"   yace    "ai daman zafin nike son kiji don ki gano nima yanda naji zafi da bakiyi man addu'ar ALLAH ya mallaka man ke a matsayin matata ba, don haka sai kin fad'a ko inyi maki wata muguntar wadda zaki fad'a dolenki."    duk da laushin hannunshi da ke ratsata k'arfin kwanjinshi yasa ta jin zafin rik'on har cikin zuciyarta, dolenta tace

"ALLAH  ya baka mace ta gari."    yana 'yar dariya yace   "Ai na samu kice kawai ALLAH ya baka ni."

Cikin Jim nauyin fad'ar maganar tace
"ALLAH ya baka Munawwara."  aikuwa maganar ta shige shi har cikin birnin zuciyarshi,  saboda jin dad'i jikinshi ya mutu murus yana YI mata wani kallo mai cike da tsantsar sonta da k'aunarta, ganin yanayin kallon da yake mata da sauri ta k'wace hannunta ta nufi cikin gidan, zuciyarta cike da k'aunarshi wadda izuwa yanzu ta k'ara linkuwa, saboda bajintar da ya nuna wa soyayyarta. 

Aiko cike da farin ciki ya baro gidan suka nufi Hotel d'in da suke kwana shida Nuradden, sun dad'e suna tattauna matsalar Munara,  tareda neman hanyoyin da zasu ladaftar da ita  sannan suka yi bacci.

K'arfe goma dam suka nufi sabon gidan ALHAJI Baba, sunci manyan kaya kowane sai baza babbar riga yake, ga wani sihirtaccen k'amshi dake tashi a jikinsu, har k'asa suka kai wajen gayar da ALHAJI baba a cikin ladabi da kunyar surukuntaka, ALHAJI Baba shima ya amsa cike da jin nauyinsu ga wani girma da yake ganinsu dashi, saboda aduk lokacin da ya tuna wannan gida nashi ne halak malak sai yaji wani tsananin dad'i ya mamayeshi, Musamman da yaga iyalinshi kowa cikin walwala duk sunata  murna, sai yaji Farin ciki ya ziyarci zuciyarshi saboda daman shi farin cikin iyalinshi shine nashi.

Basarake ya b'ata fuska yace     "Kai wannan ya dama ni gurina d'aya abani amaryata Alaji, ku kuje cen kuci gaba da event's d'in don NI ko d'aya ma bazamu halarta ba"

Nuradden yana dariya yace,     "Wannan zumud'in fa yaso yayi yawa, ka bari mu muyi muda bamu san ma miye dad'in auren ba, kai ko fa kasha man farko fa"

Basarake bakinshi har rawa yake akan ya maida masa martani sai ga sallamar Zulaiha Munawwa tana bayanta, dole yayi gum yana k'arewa amaryarshi kallo da taci kwalliya da wani farin hijab mai kama da riga, tareda d'an gyalenshi dake mak'ale dashi, zagayayyiyar fuskarta ta fito wasai a ciki sai k'amshi ke tashi a jikinta, bayan sun gaisa Nuradden ya fahimci  kallon da yaga Basarake yana yiwa Munawwa, shi  yasa yace da Zulaiha ta fito suje waje su tsara yanda bikin zai kasance,  bayan fitarsu ne Basaraken yaje kusa da ita ya zauna tareda d'ora kanshi saman kafad'arta,   cikin wata siririyar murya yace, 

"Shikenan ni na zama d'an gata"      yanayin yanda yayi maganar yasa tsikar jikinta mik'ewa, ba shiri ta d'an ja baya kad'an ganin haka yasa ya d'ago tareda jifarta da wani tsadadden  kallo yace,    "Au yau guduna ma ake yi?"   tana wasa da 'yan yatsunta tayi k'ok'arin fad'in    "ba haka nan bane"     yace    "To me yasa kika matsa ko bakya son had'a jiki dani ne?"   tayi saurin d'aga kai ba shiri ya sauke ajiyar zuciya sannan yace,    "To saboda me? ban cancanta ko?"   tayi saurin dafe bakinta sannan tace,     "ba haka nike nufi ba"    

Yace     "OK koma minene ke kika sani zan kama ki ne, yanzu dai kinji abunda ake ciki ko?"     cikin sanyayyiyar murya tace.    "kamar me fa?"   

Ya had'e fuska sannan yace,     "kamar aurena da za'a d'aura dake ranar jumu'a nan da kwana biyu masu zuwa. "    ba shiri ta kai kallonta gareshi bakinta yana rawa tace,    "Wani sati dai"

Yana 'yar dariya yace

"A gidana ba saboda haka kije a k'ara had'o man ke in and out, bana son ace man wai lkc ya kur'e ba'a sami damar yiman wani abu ba, gyaran kai gyaran jiki k'unshi k'afa  da hannu, sai kuma babban gyaran don nafiso in jini ina yawo cikin gajimare a duniyar sama da kyau da kyau, saboda haka ki fad'a yanzu nawa zan bada?saboda ayi komai a wadace"      cikin sanyi jiki tareda rashin kuzari tace      "Ni k'unshi kawai za'a yiman sai gyaran KAI don haka kabarshi kawai zansa ayi man."

Ba shiri ya maka mata harara tareda janyo hannunta ya runtse da nashi, had'uwar fatar hannunsu yasa ya sauke ajiyar zuciya cikin rashin  kuzarin da ya rasa na k'ank'anin lokaci yace, 

"Bana son k'eta fa ki saki jiki kiyi abunda ya dace, yanzu Abuja zamu wuce saboda mu SANAR dasu abunda ake ciki, don haka don ALLAH kiji tsoron ALLAH ki saki jiki kiyi komai yanda ake so, idan ba haka  ba WALLAHI zaki janyo inyi punishing d'inki, kallanni da kyau kiga idanuwana sabida ina son ki gano ba wasa a cikin lamarina"     munawwa ta k'ara sunne kai saboda zantukanshi sun rikita mata lissafi, jin takeyi kamar ta nutse saboda kunyarsa amman shi ko a jikinshi. 

Ganin da gaske kunyarshi take ji yasa ya saka hannu ya zagayota ta baya sai gata ta fad'a jikinshi batareda ta  tantance ba,  cikin azama ya kai bakinshi ya tsotso lebenta a cikin wata kasalalliyar murya yace,     "kiyi hak'uri da Aliyunki saboda nima bansan ya aka YI sonki yayi man yawa har haka ba, yayi man  kamun da a kullum yake k'ara mamaye ko'ina lungu da sak'o na zuciya, sonki ya rufe man idanuwa shiyasa bana shayin fad'ar koma minene  a gabanki, kema shaida ce Aliyunki mai kawaici ne amman banda a soyayyarki don haka  ki koyi hak'uri dani kinji?."    yanayin yanda lafuzzanshi suke sauka suna kashe mata zuciya da soyayyarshi, haka ma hannunshi yake yawa a jikinta gaba d'aya gabban jikinta sunyi sanyi, sabon yanayin da ta tsinci kanta a ciki yasa tayi saurin tashi daga jikinshi, tareda mik'ewa tsaye jikinta yana rawa tace

"Akwai tafiya a gabanku gwara kuje da wuri kada dare yayi maku a hanya."

Yana YI mata wani sihirtaccen kallo yace,     

"Ba damuwa ko baki kore ni ba yanzu daman zan wuce, ga kud'i nan ki je kiyi abunda nace, kafin zuwa anjima kad'an zan kawo maki lefenki"   yana k'are maganar ya ajiye mata rafar dubu guda hud'u, da sauri ta juyo cikin sanyin murya tace,     "Don ALLAH ka daina wahalar da kanka akaina haka ya isa ko kayan da ka siyo muna jiya ai sun wadatar."

Ya kalleta cikin 'yar dariya sannan yace

"Nidai banga wahala ba, kuma ba shawararki nike nema ba muga wayarki?"

Tayi shiru tana tunanin abunda zata fad'a saboda wayar ta ci wuta itama, sai a lokacin ya tuna yace 

"Au na tuna tuba nike bari inje yanzu indawo"       ya mik'e tsaye tareda gyara zaman babbar rigarshi ganin yanda duk ta damu yasa ya rik'o hannunta yace,  

"nifa bana son ganin damuwa akan fuskarki, me akayi kuma yanzunnan"

Munawwa hawaye cike da fuskarta  tace

"Kabar zancen wani lefe plss kana da sauran abubuwa a gabanka kaji dasu don ALLAH ka daina zancen lefen nan" ta k'are maganar a cikin   kuka ba shiri ya rungumota yace     

"Oh no kada ki damu ki d'auka komai kika ga  nayi daidai ne kuma ba wahala bace hakan shi yafi dacewa shi yasa nayi, kedai kawai kibini da addu'arki mai faranta mani zuciya "

Haka suka rabu zuciyoyinsu cike da tausayin juna da k'aunar juna, awa d'aya da fitarsu sai gasu sun dawo da lefe nik'i nik'i, don har sai da aka biyo su da wasu kaya saboda motarsu bata d'auka, ko gidan bai shiga ba yasa akayi ta jidar kayan ana shiga dasu,ya baiwa Yusuf kwalin dank'areriyar waya yace ya bata  sannan ya karb'i account d'in shi yayi masa transfer ta Million uku yace ya baiwa ALHAJI Baba yayiwa Munawwa kayan d'aki kuma yayi hidimar biki, sannan yayi sallama da Yusuf bayan shima ya bashi 100k ya ajiye hannunshi ko da akwai abunda za'a buk'ata, sannan  suka kama hanyar zuwa Abuja saboda shi Alhajin baya gida a lokacin.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*43*

Munara kam tun da ta tabbatar wutar ta kama gadan gadan ta sulale tayi gida, zuciyarta cike da jin dad'in abunda ta aikata, don ya rage mata kaso mafi rinjaye na bak'in cikin da aka k'unsa mata, zuwanta gidan keda wuya tayi arba da tsadaddar motarnan, aiko ba shiri k'irjinta ya buga dam! dam! Saboda tuno da tayi ashe Haidar ne ya siye ta, kai tsaye tayi hanyar shiga gidan me ta tuna kuma sai gata tadawo ta nufi k'ofar malam Isah da take abud'e, ba shiri ta saka k'afa ta shuri k'ofar da k'arfi a cikin zafin nama, sannan ta fara bambamin bala'in  cewa  dole ne yau sai ya bar gidan tunda shima yake munafuki, sai gashi har tayi ta gaji don kanta bata ji ko motsin Malam Isah ba, ganin renin hankalin da yake YI mata yana Ji yayi banza da ita, kai tsaye ta fad'a d'akin abun mamakinta komai yana nan inda yake sai dai ko inuwar Malam Isah babu, ta fito zuciyarta cike da tunanin ina yaje tayi cikin gida, tareda alwashin ya gama zama gidan tunda har aka had'a kai dashi aka rena masu wayo ita da Daddynta. 

Kasancewar ta hango d'akin Daddy a rufe yasa ta tabbatar Daddy baya gida, musamman da bata ga motarshi ba kafin ta shigo, tayi d'akinsu zuciyarta cike da bak'in cikin renin hankalin da SU Basarake suka yimata ita da Daddynta, waton daman ashe shiyasa Munawwa ta dage akan shi take so saboda tasan munafuccin da suka k'ulla, don kawai an maidasu shashashu ita da Daddynta a cikin ranta tace   

 _waton ma ashe duk abunda ake Mummy tana sane da ita aka had'a baki don ak'wace mani saurayi abaiwa Munawwa,duk da tsananin sonshi da aka san ina yi  saboda kawai daman cen don tafi sonta akaina,shiyasa suka had'a kai suka yaudareni suka ci amanata suka raba ni da Saurayina._ 

A fili ta furta    "aiko WALLAHI sai na zame masu k'arfen k'afa mai wuyar ja, sai na hana su rawar gaban hantsi, sai hana masu kwanciyar hankali da natsuwa a zaman aurensu. 

 Tana  k'are fad'ar maganganun ta fito ta nufi d'akin Mummy inda tana k'ok'arin shiga taji sautin Mummy lokacin da take waya d'a Munawwa, zuciyarta ta k'ara tabbatar da abunda take zargi saboda kalaman da taji Mummyn tana fad'a kamar haka,  

Ni dai WALLAHI Munawwara ina jin tsoron wannan rikici da zai had'a tsakaninki da 'yar uwarki, kinsan fa yanda take sonshi kuma shima yasan hakan, duk da idan aka yiwa abun duba da hankali za'a gano baya da laifi, saboda yayi amfani da sahihiyar hanya ne wajen zab'owa kanshi mace ta gari"

Cen TAYI shiru tana sauraren kwantar mata da hankalin da Munawwa take yi, cen tace 

To ALLAH ya baku zaman lafiya kuma yasa ace gara da akayi, amman dole ne sai kin k'ara hak'uri da 'yar.............. "     tayi shiru ba shiri saboda jin da take yi ana fad'in wuta wuta ayo agaji, sautin tartar a cikin kunnenta daga nan kuma tayi ta kiran sunan Munawwa cikin tashin hankali,  amman taji shiru cikin firgici ta bud'e d'akin sai gashi taci karo da Munara, da fuskarta take a had'e tamau ba shiri k'irjinta ya k'ara k'arfin bugawar da take, ba tareda ta kula da ita ba cikin tashin hankali ta fito d'akin ta nufi kofar fita gidan ko mayafi babu, Munara ta d'aga murya cikin tsananin  fushi tace

"Kar k'i manta da kashedin da Daddy yayi maki akan rasa igiyoyin aurenki, matuk'ar kika fita da nufin zuwa wurin Munawwa"

Cak!  Mummy ta tsaya zuciyarta cike da tashin hankali ta fashe da kuka,  Munara ta matso kusa da ita ta tab'e baki sannan tace, 

"Nayi maki tuni ne don kada kiyi wasa da igiyoyinki duk da ni ba sona kike yi ba, sannan Ki adana kukanki don WALLAHI yanzu kuka fara daga ke har ita tunda har na gano had'a kai kuka yi kuka raba NI da HAIDAR"

Nusy ta zauna ido cikin ido tace,     "A yanda kika bani labari Munawwa bazata tab'a k'in auren Haidar ba, musamman yanzu da tasan wanene shi don am telling you the truth koni na same shi da gudu zan aureshi, saboda haka ki nemi wata hanyar amman kam ba wannan ba."

"Wannan ba maganar hankali bace kiyi coolling temper d'inki muyi magana ta fahimta, idan har kina son in baki shawarar k'warai to kije ki sami Daddynki da kuka ki sanar dashi abunda yake faruwa, kice kina son duk yanda za'ayi ya hana auren da Munawwa a d'aura dake, ki tub'ure masa sosai ki rikice tamkar sark'a idan ta fad'i kada ki d'aga k'afa har sai ya samo maki mafita, tunda yana sonki nasan zaiyi komai domin ya faranta maki rai, kinga daga nan idan abun yak'i bi ta lalama ya yi fito na fito da yayanshi yace bai amince da auren ba, tunda shi keda 'yarshi dole ne yayan nashi yayi zuciya ya bar masa yarshi, shi kuma lokacin da ya fidda hannunshi sai shi Daddyn yayi son ranshi kawai ya aura maki Haidar d'inki, idan ma shima yak'i bi ta hanyar ruwan sanyi sai ayi masa basaja a aura masa ke a matsayin ita, kinga shikenan gurinki ya cika kin YI jifa d'aya ta doki tsuntsaye da yawa ."

Munara tayi shiru na d'an lokaci sannan tace,     "Ni dad'i na dake kanki yana ja sosai, bari inje yanzu don nasan ya dawo gida."

Cikin zafin nama ta fito sai da ta biya shagon sai da wayoyi ta siye sannan tayi gida zuciyarta cike da fatar cikar gurinta, zuwanta k'ofar gidan da Napep ya tuno mata da matarsu da ta baro School, duk da tana da natsuwa akan ba abunda zai sameta saboda scuritys d'in dake kula da shige da fice na d'aliban  makarantar, amman taso ace ta kwana hannunta saboda zuwan da zatayi makarantar a gobe, don ita sam ta tsani shiga Napep a rayuwarta, shiyasa suke ta samun matsala da Munawwa idan ta d'auki motar.

Mummy kam jikinta yana tsuma saboda tsananin tashin hankali tace,     "Ya ALLAH ka kare mani 'yata kada ka b'ari wani abun k'i ya cutar man da ita, Ubangiji ka zamo gatan Munawwara."    Kuka yaci k'arfinta ta durk'ushe anan tana ta kuka tana sambatun yiwa Munawwa addu'ar kariya gwanin ban tausayi, sai ga Daddy ya dawo tun daga compound d'in gidan yake jiyo kukanta, a ranshi yace

_"Yanzu kika fara don bazan fasa aurena ba tunda nayi niyya"_

Yana murmushin jin dad'in tuno Amaryarshi da yanzu haka daga wurinta ya fito , ya shiga gidan duk da yaga halin da ya tarar da ita amman ya zagayeta ya nufi d'akinshi, cikin zafin nama tabi bayanshi tana rok'onshi akan yaje ya dubo mata Munawwa gidan ALHAJI Baba gobara ta tashi a gidan, duk da maganar ta daki zuciyarshi amman k'anzil baice da ita ba,  yana shiga d'akinshi ya rufo d'akin da k'arfi yasa key don ma kada ta shigo ta takura masa,  Mummy ta fara yi masa magiya akan don ALLAH ya barta ita taje idan shi bazai je ba, amman yayi banza da ita saboda bak'ar zuciyar da ke damunshi, yaci alwashin ko duk gidan ya k'one bazai jajantawa ALHAJI Baba ba, tunda har ya goyawa Munawwa bayan ta auri Aliyu duk da yasan da baya yiwa kanshi maganin k'wandala balle yayiwa wani, a ranshi yace.      _"ai inda 'yarshi ce bazai goyi bayan ta auri matsaiyaci irin Aliyun ba, tunda duk 'ya'yanshi da ya aurar masu kud'i suke aure, sai shi don baya son ci gabanshi?."_

 Da wannan tunanin zuciyarshi ta k'ara hawa yayiwa Mummy luf  har ta gaji ta sauko, kai tsaye ta YI d'akinta ta dauro arwallah ta fara kai kukanta ga ALLAH madaukakin sarki, saboda ta tabbata a lokacin bata da kowa sai shi, aiko sai gashi Ubangiji ya sanya mata salama a zuciya ta natsu da cewa duk abunda yayi daidai ne, saboda bawa bai isa ya tsallakewa k'addararshi ba, da tunanin hakan ta sanyawa kanta hak'uri sai dai bakinta bai daina yiwa 'yarta da ahalin gidan gaba d'aya  addu'a ba. 

Ko da Munara ta dawo kai tsaye ta haye sama saboda Motar Daddyn da tagani ta tabbatar ya dawo, ta fara kwankwasa d'akin tana kiran sunanshi kai tsaye ya bud'e mata k'ofar saboda jin muryarta, tana ganinshi wani kuka ya zo mata ta durk'ushe k'asa tana kuka hadda su murje murje kamar wata k'aramar yarinya, hankali tashe ya fara tambayarta lafiya?

Cikin kuka mai tsanani tace,     "Daddy kayiman rai anci amanata an raba ni da HAIDAR."      cikin jin haushin HAIDAR d'in yace,      "Ba nace ki kwantar da hankalinki ni zan nemo maki shi ba?tunda nace kiyi hak'uri kiyi hak'urin kiga abunda zanyi, ki daina d'aga hankalinki a banza"     ta k'ara fashewa da kuka tace

"Ta ina zaka nemo shi bayan yana tare damu shine fa yayi basaja a matsayin d'an aikin da muka kora, suka had'a  kai da Munawwa da Mummy shine suka yaudareni Munawwa ta lik'e dole shi take so zata aura, saboda kawai gurinsu na su cutar dani su rabani da shi don ALLAH Daddy kada ka bari ya aureta WALLAHI idan ka bari ta aureshi sai nasha fiya fiya na mutu gaba d'aya kowa ma ya huta."

Ta k'are maganar a cikin wani sabon kuka mai tsanani, shikam daskarewa jininshi yayi na d'an lokaci duk da baisan ko wanene HAIDAR d'in ba amman baiji dad'in abunda suka yi masa shi da shalelenshi ba, gaba d'aya ya rud'e ya rasa ma abunda zai fad'a, yana neman abun cewa ne ta yunk'ura ta tashi cikin kuka tace      "Wallahi fiya fiya zansha in kashe kaina tunda na gano kowa ma bai sona"     Cikin rud'ewa da firgici daddyn ya ri'kota yana cewa 

"Kwantar da hankalinki kiyi man bayani yadda zan gane, kina nufin shi HAIDAR d'in ne ya zo gidannan a matsayin d'an aiki?"    Munara ta d'aga kai tareda ruhsewa da sabon kuka tace,      "Shine WALLAHI gashi kuma yanzu har ya kawo wa Munawwa lefe gidan ALHAJI Baba, kuma da gaske nan da sati mai zuwa za'ayi auren, amman WALLAHI nikam kafin lokacin ma na dad'e lahira tunda ba'a son ganin farin cikina."

Daddy ya jawo hannunta yace,   

"Zo muje ayi komi gabanki yanzu zan kira Alhajin in sanar dashi ban amince ya d'aura aurensu ba."

Cikin jin dad'i ta biyo bayanshi suka zauna Daddy ya fara kiran Alhajin tana ta ringing bai d'auka ba, yayi kira yakai biyar ba'a d'auka ba, saboda lokacin da ya fara kiran nashi ana cikin hidimar gyaran gidan da Basarake ya basu, gashi zuciyar Alhajin cike take da jin zafin labarin da ya jiya na cewa Munara ce ta saka wutar ta k'one lefen 'yar uwarta, yaji zafin sakacin da Daddyn yayi har Munara ta kangare har haka da yawa, shiyasa da yayita kiranshi yak'i d'agawa k'arshe ma ya kashe wayar gaba d'aya. 

Daddy kam yana ganin ya kashe wayar ya ce da Munara tazo su tafi gidan, duk da gabanta ya fad'i amman ta mazge ta bishi suka nufi gidan, suna tafe tana k'ara yimasa fanfo har suka je gidan abun mamaki suka tarar da get d'in gidan rufe da k'aton  kwad'o, bai hak'ura ba ya nufi gidan wani makocin Alhajin Munara da kanta ta shiga gidan tayi sallama dashi ya fito, bayan sun gaisa da Daddyn ne yake tambayarshi ina mutanen gidan Alhaji Baban yaga gidan k'argame da kwad'o, kai tsaye mutumin ya sanar dashi surukinshi ne ya saya masa gida shine suka koma, shima gobe yake so yaje yaga gidan tareda iyalanshi suyi masu murna, cikin tsananin rud'ewa Daddy yace,     "Surukina ko nashi?." 

Mutumin yayi 'yar dariya yace,     "Eh to ai ba'a raba d'aya biyu amman tunda kana buk'atar gwari gwari to sukinka wanda zai auri 'yarka tagwainiyar wannan yarinyar"  ya k'are maganar tareda nuna Munara, ba shiri Munara ta fad'i k'asa tareda fashewa da kuka tace,      "Kaji ko Daddy WALLAHI ALLAH bazan yarda ba sai dai in baka son rayuwata."

D/Auta Novel

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI 🔥*

*44*

Ba shiri Daddy da mutumin suka yo kanta Daddy ya ri'kota tareda yin sallama da mutumin ya sakata mota, har  suka bar k'ofar gidan mutumin bai daina mamakinsu ba daga shi Daddyn har 'yar tashi, suna tafe yana bata baki har suka isa gida, har tara na dare suna falo yana lallashinta akan tayi hak'uri yace da ita "gobe da safe har gidan zamu je da ke ayi wadda za'a ayi tunda har cin amanar ma ba iya Munawwara da Mummynku bane har da shi Alhajin k'aton banza dashi"

Dak'yar ya shawo kanta tareda yimata alk'awalin auren HAIDAR d'in sannan ta sauko,  ya kaita har d'akinsu ta kwanta har sai da bacci ya d'auketa sannan hankalinshi ya kwanta ya fito ya nufi nashi d'akin, zuciyarshi cike da sak'e sak'en rashin mutuncin da zai yiwa Alhajin, ya aurawa Munara saurayinta ko ta halin k'ak'a a ranshi yace,      _"Wannan ai cin amana ne"_

Yana cikin tunanin sake kiran Alhajin ne Amaryarshi tayi kiranshi, cike da farin ciki da zumudi ya jira sai da ta tsinke sannan shi yabi da nashi kiran, suka luntsume sunata firarsu ta SOYAYYA ya mance shaf da kiran Alhajin har dare ya lula, dole bayan sun k'are wayar ya hak'ura da safe ya kirashi domin yaji unguwar da suka koma. 

Tun da sassafe Munara ta matsa suje yace ta jira sai ya dawo daga wani wuri sai ya d'auketa suje, amman tayi hak'uri taje makaranta k'arfe biyu zuwa uku  sai su tafi, aiko da murnarta ta shirya ta fita makaranta Mummy dai iyakacinta ido, duk da agabanta suke rigidimarsu amman ci kanka bata ce dasu ba, don shi kanshi Daddyn tun jiyan bata sake bi takanshi ba, shiyasa yaji haushi ko break baiyi gidan ba ya fice, ta bisu da addu'ar shiriya wadda ita kad'aice ta rage tsakaninta da su, sai ko gashi Munawwara sak'on wayar da Basarake yace abata tana zuwa hannunta,ta had'a komi kasancewar tasan Numbar Mummyn a kai ta danna mata kira, lokacin ta k'are sallah'r walha kenan tana zaune saman carpet tana lazimi sai ga kiran Munawwa ya shigo wayarta, cikin sanyin jiki ta jawota saman gadon tayi sallama, aiko sai ga muryar d'iyarta tartar cikin wayar cike da d'oki tace,      "Mummy Munawwara ce"

A zabure ta tashi tsaye ta zauna kan gadon zuciyarta cike da farin ciki tace,   "Munawwara ya wutar ba abunda ya sameki ko?"    Ta k'are maganar a cikin kuka Munawwa tayi saurin  cewa,   
"Ki kwantar da hankalinki Mummy NI ba abunda ya sameni kowa ma na gidan duk lafiya lau muke, kayan da aka kawo ne kawai suka k'one sai wasu sassa na gidan da wuta taci yanzu haka ma bamu gidan"

Cikin jin dad'i Mummy tace     "ALHMDLLH  MASHA  ALLAH, kuna ina yanzu?"    Munawwa cikin jin nauyi tace,     "ALIYU ne ya sayawa ALHAJI Baba wani sabon gida k'ato baki ganshi ba Mummy yayi kyau WALLAHI."    Cike da murna Mummyn tace

"ALLAH ya saka masa da alkhairi wace unguwa kuke?"

Munawwa ta fad'a mata sannan tace, 

"Mummy yaushe zaki zo?"    Ta k'are maganar cikin zubowar wasu hawaye,   jin sautin muryarta yasa ta karfafa tata zuciyar ta shanye kukanta tace,   

"Zan zo kwanannan idan hajiya Hindu tana kusa ki bani ita muyi magana."   Munawa ta fito sashensu tana tafe suna wayar har ta isa d'akin Hajiya Hindun, kai tsaye ta mik'a mata wayar ta fito Zulaiha ta janyota suka fito, inda  sauran 'yanmatan gidan suke k'ofar gidan suna jiranta ta fito suje su kaita wajen gyaran jikin gidan wata Hajiya da Hajiya Hindun tace Yusuf ya kaita, su kuma su zulaiha su wuce wurin rabon invitation d'in da aka buga cikin k'ank'anin lokaci, tana zuwa suka fad'a motar yusuf ya jasu suka tafi. 

Hajiya hindu kam sunyi fira sosai da Mummy ta k'ara bata hak'uri tareda kwantar mata da hankali, saboda Mummyn da taji auren Munawwa kwana uku masu zuwa fashewa da kuka tayi, saboda ganin ita da 'yarta amman batada masaniya akan komai nata sai abunda aka fad'a mata, Hajiya hindun duk da ALHAJI yace taja bakinta tayi shiru,  saboda tausayin Mummyn da taji  yasa ta fayyace mata ko  d'an  waye Basarake, duk da ita kanta Munawwa har lokacin batasan ainahin wanene baban Basaraken ba, ita dai tasan suna da kud'i saboda ganin abotarshi da Nuradden, don taji bayanin da Daddyn yayi ranar da ya fara zuwa gidansu, shi bai fad'a mata ba itama bata matsawa kanta da dole sai ta sani ba. 

Mummy taji dad'in jin wannan kyakkyawan labari, saboda tasan d'iyarta bazata wahala ba tunda mijin da zata aura yana sonta gashi kuma zata Ji dad'in zaman auren ba takura, ta k'ara girmama girman martabar ubangiji  saboda shine yayiwa Munawwa wannan baiwa ya dubi kyakkyawar zuciyarta ya bata miji d'aya tamkar da dubu, saboda taga yanda ya shiga damuwa lokacin da take cikin ciyo asibiti, kuma yanayin k'irar da ALLAH yayi masa da kamalarshi TAYI na'am sosai da ya zama mjin 'yarta Munawwara, cike da murna Hajiya Hindu ta sanar da ita sabon lefen da ya kawo, sannan tayi ta warware mata irin alkhairin da yayi masu har kud'in da ya bada ayi mata kayan d'aki, sunyi fira sosai saboda akwai isashen credit a wayar wanda idan za'a yini akwana ana waya bazai k'are ba, suna cikin firar ne kiran basaraken ya shigo wayar duk da batasan sunan da aka RUBUTA ba, amman zuciyarta ta bata angonne, nan tayiwa Mummy bayani suka yi sallama ta fito neman Munawwarar, tana ta Alla alla akan kada ace har sun tafi ta nufi d'akinsu ba kowa, ko'ina ta lek'a ba su aiko ganin gidan duk ba 'yanmatan yasa ta tabbatar sun tafi, ga wayar sai b'urari take yi da kira ya katse ya sake shigowa dole ta d'auka tace batanan. 

 Munawwa kam bata tuno da wayar ba har sai da suka kai k'ofar gidan, aiko tana k'ok'arin yin magana sai ga kiran Basaraken ya shigo wayar Yusuf, yace dashi idan suna tareda Munawwa ya bata wayar, kai tsaye ya mik'a mata wayar bayan ta fito motar cikin sanyin murya tace,     "Ranka ya dad'e ya hanya?"   A cen bangarenshi kuwa suna kan hanyar ne ya ji yana son jin muryarta ya kira Nuradden sai tsarguwarshi yake, musamman da bata d'auka ba shiyasa ya nace sai yaji muryarta, aiko yana jin muryarta ta shiga  dodon kunnenshi ya lumshe ido yace, 

"Ranki ya dad'e 'yar lab'iyatah dafatar dai kina wurin d'aya daga cikin aikin da na saka ki?"   tana 'yar dariya tace,     "Eh "   da sauri yace     "Eh me nifa bana son haka ki saki jiki kiyi magana da mijinki hajiya"   ta rufe baki tana 'yar dariya tace,       "Ni dai ya hanya ko har kun isa?"    

Shima ya dara baki a washe yace,      "Muna kai dai kiyiwa angonki addu'a ALLAH ya kaishi gida lafiya ba tareda sonki ya zautashi ba"       ta k'ara rufe baki cikin jin nauyinshi tace,     "Hakan ma bazata faru ba ALLAH dai ya tsare man kai gabanka da bayanka, kuma ya kai ku gida lafiya."

Kit ta kashe wayar ta mik'awa Yusuf tace don ALLAH idan ka kaisu ka karboman way......... "  kafin ta kai k'arshe kiran ya sake shigowa Yusuf ya mik'o mata ta noke tayi masa alama da hannu akan ya d'aga  dole ya d'auka, Basarake yana jin muryar Yusuf yace, 

"Don ALLAH kaje gida ka  d'auko mata  wayarta ta manta ta acen"  yana d'an murmushi yana kallonta yace,     "OK to yayi angama angon Munawwa"    sannan suka yi sallama yayi masu fatar ALLAH ya tsare hanya, Su Zulaiha suka rakata dukansu cikin  gidan Hajiyar sannan Yusuf ya ja motar ya koma gidan. 

Zuwanshi yayi daidai da ganin Motar Daddynsu Munara a k'ofar gidan, wanda suma saukarsu kenan suna tantamar nanne ko wani gidan ne?, saboda har lokacin Daddy bai sami Alhajin ya d'auki wayartashi ba, shiyasa Munara tace yazo ta kawo shi wannan  gidan  koda nan ne HAIDAR d'in  ya basu, saboda kasancewarta yar gari mai shiga kusufa kusufa batamance unguwar da gidan ba, aiko sai gashi zuwan Yusuf Ya tabbatar masu da nanne gidan. 

Daddyn ya fito Munarar ma ta fito tana jifar Yusuf da wata k'atuwar harara, aranta tana ji kamar ta shak'e shi har sai ya d'auke sheda saboda tsananin tsanar da tayi masa. 

Yusuf ya fito cikin girmamawa ya nufi Daddyn cike da ladabi ya gayar dashi, Daddy kam kai a sama ya amsa tareda had'e fuska yace,     "Alhajin yana ciki"    yusuf yakai dubanshi cikin get d'in yace,   "Ga motarshi cen da alama ya dawo zo muje ciki sai in sanar dashi."     Daddy ya kalli k'aton kantamemen gidan upstairs nagani na fad'a, wai ace duk girmanshi wanda zai auri Munawwarar ne ya yi masa kyautarshi, ai dole ma ne ya dawo ga babynshi yayi ta cin wannan arzik'in shima adama dashi. 

Yusuf ganin yanayin Daddyn yasa ya shige gidan ya sanarwa da Alhajin zuwanshi, kasancewar shima jiraye yake dashi musamman da yaji har da 'yar iskar 'yarshi yasa ya k'ara had'e fuska tamau ya fito tun daga nesa da taga fuskarshi hantar cikinta ta kad'a, amman saboda zamanta mara kunya ko gaisuwa a tsaye k'ik'am tayi masa ba d'an rusunawa, Alhaji Baba kam ko kallo bata ishe shi ba ganin hakan yasa tasha jinin jikinta. 

ALHAJI Baba yana Zuwa ya mik'awa Daddy hannu, Daddy ya mazge tareda fad'ar,       "Ba gaisuwa ce ta kawoni ba, nazo ne akan zancen auren Munawwara, to ban amince da ayi auren ba idan har ba Munara ce zai aura ba." 

ALHAJI Baba ya gyara tsayuwarshi sannan yace,      "kayi kad'an kasani magana biyu, aure kam idan kaga ba'a d'aura shi ba to tabbata ka turo 'yarka ta  k'ara saka muna fetur ta k'one gidan kaf duk mun mutu daga ni har ita Munawwarar"

Cikin rashin fahimta Daddyn yace,     "ka ko san waye wanda ka bawa auren Munawwara?"   kai tsaye yace     "Tabbas nasani Ko ba d'an gidan ALHAJI DIKKO Mai GWAL ba nasani shine ma ya bani wannan gidan bayan 'yar sonka ta k'one wancen gidan, da bak'in nufinta na son k'one lefen 'yar uwarta"

Ya k'are maganar tareda maka mata wata k'atuwar harara mai cike da kashedi, Daddy kam jin sunan Dikko mai Gwal yasa shi gaba d'aya ya rud'e, cikin tantama yace    "Kana nufin ALIYU D'a d'aya tilo da dikko mai gwal ya haifa shine zai auri Munawwara?" 

ALHAJI ya k'ara had'e fuska yace      "tabbas shine Sadaki da lefenshi duk ya kawo har kud'in da za'a yimata kayan d'aki duka ya bada, saboda haka kasani aure ba fashi don ni bana magana biyu "

Cikin rud'ewa Daddy yace,       "To ai Munawwra ce yake so zai aura ba Munara ba"

Cikin ko in kula Alhajin yace,      "Na fika sanin hakan shiyasa ma nike  k'ok'arin cikawa masoya burinsu"    

Munara ta fashe da kuka tareda zuwa ta ri'ko hannun Daddyn tace,        "ka dawo cikin  hankalinka Daddy nifa muka fara soyayya dashi daga baya suka had'a baki suka ci amanata shi da ita."

Sai a lokacin Daddyn ya zabura yace 

" Ai ke nike nufi ba ita ba saboda haka kar ka manta cewa ni na haifi Munawwara ba kai ba,  saboda haka ban lamunta da auren ba matuk'ar ba Munara ce zai aura ba don cin amana ba abune mai kyau ba"       

Alhajin cikin tsananin b'acin rai da kalaman Daddyn  yace,    "idan har  na d'aura auren to ka kaini kotu ina amsa maka kira."       yana k'are fad'ar maganar ya juya da nufin ya koma cikin gidan,  Daddy ya d'aga murya yace       "hanya mafi sauk'i a had'a auren da baby idan ba haka ba WALLAHI ban yafe hak'k'ina na haihuwarta da nayi ba,  kuma ka sani sai naja hak'k'ina matuk'ar kayi gangancin d'aura  mata aure"

Alhajin ko juyowa bai yi ba yaci gaba d'a tafiyarshi har ya shige gidan, Munara ta k'ara fashewa da Kuka tace, 

"Daddy ka dawo da Munawwa gida yafi da ace ta zauna wannan gidan, saboda zamanta a ciki risk ne ga rayuwata, don WALLAHI bazai fasa k'udurinshi ba tunda har kaga yak'i amincewa ta ruwan sanyi, ka shiga gidan kawai ka fito da ita ta koma gida yafi, idan yak'i badata ka d'ebo masa 'yan sanda dole ya fito da ita ko baya so."

Daddy yayi shiru na minti biyu yana k'ok'arin yin magana sai ga Yusuf ya fito da sauri zai shiga mota, Munawwa tayi wurinshi cikin zafin nama ta ciyo kwalarshi tace,     "Bak'in Munafuki ina Munawwa take?"   cikin zafin nama da jin zafin abunda ya jiyo tana fad'awa Daddynta akan wai a d'ebowa Alhajinshi 'yan sanda, ya saka hannu ya mazga mata mari sannan ya k'wace rik'on da tayi masa, yana k'ok'arin bud'e murfin motar ya fita ne ta shak'o wuyanshi ta baya yana d'agowa yayita fakarta kokawa ta kacame tsakaninsu, saboda itama k'arfin hali ne da ita na bala'i shiyasa bata shayin ya idan ya kai mata duka itama  ta kai masa dambe suke YI sosai sai jibgar juna suke yi. 

 Daddy kam baimasan me ake YI ba saboda tunanin abunda ya dameshi wai ace d'an Dikko mai GWAL ne zai auri Munawwa,  ya shagala sosai da kallon kantamemen k'aton gidan ace duk mallakin Alhajin ne kuma ta hanyar shi, zuciyarshi ta fara canki in canka akan abunda ya dace yayi, ya lula sosai cikin zurfin tunani, aikuwa sai gashi yaji  Munara ta k'wallah wata k'ara ba shiri  hankalinshi ya dawo daga dogon nazarin da yake yi, saboda Yusuf ya saki k'wanji ya jibgi shegiya sosai sannan yayi sa'ar zubar da ita k'asa ta fad'i kan wani dutse shi yayi sanadin k'arar da ta fasa, saboda hannunta da ta fad'awa yace kutttt!, aiko kafin Daddy ya k'araso har Yusuf ya fad'a motarshi ya bata wuta tareda bad'a masu k'ura, a d'ari yayo kanta ya d'agota yana tambayar meye mafari, tana sharb'ar kuka take sanar dashi Yusuf ne ya daketa bata YI masa komi ba, ta k'are maganar tareda cewa,   "Hannuna Daddy WALLAHI ya karya mani hannu wayyo ALLAH hannuna"  a rud'e ya kinkimeta ya sakata mota sai kuka take da k'arfi yaja motar ya bar k'ofar gidan, ALHAJI Baba dake tsaye rik'e da k'arfen  sama yana kallon duk abunda ke wakana, yayi murmushi mai sauti sannan ya bar wurin yayi ciki zuciyarshi cike da tunanin rashin hankalin d'an uwan nashi, saboda biyewa marar kunyar 'yarshi da yake yi, yaji dad'i sosai da Yusuf ya saki jiki ya mammazgeta don daga lokacin zata gano k'arfin Namiji daban yake da na mace yaci alwashin duk wani rashin mutuncin da Daddy zai yi bazai fasa aurawa Basarake Munawwara ba.

Yusuf kam yana tafe yana tunanin abunda ya faru tsakaninshi da Munara, shi kanshi sai da ya jinjina k'arfin hali irin nata don in banda fad'uwar da tayi da ALLAH kad'ai yasan iya tashin hankalin da za'a yi, shiyasa yayi saurin barin k'ofar gidan don baya son Daddy yaga yayi masa rashin kunya, har yaje gidan ya mik'awa Munawwra wayar ya kwaso su Zulaiha tunanin bai bar zuciyarshi ba. 

Munawwa kam hankali kwance aka fara gyara mata jiki tsakanin dilka da shaye shayen abubuwa har ma da turaren tsugunno, har dare abunda ake YI kenan Hajiya Hindu ma ta kira Hajiyar akan tayi kwanciyarta anan har da safe,  idan su Zulaiha zasu je kaita wajen gyaran kai sai a biyo a d'auketa domin kawai aji dad'in gyarata cikin natsuwa, aiko hajiyar ta saki jiki duk wani abun amfani da tasani tsakanin laushin fata da k'ara ciccikowar Dukiyar fulaninta, ta b'angaren kaya masu inganci da nagarta wad'anda babu cuta babu cutar suwa a cikinsu, tareda gyaran shi kanshi mai martaba haQ sai da ta tabbatar ko iya hakan aka tsaya komi zai  zam zam.
