Munara kam sai kuka takeyi har Daddy ya kai gidan mai gyara koda aka duba akace gocewa ce tayi ga d'an gwiwar hannu, an gyarata tsaf sannan suka juyo gida aranta sai k'ara tsinewa Yusuf take, shiko Daddy sai aikin lallashi yake yi tareda yimata alk'awalin itace zata auri HAIDAR ta kwantar da hankalinta. 

Koda suka je gida suka tarar da Mummy sai walwalarta take YI don har da kunna kallo tayi,  tayi zaune falo abinta tana cin abincinta hankali kwance,  wanda rabon da taci abinci irin haka har ta manta saboda tashin hankali, cike da mamakinta suka k'araso falon Daddy rik'e da Munara har ta zauna, sannan yace da ita sannu tareda k'ara tambayarta shin da gaske ne HAIDAR d'in yaron gidan Dikkon ne, ta tabbatar masa da cewa ai shine ma ya turo Nuradden a matsayin shi, don ko Motar da aka ce Nuradden ya siyewa Munawwa shine ya siya, sannan K'asar wajen dama ake ganin Nuradden ne ya d'auki nauyin kaita, duk shine yayi masu basaja kuma ta tabbatar masa da lalle shine don ta had'u dashi cen indiyan, lokacin da Munawwa take jinya, Daddy ba shiri yace      "Taya hakan takance duk ba tareda na gano komi ba, lalle maganarki gaskiya ce don dashi Nuradden  ya je asibitin kafin a fitar da ita indiya, tabbas sai ma yanzu na fahimci kamar yafi Nuradden damuwa a wancen lokacin, waton ni zasu yiwa yawo da hankali?don kawai suna son su cutar da ke, amman kiyi hak'uri ki bar komi a hannuna,  duk da sunyi muna laifi bazanyi  k'asa a Gwiwa ba wajen zuwa har in da Yake domin in rok'eshi alfarmar ya aure ki, idan har yak'i ta ruwan sanyi to niko sai dai ayi haihuwar guzuma d'a kwance uwa kwance, sai dai daga ke har ita kowa ma ya rasa tunda shi baya da imani, haka kawai ya zab'i ya rusa man farin ciki saboda kawai yaga suna da kud'i.  

Ya d'aga waya ya kira Nuradden bayan sun gaisa ne yake cewa dashi, ALIYU yake so ya had'a shi dashi lokacin ma duk suna kan hanyarsu, Nuradden yana 'yar dariya ya mik'awa Basarake wayar, tun daga yanayin yanda yaga Nuradden yana dariya ne ya gano akwai wata a k'asa kai tsaye ya karb'i wayar, suka gaisa sama sama sannan yace dashi,      "Haidar ko?, ina son ganinka akwai maganar da zamu yi kana ina inzo in sameka?"

Basarake ya zama serious sannan yace 

"Ina kan hanyar zuwa Abuja saura k'iris da na juyo, amman idan ba damuwa zaka iya bari sai ranar Jumu'a zan dawo Kaduna sai muyi maganar."

 Daddy cikin rawar jiki yace,      "Magana ce ta sauri gobe zan zo Abujar in sameka da kaina "

Yace    "OK ba damuwa Daddy your welcome"

Sannan ya kashe wayar yana 'yar dariya yace,     "Duk da bansani ba amman Zuciyata ta bani akan Munara ne zai yi wannan tattaki, don kada yaga na rena shi da na sanar dashi gaskiya ba sai yayi wahalar zuwan ba"

Nuradden yana dariya yace,    "Kada ka katse masa hanzari ka jira kawai kaji k'orafinshi, idan har zaka iya yimasa alfarmar shikenan sai kayi jahadi."

Basarake ya maka masa  wata k'atuwar harara sannan yace,    "Jahado ai WALLAHI ko ma me zai yi ni bazan canza ra'ayina ba, haka kawai ina tsaka da farin ciki a katse man jin dad'i cab, WALLAHI bada ni ba nikam cen gasu gada shi da babyn tashi na yafe duka akai kasuwa."

"Ki hak'ura dashi kawai ki fidda wani mijin ki aura, saboda ya tabbatar man idan dai dole sai ya dawo gunki to gara ya hak'ura daku duka gaba d'aya, don haka ina baki shawara ki share shi kawai, ki d'auka cewa ma tamkar baki tab'a saninshi ba a rayuwa."     yana k'are maganar ya mik'e tsaye ya k'ara kai kallonshi ga Mummy, saboda har ga ALLAH kwalliyar da tayi ta burgeshi sosai, sai dai hayakin dake kanshi ne ya hana shi nuna hakan akan fuskarshi, ya d'auke kanshi ya fara hawa step kenan ya jiyo muryar Munara tana cewa,     " Daddy yanzu kana nufin  shikenan hadda ita Munawwarar ya fasa auren nata?"    ba tareda ya juyo ba yace

Daddy ya biyota jikinshi yana rawa ya tsaya bakin k'ofar  cikin tashin hankali ya fara kiran sunanta,  yana bata hak'uri akan tayi hak'uri insha ALLAHU zai shawo kan matsalar, har yayi ta sambatunshi ya gaji MuMmy tana kallonshi ta gefen ido, ta  gabanta ya wuce fuuu ya haye sama zuciyarshi cike da jin tsoron furucin Munarar.

"ALLAH ya baku ladar abunda kukayi ya saka maku da gidan Aljannah." Mummy Zuciyarta fes cike da jin dad'i tace, 

"Ameeñ"   sannan suka yi sallama hankalinta ya kwanta, daga  tashin hankalin da kalaman daddyn suka sakata, kai tsaye damuwart ta gushe sai jinta take kamar tayi tsuntsuwa taje taga gidan, amman taci alwashin gobe zata tambayi Daddy fita taje a d'aura auren 'yarta da ita, ta ganta taji dad'i duk da KO a hakan jin take yi kamar suna ganin juna saboda kullum suna mak'ale a waya. 

**

Koda safiya ta waye tun da sassafe ALIYU da gayyar mutanenshi  tareda su Hajiya hindu suka yo tsinke garin kaduna, koda sha biyu da rabi tayi suna cikin garin, kai tsaye suka zarce babban masallacin da za'a yi d'aurin auren, da nufin da an k'are sallar jumu'a sai a d'aura auren, da k'arfe biyar a shiga wata gawurtacciyar walima. 

Munawwa kam tun da safe su Zulaiha suka jata zuwa gidan k'unshi, ita aka  fara yiwa lallen abu ga farar mace da bak'in lalle da ja, ya fito rad'am ga k'afarta masha ALLAH  gwanin burgewa, ana k'are yiwa Zulaiha suka yo gida suka baro sauran yanmatan acen , saboda kwalliyar da za'ayiwa Munawwa kafin 'yan d'aurin auren su dawo, aiko suna zuwa Munawwa ta sullo wanka jikinta sai sulbi yake saboda gyaran da tasha wurin hajiyar Gyara,  ba b'ata lokaci aka fara tsantsara mata kwalliya, kafin kace me fuskarta ta k'ara haskawa kyawonta ya k'ara  fitowa, bayan ank'are kwalliyar ne ta shirya cikin wani kafurin less mai bak'i da ja, aka yi mata nad'in jan dankwali  d'aurin ya zauna rad'am ga jikinta, sannan aka bita da wasu sihirtaccin turaruka masu kwantar da zuciya, ta saka talkami jajaye tareda 'yar post d'insu ja itama, wuyanta da kunnenta an kawatasu da wata kafurar sark'a wadda a duba d'aya zaka gano mai tsada ce, ga agogon gold bak'i da ta saka a tsintsiyar hannunta wanda zoben gold d'in da Basarake ya saka mata ya fito rad'am a hannunta,  Amarya ta fito sharrr da ita gwanin sha'awa kamar ka saceta ka b'oye saboda tsabar haduwar da tayi, mutane sai d'aukarta photuna suke suna kuwwa idan ta bada wani style.

Zulaiha tayi using da Wayar Munawwa ta fara d'aukarta pic dasu, sannan suka sakata rawar dole bayan sun saka music tana d'an takawa a hankali tana rausaya Zulaiha tana videoting, Zulaiha ta fakaici idon Munawwa ta shiga whatsapp ta turawa Basarake, sai da ta tabbatar sun shiga sannan ta kashe datar ta tura masa text kamar haka, 

_Ka bud'a whatsapp ka ga sak'on da na turo maka_ 

Basarake yana tsaka da mutane bayan an k'are sallar Jumu'a ana fara shirye shiryen d'aurin auren ne sak'on ya shigo wayarshi, har yaso kar ya duba amman saboda tsantsar farin cikin da yake ciki yasa ya fiddo wayar ya duba, ganin sak'on  Amaryarshi ne yasa yayi saurin bud'awa, yana ganin abunda tace,     yayi murmushi ya mayarda wayar aljihu don yafison sai ya sami natsuwa sannan ya duba, ya mayar da hankalinshi wurin d'aurin auren da aka fara, inda liman ya fara gabatar da Waliyyin amarya ALHAJI Baba da waliyyin ango ALHAJI Mustapha mai Gwal tareda anbatar sadakin Munawwa, bayan ya k'are shaidawa mutane ya fara addu'ar daurin auren ALIYU  da amaryarshi MUNAWWARA, tareda tabbatarwa mutane cewa daga yau ta zama matarshi jama'a su sheda, daganan kowa ya fara sanyawa auren albarka tareda yi masu addu'ar zaman lafiya da zuri'a dayyiba, daga nan taron ya fara watsewa bak'in da suka zo daga nesa suka nufi inda aka tanadar masu, waton wani kayataccen hotel d'in da zasu je suci abinci suhuta sannan masu, komawa Abuja su kama hanya masu kwana kuma garin suyi kwanciyarsu, shikam ango bai nufi hotel d'in ba gidan Amaryarshi ya nufa, kaitsaye inda ALHAJI Baba yasa aka saukeshi wani d'aki na musamman shi da Abokanshi da suka yo masa rakiya, duk da shi ba'a son ranshi ne suka biyo shi ba don baya son suyi ta kalle masa mata a banza, aiko zamansu keda wuya yayi tsam ya fito ya yafito Yusuf yace dashi Munawwa ta sameshi a d'akinshi, Yusuf yana dariya yayi cikin gidan inda ya tarar da gida cike fam da k'awayensu Zulaiha da abokan arzik'inta na School da suka zo taya ta murna, dak'yar ya ga Zulaiha yace ta nemo masa Munawwa ga Angonta yana son ganinta, Zulaiha cike da zumud'i tayi d'akinsu inda Munawwa take ta jawo hannunta ba tareda tayi mata bayanin komi ba har suka kai k'ofar d'akin Yusuf, cike da mamaki Munawwa tace da ita "waike lafiyarki kuwa?"

Zulaiha tana dariya cike da jin dad'i tace

"Bak'o kika yi ki shiga yana ciki zaku gaisa." 

Ba shiri gabanta ya fad'i don jikinta ya bata Aliyunta ne, cikin  sanyin jiki ta makawa Zulaiha harara sannan ta rik'o hannunta tace,     "Zo muje don ALLAH WALLAHI kunya nike ji"   dak'yar ta amince suka shiga d'akin tare bakinsu d'auke da sallama, Zulaiha tana ganin ta kaita ta d'akin ta k'wace hannunta ta fito  tana yimata dariyar mugunta. 

Basarake kam shigarshi d'akin yasa ya tuno da sak'on da tace ta turo masa, a gaggauce ya bud'a data ya shiga whatsapp, sai gashi ba shiri ya tashi daga ginciren da yake yi tareda k'ara bud'a idanuwansh yana kallon pic d'in da Video'n da take ta rawa abunta, cikin d'an lokaci yanayinshi ya canza gaba d'aya  jikinshi yayi sanyi, saboda wata kasala da ta kawo masa ziyarar bazata, yayin da tsikar jjikinshi ta tashi yarrrr,  yana cikin yanayin ne sai gasu sun shigo ALLAH ya taimakeshi babbar riga ce dashi da ba makawa dole ne sai sun gano halin da yake ciki,  ganin Zulaiha ta fita ne yasa ya Mik'e tsaye yaje har gabanta ya tsaya yana k'are mata kallo tun daga kai har k'afafu, sannan ya duk'o yakai kallonshi ga idanuwanta saboda duk'ar da fuskarta da tayi bata son su had'a ido, aiko suna had'a idon tayi saurin rufe fuskarta da tafukan hannayenta saboda tsananin kunyarshi da take ji, Basarake kam ganin zanen lallenta da ya fito rad'am ya k'ara dulmiya shi cikin shauk'in kaunarta, shiyasa ba tareda ta tantance ba sai dai kawai taji ya rungumota duka ga jikinshi ya kankameta, ba shiri ya sauke k'atuwar ajiyar zuciya, ita kam jin tudun dake gabanshi ne yasa ta k'ara rud'ewa tareda jin wata matsananciyar kunyar abunda taji.

 *🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*47*

Ta fara k'ok'arin zamewa daga jikinshi shiko ya k'ara kankameta, cikin wata kasalalliyar murya yace,       "Ki tausayawa Aliyunki"    ya k'are maganar tareda d'ago kanta ya fara kissing d'inta a rikice, tareda damk'o dukiyar fulaninta da suka yi cika k'irjinta fam, sai murzasu yake cikin zafin nama da k'warewa, saboda gaba d'aya a rikice yake ya rud'e ya manta ko ina ne suke, hankalinshi bai dawo ba har sai da yaji ta fara kuka, sannan ya dakatar da abunda yake yi idanuwa jajir dasu ya d'ago fuskarta da hannu biyu sannan yace,    "Kukan Na menene nifa bana son ana shiga hak'k'ina."    Munawwa ta sulale daga jikinshi ta zame ta zauna kan kujera saboda k'afafuwanta da take jin sunyi mata nauyi basu iya d'aukar gangar jikinta, saboda yanayin da ta tsinci kanta wanda tunda take bata tab'a tsintar kanta a ciki ba, gaba d'aya ya saka jikinta sai tsuma yakeyi tsikarta gaba d'aya ta Mik'e, abu na biyu kuma taji tsoron wani ya gansu a wannan yanayin, shi kam jiki ba kuzari ya janyo k'afafuwanshi ya zo ya zauna kusa da ita, tareda janyota Jikinshi da muryarshi da ta dusashe ciki ciki yace, 

"Ya za'ayi a bani matata yau don WALLAHI idan ban sameki ba yau akwai matsala."     Munawwa gabanta ya fad'i saboda idanuwansh da muryarshi da suka sauya gaba d'aya,    cikin sanyin jiki tace   "Da yau da gobe ai duk d'aya ne kayi hak'urin sauran watanni ballantana kwana d'aya rak!"

Ya k'urawa d'an k'aramin bakinta kallo, sannan ya jawo hannunta ya d'ora saman jarumarshi da har lokacin  take a mik'e sambal sannan ya marairece yace, 

"Kinji yanayin da Aliyunki yake a ciki saboda haka gaskiya bazan iya jurar zuwa gobe ba, idan ma zan amince to kisani har sai is kin amince inzo da dare a sace in tafi dake masaukina, idan ba haka ba akwai matsala gaskiya."

Munawwa tayi saurin janye hannunta cike da matsananciyar kunya ta kai kallonta gareshi, suna had'a ido ya yi mata wani kallo mai d'auke da tsantsar sonta sannan ya kanne ido d'aya yace,    "Yes ko in rok'i Alhaji Baba ta amince yasa akai manke yau gaskiya matuk'ar kika k'i amincewa da muradina."     Munawwa tayi saurin dafe baki tana 'yar dariya tace,    "Yanzu zaka iya yi masa wannan magana?"   yayi saurin fad'in    "Of cause me zai hana?matata ce fa zance abani to meye aibu a ciki?"   ya k'are maganar tareda rungumota jikinshi ya dora kanshi saman wuyanta yana sunsunar k'amshin turaren dake jikinta,  tayi saurin mik'ewa tsaye jikinta yana rawa ya ri'ko hannunta yace,    "Ina kuma zaki je bamu gama maganarmu ba?"   cikin had'e fuska tace, 

"Ai mungama kuma jirana ake yi kada mutane suyita nemana."     shima ya mik'e jiki ba kuzari fuska a hade yace,     "to kije tunda sun fini bari kiji tunda har nagano  ke bakida tausayi, to kisani WALLAHI k'arfe tara shaf zan zo da kaina in d'auke ki muje ki sami ladar da sauran matan aure suke samu."    yana k'are fad'ar maganar yayi fuuu ya fito d'akin sai gashi ya hango abokanshi da suka gaji da jira suka fito harabar gidan suka tsaya suna jiran fitowarshi, yanayin da suka ga fito ne duka suka haskoshi musamman da suka ga itama ta fito jiki ba kuzari har zata wucesu bata masan suna wurin ba saboda tsananin rudun da zuciyarta take a ciki, Nuradden ya yi saurin fad'in,     "Haba Amarya yau ba ko gaisuwa?"    Aliyun yayi saurin juyawa inda take nan take wani tsananin kishi ya kamashi, saboda baiso ace sauran abokanshi su ganta ba, haushinshi ya k'ara hawa ne lokacin da ta k'araso wurunsu cike da kunyarsu, ta fara gaidasu a cikin siririyar muryarta mai d'an karen dad'i, dukansu suka saki jiki sunata amsa gaisuwarta, wani d'aya daga cikin abokan nashi yana 'yar dariya yace,      "Ashe ba banza ba ake yimuna rowar ganinki,angonki  sanin yayi kin amsa sunanki na mace, macen ma wadda ta isa gaskiya ina taya abokina murnar samun zankadediyar mace irinki."     Aliyu saboda  bak'in kishin da ya rufe masa ido baisan lokacin da yace dashi ba,     "ALLAH ya isana ganin matata da kayi"   Nuradden yana dariya ya sauko daga kan motar yace,    "Ashe bama zaka bari ayi pic's d'in ba kenan?"

Kafin ya bada amsa sai ga Zulaiha ta fito daga cikin gidan, tana ganin SU Basaraken ta fad'ad'a fuskarta, tazo wurinsu ta gaidasu sannan ta jawo Munawwa wurinshi tayi masu pic's, daga nan abokan suka taso suma suka fara d'aukar pic's  d'in ango da Amarya a tsakiyarsu, Abokin Aliyun wanda ya yaba Munawwa yayi dubarar yin selfie tareda Munawwa,   Aliyu kam ganin anata kalle masa mata ne yazo wurin Munawwa murya k'asa k'asa yana maka mata wata uwar harara yace,      "Minti biyu na baki ki shiga gida WALLAHI ko in tafi dake yanzunnan batareda kin sami rakiyar kowa ba ."     aiko jikinta yana rawa ta bar wurin da sassarfa ta shige cikin gidan, ya dawo da kallonshi ga sauran abokan yace,      "In kun gaji da kallon haram d'in to kuzo mu tafi"   gaba d'aya suka fara yimasa dariya, saboda sun gano tsabar kishi ne ya rufe masa ido, shikam wanda ya yabe Munawwar sai k'ara yaba halittarta yake aranshi saboda har ga ALLAH ta burgeshi sosai, kuma ta tafi da imaninshi har suka kai hotel d'in Munawwar yake kallo a idonshi, saboda sonta na farat d'aya da ya kamashi ba tareda ya shiryawa hakan ba,  Aliyu kam da ya tuno ganinta da suka yi, har da wani photon  iya shege da suka yi da ita sai yaji kamar ya shakesu duka su mussure. 

Munawwa kam da mutuwar jiki ta shiga gidan, Zulaiha ta biyo bayanta tana yimata dariya saboda ta fahimci kishinta da Aliyun yake yi, haka dai aka ci gaba da hidima har lokacin zuwa walima, abun mamaki sai ga Mummy na gani cikin taron masu zuwa walima, tasha adonta masha ALLAH ta fito uwar Amarya sak da ita, anyi taron walima lafiya malammai sun nuna k'warewarsu wajen sanyaya zukatan mutane, tareda k'ara jawosu izuwa hanyar tafarkin tsira, anyiwa Amarya hud'uba sosai don shi angon k'in zuwa yayi don kawai kada maza suje su k'ara kalle masa mata, iya mata kawai akayi walimar aka watse bayan an yita rabon abubuwa kamarsu bokitai manyan robobi jikkuna masu d'auke da sunan Amarya da ango, kai masha ALLAH fa komi an raba  tamkar daman cen an dad'e da tsara bukin, saboda kacokam Yusuf ya d'auki nauyin yin kayan rabon, saboda kud'in da ya saki nan take aka k'are masa duk abunda yasa ayi cikin lokaci, aiko duk wanda yaje walimar ya wadatu da komi anci ansha gwanin burgewa sannan aka watse taron.

Kasancewar Mummy bata gidan Daddy ma yayi wurin Balkisa  fira,misalin k'arfe tara da rabi na dare   Munara ta fito falo ta zauna tana chat tana kallo, saboda ta gaji da zaman d'akin da take yi tana shiga status kawai tayi ta ganin abokansu na School da su Zulaiha da Yusuf tareda sauran wasu 'yan uwansu sunata d'ora status da wata Amarya, ta shiga status d'in Zulaiha ba shiri gabanta ya k'ire ya fad'i, a razane  ta tashi zaune cikin fad'uwar gaba tace

" Munawwa tayi aure?to wa ta aura?" taci gaba da duba photunan har taci karo da wanda Zulaiha tayiwa Munawwa da Basarake, suna kallon juna fuskokinsu d'auke da farin ciki, tayi saurin ajiye wayar ta dafe Zuciyarta da take jin tana zafi kamar zata tarwatse saboda tsananin bugun da takeyi, tana cikin wannan halin ne sai ga kiran k'awarta Nucy ya shigo wayarta, da sauri ta d'auka cikin kuka ta riga Nucy magana tace,     "Rayuwata tazo k'arshe Nucy Munawwa ta aure HAIDAR d'ina."     ta k'are maganar a cikin tsananin Kuka, Daddy dake shigowa maganar da yaji ta fad'a ta k'ara  firgitashi, saboda shima dalilin da ya baro dashi wurin Balkisa kenan, saboda suna tsaka da firarsu ne wani abokinshi ya kirashi yana tayashi  murnar auren, duk da ya nuna b'acin ranshi akan rashin sanar dashi da ba'ayi ba, shiyasa a rud'e suka yi sallama ya nufo gidan cike da jin haushin Alhaji Baba da yayi masa wannan bazata, saboda rashin gaya masa da baiyi ba ya k'ona masa rau sosai, sai gashi yaci karo da kalaman Munara, da sassarfa ya k'araso falon cike mamaki a fuskarshi yace,     "Me naji kina fad'a?" "Daddy ba kace ya fasa aurenta ba?to yanzu ya aka yi suka yi aure?WALLAHI Daddy kaji na rantse bazan kyale duk wanda yaci amanata ba akan auren Haidar da Munawwa tayi, daya bayan d'aya sai na d'auki fansa akan kowa WALLAHI."  Ta tashi fuuu ta fito gidan ya biyo bayanta yana kiran sunanta amman zuciyarta ta riga da ta k'one, tana fitowa gate d'in gidanma ta kwadon dake sakale da k'ofar gidan ta cire tasaka ta dantse k'ofar, tana jin Daddyn yana k'ok'arin bud'e gate d'in ta yi bakin titi da sauri akayi dace Napep zata wuce ta tareta ta shige, Daddy yana k'arasowa Napep d'in tana tashi da gudu, aguje ya koma gida ya d'auko motarshi ya bi bayansu, sai dai tun ba'a je ko'ina ba suka b'ace masa cikin tsananin tashin hankali, ya dinga bulayin ta ina zai hango Napep d'in amman duk iya nacinshi dole ya hak'ura, cikin tsananin rud'u ya fara zuwa gidan k'awayenta nemanta ido rufe, duk inda yaje sai ace masa bata nan gidan 'yan uwan Mummy da nashi duk yaje bai ganta, cike da jin haushin ALHAJI Baba ya tafi police station ya d'ebo masa 'yan sanda, mota biyu yayi jagora suka nufi gidan Alhaji Baba.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*48*

Basarake yana cikin mota zaune k'ofar gidan ALHAJI Baba sai kiran Numbar Munawwa yake akan ta fitto suje, amman tak'i d'aukar wayar ma balle har ta ji abunda zaisata wannan rashin kunya da yake so tayi gaban iyayenta, k'arshe ma kashe wayar tayi gaba d'aya saboda ta fara tsarguwa da kallon da Mummy take yimata,aiko tana ganin Munawwa ta kashe wayar cike da mamaki tace da ita,      "Halan ba Aliyu bane yake kiranki?"  a kunyace tace    "Shine Mummy" tace    "To meyasa bazaki d'auka ba?"  munawaa tace,    "Wai yana k'ofar gida inzo in karb'i sak'o,  ni dai  WALLAHI Mummy bazan je ba gaskiya shine ya baiwa Yusuf sak'on ya kawoman."  Mummy ta kalleta tayi 'yar dariya saboda zuciyarta ta hasko mata abunda take son b'oye mata, waton so yakeyi su keb'e ita kuma tana jin nauyin hakan, shine yake ta kira tak'i d'aukar wayar cikin basarwa tace,   "Da kinje ki karb'o da kanki sai ki dawo"   Munawwa tayi saurin fad'in "aike Mummy ya wuce tun d'azu ma ya tafi"     Mummy ta harereta sannan tace 

"Sai kinyi hak'uri dashi saboda  mazan zamani  ba hak'uri ne dasu ba, kiyi k'ok'ari kiyi abunda yake so kada ki bari ranshi ya b'aci akan abunda baifi karfinki ba."

Munawwa tabi lafiyar gadon ta kwanta sannan tace,    "Zan kiyaye Mummy". 
Daga nan suka ci gaba da firarsu saboda d'aki d'aya aka ware masu domin suyi firarsu cikin natsuwa. 

**

Basarakeka kam yana ganin ta kashe wayar ya fito motar ya jingina yana tunanin maganar da zai fad'awa Alhaji Baba akan yabashi matarshi, aiko sai ga motar Daddy a gaba ta  'yan sanda abayanshi  da jiniyarsu wiwiwi suka yi parkin k'ofar gidan sannan suka fiffito, Basarake ya d'ago kanshi cikin mamakin ganin Daddyn da 'yan sandan,  Daddy kam baima kula dashi ba ya fara baiwa 'yansandan umurni suje su fito da Alhajin, juyowar da zaiyi yayi arba da Basarake suka kalli juna ido cikin ido, saboda hasken lantarkin da ya haske k'ofar gidan tass, a cikin kad'uwa daddyn yaji gabanshi ya fad'i ba shiri ya fara sharce gumin dake fuskarshi da hankicif d'in dake hannunshi, yaji tsoro sannan da haushin ganin Basaraken, saboda ganin zai rusa masa plan d'inshi na ciwa Alhaji Baba zarafi don ya gano,  karen bana shi ke maganin zomon bana. 

Aiko sai ga Basaraken ya tako a hankali cike da ladabi har wurinshi ya duk'a ya gayar dashi cike da ladabi, sannan yace da Daddyn,   "Lafiya na ganka da wad'annan mutanen?" Daddy yana k'ok'arin yin magana sai ga ALHAJI Baban  'yansanda a bayanshi, kasancewar dare ya yi sosai kowa ya shige shiyasa ba wanda yasan abunda yake faruwa, ALHAJI Baba yana ganin Basarake ya fara murmushin mugunta kai tsaye ya mik'awa Daddy hannu da nufin su gaisa, Daddy ya kawar da kai sannan yace da 'yan sandan su tafi dashi station, Alhajin yayi saurin fad'in    "Ba inda zanje Sai ka fad'o man laifin da nayi maka sannan, ai ko ALLAH baya kama mu da laifin da bamusan mun aikatau ba"

Carab wani d'ansanda daga cikinsu yace,   

"Kada ka rena muna hankali Malam bayan tun cen na sanar da kai yace saboda  ka d'aurawa 'yarshi aure ba tareda izininshi ba shiyasa ya nemo mu akan mu k'watar masa yancinsa?, ko ba NAYI maka wannan bayanin ba tun kafin muzo nan?"    Cikin tsananin b'acin rai Basarake yace,     "Bayaninku ai ba shine bayani ba ku bari ya fad'a da bakinshi sai munfi fahimtar abunda yake nufi, saboda nine nan na auri yarshi kuma ban aureta ba har sai tareda izininshi, Daddy kayi masu bayani kai ka bani izinin auren Munawwa da kanka saboda suyi gaggawar barin k'ofar gidannan tun kafin su jawo mana idon Mutane anan."  Yana k'are maganar ya ciro wasu kud'i a Aljihunshi da baisan ko nawa bane ya mik'awa  wani d'ayan d'ansanda sannan yace   "ga kud'inku na mai da kuka b'ata kuyi hak'uri ku tafiyarku wannan rikicin gida ne"  har yaso kar ya karb'a ogan cikunsu ya bashi umurnin ya karb'a kawai, saboda ganin yanda Daddyn yayi tsuru tsuru yana zarar ido alamun rashin gaskiya suka bayyana a kan fuskarshi. 

Suna karb'a suka shige motocinsu da gudu suka bar k'ofar gidan, Cikin jin haushi Daddy ya kalli Alhaji baba dake ta yimasa dariyar cin nasara, yayi k'wafa buzun buzun ya shige motarshi ya bar k'ofar gidan zuciyarshi kamar ta tarwatse saboda bak'in cikin rashin cikar burinshi na wulak'anta Alhajin da bai samu yayi ba. 

Basarake da Alhaji Baba suka bishi da kallo har ya k'ule sannan Alhajin yayi 'yar dariyar manya yace,     "Aliyu ALLAH yayi maka albarka yasa kaima ka sami masu yimaka fiye da abunda kake yi na alkhairi. 

Baki har kunne Basaraken yace,    "Ameen ALHAJI nagode. "  sannan suka yi sallama Alhajin har ya juya zai shiga gida Basaraken yayi saurin fad'in,    "Alhaji"  ya juyo tareda cewa,    "Ko matarka kake son gani?"   Basaraken ya fara sosa kai yace,    "A'a daman zuwa nayi in sanar da kai gobe da k'arfe sha biyu zamu bar garinnan."  Alhajin yayi 'yar dariya sannan yace,     "K'arfe goma dam kazo da motoci ka tafi da matarka"   cike da jin dad'in furucin alhajin ya fara yin godiya suka k'ara yin sallama ALHAJI  ya shige gida, Basarake ya fad'a motarshi cike da jin dad'i ya bar k'ofar gidan, wucewarshi keda wuya sai ga Munara da Nucy sun fito maboyarsu suka yi dariyar Mugunta, sannan suka kama gabansu suma. 

Koda safiya ta waye gidan Alhaji Baba kowa ya tashi da shirin biki, saboda bikin na safe ne kafin lokacin da za'a wuce da Amarya, shiyasa kowa sai hada hadarshi yake hankali kwance, K'arfe tara da rabi sai ga motoci  Basarake ya turo na tafiya,  aka shiga k'ara  gyara Amarya ciki da waje sai k'amshi take duk inda ta wulga, sai ga wata takirata tace da ita ana nemanta a harabar gida, cikin tsananin farin ciki ta fito saboda tsammaninta ko angon nata ne, tafito gidan kenan bata riga da ta k'araso wajen ba taji anshaka mata wani abu a hanci, aiko ba shiri ta tafi luuu wadda tayi aikin ta ri'kota tareda lullub'e mata fuska suka fito gidan, Yusuf dake waya da Basarake yana sanar dashi kada Munawwa ta shiga wata mota shi da kanshi zai zo ya d'auketa da tashi motar, aiko sai gashi  lokacin yaga fitowarsu ya bisu da kallo ya biyo bayansu da nufin ya sanar da su sak'on, aiko kai tsaye matar  ta sakata wata mota wadda daman a kunne take suna shiga  kamar walk'iya suka bar k'ofar gidan, ba tareda sun jira sauran motocin d'aukar Amaryar ba, Yusuf yana ganin haka jikinshi ya bashi akwai matsala da gudu ya fad'a tashi motar yabi bayansu. 

Duk k'ok'arinshi na son ya cimmasu ya shawo gabansu amman ina sunyi masa nisa, kafin kace me sun b'ace masa kamar walk'iya dole yayi parking  ya fara kiran Basaraken hankali a tashe. 

**

Munawwa kam bata masan me suke yi ba har aka je gidan da za'a kaita, matar ta ri'kota har zuwa wani d'aki, inda suka tarar da wasu murdaddun katta majiya k'arfi sun kai goma, ta kwantar da ita akan gadon sannan ta fara tub'e mata kaya gaba d'aya, tana k'arewa ta kallesu d'aya bayan d'aya tace dasu, 

"Ga wuri ga waina sai ci ku tabbatar da gaba d'aya kun tayar da ita aiki sannan kuzo ku karb'i  kud'inku ku k'ara gaba."

To fa shin waye da wannan aikin kuma😔



S/Yamma daughter 🤝🏻

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI 🔥*

*49*

Basarake yana jin abunda Yusuf ya sanar dashi,yace dashi yaje ya ebo masa 'yan sanda zai kirashi kafin yaje police station d'in ya fad'a masa inda zasu sameshi,  jikinshi yana rawa ya d'auko wayarshi ya fad'a mota hankali tashe, Duk saurin da Nuradden yake don suje tare bai samu cimmasa ba saboda fisgar motar da yayi aguje, yana cikin motar ya bud'a wayar ya shiga location ya fara searching, ta inda zai gano Munawwa saboda zoben da ke hannunta wanda ya saka mata na gold, tsakiyarshi akwai boyayyiyar camera ta sirri mai d'auke da na'ura tamkar wayar Android, shiyasa abun yazo masa da sauk'i da biyar arrow d'in binciken da yake a location d'in ya sami damar biyar bayansu har zuwa unguwar da aka kai Munawwa har k'ofar gidan, sannan ya kira Yusuf ya sanar dashi unguwar tareda yimasa inkiya da kwatancen inda gidan yake, sannan ya fito motar ya fad'a gidan saboda sun manta shaf basu rufe gate d'in ba, yana kallon wayar tana nuna masa inda zai je har tsakiyar gidan sai gashi har a k'ofar d'akin, cikin farin cikin cin nasara ya maida wayar Aljihu ya saka k'afa ya bugi k'ofar d'akin da zafin nama, sai ga k'ofar a k'asa ta fad'i ba shiri saboda daman tasha wuya neman agaji take so. 

Abunda idanuwanshi suka gani ya saka zuciyarshi daina aiki na wuccin gadi, sannan ta dawo bugawa har tana k'ok'arin fitowa  daga k'irjinshi saboda dukan luguden da take yi. 

Saboda wani girsheshen k'aton wanda da alama ma duk ya fisu girma ya gani kwance a kan gadon yana shafa kirjin Munawwa da hannuwanshi d'aya  cikin rud'ewa, saboda ganin halittar da ALLAH yayi mata yasa shi zaucewa, ya b'uge da shafata a rud'e yana tsotsar dukiyar fulaninta, ya saka hannu d'aya ya jawo jarumarsa ya saita a k'asanta da nufinshi na fara shigarta, sai ga Basaraken ya bugo k'ofar ta fad'i, a razane k'aton da sauran k'attin duka suka kai kallonsu ga bakin k'ofar, ganinshi da yanayin da yake a ciki yasa k'aton da ke k'ok'arin ketawa Munawwa haddi yayi zumbur ya mik'e, kafin ya tantance yaji saukar naushi a fuskarshi, basaraken bai tsaya iya nan ba ya shak'e masa wuya idonshi a rufe gurinshi kawai ya aikashi barzahu koda zai rage zafin rad'ad'in mugun ganin da idanuwansa suka yi masa, sauran k'attin suna ganin shi kad'ai ne ba wasu suka yo kanshi da duka, yana karewa har sai da yaga ya kai wannan k'aton k'asa har lokacin yana shak'e  da wuyanshi, shi ko sai kakarin mutuwa yakeyi yana k'ok'arin lek'o k'ofar lahira . 

K'arar bindigar da suka jiyo ne yasa suka daina dukanshi a rud'e suka fara neman hanyar tserewa, sai gashi suna k'ok'arin fita gidanne 'yan sanda suka cafkesu duka, Basarake kam sai da yaga ana k'ok'arin shigowa d'akin ne yayi saurin sakinshi yayi kan Munawwa dake kwance tumb'ur  tamkar matatta bata ma san meke faruwa ba, da gudu ya d'auketa zuwa wani k'aramin d'aki da yaga ni cikin d'akin, saboda baya son wani ya k'ara ganinta a yanayin da take, hawayen bak'in ciki suna zuba a fuskarshi ya saka mata kayanta sannan ya rungumota zasu fito d'akin,  wanda yayi dai dai da k'arasowar Yusuf da 'yan sandan shima a rikice ya fara zubda hawaye, saboda ganin hawayen da Basaraken yake yi zuciyarshi ta bashi anyi raping d'inta, jiki ba kuzari ya biyo bayansu suka fito gidan tareda 'yan sandan, kai tsaye ya fad'a motarshi ya zauna seat d'in baya rungume da ita, ganin haka yasa Yusuf ya shiga motar ya jasu suka bar unguwar, har sun fara tafiya Basaraken yayi magana cikin wata raunanniyar murya yace,     "Muje Asibiti"    Yusuf ya nufi wata babbar Asibiti mai zaman kanta, kai tsaye aka karb'eta cikin gaggawa aka shiga binciken halin da take ciki, anyi mata Allurar kare wannan abun da suka shak'a mata wanda ke sakata bacci irin na mutuwa, sannan suka fito da ita zuwa wani d'aki na hutawa, saboda Basarake yayi masu bayanin ba kwanciya za'a basu ba saboda tafiya ce a gabansu,wani likita ya kirashi yana yi masa bayanin abunda aka shak'a mata, amman shi zuciyarshi tafi karkata akan raping  d'in da yake tunanin anyiwa Munawynshi, ya kasa hak'uri yace da likitan     "A nemo man wata kwararriyar likitar mata, ina son ta duba man ita ya virginal d'inta yake"    Likitan yace    "Ok ba damuwa bari na kira Dr Maryam insha ALLAH ita zata gano komi"     kai tsaye ya d'aga waya ya kirata sannan ya CI gaba da yiwa Basaraken bayanin matsalar Munawwa tareda kwantar masa da hankali, wanda shi sam bai ma san me yake fad'a ba, saboda jikinshi da Zuciyarshi duk sun amince akan anyiwa rayuwarshi illah anketaw matarshi mutunci, sai ga YUSUF ya shigo a rud'e ya mik'awa Basaraken wayar Alhaji Baba ne yake son yin magana dashi, shima yaji gabanshi ya k'ara fad'uwa, saboda baya son kowa yasan da wannan tozarcin da aka yiwa Matarshi,  yana karb'ar wayar wata dubara ta fad'o masa, bayan sun d'an gaisa a gajarce ALHAJI Baba yace,    "Munawwa muka nema muka rasa KAF fad'in gidannan ba'a ganta ba, ko kuna tare da ita ne?"    Basarake yayi saurin fad'in    "Eh tare muke Alahaji har ma muna kan hanyar zuwa Abuja, a d'auko sauran Mutanen kawai su biyo bayanmu."  ALHAJI Baba ya sauke k'atuwar ajiyar zuciya sannan ya kashe wayar. 

Basarake ya mik'awa Yusuf wayar ya fito office d'in,  wanda yayi daidai da shigowar Dr maryam ba b'ata lokaci likitan yayi mata bayani, taje har d'akin da Munawwa take ta binciketa tas sannan ta koma ta sanar dasu komi normal,  sannan hankalinshi ya kwanta duk da d'aya sashe na Zuciyarshi yana k'aryata hakan, saboda abunda ya gani da idonshi wanda har lokacin bai daina yimasa gizo a ido ba,sannan ya fito office d'in ya tallabota a jikinshi ta langab'e don har lokacin abun bai k'arasa sakinta ba, ya biya bill d'in komi sannan suka fito kai tsaye suka nufi inda motarsu take ajiye, sai da ya sakata a motar sannan ya d'auko wayarshi yayi dealing d'in wani, kira d'aya biyu ya d'auka ya tambayeshi akwai jirgin da zai tashi yanzu zuwa Abuja yace A'a sai dai ayi masa booking, yace to yana jira kai tsaye yace da Yusuf ya kaisu Air port, cikin tsananin tausayinsu duka yaja motar suka tafi, wanda kafin ma su je an kammala komi su kawai ake jira ba b'ata lokaci yayi Sallama da Yusuf yace yaje da motar ya ajiye hannunsa zai dawo next week, sannan yace ya fad'awa D. P. O ayi ta basu gwalegwale har sai ya dawo, haka ma ya rok'i Yusuf d'in da kada ya fad'awa kowa wannan sirrin ya tsaya iya tsakaninshi dashi, ya jaddada masa ba matsala sannan suka rabu, zuciyoyinsu duka biyu ba dad'i saboda Yusuf bayan dagawar jirginsu kuka yayi ta rerawa saboda tausayin 'yar uwarshi ta hannun damanshi  kuma abokiyarshi Munawwara, duk da idan ya tuna halaccin Aliyu sai yaji damuwar ta d'an rage masa, saboda yana da yak'inin cewa zai rik'eta amana kuma bazai fad'awa kowa wannan sirrin ba. 

  Basarake kam bayan dagawar jirginsu ya kai kallonshi gareta, tanata baccinta hankali kwance wani tausayinta ya kama shi, tuno abunda ya gani yasa yayi saurin runtse idonshi ya matso wasu hawaye masu zafi,  suka dinga kwarara suna sauka  tareda kaiwa iska naushi, wanda yayi sanadin dawo da ita daga nannauyan barcin da take yi, ta bud'e idonta a hankali jin saukar hawaye akan fuskarta yasa tayi saurin tashi zaune, ganin Aliyunta  yana zubar ruwan hawaye wasu nakorar wasu yasa k'irjinta yayi saurin bugawa, da sauri ta kai hannunta a fuskarshi ta fara share masa hawayen,  k'walla cike da idanuwanta tace

"My only me yasaka kuka ne?kayi hak'uri in nice nayi alk'awali zan zamo mai yi maka biyayya akan duk abunda ka ke so"    da sauri ya share hawayen ya rungumota a jikinshi yace    "Ba komai kukan farin cikin ganin cikar burinmu ne"    Munawwa ta d'ago ta kai kallonta gareshi sannan tace,     "Ka fad'a man gaskiya saboda nasan Aliyuna Jarumi ne bazai yi kuka don wannan dalilin ba."     ya k'ara rungumota tareda aza kanshi saman fuskarta yace,     "Bana son surutu ki natsu da kyau kin kusa zuwa gidan surukanki, kada kije kiyi abunda Kakale zata ga wallenki"   da sauri k'irjinta ya k'ara bugawa sannan ta rufe fuskarta tace,    "Ni dai WALLAHI kunyarsu nike ji."    Yayi 'yar dariyar k'arfin hali sannan yace,    "Kunfi kusa nan da kwana biyu zuwa sati nasan za'a fara nunaman 'yar wariya."

Munawwa tayi shiru tana dariya k'asa kasa har suka je Garin Abuja suna 'yar firarsu, koda suka sauka bai wuce da ita gidan kai tsaye  ba, sai da yayi waya aka kawo masa wata motar sannan ya d'auketa suka tafi wani katafaren shago, ya siyo mata wani dankareren less da zai kai 100k, tare da wata alkyabbar mata mai masifar kyau da talkama da komai wanda zata yi using dashi, a take ya bada d'inki minti talatin aka had'a ya karb'a suka nufi wani shagon kwalliya yace a had'a masa ita cikin minti ashirin kacal, aiko ba b'ata lokaci ta shiga wanka ta dirje jikinta sannan ta fito aka tsantsara mata kwalliya, koda lokacin ya cika har an kammala had'ata ta fito amarya sak sannan ya d'auketa yana tsokanarta suka nufi gidansu, waton katafaren  gidan ALHAJI DIKKO MAI GWAL.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*50*

Munawwa tana ganin katafaren gidan ta k'ara girmama lamarin Ubangiji, aranta tace,   _"Ashe ba banza ba ALIYU  yake yiwa kud'i kisan gillah."_

Shigarsu gate na biyu ne suka hango motocin 'yan kawo Amarya, Munawwa tayi saurin kallonshi tace,      "Ya akayi tafiyar tamu ta zama daban daban?."

Basarake yayi 'yar dariyar yak'e sannan yace,     "Nima tambayar da zan yimaki kenan, ke dad'i miji kika biyoshi kika baro masu kawoki acen."    ba shiri k'irjinta ya buga fuskarta d'auke da mamaki tace,     "Na shigesu ya aka yi ka tafo dani bansani ba, WALLAHI banmasan na biyoka BA abunda kawai zan iya tunawa lokacin da muke hidimar bikin gidan ALHAJI Baba, yanzu saboda ALLAH ka kyauta man kake gani haka kawai kasa mutane suyi ta yiman wani kallo?"    ta k'are maganar cikin zubar wasu hawayen takaicin abunda yasata ta aikata, saboda tsammaninta  mayen sonshi ne ya jata bata ma san tayi ba, Musamman da ta tuno ko sallama batayi da Mummynta dasu Alhajin ba, aiko ta b'are da kuka cikin 'yar dariya yace,    "Salon kisa ace wani abu nayi maki ko? Yanzu shikenan don kin biyoni shine abun kuka?, cabd'i lalle wannan shagwab'a taki babba ce don haka bari insa a shiga dake sai Mummy tayita aikin lallashi."    yana k'ok'arin fitowa ne tayi saurin fad'in,     "wai waya gaya maka haka ake d'auko Amarya?"   yana dariya yace,    "To ni dai a haka nayi nawa"  ya fito motar ya nufi wata gwaggonshi da itama tana hango shi ta yo wurinshi cikin fad'a tace dashi,     "KAI Angon zamani  kasan kaine zaka d'auko matarka kuma shine ka tura mu?, kasa hankulan mutanen gidansu yarinya ya tashi, Mahaifiyarta fa WALLAHI har kuka tayi fa da aka ce ba'a ganta ba."   cikin sigar lallashi Aliyun ya fara sosa kai yace,     "Ayi hak'uri dani Gwaggo nima ba haka naso ba,  gata cen NA kawo maku a shiga da ita gidan."  Gwaggon tana 'yar dariya tace,    "Ai da ka k'arasa aikin tunda ka ishe kanka."

Yana 'yar dariya ya sunkuyar da kai yace,   "Tuba nake gwaggo nayi laifi ayi man afuwa."   sannan ta wuce shi ta nufi motar inda Munawwa keta sharar kukanta, kanta a duk'e hular alkyabbar ta rufe mata fuska duka, Gwaggon cike da murnar ganinta ta ri'ko hannunta ta fito motar jiki ba kuzari, suka nufi cikin gidan sauran 'yan kawo Amarya da wasu dangin su Aliyu suka dafe masu baya,har suka shige part d'in Mummy, Aliyu bai d'auke idonshi akansu ba har sai da yaga sun shige duka,  sannan ya sauke ajiyar zuciya tuno abunda ya gani yasa zuciyarta ta k'ara bugawa, wani sabon b'acin rai ya ziyarci Zuciyarshi cikin tsananin bak'in ciki tareda zafin da Zuciyarshi take yi ya juya da nufin shiga motar, ya had'a ido da Nuradden dake bayanshi shima fuskarshi cike da damuwa, ba tareda Aliyun yace dashi k'anzil ba ya bud'a motar ya shiga, ya aza kanshi saman starring d'in motar wasu hawaye masu zafi suka zubo masa, Cike da mamakin Abokin nashi ya bud'e gaba ya zauna fuska d'auke da damuwa yace dashi,    "NI tun lokacin da naga ka fito hotel a rud'e nasan akwai abunda ya faru, shiyasa na biyoka abaya sai kuma kafin in k'araso ka fice, nayi tunanin watak'ila baka son rakiyata ne shiyasa na koma amman hankalina gaba d'aya yana wurinka, sai gashi kuma wad'anda muka tura su d'auko amarya suka kirani suke sanar dani wai ba'a ga Amarya ba, an nemeta ko'ina bata gidan nayi ta kiran wayarka tanata ringing baka d'aga ba,  shin meke faruwa ne? don ganinka a haka ya tabbatar man akwai matsala."   Basarake ya d'ago fuskarshi da ta jik'e da hawaye yace,     "Bazan iya fad'a maka ba saboda sirrinmu ne nida Matata, ka d'ai yi mani Addu'a ALLAH ya yaye mani damuwar dake raina ."   yana k'are fad'ar maganar ya tada motar yayi Rivas suka fito gidan,  Nuradde ya sauke k'atuwar ajiyar zuciya sannan yace,    "ALLAH ya yaye maka koma menene."

**

Munawwa kam fuska a rufe tana sharb'ar hawaye aka shiga da ita cikin gidan,  Zulaiha da sauran 'yanmatan gidan ALHAJI baba sai dariya suke yimata k'asa k'asa,  kan ta biyo mijinta ta baro 'yan kawo Amarya saboda a tarihi ma ba'a tab'a jin labarin hakan ba, sai gareta uwar son miji ta mance da ana sallama da mutane ta biyo abunta. 

Da murna Ummi ta tarbi Munawwa cike da tsantsar  farin cikin ganin surukarta ta rungumeta, Munawwa ta k'ara sunne kai saboda kunyar Ummi da taji ba shiri, da kanta tayi masu jagora zuwa d'akin Kakale, sai gata itama bata jira har su ISO ba ta dogara 'yar sandarta ta fito cike da d'okin ganin Amaryar Aliyu, da kanta ta rik'o hannunta suka k'arasa d'akinta, inda ta zaunar da Munawwa ta bud'e wani had'ad'd'en frij ta ciro ruwa masu sanyi tareda cup ta tsiyaya mata tace,    "Sha kiji sanyi duk da ba'a murna da zuwan kishiya, amman ni yau an fara takaina saboda haka ki bud'e fuskarki don kisan dawa zakiyi zaman kishin"  gaba d'aya aka saka dariya Munawwa kam k'ara rufe fuskarta tayi cike da jin kunyarta, sai da Hajiya Hindu ta zo ta D'an janwo hular alkyabbar baya fuskarta ta fito, amman tayi saurin rufe fuskarta da tafukanta, Kakale da Ummi da sauran mutanen d'akin wad'anda basu Santa ba duk sun yaba da tsarinta uwa uba kunyarta ta burgesu, cikin jin dad'in ganin kyakkyawar matar Aliyu Kakale ta ce,    "Masha ALLAH ashe ba banza ba kyawonki ne yasa maigidana rud'ewa ya rikice ya rufe idonshi ya dankaro man kishiya."   gaba d'aya falon aka saka dariya sannan kakale tayita yi masu addu'ar zaman lafiya ga aurensu, tareda rok'on ALLAH ya basu zuri'a dayyiba, sannan ta d'auko wasu awarwaro masu bala'in kyau ta saka mata tareda wata 'yar k'aramar sark'ar zinari tasa mata a wuya da 'yan kunnenta, da kanta ta rik'ata ta kaita inda ALHAJI DIKKO shima ya yaba sosai da zab'in Aliyun, kai tsaye ya bata kyautar makullin mota da kyautar kud'i tsabarsu cike da wata 'yar k'aramar jaka sababbi fil fil,  sannan suka fito suka nufi d'akin Ummi inda itama tayi mata kyauta ta bajinta, sannan tace da ita akaita d'aya sashen matar baban Aliyun, itama tayi na'am da ita a fuska har ma tayi mata kyautar itama saboda ganin idanuwan mutane, sannan aka fito da ita kai tsaye aka sakata mota suka fito gidan zuwa katafaren gidan Aliyun, inda masu kawo Amarya kallo ya koma sama, saboda had'uwar gidan kasancewarshi hawa hawa har da lifter's, gwanin burgewa mata suka yi ta doruwa cikin lifter zuwa saman inda d'akin Munawwar yake, aka zaunar da ita kan gadonta su Hajiya hindu suka sakata a gaba sai nasihohi suke yi mata agurguje,  akan tayi hak'uri da halin mijinta ta kyautatawa iyayenshi. 

Munawwa sai kuka take yi saboda jin kunyar abunda tayi ko sallama bata yi da Alhaji Baba da Mummynta ba, su Zulaiha kam suna ganin su Hajiya hindu sun fita d'akin suka tasa ta agaba da tsarguwar biyo Aliyun da tayi, tun tana kuka har ta ware tana 'yar dariya  saboda iskancin da suke ta yimata, kasancewar a ranar zasu koma Aliyu ya baiwa Nuradden komi ya sallame masu kawo Amarya k'arfe uku da rabi suka kama hanyar zuwa kaduna, kowa cike da murnar kyautar bajintar da aka yimasu, suna ta yiwa munawwa farin cikin dace da gidan aure da tayi. 

  Bayan tafiyarsu ne aka barta daga ita sai wasu dangin Aliyu, ta mik'e taje toilet ta d'auro arwallah ta fito ta rama sallar azahar d'in da bata yi ba, sannan ta zauna tana lazimi har akayi la'asar. 

Da k'arfe takwas na dare sai ga Aliyun ya shigo d'akinta, tana kwance kan gado jin shigowarshi yasa tayi luf kamar mai bacci, ya k'araso bakin gadon ya tsaya yana kallonta zuciyarshi ba dad'i ya rankwafo dai dai fuskarta har ya Kai bakinshi da nufin ya d'an tsotsi bakinta da har lokacin yake rad'am da janbaki, amman tuno abunda ya gani yasa yayi saurin runtse ido, ya taso ba shiri ya fara kiran sunanta a hankali, ita kam da duk abunda yake tana jinshi fushi take yi dashi shiyasa ta kyaleshi, da ma taga ya cika kiran sunan nata ta juya kwanciyarta ta bashi baya, ganin bombom d'inta MASHA Allah yasa shi jin tsikarshi tashi yarrr, duk da yaji yanayinshi ya fara sauyawa amman hakan yayi kokarin control d'in kanshi, yace da ita   

" Nuradden yana  falo kizo ku gaisa tafiya zaiyi"   ba tareda tace dashi komi ba ta sauko gadon zata fita, yayi saurin rik'o hannunta yace    "Wai fushin meye ake yi dani har haka nikam?" Munawwa ta k'ara b'ata fuska sai ga hawaye sharrr, ya zagayo ya tsaya idonshi akanta yace, 

"in har tun abunda nayi maki ne kiyi hak'uri saboda bana son ganin kina wannan fushin, zuciyata zafi take k'ara yi ki d'auka cewa komi ya wuce don ALLAH kinji?"   ya k'are maganar cikin zubowar wasu hawayen da suka kasa barin idanuwanshi tun mugun ganin da suka yi tozali dashi, kasancewarshi mai tsananin kishi  akanta hakan yasa shi zubar da hawayen da har ya manta ranar da zai tuno yayi kuka. 

Ganin hawaye a idonshi yasa ta shanye nata kukan ta fad'a jikinshi tace,    "Am very sorry my only ka daina kukan nima na bari kaji?ni daman haushin banyi sallama  da Mummy da ALHAJI Baba bane yasa nayi fushin, amman tunda baka so na daina kayi hak'uri kaji? "    Basarake ya saka hannu ya rungumota a jikinshi sosai sannan ya sauke k'atuwar ajiyar zuciya, ya fara shafo kanta yayi k'ok'arin fad'in 

"Komi ya wuce zo muje kada yaga mun barshi."   ya rik'o hannunta suka fito falon,    Nuradden yanayin fuskokinsu da ya gani yasa yaji ba dad'i, ya dai wayance kawai ya fara tsokanar Munawwa tana 'yar dariya, sannan ya fara yimasu addu'ar zaman lafiya a guguje yayi sallama da su ya nufi nashi gidan, zuciyarshi cike da damuwar ganin halin da Abokinshi yake ciki, don duk barkwancin da suka saba ko d'aya basu yi ba, haka suka rabu kowa  jiki ba kuzari. 

Basarake kam bayan ya dawo daga rakiyar Nuradden ya baiwa Munawwa Umurnin ta d'auko ledar da suka shigo da ita,  ya nufi d'akinshi ta biyo bayanshi zuciyarta ba dad'i saboda yanayin da taga Aliyunta a ciki, ya tabbatar mata da akwai abunda ke damunshi, da kanshi ya zaunar da ita ya bata kosassun kajin da ya siyo mata, shima yad'an tsakura kad'an, bayan sun k'are ne ya shiga toilet yayi wanka itama yace da ita  taje tayi wankan, ba musu taje tayo ta fito d'aure da guntun towel tana tsane ruwan jikinta,  yana kai kallonshi ga santala santalan cinyoyinta yayi saurin runtse ido, tareda kawar da kanshi a k'ok'arinshi na gudun zuciyarshi ta shagala tasashi rud'ewa yayi abunda bai shirya ba,yayi jarumtar saita kanshi  ya mik'e tsaye ya d'auko mata wata rigar bacci ya bata ta saka, sannan ya janyota suka kwanta, duk da jikinshi da ya sauya saboda tsantsar sha'awarta da ke fisgarshi hakan ya daure yayi lamo kamar yayi bacci, Munawwa ta k'ura masa ido zuciyarta cike da tunanin sauyin da tagani gareshi har bacci yayi awon gaba da ita,  sai da ya fara jin saukar numfashinta alamun tayi bacci,  sannan ya zare jikinshi da nata ya sauko gadon ya nufi wata durowa ya fiddo wasu k'wayun magani ya kora, sannan ya fita d'akin yaje ya kwanta kan kujera, sannu a hankali har yaji ya dawo normal sannan ya k'ara gyara kwanciyarshi  yana tunanin abunda ya dace yayi, gashi kuma bayason ta gano wani abu da ya samo wata likita ta k'ara duba masa ita,  amman to ta wace hanyar zaiyi hakan ba tareda ta gano ba?.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*51*

Munawwa ta falka cikin dare taga wayam bashi cikin d'akin, cike da tsoro ta sauko gadon tayi toilet sannan ta fito falon domin taga a ina yake, hangoshi kwance saman kujera yana bacci yasa taji k'irjinta ya buga, saboda mamakin abunda yasa shi zuwa nan ya kwanta bayan a tare suka fara kwanciya, jiki ba kuzari ta k'araso wurinshi ta zauna kan k'aramar kujerar dake kusa da wadda yake kwancen, kasancewar kanshi ta gefen yake yasa ta saka hannu ta shafo sumar kanshi a hankali,don bata son ta tayar dashi daga baccin duk da tana buk'atar ya sanar da ita dalilin abunda yasa yayi hakan, cikin sanyin jiki ta d'ora kanta saman kanshi tareda runtse ido tana tunanin sauyin da ta gani a tattare dashi,  daga nan har itama tayi baccin.

Asubar farin ya fara falkawa da salati a bakinshi, yana k'ok'arin tashi ne yaji nauyi a kanshi a take zuciyarshi ta bashi munawynshi ce, daga kwancen ya zura hannu ya shafo  fuskarta da lallausan hannunshi, yaji idonta  a rufe da alama  baccin take, kai tsaye ya k'ara zura hannu ya shafo k'irjinta tareda damk'o na shanunta, ba shiri ta zabura tareda mik'ewa zaune tana mutsutsuke ido, saboda baccin ya d'ebeta da yawa, yana 'yar dariya ya tada ta ta mik'e sannan suka nufi toilet, a tare suka yi arwallah ya ja masu jam'in sallah tareda jan dogayen addu'o'i, wad'anda duk rabi na neman zaman lafiya ga aurensu ne, tun anan carpet d'in munawwa ta baje tana k'ok'arin komawa bacci, ya hanata har sai da ya kaita kan gadon ta kwanta sannan shi ya fice falo ya zauna yana nazarin abunda ya dace,   don a zahirin gaskiya bazai iya kusantarta ba har sai ya k'ara jin gaskiyar zance ga bakin wasu likitoci, bayan son ace da gaske anyi mata fyad'e sannan shi yazo ya zuba nashi ruwa, wad'anda idan har tsautsayi ya gitta arasa na waye, kai tsaye ya fice gidan saboda baya son ingizashin da zuciyarshi take yi akan ya aikata abunda take ta k'awata masa arai. 

Munawwa kam har k'arfe takwas na safe tana ta baccinta sannan ta falka, ta fito d'akinta ta sullo wankanta duk da zuciyarta ba dad'i saboda rashin ganin Aliyu da bata yi ba, bayan ta fito ne ta fara shiryawa cikin wata atamfa super Holland wadda tasha d'inkin riga da siket, ya kama jikinta tsam tsam ta ko'ina ya zauna cif da jikinta, sannan ta murza d'aurin d'ankwali gwanin burgewa, ta feshe jikinta da turaruka sannan ta dawo gefen gadon ta zauna, sai ga kiranshi ya shigo a  wayarta, da sauri ta d'auka cikin d'okin jin muryarshi aiko sai gashi taji yace,      "ki shirya kizo muyi break saboda akwai  bak'in da zasu zo ganinki."

Ba tareda tace k'anzil ba ta kashe wayar jiki ba kuzari tayi tagumi da hannunta biyu, saboda ta rasa me ke damun zucitarta haka ma ta rasa da wace fuska zata karb'i wannan sabon yanayin nashi, aranta tace,      _"shin wai ko dai ya karb'i  budurcina ne bansani ba shiyasa yake yiman haka, in ko har hakane tabbas Aliyu ya bani mamaki, daman ashe jikina yake so bani ba?"    Kai tsaye ta fara hawaye zuciyarta cike da takaicin yanda ma akayi ya ciyo galabarta ta mik'a masa ragamarta tun kafin ta shigo gidanshi, tana cikin kukan ne ya turo k'ofar d'akin ya shigo, ganin yanayin da ya isheta yasa k'irjinshi bugawa dam! dam!  Jiki ba kuzari ya k'araso wutinta ya zauna, sannan ya rungumota jikinshi  yana lallashinta, amman maimakon tayi shiru sai ma ta k'ara sautin kukanta,    cikin tashin hankali yace,     "Haba wify me nayi maki ne da kike ta kuka? Yanzu duk wannan kwalliyar da aka cab'a man baza'a bari in yaba ba? haka kawai abata man ita da kuka, plsss don ALLAH kiyi shiru kinji wai ma shin waya tab'a man mata ne?"    Munawwa ta zare jikinta daga nashi cikin kuka tace,    

"Meyasa ka zab'i ka ajiye soyayyar da nike yimaka a bigiren sha'awa? Anya ko kayiwa soyayyata adalci kuwa?, me yasa ban cancanci kaso ni so irin na tsakani da ALLAH ba, koko  ba irina ne ake yiwa irin wannan son ba?, don ALLAH ka sanar dani wane hali ne nawa wanda baka so wanda har kaga soyayyar sha'awa ce tafi dacewa dani?" 

Basarake ko kam cike da mamakin maganganunta yake kallon d'an k'aramin bakinta, bai katseta ba har sai da ta k'are sannan ya sauke k'atuwar ajiyar zuciya yace,     "Wa ya sanar dake ina yi maki son  sha'awa?"    Munawwa ta k'ara sautin kukanta sannan tace,       "Idan ba shi bane to me yasa tun jiya da ka karb'i budurcina ka sauya man gaba d'aya, shikenan kana nufin tunda ka sami abunda kake so ajikina na tashi aiki a wurinka."

Dam!!! dam!!!! dararam!!!!  Kawai yake ji a zuciyarshi take yi saboda tsananin tashin hankalin da ya shiga,saboda jin kalaman sa suke fita abakinta,  a rud'e ya furta a ranshi,    

_waton dai zargina ya tabbata wani ya rigani gane k'ofar Munawyta_

 Ba shiri sai ga hawaye shaaa akan fuskarshi ya dafe kanshi da hannu biyu yace,       "Ya ALLAH idan har laifi nayi maka kayiman wannan jarabawa da abunda nafi so a rayuwata, na tuba ALLAH astagfirullah ka yafeman."      ya mik'e tsaye yana rangaji ya fice d'akin idonshi a rufe ko ganin gabanshi  baya yi, da lalabe ya shige d'akinshi ya rufe gam sannan ya haye gado yana ta kuka kamar wani k'aramin yaro, yana cikin kukan ne yaji yana buk'ata ya sanar da Nuradden kawai koda zai samo masu mafita, ya jawo wayarshi yana k'ok'arin kiran Nuradden sai ga text ya shigo wayarshi, cikin bugawar k'irji ya bud'a text d'in ba shiri ya mik'e tsaye, saboda abunda ya gani ya k'ara girmama zarginshi, saboda maganar da yaga rubuto masa kamar haka

_"Congrats ango ina taya ka murnar auren mace mai kamar maza irin fanko, don nasan izuwa yanzu kana cike da tsantsar bak'in cikin rasa budurcin matarka da kayi, har ma da jinta kamar tsohuwar rijiya kwakwararra, wadda namiji biyar idan sun zura  gugarsu zasu jawo ruwa ba tareda sun takura ba, alk'awali ne na d'auka ba kai babu farin ciki a zaman aurenku kai da ita matuk'ar ba kun kwance auren cin AMANAR da kuka yi ba, duk da naji wai ka kama wad'anda nasa suyi aikin, to kasani makaman yak'in da na tanadar maku suna da yawa fa, idan ka toshe wannan zan bud'a wata wadda sam tunaninka bazai tab'a zuwa kusa da wurin ba."_

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*52*

Munara kam lokacin da ta fita gidan bata zame ko'ina ba sai gidansu Nusy, acikin zafin nama ta fito Napep d'in ta shige gidan fuskarta d'auke da fushi mai tsanani, Mai napep d'in ya bita da kallo yana sauke numfashin wahalar gudun bala'in da tayita bashi umurni yayi tayi don gurinta nason  b'acewa Daddy, tana shiga cikin firgici da tashin hankali ta shiga gidansu Nusy. 

 kasancewar Nusy Mahaifiyarta ta dad'e da rasuwa  a hannun matar babanta take, kuma ba jituwa tsakaninsu abunda take so take yi agidan, duk da fad'an mahaifinta sai da ya gaji ya shafa mata lafiya, don har korarta yaso yayi a gidanshi saboda mazan da take bi kamar karya, matar uban nata ce tayi ta  lurar dashi akan yaci gaba da yimata addu'a yafi ace ya koreta, don agabanshi ma talalace inaga ya korata ta shiga duniya, dole badon yaso ba yake zaune da ita don ko karatun da take matar Babance tasa ya sakata, don rage matsalolin da suke faruwa sai gashi maimakon ta gyaru sai abun ya k'ara lalacewa, shiyasa iyakacin matar da ita ido bata shiga shirginta don gudun ta CI mata zarafi abanza. 

Tun daga bakin k'ofar d'akin Nusy ta fara gunjin kuka, Nusy ta fito da sauri jikinta yana rawa ta rik'o hannunta suka shige d'akin, cike da rudu tace da ita   "Lafiyarki Munaa? Don ALLAH k'i daina wannan kuka saboda yanzu ba shine agabanki ba, ki natsu ki share hawayenki mu gano miye mafita akan wannan CIN amanar da mutane da yawa suka had'a kai suka yi maki, don a gaskiya anyi maki zalunci sosai kuma an cutar da rayuwarki, wanda ya zama dole kema ki rama kuma ramuwar irin wadda zata tab'i zuciyar duk wani mai hannu a cikin wannan cin amanar da aka YI maki"   
Ba shiri Munawwa ta kai kallonta gareta da idanuwanta da suka yi jajaje tamkar gauta, hawayenta suna sauka wani bayan wani tace,     "Ko baki fad'a ba WALLAHI ya zama dole sai na d'auki fansa, akan Munawwa da Mummy da k'atoton kasurgumin babban azzalumin, don WALLAHI sai na hanashi rawar gaban hantsi a cikin gidanshi, sai nazame masa k'adangaren bakin tulu abarni na b'ata ruwa akarni a fasa tulun, haka ma dole ne nazamar masa sark'a in dabaibaye aurensa daga shi har ita Munawwarar."

Sannan ta share hawayenta ta k'ara cewa,     "Bani shawara me zanyi masu wanda zan rama abunda suka YI mani a farat d'aya?"

Nusy ta mik'e tsaye tareda taku d'aya biyu zuwa uku sannan ta waigo,   tana wata 'yar dariya tana hurar hanci ta dafa kafad'ar Munawwa  tace, 

"Niko nike da shawara idan har zaki iya aikatawa"

"Munara ta tsura mata idanuwa zuciyarta cike da tunanin koma menene zata iya aikatawa,  matuk'ar zai sanadin da zan jefa zuciyoyin maciya amana a damuwa, wadda zata zame masu tsanin da aurensu zai rugujewa, yi gaggawar sanar dani abunda kike gani ya dace da d'aukar fansata"    Nusy ta mik'e tsaye tana 'yar dariyar mugunta tace, 

"Kisa k'atti majiya k'arfi su yaye labulen budurcin Munawwa ta k'arfin tsiya ayau d'innan"  

Munara ta zaro ido ba shiri bakinta yana kyarma tace,      "Canza shawara don gaskiya wannan ba mafita bace" 

Nusy ta dawo da ita ta zauna sannan ta rik'o hannunta cikin sigar kwantar mata da hankali, tace

"Ina da tabbacin hakan zai YI sanadin rugujewar auren, abu na biyu kuma koda yayi jarumtar zama da ita a hakan to ina tabbatar maki da auren bazaiyi wani lasting ba, don ko wata bazai rufa ba zakiji labari yasha bambam, ke kuma daga lokacin sai ki fara kafa taki gwamnati, wadda a take zai dawo gareki har ma ya bata tikinta ta k'ara gaba ke ki maye gurbin nata hankalinki a kwance."

Jikin Munara yayi sanyi k'alau a take wata zuciyar tace da ita,         "Miye tausayin wadda bata tausayinki bata gudun shigarki a kowane hali saboda biyan buk'atar kanta, ki tuna fa tun farko bata son tarayyarki da Haidar saboda sonshi da take yi, haka ma itace tayi tsanin rabaki da Haidar ta hanyar yin receiving d'in wayarshi a lokacin kina fad'in bak'ank'anun maganganu akanshi, wadda har yaji yayi fushi ya yanke alakarku, bata barki a haka ba kina gadon asibiti ta goge komai na HAIDAR d'in don kawai ta k'ara nisantaku ita ta samu shiga a wurinshi, Uwa Uba Mummynku ta goya mata baya suka had'a kai da HAIDAR d'in suka yaudareki keda Daddynki, ki tuna wulak'ancin da yayi maki tareda abokinshi har da yi maki barazana da sojoji,, saboda me ke ba zaki d'auki fansa ba?"

Zuciyarta ta k'ara zafi b'acin ranta ya k'ara  hauhawa cikin rashin tunanin abunda zaije ya dawo ta mik'e tsaye fuska a had'e tace 

"Na amince idan har kina da tabbacin aikata hakan shine  mafita."  

Nusy tana k'ok'arin yin magana ne yaro yayi sallama, daga nan suna jiyoshi yana fad'in    "mai Napep yace abashi kud'inshi shi sauri yake YI dare ya yi"

Nusy cikin fad'a tace 

"Kace ya jirata gatanan zu..... ."  Munara tayi saurin katseta da cewa,    

"Biyashi kawai ni anan ma zan kwana."

 Cikin mamaki Nusy tace,    "Kar fa ki tsokalo fushin Mummy ta jibgeki abanza"

Munara tayi wani mere sannan tace,  

"Bata ma gidan saboda tayiwa Daddy k'aryar Hajiya Hindu bata da lafiya, har yaso fa kar ya barta na saka baki ya barta ta tafi, ashe wai rufa rufa tayi muna nida Daddyn tana sane bikin 'yarta zata je."

Nusy tayi wata ajiyar zuciya sannan tace,   "to shiDaddynku bazaiyi fad'a ba halan."

Munara cikin tasananin fushi tace, 

"Shima fad'a nakeyi dashi ki sallamesh kawai ya tafi don WALLAHI bazan kwana gidannan ba."

Nusy ta jawo jakarta ta fito da kud'in har zata fita sannan ta juyo tace,    "Me zai hana mu bishi ya saukemu gidan talatu   domin mu fara aiwatar da shirinmu, kinsan fa da zafi zafi akan daki k'arfe."

Munara ta mik'e tsaye tareda gyara d'an fingilin gyalenta tace, 

"Muje to dad'ina dake komai azafafe kike yinshi,  shiyasa ma na zabeki a cikin dubban abokaina"

Ta k'are maganar tana 'yar dariya,    Nusy ta ri'ko hannunta suka fito,    har sun kai bakin k'ofar fita Matar Babanta tace,    "Yanzu Nusy ina zakije cikin tsohon darennan, shikenan ke bakya gudun zuciyar mahaifiku ko?"   Nusy ta waigo tana yimata wani kallon reni tace, 

"To ba inda kike zato zanje ba k'awata zan rakiya"     tana k'are maganar tayi tsaki ta wuce fuuu tareda janyo hannun Munara suka  bar gidan. 

Koda suka fita har mai Napep ya tayar yana k'ok'arin barin k'ofar gidan cikin fushi, saboda ganin renin hankalin da Munara tayi masa na shikka shi k'ofar gidan sauro yana ta cizonshi. 

Yana hangosu yayi saurin fad'in,    "bani kud'ina malama don ba dutsene kika ajiye cikin wannan bak'in kwararon ba."   cikin b'acin rai Nusy tayi saurin fad'in,      "Ka iya bakinka dai don wannan unguwa tamu tafi k'arfin zaginka."

Tana k'are maganar suka fad'a napep d'in cikin tsananin  fushi yace,     "Ba inda zanje daku kud'ina kawai zaku bani."    Cikin fushi Munara ta fisge jakar Nusy sannan ta fito Napep d'in, ta ciro 1k ta jefa masa a fuska tace,     "bak'in matsiyaci kaje da rashin Mutuncinka tun kafin insa ayi maka dukan tsiya anan wurin WALLAHI."  ya kalleta kallon bakisan koni waye ba yayi 'yar dariya yace,    "Haduwata dake ta biyu WALLAHI sai kinyi nazamar sanina, saboda ba irina ne ake yiwa haka BA."  yana k'are fad'ar ya fisgi motarshi da k'arfi ya fice lungun, Munara tabishi tana jifar Napep d'in da guma guman duwatsuna, tana dannan masa zagi ta uwa ta uba tamkar wata tsatson maguzawa. 

Suka fito bakin titi suka tari Napep suka haye sai gidan Taly, a gurguje suka yimata bayanin abunda suke so, a take ta tanadar masu wasu kattan 'yan daba majiya k'arfi wad'anda take aiki dasu idan rana ta b'ace, bayan ta basu abunda za'a shak'awa Munawwa suka fito suka nufi gidan ALHAJI Baba, inda tun kafin su isa suka hango motar Basarake, Munara take tasa aka ajiye napep d'in suka fito, da sand'a ta kai inda wani kusurwar gini ta lab'e tareda lek'a kanta, aikuwa saigashi idonta sun gane mata Basarake da ALHAJI Baba,  cikin tsananin fushi ta yafito Nusy itama  ta lallabo suka lab'e suna sauraren abunda suke fad'a, bayan sun wuce ne suka fito Nusy tace da ita subar shirinsu sai gobe su aiwatar, suka koma gidansu Nusy inda sai da mutanen unguwa suka taru suna baiwa Babanta hak'uri, sannan ya barsu suka shiga gidan amman da sharad'in gobe tun da safe Munara tabar masa gida. 

Koda safiya ta waye tun da safe suka fito gidan Nusy da nufin taje makaranta suka nufi gidan Taly, inda suka rok'i alfarmar taje da kanta ta aiwatar masu da komi, saboda gudun mutane su gansu su ranfo su shirinsu ya watse,  don ita Munara tasan ko giyar wake tasha bata isa ta shiga gidan ba, haka ma Nusy da yawa ansan k'awar Munara ce da wani abu ya faru za'a yi saurin gano shirinsu. 

Aiko Taly da kanta taje ta tafo da Munawwa inda ta kaita wannan tsohon gidan, kasancewarshi gidan wani d'an uwanta da baya garin daga shi har iyalanshi. 

Abun Mamaki bayan ta baiwa k'attin umurni suyiwa Munawwa fyad'en ta fito tana waya da su Munara tana sanar dasu aiki ya kammala, sai ga motar Basarake ta doso k'ofar gidan da sauri ta lab'e tareda lek'o kanta, wazata gani ba tsohon mijinta ALIYU ba, da sauri ta zaro ido tareda dafe baki har ya shige gidan,  sannan ta fito zuciyarta sai dakan lugude take yi cike da mamaki tace,    "Daman matarka ce wannan? Cabd'i jam kura zata ci kura kenan?"  ta k'are maganar cikin tsantsar farincikin wannan aiki da aka sakata, duk da bata so shiga cikin lamarin ba, amman yanzu ya zama dole ta shiga tayi ruwa tayi tsaki akan wargaza masa farin ciki, tana cikin wannan tunanin ne sai ga Yusuf da motocin yanda da gudu ta k'ara labewa har sai da suka shige gidan, sannan ta fito cikin tsananin tashin hankali ta shiga motarta tabar unguwar kamar zata tashi sama saboda sauri.

**

Basarake kam cikin fushi ya d'auko key d'in motarshi ya fice gidan batareda ya jira sunyi breakfast d'in ba,  kai tsaye gidan Nuradden ya nufa don ko Motar bai rufe ba ya shige, zuciyarshi sai tafasa take yi tareda tsananin zafin da take yimasa, koda ya shiga ya taradda Nuradden yanata barcinshi, ba tareda ya YI wani jinkiri ba ya tada shi sannan ya fara safa da marwa a tsakiyar falon, cike da firgici Nuradden ya tashi tareda k'arewa abokin nashi kallo, a duba d'aya ya gano tsantsar tashin hankalin da Aliyun yake a ciki, cikin damuwa yace dashi 

"Ka zauna abokina duk nasan bazaka fad'a man damuwar da na hango k'arara akan fuskarka ba."

Basarake ya zauna tareda duk'ar da kanshi yana fesar da iska abakinshi, Nuradden cikin sanyin murya mai cike da tausayin abokin nashi yace,    "cool down my man komi fa yayi tsanani maganinshi ALLAH, ka rok'i ALLAH da sannu zai yaye maka abunda yake damunka."

 Basarake hawaye masu zafi suka sauko masa, tashin hankalin Nuradden ya k'aru cike da mamakin abunda yasa jarumin abokinshi kuka yace, 

"Ga dukkan alama abun ba mai sauk'i bane tunda har zaka iya zubar da hawayenka akan lamarin shin miye wannan gagarumin abun?,  don ALLAH ka sanar dani koda zan iya taimakonka mu had'a k'arfi da k'arfe mu shawo kan lamarin, kamar yanda muka saba yi a kullum."

Basarake ya ciro wayarshi ya kamo text d'in ya mik'awa Nuradden wayar, Nuradden ya karb'a jiki ba kuzari ya fara karantawa, cikin tashin hankali Nuradden ya mik'e tsaye tareda fad'ar, 

"INNALILLAHI WA INNA ilaihirrajiun, ya akayi hakan ta faru bansani ba?"

Basarake ya shanye kukanshi ya fara yimasa bayanin duk abunda yasani, Nuradden ya sauke ajiyar zuciya sannan yace,     "Alhmdllh"  da sauri Basarake yakai kallonshi gareshi fuskarshi cike tsantsar mamaki yace, 

"Alhmdllh?"  Nuradden ya d'aga masa kai yana d'an murmushi  sannan yace,      "Godiya nayiwa ALLAH akan nasarar da aka yi wajen kare maka matarka da ALLAH Ubangiji yayi, kaima ya kamata ace ka gode masa."

Basarake yayi masa kallon bakada hankali sannan ya tab'e bakinshi yace,     "baka ga abunda ke cikin  text d'in da kyau ba halan?"   Nuradden yayi d'an murmushi sannan yace,    "Nagani amma ban yarda da hakan ta faru ba?, saboda kai ba mazinaci bane haka ma baka tab'a b'ata 'yar kowa ba balle matar wani, don haka ALLAH bazai bari kaima a tab'a matarka ba, saboda farin cikin wasu wawaye da masu fito na fito k'addarar Ubangiji, kada ka bari wannan ya shiga k'wak'walwarka ya hana jin dad'i da matarka, saboda kafa hujja da zancen Dr's da kace ta dubata, saboda shifa g gn fyad'e ba abu bane mai sauk'i da har za'a keta haddinta da k'arfi amatsayinta na cikakkiyar budurwa ace ba'a ga jini ba, ko kuma wani signing da zai nuna anyi hakan ko da ko canzawar tafiyarta ne, kayi tunani ya kaga tanayinta bayan ta dawo hayyacinta?, haka ma ya kaga yanayin tafiyarta a lokacin?"

Basarake ya sauke ajiyar zuciya sannan yace,     "To ya aka yi ita da bakinta ta tabbatar man na karb'i budurcinta?"      Nuradden yayi shiru sannan ya d'ago fuska cike da damuwa yace, 

"Kayi hak'uri ka k'ara saka wata kwararriyar likita ta k'ara binka maka lafiyarta, idan kaji gaskiyar zance shikenan sai muyi solution d'in matsalar cikin ruwan sanyi."

 Basarake yayi shiru yana nazarin har sau nawa wasu mutane zasu ta kalle masa k'ofar mata?, shifa wallahi ya gaji don ko wanda ya faru a baya bada son ranshi bane, ganin kamar bai aminta da zancen bane yasa Nuradden yace,      "Idan kuma hakan bai yimaka ba to ka je k'a gwada da kanka?"  yayi saurin kallon Nuradden sannan ya mik'e tsaye jiki ba kuzari yace,   

Bazan tab'a zuba tsabtataccin ruwana cikin gurbatattu ba, haka kawai aje tsautsayi ya gifta a sami ciki a shiga kokwanton nawa ne ba,  dole ne na bata lokaci har zuwa iya watannin da ake tantance tsalkin mace, haka kuma dole ne in kaita asibiti ayi mata awon H. R. V kafin na kusanceta, don walillahil azim  bana ra'ayin kusantarta, duk ko da Zuciyata take ta azalzalata akan in aikata hakan, nafi son inyita had'iye kwadayina har zuwa lokacin da gaskiya zata bayyana"

  Nuradden yayi shiru yana kallonshi sannan ya fesar da iska a bakinshi yace,   "Kayi k'ok'arin neman wadda zata duba maka matarka a tsanake, kada ka bari shed'an yayi wasa da tunaninka har  kayi dogon shak'o ba tareda ka daidaita da matarka ba, saboda yin hakan zai k'ara jefo wata matsalar da tafi wannan."

Basarake ya haresa yace,    "Kai aganinka akwai wata matsalar da zata fi wannan?"

Kai tsaye Nuradden yace,   tabbas akwaita matuk'ar ba zaka yak'i shed'an da bak'in kishin dake d'awainiya dakai ba, don WALLAHI ko rantsuwa nayi bazanyi kaffara ba shine yake wahalar da kai, sannan ka sani Munara kangararriyar macece komai zata iya aikatawa don tawargaza maku zaman lafiyar aurenku"

Basarake yayi kwanciyarshi akan kujera tareda rufe idonshi ruf yana nazarin maganganun Nuradden, tareda zurfafa cikin tunanin yanayin da yaga Munawwa bayan ta farfad'o, yana cikin nazarin ne har bacci yayi awon gaba dashi. 

Nuradden ya ciro wayarshi dake caji ya shige k'uryar d'aki yakira wata babbar Auntynsu, wadda suke uwa d'aya uba d'aya, tana d'auka yace da ita  " Aunty Binta don ALLAH wani taimako nike so kiyi mani, kije gidan Basarake ki gano muna Amaryarsa saboda bata da lafiya, kuma bata son asani don ALLAH  cikin hikima ki binciketa kiji matsalarta  zan kiraki inji yanda kuka yi, saboda kinga Aliyun nan duk ya dami kanshi akan rashin sanin meke damunta."

Cikin jin dad'i Auntyn tace,     "Da sai anyi sati nayi niyyar zuwa inga Amarya, amman bari in tashi inje, sai dai wannan babban al'amari ne da ba kowace mace take so a san dashi ba amman bari inje in jaraba."     yace  "yawwa Aunty don  kije saboda ya damu sosai sai munyi waya"

 
Ya kashe wayar ya fad'a wanka zuciyarshi cike da fatar ALLAH yasa komi lafiya, shiko tabbas sai yayi abunda zai kusanci matarshi dolenshi yau ko yana so ko baya so.

Munawwa kam ganin kuka ba shine mafita ba yasa taje ta d'oro arwallah ta fara karatun alk'ur'ani har sai da taji natsuwa ta ziyarci zuciyarta,sannan tayita jero addu'o'inta  ta shafa,  ta mik'e cikin wani kuzari  ta k'ara shafa 'yar powder da janbaki, ta fito d'akin ta nufi sashen Aliyunta, abun mamaki koda ta tura k'ofar tajita gam sai ta fara kwankwasar k'ofar, nanma taji shiru atake zuciyarta ta bata ya sauka k'asa saboda tuno cewar da yayi  ta fito suyi break akwai wad'anda zasu zo ganinta,  idanuwanta suka sauka saman dinyn dake falon, ganin kuloli reras yasa ta nufi wurin ta jawo kujera d'aya ta zauna, ta fara bubud'a kalolin kulolin ta zuba abunda take so, ta fara ci tana tunanin watak'ila yaci abunshi ya fita, to amman sai dai bata ga alamun antaba kulolin ba, bayan ta k'are ne ta sauko k'asan inda tun kafin ta k'arasa ta hango wasu kabilun mata biyar zaune k'asan tsadadden carpet d'in dake malale,sai guda d'aya wadda da alama ita bahaushiya ce,  wanda bata raba d'aya biyu akan ita suka jira, da fara'arta ta tarbesu yayinda suma suka yita rawar jikin gaisheta, bayan ta zauna ne babbar cikinsu ta fara yimata bayanin su 'yan aikinta ne, kowace da abunda zata dinga yi, tayi 'yar dariya sannan tace,    

"Wadda take a b'angaren dafa abinci zata iya komawa cikin masu gugar tyles, dafatar hakan bazai b'ata mata rai ba?"   suka yi saurin fad'in ba "komi madam" sannan ta sallamesu kowa ya tafi ya fara aikinshi,haka tayi ta tarbon bak'i har zuwa k'arfe sha biyu na rana, inda wata kamilallar mace ta shigo da sallamarta fuskarta d'auke da farin cikin ganin matar Aliyu, bayan sun gaisa a mutunce tace da ita, 

"Nasan baki gane ni ba, to ni yayar su Aliyu ce da Nuradden sunana Hajiya Binta"

Munawwa ta k'ara girman fara'arta tace

"Sannu da zuwa Auntynmu"   sannan ta mik'e da nufin kawo mata wani abu cikin kuzarinta, da sauri Auntyn tace   "Yi zamanki k'anwata keda na fahimci kamar ma baki da lafiya"      Munawwa tayi 'yar dariya tareda sunne kanta tana wasa da y'an yatsunta tace,      "Ni lafiyata k'alau Aunty"     Auntyn ta bita da kallo tana d'an murmushi tace,      "Haba k'anwata ko baki fad'a ba nasan bakya jin dad'in jikinki ina fatar samun sauk'i dai"

Munawwa ta d'ago tana 'yar dariya tace

"WALLAHI Aunty gaskiya na fad'a maki lafiyata k'alau ko ciyon kai bana ji, asalima ni garas nake jina Aunty ki bari kawai in sami wannan lada."       Auntyn tana 'yar dariya tace,     "To yayi ALLAH ya tara mu a ladar."    Munawwa ta mik'e tareda nufar wata k'atuwar abur sanyaya ruwa da tagani kai tsaye ta bud'a, ta d'auko mata kalolin lemuka tareda gorunan ruwa, sannan ta kira wata 'yar aiki  tace taje   kitchen ta d'auko mata cup's, sai gata ta kawo jikinta yana rawa Munara ta karb'a tafara zubawa Auntyn wani lemu sannan ta mik'a mata, tana 'yar dariya tace

"Bismillah Auntynmu"

Auntyn da tunda Munawwa ta tashi ta bi yanta da kallo har ta dawo bata dainakallonta ba, saboda a zahirin gaskiya Munawwa ta burgeta sosai Musamman Mutuncinta, cike da farinciki ta karb'i cup d'in tana  yiwa Aliyun Murnar samun mace mai kyau kuma  tagari mai halin kirki, aranta tace   _"ALLAH ya baiwa Nuradden irinki yar gidan girma mai halin kirki"_

 Sanna ta d'an kurb'a ta ajiye tace,   "Nagode k'anwata ALLAH ya baku zaman lafiya,  idan Aliyun yazo kice dashi tazo tana gaidashi."

Munawwa tace insha ALLAH sannan ta rakata har bakin k'ofar fita ta dawo, tana ta tunanin ya aka yi matar ta gano kamar tana da damuwa? wadda tayi kawaici ta alakantata da ciyo,cike da mamakin zurfin tunanin Aunty bintar ta koma sama, saboda lokacin SALLAH yayi taje ta dauro arwallah tayi sallah sannan ta shiga kitchen tana neman abunda zata dafawa Aliyunta, don ita zuciyarta tak'i amince mata da zargin kalar son da yakeyimata, tafi barin abun akan watak'ila bayada lafiya ne shiyasa ya shiga damuwa. 

Aunty binta kam tana fita ta kira Nuradden cike da jin dad'in abunda munawwa tayi mata tace,      "Ka sanar da Aliyu matarshi lafiya k'alau take baka ga yanda tayi hiduma dani ba, kamar ta dad'e da sanina kuma ta tabbatar man da cewa lafiyarta k'alau. "

Cike da jin dad'i Nuradden yace, 

"Aunty ko a tafiya ai ana gano mara lafiya don ya tabbatar man da bata da lafiya."

Aunty tayi 'yar dariya sannan tace,    "Lafiyarta k'alau don ko a tafiyarta zaka gano bata da wata matsala kace da Aliyu ya daina yarda da wayonta, ya saki da jiki ya kula da matarshi saboda na hango kunya cetasata tace masa bata da lafiya.",      Kit ta kashe waya,    Nuradden ya dawo falon ya taradda har lokacin bai tashi barcin ba, ya ja key d'in motarshi ya fice har lokacin da Aliyun ya tashi bai dawo ba, ganin lokacin SALLAH har ya gota yasa yayi arwallah ya tada SALLAH, yana cikin sallar Nuradden ya dawo da wasu ledodi ya ajiyesu saman glass table d'in yayi kitchen ya d'auko plate da cup's ya yi ajiye sannan ya kwabe rigarshi daga shi sai singlet da dogon wando, ya juyo wata kaza dahuwar ruwa ruwa tayi luguf tareda kayan had'inta, ya d'auko cup's d'in ya tsiyaya lemukan ga kowane cup's, sannan ya fakaici idon Aliyun ya jefa wasu k'wayu da zasu kai biyar haka sannan ya saka spoon ya motse, wanda yayi daidai da juyowar Basarake bayan ya k'are addu'o'i, ya kalleshi ya girgiza kai sannan yace, 

"Dad'i na da tuzuru kwad'ayi komai so yake."       Nuradden ya fara guma lomarshi yace,     "Bismillah mai aure."

Basarake yayi 'yar dariya sannan yakai hannunshi ga cup d'in da Nuradden ya ajiye gabanshi yace, 

"Tuzuru ko ka mance da matata a gida?"

Nuradden yayi saurin rik'e cup d'in yace, 

"Tashi kaje kada ka tab'a man komi tana jiranka"   yana k'are fad'ar maganar ya d'auke cup d'in ya zuk'e, ganin haka yasa Basaraken yin sauri ya d'auke d'ayan cup d'in ya kai bakinshi ko second biyu ba'a yi ba ya kwankwad'e, da nufinshi na yiwa Nuradden mugunta sai gashi bayan ya k'are shan ne yaji wani iri a bakinshi, ya d'auki gorar ruwa d'aya ya k'ara zuk'a sannan ya Mik'e yace,      "Ni na tafi.    Nuradden bakinshi a cike yace "sauka lafiya",  Basarake yaja key d'inshi ya fice yana k'ok'arin shiga motar ne yaji jikinshi yayi tsam! tsam! Tsikarshi ta fara tashi ba tareda ya damu  ba ya fad'a motarshi yayi gidansu inda a gurguje ya gayar da Ummi da kakale ya baro gidan, saboda yanayin da yake jin kanshi abun ba dama, don ko jarumarshi a tsaye take sambal tana haniniya, cike da mamakin kanshi ya isa gida jikinshi yana rawa ya fad'a d'akinshi, tareda shigewa toilet yayita sakarwa kanshi ruwa da nufinshi na samarwa da kanshi Natsuwa, amman banza abu sai k'ara yin gaba yake, dole ya fito toilet d'in ba shiri ya fara neman kwayunshi na rage disire  amman  bai gansu ba, ya zura doguwar jallabiya bak'a ya fito d'akin dafe da jarumarshi  kamar yace ihu,  saboda tsananin firgici da rud'ewar da ya shiga,don tsantsar sha'awar sai  k'ara raunana masa jiki take, yana cikin wannan yanayin ne ya fito zuwa d'akin Munawwa bai ganta ba, sai k'amshi da ya jiyo kitchen yana tashi jikinshi yana rawa yayi cen, ashe da rabon ya k'ara dulmiya don kuwa riga da siket d'in dake jikinta, sun kamata inda har d'uwawunta suka fito b'ul b'ul tayi duk'e tana goge hannunta da tissue, sannan ta juyo duk a duken dukiyar fulaninta duk rabi a waje batareda  ta kula dashi ba ta jefa a cikin dustbin, aiko d'agowar da zata yi ta hangoshi tsaye jingine da bakin k'ofar, idanunshi sunyi jajawur  sannan ga jarumarshi a cikin riga tayi tsinke, cike jin kunyarshi tazo zata rab'a ta gefenshi ta wuce, cike da wani bala'in k'arfin da bai ma san yana da irinshi ba ya fisgota ta fad'a jikinshi.
