*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*53*

Cikin tsoro ta zaro ido amman yanayin kallon da yake yimata da jajayen idanuwanshi,  dole tayi saurin janye idonta tareda kawar da kanta a gefe tana k'ok'arin k'watar kanta, amman yanayin rik'on da yayi mata ita kanta tasan bazata iya ba. 

Jinta a jikinshi ya saka jikinshi kyarma cikin zafin nama yakai bakinshi cikin nata yana tsotsa da k'arfi tamkar wanda zai cenye mata shi, cikin jin azabar yanayin tsotsar da yake mata,  ta fara tureshi amman kamar tana k'ara masa k'aimi, jikinshi sai b'arin wahala yakeyi sai saukar numshinshi kakaji a wahale, saboda shi kad'ai yasan abunda yake ji a jikinshi, ita kam tun tana juriya har takai bata iya tsayuwa, kafafuwanta sunyi sanyi tana k'ok'arin fad'uwa ne ya tallabot gaba d'aya a jikinshi, kai tsaye d'akinshi ya nufa da ita har lokacin jikinshi bai daina mazari ba, saboda k'waya d'aya ko biyu akesha amman don mugunta irinta Nuradden ya saka masa har biyar, da nufinshi na duk runtsi su hanashi zaman lafiya har sai yayi zai samu relief, aiko yana kaita kan gadon ya fara wancakali da kayan jikinta, sai da ta koma neck sannan ya fara murza na shanunta a haukace yana tsotsa, Munawwa kam saboda azaba bata san lokacin da ta fashe da kuka ba, sai ga hawaye suna biyar gefen da gefen fuskarta, sai da ya tabbar ta murzu sannan ya fara k'ok'arin saita jarumarshi k'asanta,ya fara shiga da k'arfinshi  a nufinshi tunda a bud'e take bazai sha wahalar lumewa ba, sai gashi yaji k'ofa tamau ba hanya cikr da mamaki ya k'ara kutsawa da k'arfinshi, Munawwa ta fasa k'ara saboda azabar da ta ratsata, daga shi har ita sun sha wuya dosai kafin ya sami hanyar da zai b'ullewa, yana samun sa'ar fasa k'ofar  ya fara hakarta da k'arfi yana kuwwa, saboda wani bala'in dad'in dake ratsa kwakwalwarshi, bakinshi yak'i yin shiru sai sambatun dad'i yake, Munawwa kam azaba da ta kai azaba kasa kukan fili tayi, saboda muryarta bata fita  sai dai hawayen da suketa ambaliya, shikam gogan dama yaji dad'i sai gashi da canza style's kala kala wanda har lokacin bai daina ihun ba, sai da ya d'auki awa biyu akanta sannan ya saurara mata bala'in da yake ji ya d'an fad'a, amman har lokacin jarumarsa a tsaye take k'ikam, tausayinta ne yasa ya saurara mata amman fa duk da haka ta gurzu iya gurzuwa, don kwance kawai take amman ko motsi ta kasa yi sai ruwan hawayen da suke ta malala tareda sheshekar kukanta da ko fita bayayi saboda azaba, Basarake ya kai kallonshi gareta zuciyarshi cike da tunanin ya aka yi hakan ta faru?, bayan shi yaga abunda idanuwanshi suka gani, Musamman shegen k'aton da ya gani yana k'ok'arin saita jarumarshi jikinta a ranshi yace, 

_"To ko dai bai samu sa'ar shiga bane ALLAH ya jefani? da gaskiyarka aboki nagari ALLAH bazai bari a tab'a man mata ba, Alhmdllh Allah"_

Yana k'are maganar ya janyota jikinshi cikin tsananin farin ciki ya rungumeta tsam tsam, yana sanya mata albarkoki kala kala, tareda yabon jarumtar da tayi masa duk da wannan ne karonta na farko amman tayi jarumta matuk'a, don shi kanshi yasan ta gurzu a hannunshi duk da har lokacin shi tashi disire bata kwanta ba, ta dai d'an rage kaso hamsin cikin d'ari na abunda yake ji, ya saka baki ya fara tsotsar breast d'inta a hankali, yana sha d'aya da hannunshi ba shiri ta fashe da kuka saboda zafin da suke yimata, amman aranta taji bazata iya hanashi ba komi ukubar da zata ji kuwa, aiko d'an kukan da take ya k'ara jefashi cikin wata muguwar sha'awarta, ba shiri ya koma jik'a alkalaminshi ta fasa k'ara yana ta lallashinta da wayo da dabara har ya k'ara jinshi normal sannan ya d'agata, Zuciyarshi cike da jin dad'in ganimar da ya kwasa, da dabara ya tada ita zaune da nufinshi na son suje ya gasa ta, amman tana jin ta a zaune ta fara cije leb'e tana runtsar ido saboda azabar da take ji, duk jarumtarta sai da ta kasa hakurewa ta koma ta kwanta tana kuka gwanin ban tausayi, hankalinshi ya tashi cikin jin tsoron kada ace yayi mata illah, ya duk'o bakin gadon saiti da fuskarta cikin muryar tausayi yace,    

"Na Ji maki ciyo ko?don ALLAH kiyi hak'uri da Aliyunki kinji?tashi muje in gasa maki wajen kada yayi tsami"      Munawwa ta ture masa hannunshi da yake murza kan nipples d'inta, cikin dusashiyar murya tace,      "sai na huta"

Yana 'yar dariya tareda shafa d'an k'aramin gemunshi yace,     "na baki wahala ko?"  ya k'are maganar cikin d'aga mata gira ba shiri ta juya fuskarta saboda kunyar da ya bata, ya zauna kan gadon yace,    "Muga wajen inga wace kalar b'anna ce nayiwa Abuna"   ba shiri ta yunk'ura zata tashi da k'arfi sai gata da fasa wata k'ara ta koma ba shiri, cikin fargaba ya sunkuceta tana tirjiya ya sauko da ita kan gadon, sannan ya rungumata a jikinshi tareda taimaka mata suka nufi toilet, duk taku d'aya idan tayi sai ta cije baki saboda ba k'aramar dagargaza yayiwa k'asanta ba, kai tsaye ya fara gasata da ruwan zafi masu zafin gaske,  tana runtsar ido tana jibi har ya k'are sannan  suka yi wanka a tare, wanda duk rabin wankan wayone yake YI mata yana lagudeta, a gurguje suka k'are suka yi arwallah ya ri'kota suka fito,  amman har lokacin tana jin zafi k'asanta sosai shiyasa ma ko tafiyar a hankali take yinta.    Sai da ya kaita har d'akinta ya taimaka mata ta saka wata doguwar riga marar nauyi, sannan ya d'auko hijab ya saka mata ya ja masu jam'in sallahr la'asar, duk da lokacinta ya wuce sosai sannan ya nufi kitchen domin yaga me ta dafa masu, duk da yaga abincin da Ummi ta aiko masu a k'asa lokacin da ya shigo, amman yafi son yaci na matarshi domin itama yaji nata d'and'anon girki, ganin kuloli yasashi bud'awa sai gashi da lumshe ido saboda kamshin da ya bugi hancinshi, ya d'aukosu tareda plate biyu da yagani ta kife, da alama wankesu tayi ya d'auko sauran tarkacen da zasu nema ya had'a a tire d'aya ya nufi d'akinta, koda yaje ya tatar daita a kwance ta k'udundune tana rawar sanyi, cike da fargaba ya ajiye tiren sannan yayi saurin zuwa ya rik'o hannunta, sai gashi yaji zafi rau ya ratsa shi, a kid'ime ya zauna tareda  rungumota a jikinshi yace,     "Wayyo na jawowa Munawyta zazzab'i, tashi muci abinci sai in nemo likita ta duba man ke"      Munawwa ta k'ara narke masa tareda zubowar wasu sababbin hawaye tace,     "Bana iya cinshi bakina ba dadi"

Yayi saurin d'agota yace,    "Da bana nan kinci abinci ne?"   ta girgiza masa kai yayi saurin fad'in,   "Wayyo ni na jawowa matata zazzab'i, ga yunwa ga nauyina da ta d'auke."

Munawwa ta rufe idonta cike da jin sabuwar kunyarshi, ya taimaka mata ta tashi zaune a hankali tana k'ok'arin shanye zafin da take ji, ya fara zuzzuba abincin k'amshi sai bugun hancinshi yake yi, ya saka spoon ya d'ebo yakai bakinta ba musu ta bud'e ya saka mata, yayita bata sai da tace ya isa sannan ya fara kaiwa bakinshi, ba shiri ya lumshe ido aranshi yace,     _"Yanda take da dad'i haka ma abincinta yake"_      Yana ci yana sambatu har ya kusa tada kular abincin, saboda dad'in da yayi masa sannan shima kanshi tun safe komai baici ba, sai d'an lemun da shawo gidan Nuradden, saboda bak'in cikin da ya shiga, sai gashi cikin hikimar Ubangiji yanzu komi ya wuce farin ciki ya mamaye bak'in cikin linkin ba linkin, don wannan dad'i da ya sha ko aurenshi na farko da yayi wata biyar da matar baisha shi ba, amman ita gashi rana d'aya ta shayar dashi zumar da misalta zakinta abune mai wahala. 

Suna nan wurin har aka kira sallah'r magrib suka yi sallahr sannan Munawwa ta lallab'a ta koma kan gado ta kwanta tareda dukunkunewa,  saboda sanyin da take ji yana ratsata, Basarake kam har lokacin yana kan sallaya yana yiwa ALLAH godiya da ya kare masa martabar matarshi ta kawo masa budurcinta ba tareda cikar bak'in nufin masu so su cutar dasu ba. 

Bayan ya k'are tofa addu'ar ne ya waigo yaganta tana rawar sanyi, gabanshi ya fad'i ba shiri ya k'araso jikin gadon ya dafa goshinta, jinshi da yayi da zafi rau yasashi mik'ewa da sauri yace, 

"Bari in nemo wata likita ta duba man ke" ya sakar mata peck a kumatu  ya fice da sauri, Zuciyarshi cike tunanin wazai kira?donshi baya huld'a da wasu mata cen sai in ta kama dole, wata zuciyar tace dashi 

_"Ka kira DR Hafis mana yazo ya duba maka ita"_     wata tace,     _"Wannan d'an iska bazan kirashi ba, don ko ganin da yayi mata na farko bada son raina ba, shege d'an iska namamajo"_

Ya k'are tunanin ya jawo wayarshi ya kira wata Nurse da suke harkar siye da siyarwa ta gwalagwalai da ita, yayi sa'a kira d'aya biyu ta d'auka bayan sun gaisa a mutunce yake sanar da ita matarshi ce ba lafiya yana so tazo ta duba masa ita, tace dashi ya bata minti ashirin  zata zo insha ALLAH, sannan yaji dad'i ya koma d'akinta ya zauna inda ya taradda Munawwa rufe da ido kamar mai barci, zamanshi keda wuya sai ga Wayar Nuradden ta shigo yana 'yar dariyar jin dad'i ya d'auka,   A cen gefen Nuradden ya fara fad'in,    "Ango kasha mai"    ya k'are maganar tareda fashewa da wata dariyar mugunta,   Aliyu ya kai kallonshi ga fuskar Munawwa ya shafi gemunshi yana 'yar dariya yace,    

"Kamar kasani na sha mannan gaskiya"   Nuradden cikin dariya  yace,     "Au duk angwancinne yasa ake ta kiran waya  baka d'auka?cab to a daibi yarinyar mutane a hankali don ALLAH."

Aliyu ya shafi kanshi cike da jin dad'in tuno ganimar da yasha yace,     "Ka tayani murna Abokina Matata Virgin ce, ashe duk hasashena zato ne kawai, bansan ya aka yi zuciyata tayi saurin amincewa da zatona ba"     Nuradden ya sauke ajiyar zuciya sannan yace    "ka godewa ALLAH  to da bai barka kayi abunda kayi niyya ba"    sannan suka yi sallama saboda kiran Nurse d'in da yaga yana shigowa wayarshi, yayi saurin d'aukar kiranta nata, kai tsaye ta sanar dashi gata a k'ofar gidan, ya kai kallonshi ga Munawwa sannan yace da ita ta jirashi yazo ya shigo da ita sannan ya kashe wayar ya fita. 

Munawwa da tayi lamo kamar mai bacci firar da taji yayi da Nuradden tasata fad'uwar gaba ba kad'an ba, a take zuciyarta ta bata waton ma ashe zarginta yakeyi, shiyasa ya shiga damuwar da ta ganshi da ita, ta tariyo maganganun da ya fad'a lokacin da take yi masa k'orafina don ya karb'i budurcinta yake yi mata hakan, ashe shi tunaninshi daban ne, kai tsaye ta fashe da kuka zuciyarta cike da bak'in cikin amince masa da tayi ya kusanceta, tana cikin kukan ne Aliyun ya shigo da dattijuwar nurse d'in, ganin kukan da take YI ne yasashi k'ara susucewa ya fara yimata sannu, Nurse d'in ma ta fara bata hak'uri tareda fara dubata, tayi mata allurai ta bata magunguna sannan tayi sallama dasu ta fice, Aliyu har zai rakota tace dashi yaji da matarshi kawai ALLAH ya bata lafiya, yaji dad'in wannan karamci da tayi masa yayi mata godiya, sannan ya koma kan gadon yana lallashinta tayi saurin fisge hannunta tareda nunashi tace,    "Koda wasa kada ka kuskura ka koma tab'ani ALLAH ko"   ta k'are maganar cikin fushi hankalinshi yayi k'ololuwar tashi zuciyarshi cike da mamakinta yace,    "Bangane abunda kike so in sani ba, nida Matata ayi man iyaka da ita?, kema kinsan abunda bazai tab'a yiyuwa bane"    Munawwa ta galla masa wata k'atuwar harara sannan tace, 

"Matar da kake zargin ba Virgin bace har wani abin tinkaho zakayi da ik'irarin kiranta da sunan matarka?"      Basarake kanshi ya sara ba shiri sai lokacin ya tuna ba k'aramar wauta yayi ba, na yin maganar da yayi da Nuradden a d'akin, duk da shi tsammaninshi kwana takeyi, cikin rud'ewa ya fara bata hak'uri sannan yace,     "Manufarki daban da tawa saboda haka kada ki canza man manufata, ke kanki kinsan bazan tab'a yimaki wannan zargin ba, don nafi kowa sanin tarbiyyarki ballantana da yanzu na k'ara tabbatarwa don ni na karb'i budurcinki da hannuna"    Munawwa ta fashe da kuka tace, 

"Ba wani dad'in baki da zaka yimani ni ka riga da ka cutar min da rayuwa tunda ka rabani da martabata alhalin kana zargin ni fanko ce, to WALLAHI kasani kayi kad'an ka k'ara  kusantata, ko wannan ma da nasan nufinka tun farko da ban amince da kai ba,kasani hannuna ma idan ka sake ka tab'a WALLAHI ALLAH ban yafe maka ba." 


😹😹Kina ruwa yarinya gara dai ki yafe yafi😹. 




D/Auta ce🤝🏻

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*54*

Basarake ya zaro ido sannan yayi 'yar dariya yace,      "Aliyunki ne fa"   ya k'are maganar cikin wani tsadadden murmushi  sannan ya kashe mata ido d'aya, duk da take cikin fushi amman kallon nashi ya saka tsikarta tashi yarrrr! Ta wayance da k'ok'arin juya kwanciya ta maida fuskarta gefe, yana ganin haka ya ri'ko hannunta tayi saurin fisgewa tareda nunashi tace,      "Baka ji me na fad'a bane?"  yana 'yar dariya yace,  "Banji ba ko kema tsakaninki da ALLAH ai kinsan bazan ji wannan kashedin naki na iska ba, don halal d'ita ce ke duk wani kashedinki ni a iska na daukeshi na ajiye, ko hak'urin da kika ga nayi dake a farko daliline ya janyo hakan,  amman yanzu da na san wacece Munawyta WALLAHI bazan iya d'aga maki k'afa ba, har sai na tabbatar ball d'ita ta fad'a cikin raga saboda haka kima ajiye komi mu fahimci juna, don nikam ba wani kashedinki da zai bani tsoro."   Munawwa tayi masa shiru idonta rufe  kamar mai bacci aranta tace,     _"Me yasa duk girman wannan lafi  da yayi mani har yanzu ko d'ar banji a soyayyarshi ba?"_    tayi tsaki aranta saboda jin haushin kanta da takeyi,    shi kam yana ganin haka ya zagayo ta gefen fuskarta, cikin murya mai kamar rad'a yace,    "Da kin tashi munyi SALLAH kici abinci kafin kiyi baccin."  idonta a rufe ta b'ata fuska tareda turo d'an k'aramin bakinta, aiko hakan da tayi ya tsokaloshi kafin ta tantance taji ya cafko bakin da nashi, sai tsotsa yakeyi tana tureshi da d'an kingin k'arfin da ya rage mata, amman bai daina ba har ma da guzurin murza nashanunta, wad'anda har lokacin basu daina yimata zafi ba aiko sai gata da fashewa da kuka ba shiri, sai da ya gaji don kanshi sannan ya mik'e tsaye da jajayen idonshi yace,    "Ki tashi kiyi SALLAH kici abinci sannan kisha magungunanki."  yana k'are maganar ya fice d'akin don baya son ya biyewa zuciyarshi ya k'ara fama mata ciyo, tabishi da kallo har ya fice sannan tayi shiru tana nazarin zargin da yayi mata, wai ace duk soyayyar da ta nuna masa wai ashe shi dubinta yake ba cikakkiyar mace ba, ranta ya k'ara b'aci cikin fushi ta mik'e taje toilet ta d'oro arwallah tayi SALLAH, ko abincin bata ci k'ara ci ba taje ta rufo d'akinta da key ta haye gadon abunta tareda kudundunewa saboda sanyin Ac'n dake ratsata, don har lokacin bata daina jin sanyin ba kafin kace me har tayi bacci, shikam misalin k'arfe goma ya nufo d'akin da shirinshi na bacci amman yaji k'ofa gam gam, ba tareda ya b'ata lokaci ba ya koma d'akinshi zuciyarshi cike da ganin wautarta, don da zai iya da ya fad'a mata gaskiya koda zata rage ganinsa a matsayin wanda bai aminta da ita ba. 

***

Mummy kam tun ranar bukin Munawwa tana gidan ALHAJI Baba, saboda bayan ya koma gida ne haushinta ya taso masa, saboda rufa rufar da aka yi da ita tana sane aka d'aura auren 'yarta amman shi bata sanar dashi ba, sannan tazo tayi masa k'aryar Hajiya Hindu ba lafiya sosai tana asibiti ashe tasan bukin 'yarta zata je, da ma abun yayi mata dad'i yana wurin Balkisa ta kirashi tana sanar dashi wai a Asibitin zata kwana da ita, don baya son ya b'atawa  Balkisa rai yasa kawai ya kashe wayar batareda yace da ita k'anzil ba. 

 Aiko shin bai san inda Babynshi ta taffi ba yasa Kai tsaye ya  dannawa Mummyn  kira, lokacin idonta biyu suna firarsu da Munawwa kiran ya shigo, har taso karta d'auka wata zuciyar tace d'auka kiji aiko tana d'auka ya fara balbaleta da ruwan masifa sannan yace, 

"Kada ki kuskura ki tako k'afarki gidannan,idan an tashi kai munawwarar gidan Miji a had'a dake ki tamata zaman auren."     Kit ya kashe wayar yana huci tareda tsinewa Aliyu kwando kwando, don aganinshi duk shine tsanin tarwatse masa gida, gashi Baby duk iya neman da yayi mata bai ganta ba, shikam ina zaisa kanshi yaji sanyi, atake wata zuciyar tace dashi,    _"Wurin 'yar Amaryarka Balkisa kaima ka gaggauta akawo maka ita gidanka domin ka rage kaso hamsin a cikin d'ari na damuwarka."_

Zuciyarshi ko tayi Amanna da hakan kai tsaye yasa aranshi ranar monday insha ALLAH shima a ranar zai angwance, zuciyarshi cike da d'okin zuwan ranar yayi bacci, duk da wani sashen na zuciyarshi cike yake da taikaicin Hajiya Asiya, da fargabar halin da 'yarshi zata shiga. 

***

Taly kam a k'agauce ta isa gidanta inda har lokacin Munara da Nucy suna gidanta, sai gata ta shigo afujajan da firgici a fuskarta duk da wani gefe na zuciyarta cike yake da jin dad'in abunda tayi, don ta jefi tsuntsu da dutse d'aya, ganinta yasa duka suka to kanta suna tambayarta an k'are aikin, jikinta yana rawa ta fara had'a kayanta tace dasu,     "Abuja zanje da wani saurayina yanzunnan."  dukansu suka had'a baki da cewa,    "Abuja?"  tace "yes Cen zanje"   Nusy tace 

"Aikinmu fa da fatar komi ya kammala an gama muna komai?"

Tace   "Aikinku ya kammala sai dai kamar akwai matsalar da zata biyo baya  saboda Mijin Amaryar ya zo ya tafi da su Niga saboda haka kuma idan balli ya tashi kuna cikin matsala, saboda haka kuyi gaggawar barin gidannan nikam garin ma zan bari, kafin a tatsi su Niga su fad'i gaskiya azo ayi muna wulak'anci a banza."

Cikin Tsoro Nusy tace,   "Nashigesu ni Nusy idan aka kamani bansan ya zanyi da rigimar Abbana ba."

Munara tayi 'yar dariya sannan tace,    "Kefa bak'in tsoro ne dake ni kin ganni WALLAHI ko kad'an bana danasanin abunda nayi, ke nifa WALLAHI nama first son gaskiya ta bayyana asan ni nasa a aikata, don kawai in dasa masu bak'in cikin da har su bar duniya bazasu daina jin haushina ba."

Taly ta jawo jakarta jikinta yana rawa tace, "To guy's ni zan fece kuma ku nemi mab'oya duk da nasan sai ina nan ne naku asirin zai tonuwa, ku fito in rufe gidana duk yanda akayi zamuyi waya."    Suka fito tare ta dantse gidanta ta fad'a mota kamar wadda zata tashi sama ta barsu k'ofar gidan, Munara ta kalli Nusy tayi 'yar dariya saboda yanda duk tabi ta tada hankalinta, sannan ta jawo hannunta tace,   "To farar kura zo muje tun kafin 'yan sandan suzo kama Taly su tafi dake, donni ko kad'an bana shayinsu WALLAHI."    Aiko Nusy jikinta yana rawa suka bar gidan Zuwa School, lokacin ma har ankusa k'are masu lectures bayan an tashi ne Nusy tace, 

"Amman dai ke gida zaki je ko?"     Munara tayi mata wani shegen kallo  tace,    "Ance maki halan zan koma gidanku ne Abbanki yayita tozartani gaban Mutanen unguwarku? Kinfi kowa sanin gatan da nike dashi a gidanmu, ballantana ki nuna bakya muradin zamana gidanku inji zafi."   Nysy tana 'yar dariyar wayancewa tace,    "Ba haka nike nufi ba k'awata ganin nayi gara kawai kije a ganki koda hankalin Daddy zai kwanta."

Munara ta yi mata wani kallon uku saura kwata  sannan buga mata tsaki ta tari Napep ta shige ta barta anan Wurin, Zuciyar Nusy cike da fargabar abunda zai je ya dawo in har wannan asiri ya tonu. 

Munara kam kai tsaye gidansu ta wuce inda ta tarar da wani sabon matashin maigadin gidansu, inda yana ganinta tsikarshi ta tashi yarr! Don shigar da ke jikinta ta k'ananan kaya ta fito mata da halittarta a fili, k'irjinta ya ciko ga ukunta kamar su fad'o saboda girgizar da suke yi, saboda tsaye yake sake da baki yana kallonta har ta shige gidan, duk da ta gano tass kallonta da yake amman kasancewar baya gabanta yasa tayi kamar ma batasan yanayi ba, amman aranta tayi alwashin had'uwarsu ta gaba dashi akwai kallo. 

Kai tsaye d'akinta ta nufa ta sullo wanka ta fito tayi sallah a gurguje tayi kitchen, tana cikin dafa indomie ne taji k'arar shigowar motar Daddy gidan, ta juye indominta ta fito falo ta zauna ta fara ci kenan sai gashi ya shigo, ganinta da yayi ba k'aramin dad'i yaji a ranshi ba, cike da murnar ganinta ya k'araso falon bakinshi yana rawa yace,     "Ina kika kwana jiya? Kika tada man hankali yanzu haka daga rangadin nemanki na fito."  ya k'are maganar tareda zaunawa yana kallonta cike da jin dad'in ganinta, Munara ba tareda tace dashi k'anzil ba taja plate d'in abincinta ta shige d'akinsu ta dantse, ran Daddy ya b'ace saboda ganin renin wayon da tayi masa, amman don kawai ya wanke laifinshi yaje d'akinshi ya d'auko himilin kayan da Nuradden ya kawo wa Munawwa yazo kofar d'akinta ya tsaya, sannan ya saka wani safaya key ya bud'e d'akin saboda ta zare key d'in  a jikin k'ofar, tana cin abincinta ya shiga ya fara ajiye kayan sannan ya zauna, yana kallon ko in kular da tayi masa ranshi ya k'ara b'aci, amman ya dake ya fara kiran sunanta yace,      "Ga kayan da marasa kunyar yaran cen suka kawo wa Munawwara, ki d'auki abunki duka na bar maki."    ya saka hannu aljihu ya fiddo key d'in sabuwar motar da Aliyu ya saiwa Munawwa da sunan Nuradden ya mik'a mata, cike da mamaki ba tareda ta karb'a ba tace,   "Makullin mene?"  cikin jin dad'i yace, 

"Key d'in motar da aka baiwa Munawwara ne, don haka ki karb'a  na baki motar kyauta ga key d'inta kiyita hawan abarki kina zuwa School"

 

S/Yamma daughter 👌🏻

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*55*

Munara tabi hannun Daddy da kallo Zuciyarta cike da farin ciki, sai dai har aranta taji haushin ace wai albarkacin Munawwa ne zata ci, amman kasancewar tana bala'in son Motar yasa ta karb'a, tareda yi masa godiya fuskarta a had'e don ita yanzu ba komai ne yake burgeta ba, tunda ta rasa Haidar haihata haihata don ita kanta tasan ya riga da yayi mata nisa, sai dai kuma da ta tuna motar zata taimaketa wajen zirga zirgar cikar burukanta na wargaza zaman lafiyarsu, taji dad'i sosai wanda har ya bayyana akan fuskarta wanda sai a lokacin Daddyn yaji dad'i a ranshi, saboda fushinta yana sakashi fargabar kada taje ta aikata abunda zai zo ya damesu duka,  shiyasa cikin fara'a yace da ita,   "Ki duba kayan sunyi maki?sai ki fiddo wad'anda zaki kai d'inki in baki kud'in kije ki kai."    Munawwa ta yatsina fuska sannan tace,    "Nifa Daddy kasan wad'annan tarkacen ba damuna suka YI ba amman zan zab'i wasu in kai d'inkin koda ALLAH zaisa inji ina sha'awar sakawa."

Yace   "Yawwa my daughter bari in turo maki kud'in d'inkin a account d'inki sai ki k'ok'arta k'i kai a d'inke maki su gobe ko jibin nan, saboda kiji dad'in tarbon Auntynki Amarya da zata zo kwanannan, don naso ace goben AYI komi agama, to ta nemi alfarmar abari sai ranar jumu'a sai ayi bikin ta tare,yanzu haka da na tashi zanje in nemo masu aiki su k'ara gyara abunda duk keson canji a gidannan, sannan a fente muna gidan duka gaba d'aya." 

Munara tayi sakato tana kallonshi kamar wata gaula, cike fad'uwar gaba tace

"Wai da gaske Daddy auren zakayi?"   yana 'yar dariya ya mik'e tsaye yace,     "Da gaske Baby har ma na kaiwa Balkisu Sadakinta da lefenta ranar jumu'a za'a d'aura aure kuma a ranar za'a  kawota."

Munara ta bishi da kallo har ya fice haka kawai taji wasu hawaye,  suna gangaro mata a fuska aranta tace,     _

_"Shikenan Daddy zaka kawo wadda zata raba ni da kai, Mummy ma ta tsaneni balle wata cen daban?"_

Sanin ba mai bata hak'uri yasa tayi shiru dolenta, amman taci alwashin wajibi ne sai taci uban ko wacece zata shigo, matuk'ar taga take taken  zata shiga tsakaninta da Daddynta. 

***

Mummy kam tana gidan ALHAJI Baba matanshi suna nan nan da ita, ga yaran gidan da suke bala'in girmamata bata da damuwar komi sai ta kewar Munawwara, musamman da Alhaji Baban ya goya mata baya da ta fad'a masa duk abunda yake faruwa, da kanshi yace tayi zamanta anan ta barshi da 'yar son tashi  da kuma matar da zai aura, har sai sun koya masa hankali sannan idan ya gano gaskiya ya nemeta, to a lokacin sai tayi hak'uri ta koma tunda ba sakinta yayi ba, aiko ta saki jiki da matan gidan da yaran gidan tamkar daman cen sun saba zama tare. 

***

Munawwaa kam cikin barci taji wani zafi ya ziyarceta duk ta jik'e da jib'i sharkaf, saboda zazzab'in dake jikinta ya sauka sannan ga wata yunwa dake kwakular cikinta, dole ta diro kan gadon taje toilet ta YI wanka sannan ta fito daga ita sai towel da nufinta na samarwa da cikinta wani abun tabawa,   don bata iya jurar kai safe ba tareda taci wani abun ba. 

Ta bud'e d'akin ta fito kai tsaye ta nufi kitchen,  Basarake da ya kasa bacci ya fito falon ya kwanta, saboda baya son ya je wajenta ya takurata, don matuk'ar ya je bazai barta hakanan ba sai ya moreta gashi bata da lafiya, aiko kwanciyarshi kenan yana tunanin hukuncin da zai yankewa Munara wanda bazai tab'a zuciyar Mummy ba, yana cikin nazarin ne yaji k'arar budewar k'ofarta, ba shiri ya bud'e idonshi yana kallonta cikin d'an hasken da yayi saura har ta shige kitchen, a take yaji wata mahaukaciyar sha'awarta tazo masa,  ya yi murmushin mugunta ya shige d'akinta yayi kwanciyarshi akan gado ya lafe, zuciyarshi cike da farin cikin ganimar da zai kwasa, saboda yanayinta duk ya nuna zazzab'in ya sauka ta sami saukin har tana neman abunda zata ci, Munawwa kam a gurguje ta had'a tea mai kabri ta fito sad'af sad'afa ta shige d'akinta ta dantse, sannan tazo bakin gefen gadon ta zauna ta fara kurb'a a hankali tana runtse ido, har ta k'are tana k'ok'arin ajiye cup d'inne taji anyimata cakulkuli, a razane ta juya tareda fasa k'ara idonta suka yi tozali dashi yana saukowa kan gadon daga shi sai d'an trouser ga jarumarshi ta tona asirin yanayin da yake ciki, ba shiri jikinta ya fara kyarmar tsoro tareda b'ata fuska ta nunashi da d'an yatsa tace,    "Kada ka matso kusa dani, kada kayi gangancin zuwa wajena."  tana yin baya yana biyarta tareda yin  Murmushin ganin wautarta, na haramta masa abunda ya zama halalinshi, har ta kai jikin ginin ta tsaya shima ya isa wurinta,  tareda fisgo towel d'in dake jikinta cikin zafin nama ya cillar dashi, da gudu ta nufi  inda yake ta duk'a da nufin ta d'aukosu,  ya duk'o ta bayanta ya zura hannu ya damk'o na shanunta duka da k'arfi, dole ta fasa d'aukar towel d'in ta mik'e tsaye fuska a had'e saboda azabar zafin  da taji ya ziyarceta ba shiri,tana k'ok'arin kwacewa daga rik'on da yayi mata,  shima ya had'e fuska tamkar bai tab'a dariya ba haka ma bai fasa murzar nashanunta ba cikin k'warewa tareda saukarda numfashi sama sama, alamar yana jin dad'in yanayin, ganin sai k'ara rikita mata kwakwalwa yake da soyayyarshi mai zafi, ta fara  k'ok'arin kwatar kanta ya biyeta har ta jigata, cikin wata dusashiyar muryarshi k'asa k'asa yace da ita,    "Ki tausayawa kanki k'i bari ayi komi a hankali, don WALLAHI baki isa ki hanani hak'k'ina ba"    duk da yanayin da yayi maganar yasa jikinta yin sanyi amman hakan bai hanata fasa k'watar kantan da take so tayi ba, ganin hakan yasa k'arfi ya dauketa kamar wata 'yar k'aramar yarinya ya jefata gado, tana k'ok'arin tashi ne yabita ya danne sannan ya bankareta, da hannunshi duka biyu ya saka k'afarshi d'aya ya ware k'afafunta, sannan ya saita jarumarshi tareda addu'ar saduwa da iyali a zuciyarshi, cikin shauki ya zurata da k'arfi har ya lume cen koli, sannan ya ci gaba azabtar da ita da niyyarshi ta bata wahala don gobe taji tsoron yi masa gardama, aiko Munawwa da wuri bai gama warkewa ba sai ga wata sabuwar azaba, sai kuka take tana birgima cikin jin zafin saukar sak'on da yake so isar,  domin kuwa gaba d'aya ya cire tausayinta aranshi sai da ya ladaftar da ita dakyau dakyau, tayi ta magiya tana bashi hak'uri tareda d'aukar alk'awalin bazata koma ba,  sannan ya d'agata ya koma gefe yana maida numfashin daddaya,zuciyarshi cike da kaunarta dake ta yawo lungu da sak'o na gangar jikinshi,  tareda k'ara jin kanshi sabon ango don har ga ALLAH ya mori amarcinshi da kyau, sannan ya koma lallashinta wajen yimata kalamanshi masu kwantar mata da zuciya, tun tana kuka har ta koma ajiyar zuciya tana sauraren kalamanshi masu kashe mata jiki da zuciya da ruhi gaba d'aya , don ita samna ce a fagen soyayyar Aliyunta duk rikicin da take tak'ama dashi sai da yasa ta ajiyeshi dolenta suka koma begen junansu.      Don taji tsoron yi masa tirjiya saboda gudun ya sauke muguntarshi akanta, amman fa zuciyarta sai jinjinawa jarumtarshi take yi, don k'wazon da ya nuna ya tabbatar mata da Aliyunta ko a cikin jarumman shi na daban ne, ga wani girmanshi da take gani a idonta saboda yasa ta gano ba irinshi ne mace zata yiwa rowar kanta ba, don zai iya saka mace ladabin da har zata durk'usa kasa ta gaidashi batareda ta sani ba . 

Koda suka tashi da safe dukansu cikin shauk'in son junansu suke, tareda k'ara tarairayar juna wanda yasa dukansu har k'arfe biyu ba wanda ya sauko k'asa suna manne tamkar wasu tsuntsayen soyayya. 




 🏃🏻‍♀️🏃🏻‍♀️🏃🏻‍♀️🏃🏻‍♀️


D/Auta Ce🤭

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*56*

Basarake dai yana manne 'yar Amaryarshi suna nunawa junansu k'auna, izuwa lokacin shak'uwa ta k'ara shiga tsakaninsu sosai, don ko kitchen tare suke shiga su girka abinda suke so,don ya hanata girka na gida gaba d'aya wai acewarshi kada wani yaci abincinta yaji dad'i har ma ya yabeta, sai dai yasata at fidda masu aikin da zasu girka abincin ma'aikatan gidan gaba d'aya,  haka ma abinci idan zasu ci ciyar da junansu sukeyi basu yarda wani yaci da kanshi ba, suna ci suna wasarsu ta masoya, haka ma wanka tare sukeyi tun tana jin kunya har ta ware dolenta, don Aliyunta baya da kunya kokadan a fagen nuna mata gaskiyar soyayyarshi, don ko fita ya daina yi cikin kwanakin iyakacinshi yaje ya gaida Alhajinshi dasu Ummi, kuma da yaje baya dad'ewa yake dawowa wajen Munawynshi, kakale  har ta ganoshi ta fara yi masa tsarguwa, Ummi kam iyakacinta dariya saboda jin dad'in ganin yanda hankalinshi ya fara kwantawa waje d'aya, don har fari ya k'ara da wata 'yar k'iba da ya fara azawa, duk da daman cen shi ba siriri ne sosai ba yana da kibarshi dai dai jikinshi. 

Munawwa ma ba baya ba don kuwa fatarta ta k'ara gogewa,  tayi mulmul abunta gwanin burgewa, ga hips d'inta ya k'ara bud'ewa tareda turowar bombom d'inta masha Allah, haka ma dukiyar fulaninta tantsan cike fam da kirjinta, duk da daman cen ita da Munara ALLAH ya albarkacesu da Cikar nonuwa gwanin burgewa, bata da wata matsala a yanzu sai ta kewar gida da takeyi, duk ko da wayar da takeyi da Mummy da sauran matan gidan akai akai. 

  ***

Ranar jumu'a Basarake da Nuradden suka shirya zasu je kaduna akan case d'in, Munawwa taso aje da ita amman yace ba yanzu ba, tace dashi to ya kaita wurinsu Kakale ta yini acen kafin su dawo, aiko tun da asuba  ta shige kitchen tana ta aikin abincin da take so ta kaiwa su Ummi, cikin barci ya jiyo k'amshi ya ziyarci hancinshi, koda yazo kitchen d'in yaganta sai aikinta take yi hankali kwance cikin k'warewa, ya k'araso ya rungumota ta baya tareda aza kanshi saman kafad'arta yana kallon yanda take aikin, cikin siririyar murya yace, 

"Keda zaki fita dafuwar abincin me zakiyi kuma?"   tana 'yar dariya tace,     "Shikenan sai inje masu hannu biyu? a'a gaskiya bazan iya ba, kana kallon yanda Ummi keta hidima damu fa duk da kace ta daina bamu abinci muna dafawa bata fasa aiko muna ba, aiko muma yakamata mu nuna tamu bajinta, kuma ina so suci abincina nima su saman albarka" 

Basarake ya ji dad'in maganganunta ranshi fes ya k'ara yarda dacewa shi mai sa'a ne, saboda ALLAH ya bashi mace mai kyau kuma da kyakkyawan halaye, haka ma uwa uba mai sonsa da zuciya ta gaskiya wadda ita kokadan dukiyarshi bata gabanta, itadai shi d'in take so ba wani abun hannunshi ba, sab'anin matarshi ta farko da idanuwanta suka rufe ruf da son abin duniya, duk ko da itama ba d'iyar talakawan bace iyayenta suna da abun hannunsu dai dai gwargwado, sai dai ko kad'an basu kama ko rabin rabin kafarsu a dukiya ba. 

Yana lakame da ita har ta kammala ayukanta, ta zuzzuba cikin wasu tsadaddun kuloli sannan ya jata suka k'ule d'aki da dabara ya sha romonshi sannan sukaje suka yi wanka. 

Bayan sun shirya ne sai tashin k'amshi sukeyi fuskokinsu cike da annuri suka d'auko kulolin da kansu suka sauka katafaren  falonsu na k'asa, inda sauran ma'aikatan suka fara gayar dasu tareda  rige rigen karb'ar kulolin dake hannunsu cikin ladabi, saboda ya hana kowace mai aiki shigar masu sama sai mutum d'aya, wannan bahaushiyar da ita kad'ai ce bahausa acikinsu, ko ita ma natsuwarta yasa ya amince data shiga ta dinga shara da goge goge, amman ko kitchen d'insu idan ta shiga bai wuce ajiye kaya ta fito, saboda  wanke wanke ma Munawwa take yin abunta. 

Kai tsaye suka fito gidan yana wani sham k'amshi saboda ganin matarshi da yaga mazan dake aiki gidan suna yi, suna cikin mota yayi shiru sai tuk'iinshi yake yi, Munawwa ta d'ora hannunta saman nashi tace,    "My had'e fuskar kuma na menene?"   Ya cire fularshi ya d'an shafa kanshi a hankali, sannan ya furzar da iska abakinshi yace,

"Har ga ALLAH ina jin zafin inga wani ya kalleki, da ace ina da iko ba wani Namiji da zan bari ya ganki WALLAHI, amman ko yanzu dole ne in tanadar maki manyan hijabs da nikafs da yawa da safunan hannu da ta k'afa, don WALLAHI zuciyata raguwa ce wajen jure ganin wani k'aton banza ya kalle mani ke haka kawai."  

Munawwa tayi murmushin jin dad'i tace,      "Sannu Akuri sarkin aure sai kace Matar taka  tafi  kowace mace"

Yayi saurin kallonta tareda maka mata harara yace,     "Ko haka nace ai banyi k'arya ba, don ni dai tawa kawai nasani ba abunda ya shalleni da sauran wasu mata don haka yanzunnan da na ajiyeki zanje in siyo komi da na fad'a kafin in bar garinnan."


Munawwa ta rufe baki tana 'yar dariya tace, 

"Ka kosan ban tab'a saka Nikaf ba amman dai yana burgeni, ashe da rabon d'an sunnar mijina yasakani saka su"    ta k'are maganar cikin dariya, yasha mur yace     "ke kikaga abun dariya ji wancen shegen ke yake kallo don ubanka ALLAH ya isa WALLAHI, shege idan ka fad'i ai zaka gano zunubin kallon haram ba wasa ba"

Munawwa ta k'ume dariyarta saboda ganin yanda ranshi yake a had'e sai zabgar fad'a yake, har suka kai gidan bai daina mita ba, yana ajiyeta ko gidan bai shiga ba ya juya yaje siyo dogayen hijabban, dole Masu aikin gidan mata ne suka zo suka kwashi kayan suka shiga dasu. 


***

Daddy kam yau jumu'a yau Amarya take yinin bikinta, bayan an d'aura aurenshi da Amaryarshi Balkisa,  shima kam ba laifi ya tara abokanshi suka ci suka sha suna ta firarsu ta dattijai, Munara kam shigewa tayi sabuwar motarta ta nufi gidansu Nusy ta d'auketa suka garzaya yawon zagayen gari, don bata jurar jin hayaniyar da su Daddyn sukeyi na murnar wannan bak'in auren  da yayi a cewarta. 

  ***

Basarake kam bayan ya siyo ne yaje ya d'auko Nuradden gidanshi suka garzayo nan gidan,  tare suka d'auki ledodin kayan suka shiga dasu, bayan sun gaisa da su Ummi ne suke sanar dasu Kaduna zasu je amman ta jirgi don suyi saurin dawowa, sunyi masu fatar ALKHAIRI tareda yimasu addu'ar dawowa lafiya, ya kalli Munawwa yace ga kayanki nan mu zamu wuce,    tana jin nauyin su Ummi tace,   "Nagode ALLAH ya tsare hanya"   Kakale cikin tsokana tace 

"Kayanta ko namu nida ita?nifa bana son wariyar launin fata"  gaba d'aya suka saka dariya,   Basarake yana dariya ya ciro hijab d'aya ya saka mata, sannan ya d'auko nikaf ya d'aura mata kamar yanda yaga anayi, sannan yace,    "Kema ga naki kiji dad'in biyayyar aure"   dukansu suka kwashe da dariya, kakale cikin Nikaf tace

"Wayyo ni Bintu nidai na yafe a sauwak'e mani igiyata guda in dai har sai nayi ta saka wad'annan kayan zafi."

Aiko me zasuyi ba dariya ba musamman da ta cire Nikabin duk ta fara yin jib'i ido sunyi ja, haka dai suka yi ta barkwancinsu har suka yi sallama suka fita, Kakale tace da Munawwa,    "Ke bak'auyiya je ki raka mijinki mu ba ruwanmu kada kiji kunyarmu"   Munawwa ta rufe fuskarta cike da jin nauyin Ummi dake  wurin tana kallonta.   

Ganin tak'i tashi ta bishi yasa kakale tana ta yimata dariya tareda tsarguwar cewa,     "Yau naga Amarya irinta zamaninmu"

 

Princess Dija👌🏻

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*57*

Munawwa dai iyakacinta dariya don har ga ALLAH nauyinsu take ji sosai, sai dai kuma ganin yanda suke janta a jiki kamar 'yarsu yasa ta d'an fara warewa suna fira, musamman da kakale ta bud'a kulolin k'amshin dad'in girki ya bugi hancinta cikin jin dad'i tace,   "Ita wannan gara tawa da tawa ce?naga kuloli da yawa"   Munawwa tana 'yar dariya tace,  

"wancen na Abbanmu ne, wannann kuma na Ummi ke ga naki nan."  ta k'are maganar tareda d'auko mata kular ta ajiye gabanta, Cikin tsantsar farin ciki Kakale tace,    "A gaskiya wannan kishiya tawa bata da haushi, mun gode sosai ALLAH ya saka maki da ALKHAIRI ya k'ara baku zaman lafiya ya kawo zuri'a dayyiba."  Ummin sai Ameen take fad'i saboda ita kanta taji dad'in wannan karamci na Munawwa,ashe da gaskiyar hausawa da suka CE Alkhairi dank'o ne, don komi kankantarshi idan aka yi maka zaka ji dad'i koda ko ace kafi k'arfin abunnan. 

Ummi taje wajen Alhajin ta sanar dashi zuwan Munawwa zata shigo su gaisa, saboda Basarake da Nuradden kawai suka shiga wurinshi, aiko yaji dad'in ganinta musamman da ta durk'usa har k'asa ta gayar dashi sannan ta ajiye masa tashi kular, farin cikinshi yak'i boyuwa akan fuskarshi don ba k'aramin dad'i yaji ba, sannan ta fito zuwa d'akin Kakale inda ta tsiri aikin da gangan, goge nan share nan nad'e wad'ancen kayan duk ko da daman cen d'akin fes fes yake abunshi, Kakale sai jinjina mutuncinta take tana ta yiwa ALLAH godiya daya had'a d'an guda gudan jikanta da mace tagari, uwa uba kuma gata kyakkyawa masha ALLAH  son kowa k'in wanda bai samu ba. 

***

Basarake kam k'arfe d'aya  dam suna garin kaduna inda suka wuce kai tsaye zuwa police station, bayan sun kira Yusuf suna sanar dashi zuwansu ya samesu station, kai tsaye Basarake yasa a fiddo masu da 'yan iskan, koda aka fito dasu sunyi laushi tubus kowanensu ido ya rena fata, saboda azabar da suka sha a hannun 'yan sandan ba kad'an bace, ganin fuskokinsu yasa yaji sun d'an bashi tausayi sai dai kuma da ya tuna Munawynshi suka so yiwa fyad'e yaji gaba d'aya bai k'i ma ya gansu kwance rai a hannun Allah ba, nan take annurin fuskarshi ya b'ace yace da wani wanda yaga duk ya fisu jigatuwa yace,     "Wane shegen ne yasaku wannan k'azamin aikin?"   Jikinshi yana rawa murya a harde yace,   "Taly ce ta saka mu"  ba shiri k'irjin Basarake da Nuradden ya buga a lokaci d'aya saboda firgici, Cikin zaro Ido Basaraken ya sake cewa dasu,    "Wacece Taly kuma?"   kai tsaye wani wanda kamar ya fisu zafi yace,    "Wata 'yar iskar karuwa ce."ya k'are maganar cikin had'e rai, Nuradden ya gyara zamanshi tareda k'ara kallonsu d'aya bayan d'aya yace,    "A ina Talyn take?"   wani daga cikinsu ya fad'i sunan unguwar da take, kai tsaye aka bada umurnin aje a tafo da ita aka had'a 'yan sanda da wani d'aya daga cikinsu suka tafi, wanda tafiyarsu keda wuya Yusuf ya shigo da fara'arshi ya basu hannu suka yi musabuha, sannan suka fara gaggaisawa tareda tambayar juna mutanen gida, suna nan zaune suna 'yar fira sama sama sai ga police d'in sun dawo tareda tabbatar ma D. P. O cewa Taly bata gidanta a rufe yake, wata majiya ma da suka ji ance kamar ta kwana biyu bata garin, inji wani matashin saurayin dake unguwar tasu.     Amma an bada kambadar da anga dawowarta ayi sauri akai report ga 'yan sanda, Basarake yaji haushin rashin ganinta da ba'ayi ba sai dai ya bada Umurnin a sake wad'annan mutanen amman su rik'e wanda yaso farka masa mata akaishi gidan yari, yayi zaman kaso na shekara biyu da azaba mai tsanani, ya ciro kud'i masu uban yawa ya baiwa d p o sannan suka fito tareda wad'annan sauran k'attin tara, yace dasu SU shiga motar Yusuf dukansu tareda aron wasu 'yan sanda suka fito police station d'in, kai tsaye ya wuce dasu hotel d'in da zasu sauka, yasa aka ware masu d'aki d'aya dukansu aka basu abinci suka ci suka yi wanka aka basu wasu tufafi suka saka, sannan yace da yusuf ta ina zai gano masa inda Munara take?, Yusuf yana 'yar dariya yace,   "yanzunnan nagansu ita da k'awarta  wani wurin shakatawa suna futawa, Basarake yace dashi    "Kayi k'ok'arin kawo man ita hotel d'innan duk da nasan ba abu bane mai sauk'i ba, amman kaga wannan turaren da ka ganta kayi dubarar fesa mata shi a take zata sume, sai kayi dubarar d'auko muna ita a zuwan asibiti zaka kaita, dafatar ba zaka ji tsoro ba?."  Yusuf ya shafa keyarshi yace,     "Insha ALLAH baza'a sami wata matsala ba, ku bani minti goma kacal zan zo maku da ita sai dai ina son kabar abun fesawar ka bani mutum d'aya daga cikin wad'annan k'attin banzan muje dasu,  sannan a had'ani da wani d'an sanda d'aya don muji dad'in yi mata barazana"      dukansu suka ji dad'in wannan fasaha tashi, aka had'a shi da mutanen kamar yanda ya nema suka fice yana kokwanton wane irin hukunci ne Basarake zai yankewa Munara?, duk da jikinshi ya bashi ba zai iya cutar da ita ba duk ko da ta kasance mai laifi a wurinshi. 

Bayan fitarshi ne Nuradden ya kalli Basarake yanda yake safa da marwa a tsakiyar d'akin, fuskarshi ta tona asirin zuciyarshi da take ab'ace tamkar ranar ne abun ya faru, duk da wani sashe na zuciyarshi yana jin afuwa akan sassafcin laifin da suka yi masa, saboda samun Munawynshi da yayi a cikakkiyar budurwa, wanda ko hakan kawai ya isa ya wanke masu laifin da suka aikata, amman yazama wajibi ya tauna tsakuwa ko don aya taji tsoro. 

Nuradden yace dashi "Miye ma'anar sa azo maka da Munara?baka gudun ta kunna maka fitilar sharrinta?"

Basarake yana 'yar dariya yace,    "Fitilarta d'in  banza don nima ai inada k'arfin iskan da zan hure mata fitullun har sai sun d'auke d'if ko hannunta bata isa ta gani ba."  yana k'are maganar ya fita zuwa d'akin wad'ancen k'attin, yayi wata magana dasu sannan ya dawo ya zauna yana 'yar dariyar mugunta, har Yusuf ya kirashi a waya yace dashi gasu nan a bakin hotel d'in ta kafe tace bazata shigo ba, Yace dashi d'an sandan yayi mata burga kamar zai halbeta ya sakota a gaba su shigo ya kaita d'akin da kattinnan suke shima zai zo, aiko suka taso k'eyar Munara da k'awarta Nusy inda ta jik'e siket d'inta sharkaf da fitsari saboda tsoro,

Mutane suka fara maido hankulansu garesu ganin yanda aketa artabu da ita akan bazata shigo ba, dak'yar aka taso keyarsu zuwa d'akin bayan d'an sandan yayiwa shugaban mai kula da hotel d'in cewa ba wani abu kada su tada hankalinsu, aiko Munara da Nusy suna yin tozali da fuskokin wad'annan k'attin hankalinsu ya tashi, saboda ganin asirinsu ya tonu anganosu, kai tsaye wani daga cikinsu ya dantse k'ofar  d'akin  ya fara zare wando ya tunkarota jarumarshi a mik'e yana dariyar shakiyanci, a rud'e Munara ta mik'e jikinta yana rawa tace,    "WALLAHI kayi gangancin tab'a ni sai na hallaka ka sai dai in k'are rayuwata a zaman prison,  amman bazan bari ka rabani da mutuncina ba"  jin abunda ta fad'a yasa dukansu suka tub'e zigidir suka durfafota gaba d'ayansu, daga mai falla mata mari ya fisge mata d'an gyalen dake kanta,  sai mai  jawo rigarta yana k'ok'arin cirewa, dambe sosai suka sha da ita kafin su tub'e ta daga ita sai pant da bra, suma basu barta dasu ba sai da suka fincikesu duka , bayan jibgar da tasha a hannunsu duk sun buge mata damtse, ta kai gaba d'aya sai da ta galabaita bata iya wani katabus sai kukan da take tareda rok'arsu suyi hak'uri saboda gaba d'aya sun rufu kanta kamar zasu cenyeta danya,  Nusy kam tana rakub'e tana kukan tausayin k'awarta, saboda tunda tazo da nufin cetonta aka mazga mata marora taja baya dolenta,  Munara kam tun tana da k'arfin kare kanta har ta galabaita sosai  sai nishin wahala take saukewa saboda ta rigada ta sadakar akan cewa shikenan ta rasa budurcinta, sai kawai aka fara kwankwasa k'ofar  taga sun fara mayarda kayansu, suna mata dariyar mugunta aiko da sauri Nusy taje wajenta tareda yin sauri ta mayar mata da rigarta da dogon wandonta, Munara kam sai kuka take gwanin ban tausayi, saboda gaba d'aya jikinta ko'ina ciyo yake ga wani jini dake bi ta gefen bakinta  saboda maroran da tasha, ana bud'e k'ofar  ne sai ga Basarake ya shigo fuskarshi d'auke da murmushi, yana jinjina masu sannan ya kalleta ido cikin ido yace,    "Madam Munara yanzu kin gano bakin rijiya ba wurin wasar yara bane ba ko?, koko Kin d'auka ke kad'aici kika iya had'a sharri?wanda saboda tsabar rashin imaninki ko 'yar uwar haihuwarki bata tsallakewa muguntarki ba, saboda kawai biyan buk'atar kanki kike k'ok'arin jefa zuciyoyi da yawa adamuwa, to bari kiji this is the first and last da zaki k'ara shiga hurumina nida matata Munawwara, idan kunne yaji to gangar jikinki ya tsira don WALLAHI ko yanzu darajar ciki d'aya da kuka fito da Munawyta yasa zan kyaleki, da sai nasa wad'annan k'attin da kuka yi hayarsu sun farka maki jiki gutsi gutsi tamkar tsohuwar takarda, amman kisani haduwata dake agaba zaki rena kanki da kanki, matuk'ar baki zubar da makaman yak'in zaluncin naki ba, wanda har kike da k'arfin zuciyar turo man text, kina ik'irarin ke kika aikata kuma bazaki barmu muji dad'in aure ba."

Sannan yaje har inda take tsaye tana rabar ido cikin tsawa yace da ita,     "Wacece Taly wadda ta taimaka maku wajen had'a wannan bala'in?"   ta  k'ara had'e  fuska tareda maka masa wata harara, Basarake yayi 'yar guntuwar dariya yace,     "Ki sanar da ita sak'ona Wulak'ancin da zan mata sai ya linka wanda tasa ayiwa matata linkin ba linkin, ki sanar da ita ta k'ara b'uya duk inda take kada tayi sakacin da zanyin ido biyu da ita, don sai ta yi danasanin abunda tayiwa Matata. "

Yana k'are fad'ar maganar ya  fice d'akin sauran k'attin suka biyoshi suna k'ara bashi hak'uri, yace dasu suje kawai amman suji tsoron ALLAH su daina bari ana sakasu aikata abunda zai jefasu a wahala idan rana ta b'ace, suka yi masa godiya sannan suka fito kowanensu cike da jin dad'in ganinshi free da sukayi. 

Munara kam cikin fushi ta fito d'akin Nusy tana take mata baya suka bar hotel d'in, kai tsaye Nusy kam tayi gida ganin yanda Munara ta tare Napep ta haye abunta ta tafi, sai da tabiya ta  inda suka bar motarta ta d'auka tayi gida Zuciyarta cike da jin zafin tozarcin da Basarake yasa ayi mata, abun takaici tana zuwa gidan ta taradda gida cike fam da masu jere, sai hada hadarsu sukeyi suna shewa, kasancewarsu 'yan duniyan mata zanzararri wad'anda shigowarta yasa kowace tabita da kallo tana had'iyar yawu har ta shige d'akinta,  ta bugo k'ofa da k'arfi ta haye gado tana kuka mai tsanani,saboda  damuwar da tayi mata yawa goma da ashirin ta rasa ina zata rab'a taji sanyi, batada kowa sai Daddy gashi shi kuma yau baya ta tata sai ta zumud'in zuwan Amaryarshi. 

**

Basarake kam ya sallami 'yan sandan sannan  ya koma inda su Nuradden yana dariyar mugunta yayi masu bayanin komi, dukansu suma sunji dad'in tsoratar da ita da aka yi, koda ALLAH zaisa aci nasara ta sakar masu mara suyi fitsari, sannan suka garzayo gidan ALHAJI Baba suka kai gaisuwarsu, inda ALHAJI Baba yasa aka kawo masu abinci mai rai da motsi suka CI, sannan yayi masu jagora har d'akin da  Mummy  take suka gaisa,  Mummy taji dad'in ganinshi a matsayin mijin 'yarta Munawwara, ta k'ara tabbatarwa kanta 'yarta ba k'aramar sa'a tayi ba, yayiwa Mummy dunkulallen Alkhairi sannan yayiwa matan gidan da  yaransu duka, kowa sai sambarka yakeyi bayan sun fito cikin gidanne, Basarake yake tambayar ALHAJI Baba meke faruwa yaga Mummy a gidan har lokacin?kasancewar anzama d'aya yasa bai b'oye masa ba ya sanar dashi abunda yake faruwa, Basarake yayi shiru na d'an lokaci sannan yace,     "Don ALLAH Alhaji idan ka amince zan saya mata gidanta ta koma cen ta zauna, har zuwa lokacin da komi zai daidaitan, shikenan an raba masu gida ita da Amaryar kowa ya tsaya nashi muhalli ba sai an hadesu waje d'aya ba."     Alhajin cikin jin dad'i  yace

"Bazan hana kayi mata Alkhairi ba  amman zamanta a nan tare damu ma ba matsala bane, sai dai zaka iya siyen gidan duk ranar da suka shirya sai ta tare abunta."     Basaraken yaji dad'in abunda ya fad'a sannan yayi sallama dashi akan zasuje su binciko gidan, duk yanda aka yi zasu zo su sanar dashi, yayi masu addu'ar sosai sannan suka tafi cikin mintunan da basu wuce talatin ba aka nemo masa wani gida da yake a kasuwa, wanda atake yanke ciniki ya fad'a ya siye mata shi, wanda kud'inshi ya lak'ume miliyoya, sannan yaje ya sanar da Alhajin  suka zo dashi yaga gidan, ya yaba sosai da sosai sannan yayi ta saka mashi Albarka, ya bar komi a hannun Alhajin da yusuf akan 'yan gyare gyaren da yake so ayiwa gidan, sannan yayi sallama dasu a mutunce suka bar garin Kaduna zuwa Abuja.
