*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

*60*


Tsuntsayen soyayya kuwa suna tsaka da cin amarcinsu ne wata sabuwar k'addara ta kutso cikin aurensu, domin kuwa takanas matar baban Basarake waton abokiyar zaman Ummi, ta kawo masu ziyarar bazata Munawwa tana sama sai ga Abulle wannan 'yar aikin tasu bahaushiya tazo ta sanar da ita tayi bak'uwa, ta sauko cike da fara'arta saboda a kullum  da kalar bak'in da suke zuwa ganinta, daga dangin ALHAJI Dikko har ma da na Mahaifiyarshi Ummi waton Mu'azatu, kasancewar dangin babanta duka 'yan  Nigeria ne, kuma sun shigo jikinta sosai saboda sunga ni'imar da Ubangiji yayi mata, basa bak'in cikin yin tattaki tun daga gombe har Abuja wajenta,  saboda sun san bazasu dawo hannu biyu ba don a kullum bata gajiya da YI masu hidima. 

Hajiya Falmata tana zaune kan kujera ta hakimce sai wani shan k'amshi take yi, tana kallon falon a yatsine kamar taga kashia cikin ranta tace

_"Shege gaba d'aya dukiyar tana hannunshi  ai WALLAHI sai na dasa maku bak'in cikin da sai ka mutu baka samu warakar shi ba  muga ta k'aryar yin aure d'an is...... "_

Zancen zucin nata ya katse ne lokacin da Munawwa take saukowa daga sama, ido ta k'ura mata saboda ganin kyakkyawar halittarta, aranta tace    _"Kin gama feleke yarinya"_

Munawwa ta k'araso da fara'arta akan fuskarta tana gaidata a mutunce, duk da bata waye ta ba amman tana yi mata kallon fuskar sani, matar ta kalleta tayi 'yar dariya tace, 

"Ga dukkan alama baki gane ni ba ko?"

Munawwa tace,     "Eh gaskiya amman dai naso na ganen"  tana maganar cikin 'yar dariya, Matar ta gyara zamanta tareda k'ara kur'e kallonta ga Munawwa tace,    "Sunana Hajiya Falmata kuma ni Matar ALHAJI Dikko mai Gwal ce."   da sauri Munawwa ta sauko kan Kujerar tana k'ara gayar da ita cikin wani sabon ladabi, Matar tayi dariyar mugunta tace 

"A'a tashi abunki ki zauna ai ke d'iyata ce ba wata surukuta a tsakanimmu"    Munawwa cike da fara'a zata yi magana sai ga sallamar Basarake ya shigo dafe da kanshi da yake ta masifar sara mashi, yana ido biyu da ita yaji gabanshi ya fad'i saboda tuno lokacin da matan Babanshi suka had'a kai da Talatu tsohuwar matarshi suka so aikashi Lahira, jikinshi yana rawa yazo ya janyo hannun Munawwa da k'arfi suka yi sama, Hajiya Falmata ta bisu da kallo tayi wata shuumar dariya sannan ta mik'e ta fice gidan, aranta tace _"aikin gama ya gama"_   koda Basarake ya sauko da saurinshi da nufinshi na yi mata kashedi akan kada ta sake zo masa gida, yayi turus ba shiri saboda ganin bata nan ta tafiyarta har k'ofar gidan ya fito ya taradda daga ita har direban da ya kawota sun fice gidan. 

Dafe da kanshi dake ta sara ya koma inda Munawwa dake zaune tayi tagumi tana nazarin me yasa Aliyunta yayiwa matar babanshi haka?,. 

Koda ya shigo yanayin da ya taradda ita yasa shi zama kusa da ita da sauri ya ri'ko hannunta, cikin rud'ewa yace    "Kiyi hak'uri na rufe da wani sirri nawa da ya dace ace kin sani tun kafin muyi aure, Matan Babana suna adawa dani sosai akan kasancewata d'a d'aya a wurin Mahaifina, saboda tun daga kaina bai sake samun wata haihuwa ba, matanshi hud'u had'i da Ummi amman ko b'atan wata basu tab'a yi a gidanshi ba, dalilin hakan yasa suke ta neman hanyoyin da zasu hallakani tun ina matashi, ALLAH bai basu sa'a ba har nayi aurena na farko inda suka had'a kai da matar akan su kasheni."    Munawwa tayi saurin zaro ido waje,    Basaraken yace      "Yes Haka ko akayi Talatu ta zuba man guba naci kad'an a abinci, nasha jinya sosai asibiti da yake ALLAH yanufa da sauran rayuwata a duniya, lokacin da asirinsu ya tonu ne Na saki talatu shima Abbana ya saki matanshi biyu duka masu hannu akan lamarin, bayan sun sha horo mai tsanani a hannun 'yan sanda, to ita kanta wannan matar da kike gani har yanzu ni ban yarda da ita ba, saboda haka kiyi takatsantsan sosai akanta don yanzu mutane abun tsoro ne, shiyasa ma ban barki kuka yi dogon zance ba don jin tsoron ta shigo jikinki ta cutar da mu gaba d'aya, don ko Ummi har yanzu ba wani shiri ne a tsakaninsu ba don haka ki kiyaye."

 Cikin sanyin Jiki Munawwa ta kwanto jikinshi tace,    "Insha ALLAH ma bata isa tayi muna komi ba, amman zan iya takuna da ita kamar yanda kake so"    

Basarake ya zagayo hannunshi ya k'ara mannenta da jjikinshi yace,     "Me kika dafawa Aliyunki yau?duk da bana jin dad'in jikina WALLAHI."

Munawwa tayi saurin mik'ewa zaune tace, 

"Subhanallah meke damunka?"  yayi 'yar dariya sannan yace
"Kwantar da hankalinki hajiyata bafa wani ciyo bane kaina ne kawai nike jin yana d'an sarawa a hankali."    sannan ta mik'e da sauri tace,   "Zo muje kaci abinci to sai kasha magani ka d'an kwanta kafin k'arfe hud'u tayi."

Ya biyota suka zauna a diny suna cin abinci suna 'yar firarsu har suka k'are,    Munawwa ta kawo masa maganin yasha sannan yaje ya kwanta, tana yi masa tausa har yayi barci sannan ne ta zurfafa tunaninta akan wannan labarin da ya bata, tasa a ranta dole ne ta dukufa da yiwa mijinta addu'ar kariya daga sharrin mutum da na aljan har ma da sharrin shed'an jefaffe, don taji tsoro sosai da halin mutane Musamman ita wannan Matar Baban tashi da ta rage a yanzu. 

Jikinta yana rawa ta koma gefen kanshi tana ta tofeshi da addu'o'in tsari, hawayenta suna ta zuba saboda tausayin Aliyunta, aranta tace

_"Ashe kowane gida da nashi kalar kalubalen da yake fuskanta, mu gidanmu Daddy ne matsalarmu, shi kuma nasu gidan Matan babanshi Oh ALLAH ka zama gatanmu ni da Mijina."_


***

Munara kam sai kusan Asuba tayi bacci, k'arfe tara dam sannan ta falka tayi sallah  ta fito ta nufu kitchen domin ta nemawa cikinta abunda zata CI, don har lokacin su Daddy basu fito ba bayan ta had'o abun break d'in ne ta zauna tana ci tana kallon saman Daddy, saboda ganin k'arfe goma tayi ko motsinshi bata jiyo ba, hankalinta ya tashi haka kawai taji tsoron kada ace wani abu ya sameshi, a gurguje ta k'are break d'in ta mik'e jikinta yana rawa ta nufi sama, k'ofar d'akinshi ta tsaya ta fara bubbuga k'ofar, jin k'arar bud'e waccen d'akin dake kusa da nashi wanda yake mallakin  Amarya, yasa ta maida kallonta ga k'ofar d'akin aiko sai ga Daddy ya fito daga shi sai wata guntuwar jallabiya da bata kai k'asa ba,  bakinshi kamar gonar auduga yana fad'in 

"Bily Bilynah bari inzo minti biyu."

Munawwa ta saki baki tana kallon Daddy waton ma shi hankalinshi a kwance, saboda jin haushi Munara ta juya ba shiri zata sauka, Daddy ya kira sunanta cike da fara'arshi har lokacin yace,     "Baby Lafiya? KO makarantar zaki je ne?"   Munawwa ta juyo idanuwanta cike da hawaye duk da ba wani uzurin da zai kaita makarantar amman ta tsinci kanta da cewa,    "Eh" ya ciro kud'i a aljihunshi ya mik'a mata yana k'ok'arin yin magana daga cen cikin d'akin suka jiyo Bily tace,     "Alhajina kazo don ALLAH"  ta k'are maganar cikin shagwab'a, aiko  jikinshi yana rawa ya juya sannan ya waigo ya kalli Munara dake tsaye kamar an dasata  yace,      "Baby kije sai kin dawo ALLAH ya tsare hanya"  yana k'are fad'ar maganar   ya fad'a d'akin ya bugo k'ofar Munara ta sauko da gudu ta nufi d'akinta, idonta a rufe ta fad'a kan gado tana ta kuka gwanin ban tausayi, saboda tsananin Kukan da tasha ne ma tsakanin jiya da yau  gaba d'aya idonta sun kumbure, ganin kukan bazai fiddata ba ne  yasa ta yi tashi taje tayi wanka ta shirya ta fito harabar gidan zata shiga motarta, Ila mai gadi 'ya kurewa bayanta kallo kamar maye, saboda yanda take tafiya d'uwawunta suna juyawa kamar su fad'o, har ta shiga ta tada motar tareda gyara zamanta yana zaune yana kallon yanda ukunta suke juyawa a idonshi kamar majigi, tayi ta horn bai masan tana yi ba har sai da ta buga wani uban horn,  sannan ya dawo hayyacinshi zumbur ya tashi ya fara bud'e get d'in, ranta a b'ace  ta bud'e motar ta fito kamar wata zakanya tana zuwa ta mazga masa wani kafurin mari, sannan ta fara zazzaga masa ruwan Bala'i shi kam ko kad'an baiji marin ba, saboda yanda jikinta yake ta rawa nonuwanta sai girgiza sukeyi, aiko ya kurewa Breasts d'inta  kallo jikinshi sai tsuma yakeyi akan ya cafko su, Munara kam gano inda yake kallo yasa ta k'ara wanka masa wani wawan Mari, tareda ciyo kwalarshi da k'arfi ta wullashi k'ofar gidan, saboda yanda jikinshi yayi weak sosai ko k'aramin yaro zai iya bashi kashi. 

Ta nunashi da d'an yatsa fuskarta a had'e tace,     "Daga yau ka dena aiki a gidannan d'an ISKA kawai, kada ka yarda in dawo in taradda kai a gidannan WALLAHI ."   ta koma motarta cikin sauri  ta fisgo motar da k'arfi ta fito gidan  ta cilla kan motarta saman titi tabar unguwar aguje. 

Ila ya dafe jarumarshi da ta mik'e sambal yace,     "Ai WALLAHI don ubanki sai na yi wasar kura da jikinki, tunda har na k'yasa ki to dole ma ne sai na ciki shegiya."

Ya koma ya fara rufe gate d'in aranshi yasha alwashin ba inda zai je, kuma duk hanyar da zai bi sai ya bita ya ciyo nasara akanta. 

Munara kai tsaye gidansu Nusy  ta nufa tana ta Alla Alla kada ta iske Babanta d'an masifa,    aiko ta taki sa'a koda taje baya nan sai dai itama kanta Nusy bata gidan ta fita, cikin takaici ta fito gidan ta nufi gidan wata k'awarsu Jidda, sannan ta kira Nusy tace da ita gata gidansu Jidda tazo tana nemanta. 

Sannan ta fito ta shiga gidan da murna Jidda ta tareta, suka gaisa da Maman Jidda sannan suka yi d'akinta, shigarsu keda wuya sai ga kiran Dr Hafis ya shigo wayarta, aiko da sauri ta d'auka nan take taji farin ciki ya lullub'eta da dusashiyar muryarta da tasha kuka suka fara gaisawa, a rud'e yace da ita    "Baby lafiya naji muryarki a haka?"

Tayi d'an murmushi tace,     "Me ka gani?"

Yace.  "Naji muryarki kamar akwai damuwa a ranki, ki kwantar da hankalinki Dr Hafis zai saka ki cikin farin ciki mara misaltuwa, ki shirya zuwa na gobe gobennan zanzo Kd donki kawai my Baby"

Munara tayi wata 'yar dariya sannan tace,   "Da naji dad'i kuwa"

Har Nusy tazo Munara basu daina waya da Dr Hafis ba, sai dorata yake tana hawa tana jin kanta tamkar tafi kowace mace had'uwa a duniya.  



D/Auta ce

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*


*61*


Bayan ta k'are wayar da Dr Hafis d'inne Nusy da ke tagumi tana kallonta, ta cire tagumin tace

"'yar gaban goshin Daddy sannan da sabuwar Auntynki Bily danger, ina kika samo wannan shi kuma?ko dai HAIDAR d'in NE ya dawo?"    Munara kam da tunda taji ta ambaci sunan Bily danger taji gabanta ya fad'i, Zuciyarta sai  bugawa takeyi da Sauri da sauri, bakinta yana rawa ta nuna Nusy tace,    "Ina kika santa ne? Don ga dukkan alamu kina da labarin auren da Daddyna yayi."

Nusy ta gyara zamanta tana 'yar dariya tace,    "Bily dangerous ai ya zama dole in santa ko kema don baki da dalilin saninta ne shiyasa baki waye ta ba, amman NI HAR unguwarsu na sani da gidansu, sai dai ba wata huld'a da ta tab'a had'ani da ita saboda tsarina daban nata daban."     Munara ta kalleta cike da fargaba tace,    "Baki tab'a huld'a da ita ba kuma kika santa da unguwar da take kuma har da gidansu duk?."   Nusy tayi dariya sannan ta janyo wayarta ta fara dannata har ta kamo photon wata matashiyar budurwa, ta nunawa Munara sannan tace,   "Kinga wannan two a sanadinta nasan Bily danger saboda unguwarsu d'aya, watarana naje gidansu Ruma ne ta rako ni, to anan ne naga Billy da k'awayenta motarsu tayi parking a k'ofar gidansu, sun fito dukansu sai mashalo su keyi da alama sun yi  shaye shaye sun bugu sosai, har suka shige gidan ina tsaye ina kallonsu, Rumah tayi dariya ta ri'ko hannuna tace

"Don ALLAH ZO muje k'i daina kallon wad'ancen 'yan iska"

Na kalli Rumah nace 

"Idan su 'yan ISKA ne to mu miye banbancinmu dasu?tunda shan kayan mayen NE kawai bama yi"    Rumah ta tab'e baki sannaan tace

"Rufawa kanki asiri don WALLAHI wad'ancen da kike gani manyan shegu ne, ita wannan jibgegiyar da kika gani itace shugabarsu duka, kuma wannan gida da kike gani gidanta ne take zaune da Mahaifiyarta, Billy Danger bala'i ce sosai WALLAHI less bin maza shaye shaye duk yi take yi, duk Namijin da ya tsautsayi yasa ya shigo hannunta ya gama yawo WALLAHI, don taci dubu sai dai kuma ceto kawai." 

Nusy tana zancen zuciyar Munara tana rawar disco har ta kammala, sannan ta k'ara da cewa 

"Bansan ya aka YI Daddynki ya zab'i ta zama matar aurenshi ba, haka kawai ya jefa kanshi a matsala ya jefa ku,  ke ni fa lokacin da Ruma take sanar dani wai Bily danger zata yi aure WALLAHI ba k'aramar  tausayawa wanda zai aureta nayi ba, ashe ma wai Daddynki ne ta cafkewa k'ugu cabd'i  WALLAHI SAI kin zage damtse sosai don kam akwai aiki a gabanki ja kuma k'ato"

Munara ta makawa Nusy  wata k'atuwar  harara, Jidda tayi 'yar dariya tace 

"Dad'i na dake Nusy akwai zuzuta abu WALLAHI, kin fad'a kuma sai k'ara yiwa labarin kwalliya kike yi"      Nusy ta b'ata fuska cikin wasa tace.  "Barni in fad'a don ta d'auki  matakin kare kanta, tun kafin Bily ta  wancakalar mata da mutuncin Mahaifi, don WALLAHI ita kanta sai tayi dak'yar zata kwaci kanta daga tarkonta, don muguwar 'yar less ce don ni dai kam WALLAHI ALLAH Munara da ace ta d'oraki kan hanyar biyar mata, gara ki zo in bud'a maki k'ofar da zaki BI mazanki hankali a kwance, don yafi maki  so dubu da ace kina neman mace 'yar uwarki."   Jidda tayi shiru sannan tace

"In don wannan kuma kina da gaskiyarki sai dai mafi a'ala shine ta kauracewa duka biyun, don ko ke da zaki daina bin mazan da kinji dad'in rayuwarki, amman kullum kafafunki a bud'e ana hak'arki a banza, ba lada sai tarin Zunubin da kike kwasa gaskiya ki yiwa kanki fad'a kema."

Nusy ta kai mata duka sannan tace,     "Jeki da wa'azinki ustaziya amman nikam a gaskiya bazan iya matse k'afafuna ba, sai dai in rok'i Ubangiji ya yafe mani kawai don mai yafiya ne ga bayinshi komi girman zunubin da suka aikata, yanzu dai Ke Munara kinji halin da ake ciki saboda haka kisan yanda zakiyi da matsalar da ke gabanki."

Munara ta sauke k'atuwar ajiyar zuciya sannan cikin sanyin jiki tace,, "Naji dad'in da kika sanar dani don har ma k'awayenta sun fara nuna man halinsu na dabbanci, amman duk ranar da suka yi gangancin shiga hurumina WALLAHI sai sunyi danasanin zuwansu duniya har ma su shiga rayuwata."     

Nusy tace.  "Yafi dai gaskiya don ni kaina bana son suyi nasara akanki, don yanda kike da wad'annan breasts haka b'ulla bulla tsaf zasu janyo raayinsu akanki"

Munara ta mik'e tsaye tana gyara wandon jean's d'in dake jikinta tace,, "Har kinsa na tuno shegen maigadinmu, shi d'an iska bak'in maye ina yi masa fad'a yana kallonsu, bari inje inga shege in har  bai tafi ba zai gane kuskurenshi"   Jidda ta Mike ta sako hijabinta da nufin raka su, Nusy ta biyo bayansu suka fito gidan, Suka tsaya jikin motar Munara suna sallama, sannan Nusy ta shiga motar munara taja suka bar unguwar. 

Nusy ta kalli Munara da ke tuk'i fuskarta a had'e saboda tunanin yanda Daddynta ya rikice akan Munafukar Amaryar tashi,  kuma ashe ma bara gurbi ce ya kwasowa kanshi haka kawai sai kace wanda aka yiwa dole.

Nusy tana kallon titin tace,     "Yanzu kuma sai ina hajjaju? sannan kuma me yasa kike nema na?"   Munara ta dafa goshinta tace,   "Oh Nusy NI har ma kinsa na manta abunda ke gabana, tunda naji labarin wannan shedaniyar matar, duk d'a bana son in d'orawa kaina matsalarta, gara shi da Daddyn da ya jajibota yayi ta kaya da matsalolinta."

Nusy tayi 'yar dariya sannan tace,,  "Aiko ya zama dolenki ki damu ko don kisan ta hanyar da zaki bi ki korata ta bar gidanku tun kafin ta fara dasa maku sharroranta a cikin gida, Ni WALLAHI sai yanzu ma naji dad'in da Mummy bata gidan, don Bily danger a yanda naji labarinta komai zata iya yi WALLAHI don ta rabata da gidan, to kin ko ga hakan yafi zama alkhairi ko kema da da hali da kin koma cen gidan Alhaji Baban, tun kafin ta fara tunanin hanyar cutar dake."     Munara tayi saurin fad'in 
"Allah ya kiyaye ni ace inje wannan bak'in gidan in zauna, ai gara zamana gidanmu so dubu da zama cen WALLAHI, in yaso in tayi man in rama duk wanda yafi wani k'arfi ni da ita ya kashe wani kawai, WALLAHI nafi son haka da in k'ara ma taka k'afata wannan gidan, ni kinma b'ata man rai gaskiya."      Nusy tana 'yar dariya tace,    "ALLAH ya huci zuciyar Babyn Daddy gaki ga Daddynki tunda ba kya son rabuwa dashi, to kiyi gaggawar fidda shi a k'aton ramin da ya fad'a"      Munara ta furzar da iska a baki tace,     " Don ALLAH kiyi Closing d'in wannan issue a bakinki na gaji da ji gaskiya"      Nusy taji zafin kwatsiyar da munara tayi mata ranta a  b'ace  saboda ganin tana k'ok'arin nusar da ita akan bala'in Bily Danger amman ta mayar da abun shirme, kai tsaye tace da ita,     "Ajiyeni anan zan tari Napep ta kaini gida."    Munara ta kai kallonta gareta saboda jin yanayin muryarta tayi 'yar dariya tace,,     "Ba zaki je kiga Billy danger d'in bane?"    Nusy ta had'e fuska tace,    "Me zan gani da ita ne wanda banji labari ba?kece dai yafi dacewa ace kiyi ta kallonta ko don ki gano zancen da nike sanar dake gaskiya ne"   Munara tayi 'yar dariya tace, 

"WALLAHI kamar kin san ko har yanzu NI ban ma ganta ba"

Nusy tace  "ai gara hakan kuyi nesa nesa d'in gaskiya"

Daga nan kowa ya shiga nashi zancen zuci har suka je bakin k'ofar gate d'in gidan, har Munara tayi hoan da nufin abud'e mata gate d'in, amman sai ta tuno ai ta kori Maigadin ta fito da sauri tana k'ok'arin shiga 'yar k'aramar k'ofar ne taga Ila mai Gadi ya fara bud'e gate d'in, da mamaki tayi tsaye tana kallonshi har ya wangale gate d'in gaba d'aya, cikin sauri ta maka masa harara  tareda nunashi da yatsa tace,     "Ashe baka tafi d'in ba ko?uban me kake jira ne da har yanzu baka bar gidan ba?"    Kai tsaye Ila yace da ita    "Ubanki NE yace in zauna ai"    Munara ta zaro ido a waje jikinta har rawa yakeyi ta isa wajenshi ta shak'o wuyanshi tace,    "Ni zaka zaga? Ai WALLAHI sai ka rena kanka don ubanka"  tana k'are maganar ta wanka masa Mari  wanda yayi Daidai da dawowar Daddy, idonshi akansu yayi saurin fitowa daga motar yana fad'in,    "Subhanalla Baby me ya had'aki da shi kuma yanzu?"     Munara cikin fushi tace,    "Zagina fa yayi Daddy don na tambayeshi ya aka yi bai bar gidan ba bayan nace dashi ya tafi na koreshi"     Daddyn ya rik'o hannunta yace   "Ni nace ya zauna HAR sai na sami wani sabon Maigadin sai shi ya bar gidan, je ki shigo da motarki fita iskanshi kinji,  kai kuma WALLAHI ka kuskura ka k'ara yin abunda zai b'atawa Babyta rai sai kaci Ubanka a garinnan."

Ila ya fara baiwa Daddy Hak'uri Nusy dake tsaye tun d'azu tace,       "Daddy barka da yau?"    ya juyo ya kalleta yace,    "Barka dai lafiya kike"   tace 

"Lafiya k'alau  Daddy"

Munara ta shigo da motar tayi parking suka shiga gidan  shima Daddyn yaje ya shigo da tashi ya ajiye, Ila yabisu da kallo yana huci a ranshi yace,     

_"Da albashin farko idan an bani WALLAHI zan tauye kaina is narko maku asiri don Ubanku shegu, sai kunsan dani kuke zance don da kud'inku zan ci ubanku duka."_



 Princess Dija✍🏻

*🔥BAK'ON YANAUI🔥*

*62*

Munara da Nusy suna falo a zaune sai Bily taci kafurar kwalliya tana saukowa daga sama, a take k'amshi ya gauraye falon duka saboda muguntar da tayi wajen bad'a turaren son ranta, don gurinta na son k'ara susutar da Alhajinta, aiko su Munara suka YI saurin kai kallonsu gareta, Munara kam kallo d'aya da tayi mata sai da taji gabanta ya fad'i, ba shiri ta kawar da kanta gefe tareda k'ara had'e fuska tamau don kada ma taga damarta, aiko tana k'arasowa ta tsaya saman kan Kujerar da Munara take, ta d'an rank'wafo saiti da fuskarta tace, 

"Baby haka ake tarbon sabuwar Mummy ba gaisuwa?"    Munara a kufule ta Mik'e jikinta yana rawa tace, 

"Wacece Mummyn?cabd'i jam LALLE kin taro wa kanki ruwa, to ki jira idan har zamanki YAYI k'arko a gidan, sai ki haifo 'ya'yan da zasu ce maki Mummy, don NI kam ballagaza irinki WALLAHI bazata tab'a zama Mummyta ba."  tana k'are maganar tayi fuuu ta fara taku da k'arfi bombom d'inta suna juyawa kamar su fad'o ta shige d'akinta, Nusy ma ta mik'e ta bi bayanta jikinta sai rawa yakeyi saboda tsoron Bily danger. 

Bily kam binsu da wani kallo tayi tana wani murmushin mugunta tana kad'a halshe,sai ga Daddy ya shigo da sallamarshi abaki ganin Bily yasa sauran sallamar  ta mak'ale masa, jikinshi yana rawa ya isa wurinta inda itama ganinshi yasa tayi saurin saita kanta, tayi wata kuwwar jin dad'i  aguje  ta isa wurinshi ta rungumeshi tana sakar masa sumba ta ko'ina, Daddy kam daskarewa yayi kamar wani gunki saboda abunda yake Ji yana zillo a cikin jikinshi, a d'imauce ya k'ara manneta a jikinshi yana shak'ar daddad'an turarenta yana sauke Numfashi, Da gayya Bily tayi 'yar k'ara tace,     "Alhajina kana jiyar dani dad'i sosai, wash Alhajina kada ka kasheni da soyayyarka zo muje kaci abinci kafin in sakaka manta kowa ma a duniya sai ni kad'ai Balkisatu"

Aiko da kwarkwasarta ta mak'alewa Daddy tana yi masa kissa kala kala har suka haye sama, Munara da Nusy dake lab'e jikin k'ofa suna kallo suka BI saman da kallo, Hawaye masu zafi suka sauko mata cikin sanyin jiki ta saki k'ofar ta koma kan gado ta zauna, tareda yin tagumi da hannunta duka biyu hawayenta sunata kwaranya, Nusy ma jiki a sanyaye ta zo ta zauna kusa da ita tareda dafa kafadarta tace,    "ba kuka ne a gabanki ba mafita zaki nemowa kanki, ta hanyar zaunar da Daddynki k'i sanar dashi gaskiyar wacece Bily danger, koda ALLAH zai sa ya hankalta tun yanzu ya kwaci wuyan hannunshi daga bala'in rik'on da tayi masa."

Munara ta mik'e tsaye ta fara taku a hankali sannan ta juyo da idanuwanta jajawur tace,,    "Me zai hana in sanar da ita nasan komi akanta, in tsoratar da ita sosai domin in ragewa  rashin mutuncint kaifi, sannan inyi mata barazana da ko ta bar gidan ko in had'ata da 'yan sanda a matsayin wadda take son kashe Daddy, wanda ina zaton hakan zai sa taji tsoro ta kama gabanta cikin ruwan sanyi"


Nusy ta mik'e tayi tsaye suna facin d'in juna sannan ta rungume hannu a k'irji tace,     "Ina ga har yanzu baki gano abunda nike son nusar dake ba, ai mune ke jin tsoron 'yan sanda amman ba irin bily danger ba, don idan ma za'a bincika WALLAHI bazata rasa abokan huld'a a cikinsu ba, kin ko ga ba wani tsoronsu da zata ji balle har AYI mata barazana dasu, don WALLAHI ko mutum ta kashe tsaf zata sami mai wanketa tass akan case d'in, kuma har ta fito salun alun ba tareda an d'auki wani kwkkwaran mataki akanta ba, mafita d'aya ce ki sanar da Daddy komai akan halayenta da na fad'a maki, idan ma ya nuna rashin yarda akan zancen kice dashi yaje ya binciko halinta dakanshi, kinga da ya gano gaskiyar halinta zai sauwak'e mata asauk'ak'e ba tareda wani tashin hankali ba."      Munara ta kai zaune ba shiri sannan tace "idan haka ne ko zan sanar dashi yau yau d'innan, don is son samu nane kada ta wuce sati d'aya a cikin gidannan"

Nusy tace,   "Yawwa to yafi dai kam don gara ya tabbata ba aure da ace ya zauna da ita, nifa WALLAHI yunwa ma nike ji me zaki samo muna muci nikam?."    Nusy tana kai k'arshen zancenta sai ga Bily ta turo k'ofar d'akin tana wani shu'umin murmushi tace,, "Ku taso Baby ga abincinku cen na ajiye maku a falo keda kawarki"     Nusy cike da tsoro tace,     "A'a Aunty Bily ni na k'oshi gida ma zanje yanzu."

Nusy jikinta yana rawa ta mik'e tace,    "Na tafi Munara sai munyi waya."   ta fice kamar walk'iya Bily tazo kusa da munara ta zauna tana wani gajeren murmushi tace,    "Naji zancen da kukeyi da kawarki amma ina baki shawarar kada kiyi wautar sanar da Alhajina wannan maganar, saboda koma kin fad'a bazai tab'a amincewa da maganarki ba, gara kawai ki adana maganarki kada ki wahalar da kanki abanza"   takai k'arshen maganarta da janyo Munara a jikinta da k'arfi tace

"Me Babyna ce kamar yanda kike babyn Alhajina bana son kisan ko d'aya daga cikin mugayen halayen Bily danger, don ba kya iya jurar ko d'aya daga cikinsu saboda na fahimci kina da k'aramar kwakwalwa, amman fa ki sani WALLAHI muddin kika yi gangancin sanar dashi kamar yanda kawarki ta baki shawara ko ki sani zaki jefa kanki a matsala, ta farko itace zan yi duk yanda zanyi in nisantaki da Daddynki, na biyu zan saki jiki in huta da wannan kyakkywan jikin naki har ma in gayyato k'awayena suma su more, na uku kuma zan saka ALHAJI ya koraki a gidannan tamkar yanda kika ga ana yiwa karnuka idan ba'a son su a wuri, saboda haka kiyi nazari da kyau duk abunda kika zab'a ni ashirye nike da  koma minene zai faru ."    Kafin Munara tayi magana sai ga Muryar Daddy yana fad'in,    "Ya bata zo taci abincin bane"  ya k'are maganar tareda bud'e d'akin ya shigo, bily tana 'yar dariya tace,     "Lallashinta ne nikeyi don tace bazata CI abincin ba, ni kuma WALLAHI bana son in barta ta zauna da yunwa Alhajina"   ta k'are maganar a cikin damuwa kamar da gaske,    Daddy ya k'araso inda take yace, 

"Haba Baby me yasa bazaki ci abincin Auntynki ba? Baki ga yanda ta dami kanta ba akan tazo ta kawo maki abincin, meyasa kika zab'i k'i watsa mata k'asa a ido, to bari kiji ki d'auki Billy tamkar itace ta haifeki don yanda take sonki ko Mummynki bata yi maki irin wannan son don haka ki ajiye komi ki rungumi sabuwar Mummynki yafi maki"

Yana k'are maganar cikin fushi ya fito d'akin cike da jin haushin yanda take k'in aurenshi da Bilynshi. 

Bily kam yana fita itama ta mik'e tana 'yar dariya tace,    "To ni na tafi Baby dafatar dai kin fara fahimtar wacece bily da kalar takunta."


***

Munawwa kam tana mak'ale dashi har ya tashi daga barcin, ganin yanda ya mik'e garas yasa taji dad'i aranta, a tare sukayi SALLAH har ma ya taimaka mata suka shiga kitchen suka yi girkinsu na dare. 

A lokacin da suka zo kwanciyar aure ne labari yasha bambam domin kuwa gaba d'aya baya da kuzarin da zai biya masu bukatarsu, dole ya hak'ura inda ya jingina hakan da ciyon kan da ya kafceshi a d'azu, duk da aranshi yaso ace ya raya daren tareda jiyar da kanshi Dad'i da daddadar zumar matarshi. 

Koda safiya ta waye ya watsar da komi ya shiga sabgoginshi don ko kad'an bai d'auki hakan a wata matsala. 

Da k'arfe biyar na yamma ya dawo gidan cike da sha'awar Munawynshi, kai tsaye ya haye sama saboda bak'in da ya gani sunzo wurinta, ganin yanayinshi yasa ta sallami mutanen ta zo ta sameshi a d'akinshi, inda yake kwance yana kallon sealing saboda yanayin da yake jin kanshi, tazo kusa dashi ta kwanta tareda janyo hannushi takai bakinta ta sumbaceshi tace,    "Sannu da zuwa My dafatar ka dawo lafiya?, me zaka fara yi wanka ko cin abinci"  ya juyo fuskarshi yana kallonta fuskarshi cike annuri yace,    "Nafi son abinci amman na jikin Munawyta, don yafiye mani kowane abinci da zan iya ci a yanzu"    Munawwa ta juyo itama tana kallonshi tace,   "Munawwa taka ce kayi yanda kake so da ita don haka da kai da kaya duka mallakar wuya ne."   ta k'are maganar tareda kashe masa ido d'aya ba shiri ya janyota a jikinshi tsantsan, ya fara aika mata da sakunanshi masu mantar da ita a wace duniyar take?, yana gaf da cikar burinshi na jefa bugarshi a cikin rijiya kwatsam yaji bazai iya ba, saboda gaba d'aya baya da k'arfin yin hakan, domin jarumarshi tak'i bada had'in kai cikin tsananin tashin hankali ya koma gefe,  zuciyarshi cike da tunanin har yanzu duk ciyon kanne Bai sakeshi ba ko ya abun yake,  Munawwa kam ganin damuwar da ya shiga yasa ta matsa ta d'ora kanta a k'irjinshi tace,,    "Miye damuwa AKAN faruwar hakan?, nifa taka ce kullum kuma a shirye nike da in faranta maka, ka kwantar da hankalinka saboda har yanzu jikinka yana buk'atar hutu."    Ya shafo fuskarta yace, "Kinfi kowa sanin wanene ni don d'an wannan ciyon na mintuna k'alilan bazai sa in rasa kuzarin da zan kusanceki ba, sai dai in har akwai dai wani abun daban da bamusan komenene ba"     Munawwa ganin yanda damuwarshi tayi yawa yasa TAYI ta shashantar da zancen tareda ci gaba da kwantar masa da hankali, har ta ciyo kanshi suka YI wanka suka fito suka ci abinci. 

Wasa wasa abu sai gaba yake yi don kuwa har suka yi sati biyu a cikin matsalar, wanda a lokacin ne alamun ciki ya fara bayyana a jikin Munawwa,saboda amaye amayen da takeyi da Zazzab'in da take yi kullum dare idan zasu kwanta barci,  wanda izuwa lokacin damuwarshi ta fara fitowa fili har Su Ummi da kakale suka gane, shiyasa suka tisa shi agaba da tambaya, ya b'oye gaskiyar babbar damuwarshi ya sanar dasu ciyon da Munawwa takeyi ne ya janyo masa shiga damuwar, jin haka yasa dukansu suka yi farin ciki sosai don jikinsu ya basu cewa ciki ne da Munawwara, kai tsaye Kakale tace dashi ya kaita ta ganota cikin tsantsar farin ciki, Ya d'auki Kakale ya kaita gidanshi inda a duba d'aya da tayi mata ta gano cewa Munawwara tana da ciki, cike da jin dad'i Kakale tace suje akaita Asibiti, yaji dad'in yanda yaga Kakale take ta faman murmushi don kuwa shima ya fara zargin ciki ne da ita. 

Aiko suka nufi Wata babbar Asibitin dake garin Abuja inda anan ne DR Hafis yake aiki, kuma a b'angaren mata inda anan ne yake samu yana fakewa da aiki yana cenye matan Mutane. 

Yaji dad'in ganin Munawwa ba kad'an dashi aka yi ta treatment d'inta, wanda a tashin farko result d'inta ya fito b'aro b'aro  aka gano tana d'auke da ciki d'an sati uku,  ko kad'an Basarake baiso ya ganta ba ma balle ya duba wani abu da ya shafeta, kishin hakan yasa yayita  k'unci yak'i nina jin dad'inshi akan cikin, amman a zuciyarshi kam baza'a iya misalta farin cikin da ya shiga ba. 

Bayan sun dawo gida NE kasancewar gidan ALHAJI Dikko ya wuce dasu, Munawwa ta shige d'akin Ummi ta kwanta saboda kokadan bata da wani k'arfin jiki,  Kakale ta janyo hannu Basarake sukayi d'akinta ta zaunar dashi tace

"Me yasa banga kayi farin ciki da wannan babbar baiwa da ALLAH yayi maka ba?"

Basarake ya sosa kanshi yace,    "WALLAHI Kakale naji dad'i sosai kuma nayi murna, kawai dai wannan shegen Likitan ne da yayita jagulata yasa naji zuciyata bata yi mun dad'i, don bala'i ga mata da yawa ya bari suyi aikinsu ya tare komi sai shi."    

Kakale ta kalleshi tayi shiru tana Nazari sannan tace,     "Aliyu ka rage wannan zafin kishin naka, agabanmu fa aka YI mata komi kuma ni banga wani aibi ba tunda akan aikinshi yake"

Basarake ya mik'e yana tafiya yace,     "Don baki san wanene shi bane shiyasa"    Kakale ta bishi da kallo cike da mamakin kishinshi tace,   

"Ni dai ina baka shawara ka rage kishi da zargin abunda ba shi bane gaskiya, don watarana ake gudu kazo kayi abunda zai dameka daga baya." 


 

Princess Dija✍🏻

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*


*63*


Ya fice d'akin ya nufi sashen Mummy in da shigarshi keda wuya ya hangota kitchen, cike da farin ciki ganinta yayi wajenta yace,     "Ummi barka da aiki me ake dafa mane mukam?"   ta juyo fuskarta cike da tsantsar fara'a tace, 

"'yata nike yiwa d'an wake don tace bata sha'awar cin komai sai shi kawai"    yaji dad'in ganin take tarairayar Munawynshi Yana yar dariya yace,     "Salon kwad'ayi ne kawai Ummi da kin bari kawai nayi mata da kaina ke kije ki huta abinki."   Ummi tana 'yar dariya murna cike da Zuciyarta tace,   "Ina hutu anan ai ni bana gajiya da YI mata DUK abunda tace tana buk'atar ci, Namiji ne kai bakasan lalurar CIKI ba shiyasa amman ai dole mai ciki tayi kwad'ayi kam"

Basaraken yayi yaji zuciyarshi cike da tsantsar farin ciki saboda cikin da yaji ta ambata, amman kuma sai gashi da share hawayen da suka zubo masa ba shiri, saboda tuno halin da yake ciki a yanzu aranshi yayiwa ALLAH godiya da ba tun kafin aurensu ya shiga lalurar ba, yanzu ko gashi har da gudan jininshi a cikin marar Munawynshi, ya saka hannu ya share hawayen a fakaice ganin Mummy zata juyo ne shima yayi saurin juyawa yana fad'in,     "A dafa dani Ummi nima in tama ta jinyar"     jin sautin yasa tayi saurin juyawa tabi bayanshi da kallo, zuciyarta a cike da tantamar ko dai kunnuwanta ne don aganinta me zai sa shi kuka bayan duk wannan abin farin cikin da ya samesu?, shiyasa tayi saurin kawar da tunanin aranshi tace,    "Kai da baka da kwad'ayi me zaka yi da abincin masu kwad'ayin?"  yana tafiya yana 'yar dariya yace,    "Taimako ne zanyi ni a'i Ummi."   Ummi itama tayi dariya tace, 

"Na gano ka Kwad'ayin dai ne ke d'awainiya da kai kaima."    yayi d'an murmushi ba tareda ya k'ara cewa wani abu ba ya tura d'akin Ummi ya shige,wani k'amshin freshner mai kalar k'amshin k'asa idan anyi ruwa ya YI masa marhaba a hancinsa, shima kanshi da baya da cikin ya shaki k'amshin sosai sannan ya sauke ajiyar zuciya, ya k'ura ido kan gadon Ummi  inda ya hangota kwance dukunkune cikin bargo, cike da jin DADI yayi taku a hankali har ya kawo bakin gadon, idonshi akan fuskarta yayi durkuso saitin fuskarta ya sakar mata wani siririn kiss a goshi, wanda saukar fatar bakinshi yayi daidai da bud'e idanuwanta, saboda kamshin turarenshi da ya daki hancinta gashi cikin ya k'ara sakata tana masifar son k'amshin, shiyasa a kullum take manne a jikinshi sai dai kuma ba k'aramar damuwa yake shiga ba, saboda ganin baya iya yimata wani abunda zai k'ara sakata farin ciki, don ya fahimci a d'an kwanakin da ta fara ciyon akwai wata muguwar shaawarshi kwance akan fuskarta da k'wayar idonta. 

Jiki a sanyaye ya ri'ko hannunta yace,    "Sannu my angel ya jikin naki?"    ta lumshe idonta ta k'ara  bud'e SU a hankali sannan tayi jarumtar kai hannunta ta shafo gefen fuskarshi tareda d'an k'aramin gemunshi da take mugun so a tattare dashi, sannan cikin wata siririyar murya tace,     "Da sauk'i sai dai bakina ba dad'i ko kad'an"   Yayi saurin tashi ya zauna a gefen gadon sannan ya fara yunk'urin tada ta zaune yace,    "Tashi zaune ni bana son ganin kina wannan kwanciyar"  da taimakonshi ta yunk'ura ta tashi zaune, amman fa duk rabin jikinta yana cikin nashi domin kuwa narke masa tayi a jikin, ya shafo gashin kanta da d'an kwalin ya zame sannan ya zura hannunshi cikin rigarta tareda d'orashi akan fatar cikinta yace, 

"Nagodewa ALLAH da ya albarkaceni da samun wannan cikin, wanda watak'ila daga shi shikenan bazan k'ara samun wata  haifuwa ba."    ya k'are maganar cikin zubowar wasu sababbin hawaye masu zafi,  Munawwa daga kwancen tayi saurin rufe masa baki sannan ta saka hannunta ta fara share masa hawayen, itama nata hawayen sai sauka sukeyi cikin damuwa tace,    "ka daina wannan magana My ai har sai ma kace a tsaya da ita wad'anda ka samu sun isheka, don d'aya bayan d'aya zanyita haifo maka su har guda goma sha biyu, wannan jarabawa ce ALLAH yayi muna don haka muyi k'ok'arin mayar da komai gareshi, insha ALLAH zai kawo muna waraka a cikin gaggawa, don haka ka daina damar da kanka da yawa ka d'auka cewa komi mukaddari ne haka daga ALLAH yake fitowa, kuma babu wani bawa da ya isa ya goge nashi rubutun balle har ya cire tasa k'addara a cikin rayuwarshi, muyi Imani da faruwar hakan sannan muyi juriya insha ALLAH abun zai zo ya wuce kamar ma ba'ayi ba, sannan don ALLAH ina neman alfarmar kayi k'ok'arin danne damuwarka don bana son su Ummi su fahimci halin da muke ciki ko da bayan komi ya wuce ne ko,ina son mu  bar wannan matsala a tsakanimu iya ni da kai don ALLAH My"

Ta k'are maganar cikin saukowar hawayenta da suke ta bi gefe da gefen fuskarta, shima nashi hawayen har lokacin basu daina kwaranya ba, saboda shi har ga ALLAH tausayi take bashi sosai don ya fahimci yanda take cikin muguwar shaawarshi a 'yan kwanakin, sallamar Ummi yasa tayi saurin barin jikinshi ta koma ta kwanta har da su janyo bargo ta rufe fuskarta, saboda kunyar Ummin take in sosai don ma tana manta a ina take in tana tareda Aliyunta, da ba abunda zaisa ta biyeshi har har Ummi ta risketa a yanda suke yanzu, sai da Aliyun ya amsa yana yiwa Munawwa  dariyar shigewar bargon da tayi, ganinta da kayan a hannun yasa yayi saurin mik'ewa ya karb'i kayan ya ajiye, Ummi ta juya tace 

"Tada ita ka zuba mata bari inje in kawo sauran kayan"  ta fice Basaraken ya yaye bargon yana dariya yace,    "To Aunty kunya tashi ga kayan kwadayinki nan kici sai kin ture."   yana maganar ya bud'e wata kula sai ga wainar fulawar manja ta fito sala sala sak'ar tayi kyau gwanin burgewa, Kamshin da ya daki hancinta yasa tayi saurin tashi zaune tana leken kular, yana dariya yace "yawwa Aunty kwadayi taso kiga garar da Ummi ta had'a maki "  sai ga Ummi ta dawo ta ajiye sauran kayan tace,   

"Yawwa 'yata sauko kici ko a kawo maki anan d'in kinfi jin dad'i?"   Munawwa tayi saurin saukowa gadon tace    "Nagode Ummi ALLAH ya biya da gidan aljannah".. 

Ummi taji dad'in wannan addu'a ta Mubawwaa tana murmushi tace 

"Ameen 'yata kici da kyau kinji kai kuma ka kula da ita taci sosai bari inje wajen Hajiya anbarta ita kad'ai"  sannan ta fice Basaraken yace,   "ALLAH ya hutar da gajiya Ummi muna godiya"   tana dariyar jin dad'i ta bar masu d'akin, da niyyarta na son Munawwa ta saki jiki taci danwakenta da wainar fulawar da tayi mata."     Basarake ya zuzzuba masu a plate sannan ya barbada yaji mai d'an karen k'amshin kayan koli, Munawwa kam miyonta  sai tsinkewa suke yi jikinta yana rawa ta fara kai loma, ba shiri ta rufe ido tareda d'ora yasha a kai irinna yarannan tace,    "D'an karen dad'i" Basaraken ya bita da kallo cikin tsantsar kaunarta da ke ta k'ara kashe masa jiki, ya ciro wani plate ya kara mata a gefe yace,    "Kada ki fad'i wajen kwad'ayi hajjajuna"   Munawwa ta bud'e idonta ta k'ara guma wata loma tace,    "My wai a wace gona ne ake shuka fulawa nikam?"   Basarake ya rufe bakinshi yana dariya yace,   "a gonar kular Ummi mai had'e da itaciyar toshi da ruwan Manjan da ya malale k'asan plate d'inta"   Munawwa kam ganin sharri ne yake son yayi mata yasa taci gaba da cin danwakenta tana 'yar dariya,    shiko kallonta kawai yake cike da jin dad'i don yaso ace da lafiyarshi da ba makawa dole ne sai ya kashe kwadayinshi a d'akin na Ummi, ko don yayiwa d'an tayin cikinshi ban ruwa, sai dai kam kash ba dama yin sata a lahira. 


***

Munara kam fitar Bily d'akin yasa tabi k'ofar da kallo zuciyarta cike da fargabar taji ta fad'a, lalle sai yanzu ta gano gaskiyar Nusy Bily danger bala'i ce ta wuce duk inda take tunaninta, amman duk da haka WALLAHI kashedinta bazai hanata sanarwa da Daddy gaskiyar wacece ba, don duk wata barazana da zata yi mata bazata sa ta ta k'i fad'a masa halinta ba, koda ko bazata barta da ranta ba ya zama dolenta ta tsamo Mahaifinta daga halaka. 

Aiko cikin kuzari ta mik'e tayi kitchen domin samawa cikinta wani abu, a gurguje ta dafa 'yar taliya k'adan daidai cikinta ta dawo d'akinta taci abutta, sai da ta k'are ne ta fito da plate d'in inda taci karo da Bily da Daddy suna saukowa a sama, TAYI saurin kawar da kanta a gefe tayi kitchen ta ajiye plate d'in sannan ta fito zata shiga d'akinta ne yasa Daddy dake zaune  falo Bily a gefenshi yace,   

"Baby zo nan"

Munara ta b'ata fuska ta nufi wurinsu saboda kallon k'asan ido da taga Bilyn tana yimata, ta sami nesa dasu ta zauna tareda k'ara tsuke fuskarta tamau, Daddy ya kalleta ranshi a jagule yace,   "Baby Me yasa bakya son ganin farin cikina ne?, yanzu shi wannan abincin gashi tunda aka ajiye maki shi ko tabashi baki yi ba, me kike nufi yanzu baiwar Allah'n nan ta dafa abinci ta d'auko da hannunta ta kawo maki amman ki k'i ci, to bari kiji ko ki ajiye abunda ke ranki ki zauna lafiya da Bily ko kiga  b'acin raina, don WALLAHI bazan juri zama dake BA kina yiwa matar da nike aure wulak'anci"

Bily tayi saurin kwantawa a kirjinshi tareda saka hannunta cikin gaban rigarshi tana shafa masa gashin k'irjin tace,   "Haba Alhajina Baby fa k'urciya ce ke d'ibarta, sai an bita a hankali da lallami har a dorata akan hanyar da ake so tabi."

Daddy kam da ya fara susucewa gaba d'aya tsikarshi ta mik'e amman yayi jarumtar fad'in,      "Baki san wacece Baby bane don ko Mummynsu haka fa take yi mata, shiyasa kullum suke samun matsala da ita amman ni gaskiya wannan karon bazan zuba ido kina wannan bak'in halin ba, saboda haka ki fito da Miji kawai in auradda ke koda za'a dace kiyi hankali"   Munara da tunda suka fara magana take kallon gefe amman jin lafuzzan Daddynta yasa ta kai kallonta gareshi, tana YI masa kallon tuhumar anya ko Daddynta ne?, musamman da taji yace wai tayi aure ko zata yi hankali, Uwa uba ga wani Munafukin kallon da Bily take jefo mata daga kwancen da take a kirjin Daddy, cikin zubowar wasu hawayen takaici tace

"Yanzu Daddy akan wannan tanbadaddiyar matar taka kake son ka auraddani inyi hankali?, to WALLAHI baka yi komi ba don ka d'ebowa kanka ruwan dafa kanka da hannunka, don kuwa Billy Danger 'yar shaye shaye ce 'yar madigo kuma kuma rikakkiyar karuwa ce mai bin maza."     Billy ta fashe da kukan Munafucci tace,     "Alhajina yanzu ni 'yarka zata yiwa sharri agaban Idona?me nayi mata daga zuwana?WALLAHI ALLAH ban tab'a tunanin haka gidanka yake ba da ban shigo ba"   ta tashi da gudu tayi sama Daddy ranshi yayi tsananin b'aci, saboda ganin yanda  Munara ta saka Amaryarshi Kuka, Uwa uba Kazuffan da tayi ta jifarta dasu jikinshi yana tsuma ya isa wurinta ya wanka mata mari har sai da taga tartsatsin wuta,sannan ya zaro USB dake kan caza yayita laftarta da ita tana kuwwa, dak'yar ta sami sa'ar shigewa d'akinta ta dantse tareda fad'uwa kasa tana tumurmusau, saboda dukan ya shiga jikinta sosai har ma ya fasa mata wani wuri, Daddy kam ganin ta shige d'akin yasa yabi bayan Bily da gudu kamar ya kifa zuwa d'akinta, inda ya taraddda ita tana had'a kayanta, cike da fargaba ya fara yi mata magiya kamar yayi kuka yace

Ya sauke ajiyar zuciya yace 

"Bana fatar abunda zaisa in raba shinfid'ata dake koda ko na rana d'aya ne, kuma kinfi kowa sanin banda wannan jarabawa da ta fad'o mani da ko kwana biyu bana iya yi ban kusanceki ba, da kinsan irin tarin damuwar da ke raina dangane da hakan da baki furta cewa in barki acen ba, kiyi hak'uri insha ALLAH zan nemo magani soon don zuciyata bazata iya jure wanann k'alubalen ba gaskiya"    ya k'are maganar cikin wata muryar tausayi, Munawwa ranta ya k'ara jagulewa tace,

Aiko tun a daren ya fara process d'in nemarwa kamshi magani, koda safiya ta waye tunda sassafe ya fice gidan ya nufi wajen wani kwararren Dr mai suna Dr bulus,  inda yayi iya gwade gwadenshi amman bai gano matsalar komi ba, hasali ma shi lafiya k'alau ya hangoshi babu wata matsala a tattare dashi, cike da mamaki Basarake ya baro wurinshi yayi gida bai b'oyewa Munawwa ba ya sanar da ita komi, cike da jin dad'i suka had'u suna ta murna wanda daga nan ne ma ya k'ara gwada sa'arshi, sai dai kash har ma jiya tafi yau don kuwa ko motsi jarumarshi bata yi, cike da damuwa yayi wannan yinin Man Munawwa sai kwantar masa da hankali take yi amman ina domin kuwa shigewa yayi d'akinshi yana ta kuka kamar wani k'aramin yaro. 

Itama ganin damuwar da ta ya shiga ne yasa ta shige nata d'akin tana kaiwa ALLAH kukanta, akan ya yaye masu wannan matsala da ta kunno masu kai ko don tattalin d'an tayin cikinta da ALLAH ya bata. 

Bayan kwana uku Wasa wasa damuwa tayi masa yawa duk da nusar da shi d'in  da Munawwa take yi, akan ya barwa ALLAH komi su rungumi kaddararsu amman ina kullum abu sai gaba yake masa, wanda har ya kai da kwanciya ciyo duk a sanadin damuwar, Munawwa damuwa goma da ashirin ga renon cikin da takeyi ga tattalin lafiyar Mijinta da ya rikice mata gabaki d'aya ta rasa gano kanshi, a gefe d'aya kuma tana kewar Kaduna da Mummynta, tana d'okin ganinta koda zata rab'u a jikinta taji sanyi, amman ganin halin da yake ciki yasa ta bar abun a ranta don kuma kada ya k'ara zargin wani abu.

 A rana ta hud'u ne da zuwanshi Asibiti ya k'ara fita zuwa wajen wani likitan, Munawwa ta sauko falon  k'asa ta zauna tana kallon yanda 'yanmatan Ma'aikatanta keta kaiwa da kai da komo, inda suna hangota kowace tazo da rawar jiki tana kai gaisuwarta, inda d'aya daga cikinsu taje ta kawo mata da tasan tana sha tun bayan samun cikinta, Munawwa ta karb'a sannan ta basu Umurnin su tafi su ci gaba da ayukansu, duk suka mik'e kowace ta koma KAN aikinta,  ta fara kurbar lemun a hankali har ta fara jin bacci aiko ta langab'e a wurin tana ta sharar bacci abunta, sai ga wannan wadda ta kawo mata lemun tazo cikin rawar jiki ta rik'ata suka haye sama, kai tsaye d'akinta sannan ta fito tayi d'akin Basarake jikinta yana rawa ta fiddo wasu takardu ta saka a wata durowa, sannan ta fito ta koma wurin Munawwa ta zare zoben dake hannunta, jikinta yana kyarma ta fiddo wayarta dake mak'ale a jikinta ta fara d'aukar  zoben photo ta ko'ina, sannan ta maida mata zoben tareda k'ara mak'ale wayarta a jiki ta gyara mata kwanciya ta fito ta janyo mata k'ofa, zuciyarta cike da jin dad'in cin nasara ga aikinta. 

Munawwa tana ta barcinta har Basaraken ya dawo ganin bai ganta a falo ba yasa ya shigo d'akinta, ganin da yayi tana ta barcinta yasa wani tausayinta ya kamashi, saboda ko yanzu binciken da aka yimasa angano lafiyarshi k'alau, amman shi yasan ba lafiyar sai dai ALLAH bai basu ikon gano matsalar bane, ya zo bakin gadon ya durk'usa hawaye suna zuba akan fuskarshi ya d'aga rigar jikinta ya shafo lafaffen cikinta, sannan ya kai bakinshi yayiwa fatar cikin kiss har sau biyu wanda jin hakan yasa tayi saurin bud'e idonta, ganinshi yasa ta yunk'ura ta tashi zaune hannunshi cikin nata tace,   "Har ka dawo?"   ta d'an yamutsa fuska ta kalli agogo da sauri ta zaro ido tace,    "Na shigesu ashe na dad'e ina baccin?kai gaskiya nasha bacci sannu da dawowa"   Basarake kallonta kawai yake yi saboda har lokacin zuciyarshi cike take da tsananin tausayinta,  ya saka hannu ya shafo fuskarta yace,    "Sleeping beuty"   tayi 'yar dariya ta mik'e tsaye tace,     "Bari inyi SALLAH yau bacci ya mamayeni ko girki banyi maka ba"    ya bita da kallo har ta shige toilet d'in sannan ya sauke ajiyar zuciya ya zauna gefen gadon tareda yin tagumi yana nazarin neman mafita, don zuciyarshi ta wassafo masa kawai ya fita waje adubo shi, gashi kuma sai dai baya son wani yasan wannan matsalar, haka ma baya son rabuwa da ita ya zama dole su tafi tare don haka zai san dubarar da yayiwa SU Ummi akan tafiyar. 

Har ta fito bai ma san ta fito ba saboda yayi zurfi akan tunaninshi,aiko ta yarfa masa ruwan hannunta ya dawo firgigit ganin hararar da take yi masa yasa yayi gajeruwar dariya sannan ya mik'e  ya fice,   tabi bayanshi da kallo cike da tsananin k'aunarshi da a kulum take k'ara linkuwa a zuciyarta, sannan itama tayi dariyar ta fara tada sallar.

"ALLAH ya tsinewa Ubanda ya haifeku duka daga ke har ita k'atuwar 'yar iskar, miyagu shegu  debabbin albarka ALLAH ya walakantaku ya daidaya rayuwarku, Indai har sai nayiwa Wulakantattun  karuwai irinku biyayya zan zauna gidan Ubana to sai dai in bar shi har abadan abade,kuma ki  sanar da ita WALLAHI sai na d'auki fansana akanta ko ba yau ba, Daddy Kuma na bar mata shi idan ma tana so zan kawo mata Wuk'ar da zata kasheshi, amman tayi tsumayin zuwan ranar da zan waigo kanta."   tana k'are fad'ar maganar tayi cikin gidan kamar wata zakanya ta shiga d'akinta tayi ta jido  kayanta tana lodawa jaka, sai da ta d'auki duk abunda take buk'ata sannan ta janyo jakunkunan ta fito ganin Daddy falo yasa tayi  turus, yana ganinta yayi saurin zuwa wurinta cikin rud'ewa yace,, "Ina zaki je?"   tayi banza dashi tana k'ok'arin zagayeshi ta wuce, ya katsa mata wata k'atuwar tsawa ya sauke mata wani bahagon mari ya karb'e jakar ya maida mata d'aki, itama ya turata d'akin ya rufe da makulli sai huci yakeyi ya nufi wurin Ila yana yi masa gargad'iin kada ya kuskura ya barta ta fito koda baisani ba. 

Munara ko sai kukan takaici take yi tareda cin alwashin sai ta bar gidan gobe ko ta halin kaka, Billy ko abin nema ya samu jikinta yana rawa taje ta dafowa Munawa Indomie mai cike da barbaden maganinta, ta baiwa Daddy tace dashi ya kaiwa Munara, cike da tausayinta da take nuna masa tace, 

"Don ALLAH Alhajina ka tabbatar taci abincinnan, don ina lura da ita tsawon kwanakinnan duk ba wani abincin kirki take CI ba a gidan"    Daddy cikin tsananin jin dad'in ganin Bily ta damu da Babynshi ya karb'i abincin ya nufi d'akinta, inda ya zura key ya bud'e d'akin cike da fara'arshi ya ajiye abincin yana bata hak'uri akan dukan da yayi mata, sannan ya bata umurnin cin abincin itako saboda tasa aranta is zai kasheta bazata ci abincin ba, ta kafe tak'i cin abincin Bily tazo tana k'ara lallaminta tareda bata baki akan tayi k'ok'ari taci abincin koda kad'an ne, Munara ko ta fitittike akan bazata ci ba k'arshe ma shigewa toilet tayi ra dantse ta barsu nan zaune sake da baki,   aiko cikin jin haushi Bily ta fashe da kukan k'arya Daddy ya rikice sai hak'uri yake bata tareda yi mata alwashin bazai bar d'akin ba har sai yaga taci abincin, Munara ko sai kallon tagar Toilet d'insu take yi tana tunanin hanyar matsera, duk iya bugun toilet d'in da yakeyi akan ta bud'e amman tak'i bud'ewa dole ya hak'ura cike tsananin fushi ya fita ya k'ara dantseta yana ta tunanin ta wace hanya zai ladaftar da ita, har ya samu ta dinga girmama Bilynshi. 

Munara kam tana jin fitarshi ta fito ta d'auki abincin ta bud'a window'n d'akinsu ta jefar da plate d'in duka, sannan ta kwanta zuciyarta cike da bak'in ciki ta kwana ranar,  duk da kwanan rabi da rabi ne tayi, don ko taci alwashin dole ne ma ta bar gidan, aiko asubar farin tayi dubarar cire glasses din window'n nasu ta fara jefa 'yar karamar jakarta da ta zuba 'yan abun bukatarta na dole, sannan itama ta kurkuda dak'yar da bala'i ta samu ta fita ta bayan d'akunansu, sadaf sadaf ta kawo bakin gate ta zare k'atuwar  sakatar k'ofar gate d'in ta fice,    kasancewar lokacin duhu dulum ba wani abun hawa da ke yawo a lokacin, da k'afa ta taka har tayi nesa sosai da unguwarsu sannan ta sami abun hawa ta nufi gidansu Nusy, Kasancewar Baban Nusy ya bud'e gidan ya tafi masallaci ta sami damar shigewa gidan kuduf, inda kai tsaye d'akin Nusy ta nufa ganin tana ta baccinta hankali kwance yasa ta maka mata duka a baya, a razane ta falka saboda tsammaninta Babanta ne ya sake dawowa tada ta sallahr asuba, ganin Munara yasa tayi saurin mutsutsuke ido cike fargaba tace,    "Munara lafiya na ganki yanzu gidanmu?"   Munara ta mik'e ta nufi toilet d'in Nusy tayi wanka ta sullo arwallah koda ta fito Nusy tana sallah saboda jin da tayi Munara tana wanka ne tayi toilet d'in tsakar gidan taje tayi arwallarta, agurguje Munara tayi sallah'r sannan ta saka wasu kaya cikin rawar jiki tace da Nusy    "idan anzo nemana kice baki ganni ba kuma baki san inda naje ba, saboda Bily so takeyi takasheni da raina, Abuja zanje in jefi tsuntsu biyu da dutse d'aya, don haka kada ki sanar da kowa inda naje ba ruwanki."   Nusy cike da tashin hankali tace,    "Keda wa zaki je ne?"   Munara tace.  "Ni da Dr Hafis shi zai zo ya daukeni tare zamu je"

Tana k'are maganar ta yafa gyalenta tareda rataya jakarta tace,   "Zo ki rakani"

Nusy ta mik'e ta zunbula hijabinta suka fito gidan aiko suka ci karo da Baban Nusy cike da fad'uwar gaba tace,    "Rakata zanyi Baba ta hau abin hawa"    Baban Nusy ya kalli Munara yace, 

"Waike don ubanki ban rabaki da 'yata ba?na sake ganin k'afarki a gidannan WALLAHI sai naci ubanki wuce ki bani wuri munafuka kune kuke ta k'ara lalata man yarinya"   Munara ta wuce sum sum sannan ta waigo tace, 

"Ka tambayi 'yar taka ai itama tasan har na fita natsuwa don ni ban tab'a bin maza ba, ita ko ciki nawa aka zubar mata agaban idona?, niko duk lalacewata ban tab'a kusantar zina ba balle har inyi ciki a zubar"

Tana k'are fad'ar maganar ta gyara zaman jakarta ta fice lungun, zuciyarta cike da KUNA tareda k'ara tsinewa Bily da ta janyo mata har ayi mata gori akan gida. 

    Kai tsaye ta tare Napep ta nufi wani waje ta tsaya tana jiran kiran Dr, aiko tara da rabi tsaf taji kiranshi yana sanar da ita ya k'araso, tayi masa kwatancen inda take ya d'auketa suka kama hanyar barin garin Kaduna zuwa Abuja.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*


*68*

Daddy tun k'arfe bakwai ya nufi d'akin Munara da nufinshi na budeta ya lallasheta akan abunda yayi mata, koda ya bud'e d'akin yaji gabanshi ya fad'i saboda jakar da yagani kan gadon kaya duk a watse, jikinshi yana rawa ya nufi k'ofar toilet ya fara kiranta amman yaji shiru, ya tura k'ofar yaga ta bud'e ya shiga ko'ina ya wara idanunshi bai ganta ba, cikin tashin hankali ya fito yazo ya fara bud'a durowowinsu duka yana lek'awa namma bai ganta ba, sai gashi da yin goho yana leken k'ark'ashin gadon wai a zatonshi ko nan ta b'oye nanma wayam bata, cikin tsananin tashin hankali ya fara kiran bily, sai gata ta k'araso da gudunta don daman tana kitchen tana k'ok'arin had'awa Munara break mai had'e da barbaden maganin, itama ta wara ido ganin yanda d'akin yake cikin rud'ewa tace,     "Na shigesu Alhajina Ina Munara?"     cikin wata tsananin fad'uwar gaba yace,, "Aljanu sun d'auke Baby"    Bily ta zaro ido itama cike da tashin hankali tace.    "Daman tana da aljanune?"   Daddy ya kai zaune ba shiri cikin muryar kuka yace,     "To in ba su ba waye zai fitar da ita cikin d'akin da ke rufe  kuma key yana hannuna?"   Bily ta wara idonta zuwa windown cikin rawar baki tace,    "Wallahi ta k'ofarnan ta dira duba ka gani gashi DUK ta ciccire glases d'in"  cikin sauri Daddyn ya kai kallinshi ga tagar zuciyarshi sai halbawa take yi, cikin tsananin tashin hankali yace

"Bari inje wajen Ila ko yaga fitarta"   ya fice da sauri Bily ta rungume hannu baya tana huci tace,    "Ni wannan yarinya zata yiwa iskanci?waton dai asara take so ta jaza man kenan?, ai WALLAHI ko akan ubanta zan fanshewa don ko kwandalata bazata YI ciyon kai ba."     Daddy a firgice ya fara k'wallawa Ila kira cikin hargowa, Ila ya k'araso da gudunshi yace,    "Gani 'yar labai"    Daddy kafin yace masa komi sai da ya fara watsa masa maruka a haukace, sannan ya fara tambayarshi ya akayi ya bar Munara ta fita?   Ila ya rud'e jin Munara bata gidan ya fara rantse rantsen shima baisan ta fita ba, amman tabbas koda ya tashi yaga k'ofar gidan abud'e, aiko tashin hankalin Daddy ya k'aru cikin hargagin bala'i yace dashi duk inda zai je ya kawo masa Babynshi to yaje ya dawo da ita, idan ba haka ba WALLAHI gidan yari zai k'are sauran rayuwarshi. Ila jiki yana rawa ya fice bai zame ko'ina ba sai wurin bokanshi, akan yana so arufe masa bakin Daddy ya barshi zama gidan dolenshi, don tunda yaci alwashi akan Munara bai samu ba to dole ne ya fanshe kan Bily matar gidan. 

Daddy kam da jallabiya dake jikinshi ya fad'a mota aguje ya fito zuwa gidan Alhaji Baba, don azatonshi ko gidan taje wurin Mummynta tunda yana da labarin tana gidan tak'i zuwa gidan yayanta dake rikonta don mutuwar zuciya acewarshi. 

Ya ajiye motarshi k'ofar gidan ya shiga har k'ofar sitroom d'in Alhaji Baba ya fara rafka sallama, Alhajin Ya fito cike da mamakin ganinshi a gidan tunda sassafe, Daddy bai tsaya wani gaida shi ba yace. 

"Baby nike nema ko tazo gidannan"    Alhaji Baba ya kalleshi shekeke yace,   "Ko bebiya ce ita to mu bamu ganta ba, don haka kada ka sake zuwa gidana neman wannan sangartacciyar 'yar taka, don ko kad'an bani da muhallin zamanta anan gidan"

Ran Daddy ya k'ara b'aci ba tareda yace dashi k'anzil ba ya juya ya fice gidan, Alhajin ya bishi da kallon takaici sannan yace,    "K'adan ka fara gani tunda kace tukunyar wani bazata tafasa ba, to kai taka ko zafi bazata yi ba, ALLAH ya shiryeka idan kana da rabon shiriya"   sannan ya juya abinshi ya shige gida, Daddy kam cikin rud'u ya shiga motar ya fara zagayen gidajen 'yan uwa har ya gaji bai ganta ba, sai cen ya tuno da Nusy kasancewar yasan gidansu ya nufi gidan, kai tsaye cike da masifa koda yaje ya taradda Babanta k'ofar gida,  ba tareda komi ba ya fito yace dashi,   "Ina 'yarka ta fito man da 'yata don tasan duk inda take"

Baban Nusy yayi masa kallon reni yace,    "Aini 'yata ba gantalalliya bace shiyasa da tazo gidannan na korata, don ni sam sam bana son huld'ar da sukeyi don na fahimci itace ke k'ara rikice man yarinya"

Cike da wani tashin hankali Daddyn ya ce, 

"Au tama zo gidan kenan Ka koreta?"  Baban Nusy kasancewarshi mafadaci shima yace, 

" Kud'in ka nawa ne ka saka man lokacin da zan gina gidan da za'a ce bazan korera ba?"

Daddy ya bishi da kallon sai kaci ubanka ya shige motarshi ya bar k'ofar gidan aguje, inda ya bar Baban Nusy sai zuba ruwan bala'i yake yi, aiko bai zame ko'ina ba sai police station ya d'auko masa motar 'yan sanda, kai tsaye suka nufi gidansu Nusy ganin baya k'ofar gidanne aka fara maka masa sallama, wani k'anen Nusy ya dawo inda aka aikeshi ganin 'yan sanda k'ofar gidan ya shige da gudunshi a firgice yana sanar da Mamarshi ga 'yan sanda nan k'ofar gidansu, Baban Nusy yana ji hanjin cikinshi suka juya a firgice ya fito, Nusy ma cikin barci taji abunda ake fad'a ta fito d'akin a bala'e tayi k'ofar gida, koda ta je Wani d'an sanda yana rik'e da Mahaifinta zasu saka shi motarsu, Nusy cikin firgici ta fara kuka tana rok'on Daddy akan yayi hak'uri, don tunda taganshi tasan kan Munara ne yake so yayiwa Daddynta wulak'anci, cike da masifa Daddy yace

"Ina kika kai man 'yata ne?"    cikin kuka Nusy tace,    "Abuja tace man zata je tareda wannan mai sonta Dr Hafis, dashi tace man za su je don ALLAH ka sa a k'yale Babana WALLAHI baisan komi ba akan b'atanta"  Daddy ya baiwa 'yan sanda Umurnin su tafi dashi har sai ya dawo, sannan shi ya fad'a mota duk da rok'on da Nusy da Matar babanta suke yi masa bai ji ya tafi abinshi, kai tsaye hanyar Abuja ya nufa idonshi a rufe inda tun kafin akai ko'ina saboda tsananin gudun da yake yi motar ta k'wace masa, ya saki hanya yayi daji sai da yaje bakin wani iccce ta tsaya, Mutane suka yo wurinshi da gudu suna ta salati dak'yar aka ciroshi a motar, ALLAH ya tsare baiji wani rauni da yawa ba sai da hannunshi da ya sami karaya, sai d'an raunukan da yaji dole aka taimakeshi aka saka shi wata motar aka juya dashi zuwa Asibiti.

Munara kam Suna tafe yana kallonta yana lumshe ido,  aranshi yana ta k'issima irin yanda zai sarrafata cikin ruwan sanyi, ita kam ta lula sosai cikin nazarin ta wace hanya ce zata ci galaba akan wargaza auren cin Amanar da aka yi mata, don taci alwashin duk hanyar da zata bi sai ta bita ta raba auren Munawwara da Aliyu.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*


*69*


Ya kalleta da kyau yana 'yar dariyar shakiyanci yace   "my fiance tunanin me ake yi ne haka?"  ta yi 'yar gajeruwar dariya tace,   "Ina tunanin hanyoyin da zanbi in wargaza shegen aurennan ne."  Dr yayi 'yar dariya sannan ya sosa kanshi yace,    "Wai ma dai in tambayeki shin yanzu idan munje a'ina zaki sauka ne? Don nasan ko giyar wake kika sha ba zaki tunkari gidan Aliyu ba"

Aiko sai lokacin taji wata fad'uwar gaba ta ziyarceta, saboda ita shaf ta manta da wannan nazarin saboda idanuwanta a rufe suke akan ita dai kawai  ta bar Kadunar ta daburce  gaba d'aya bakinta yana rawa tace,    "Ka kaini gidanku mana wurin Babarka don dai nasan baka da aure balle ince muje gidanka"    DR ya yi 'yar dariya yace,   "To yanzun ma idan kin zauna gidan nawa me zai ragu a jikinki? Sannan da kike zancen inda ina da Mata sai kije aganinki matar tawa zata amince da duk k'aryar da zan zo mata da ita akanki? "   Munara tayi wani mere tace,   "Me zai hana ta yarda  tunda dai zata ga banyi kama da 'yan iska ba ai ko?"  Dr ya k'yalk'yale da dariya yace,   "Au waton su 'yan iskan ma kama ce dasu? Lalle My fiance aikinki yana kyau, amman dai be serious ina zan kaiki idan muje d'in?"   Munara ta yatsina fuska tace,   "Ka kama mani haya mana don duka duka bazan wuce sati biyu ba acen zan dawo gida."

Dr ya b'ata fuska sannan yace,, "Me yasa har yanzu baki amince dani ba?ko dai har yanzu kina shakku akaina ne? Yanzu ki duba fa aikin da kika sakani har nasa an kammala komi amman duk da haka babu yarda tsakanimmu to har sai yaushe ne zuciyarki zata natsu dani?"    Munara tayi masa wani kallon uku saura kwata tace,    "Daman ku maza ai bakuda aminci, to nikam ta yaya zanyi gangancin amincewa da kai?haka kawai kaje ka zuk'e mani ruwan jiki tatas ka juya man baya."    Dr yayi shiru yana nazarin masifaffen wayo irin nata, amman yasa aranshi duk runtsi sai ya lashi zumarta tunda har ya kwadaitu da ita,  tunda dai aurenta zaiyi don ya fara sanin wacece tun a yanzu ba laifi bane, *(aganinshi kenan fa don wannan hanya sam sam ba mai b'ullewa bace, ina kira gareku 'yanmata kannenmu da 'ya'yanmu ku kiyayi dad'in bakin maza, kuyi tattalin budurcinku don duk amincinki da namiji idan kika bashi fuska  sai  yaci amanarki, daga baya ki dawo kina cizon yatsa ku kula da kyau wasu maza mayaudara ne, wasu maza basu da tabbas wasu maza azzalumai ne sai sun gama tsotseki tass su cillar dake su samo wata sabuwa su barki da tab'on da har abada ba zai goge a rayuwarki ba, don ALLAH KU kiyaye kuyi tattalin mutuncinku ta hanyar kare budurcinku ko ana ha maza ha mata ALLAH ya k'ara tsare muna ku gaba d'aya😥).*

Shirunsu ya katse ne saboda wayar Munara da ke ta vibration alamar shigowar kira, da sauri ta d'aga saboda ganin mai kiran cike da fargaba tace,    "Nusy ya de ALLAH yasa dai lafiya?"  acen b'angaren Nusy tana kuka tace,    "BA lafiya ba Munara saboda Daddynki yazo har gida ya tafi da Babana, yanzu haka kin ganni nan a bakin police station sun hanani shiga, don ALLAH ki basu umurnin su fiddo muna shi tunda ke kinsan bai da masaniya akan tafiyarki"   cike da takaicin Daddyn nata tace,    "Bani wani d'an sandan ai wannan ma ba dacewa bace duk da koshi babban naki ai hak'k'ina ne ya bishi, ai ga irinta nan gobe ya k'ara mtssss"  ta buga uban tsaki Nusy kam duk da taji haushin maganarta amman dole ta hakure ta nufi wani d'an sanda ta mik'a masa wayar tareda yi masa bayanin wadda ake tuhumar sun batar ce gata nan suji gaskiyar zance,  aiko D'ansandan ya katsa mata wata k'atuwar tsawa yace,   "Who is on the line"   Munara  cikin rawar murya tace   "My Name is Munara Zakari nice wadda Daddyna ya rufe baban k'awata, don ALLAH ku watsar da case d'in saboda ai shi yasan inda naje kawai dai ya sa ku kama shi ne don daman cen babu jituwa a tsakaninsu, don ALLAH ku sakeshi babu ruwanshi WALLAHI."

D'ansandan yayi shiru yana nazarin maganarta sannan yace,    "Kin tabbatar da gaskiya ce kika fad'a man?"   tace    "Eh iya gaskiyar kenan sir idan yana kusa ma ku had'ani dashi zamuyi magana."    jin hakan yasa ya gamsu da jawabinta ya mik'awa Nusy wayar ya nufi inda 'yan uwanshi, suka yi k'us k'us d'insu sannan suka ce da Nusy da Matar Babanta su shigo daga ciki, jikinsu yana rawa suka shiga babban 'yansandan yace su jira akira Daddyn ayi masa bayanin abunda Munara tace,   sannan idan sunji rashin gaskiya a tattare dashi zasu sakeshi, aiko suka yi ta kiran Numbarshi akashe saboda tun wurin da yayi hatsarin aka sace wayar, ganin basu sameshi ba yasa suka ce da su Nusy su kawo 10k na belinshi kafin zuwan Alhajin, aiko jikin Nusy yana rawa ta haye acab'a ta nufi gida ta d'auko kud'in ta kawo masu sannan ta saka hannu aka fito masu dashi, suka yi gida cike da murna da farin cikin cin nasara. 

**

Munara kam tana ajiye wayar ta sauke k'atuwar ajiyar zuciya tace,    "Wai wannan shegen police akwaishi da masifa kaji shi kamar zai cenyeni da hargowarshi"    Dr yace    "Ashe kina tsoron police?"  Munara ta tuno da sojojin da Basarake yasa suka lallasata ta furzar da iska mai zafi tace,    "Aini har sojoji nayi artabu dasu balle police d'in banza,amman shi kanshi wanda yasa suyi man barazana sai na wargaza tashi rayuwa shege mayaudari sai yasan dani yake zancen, yawwa ina yarinyar da kasa tayi muna wannan aikin? don ALLAH ina son ka had'ani da ita."

Dr ya d'aga kafad'a yace    "wannan ba damuwa bace ko yau kikeso zan kirata tazo"  

Cikin jin dad'i tace,   "Yawwa nagode da kokarinka"   yana 'yar dariya sannan ya ri'ko hannunta yace,   "Ke a haka kike taki godiyar?"    Munara tayi saurin k'wace hannunta tace,   "Kalli gabanka ni dai kar kaje ka watsar damu in shiga uku ba tareda na cika gurina ba."

Dr yayi 'yar dariyar yace,    "Sarkin tsoro to duk shure shurenki ai bazai hanaki mutuwa ba"     itama tayi 'yar dariya sannan tace.  

"Kai ma idan ka isa ka tsallake  tarkonta mugani,  watak'ila ma har sai ka rigani tafiya, don nikam jikina yana bani zanyi shekaru da yawa a duniya."

DR ya kyalkyale da dariya saboda kwatantata da yayi da siffar  tsufa yace,    "Wai My fiance a tsohuwa WALLAHI sai anyi masifaffiyar tsohuwa anan"   itama ta k'yalk'yale da dariyar, haka suka ci gaba da firarsu har suka kai wani gari inda suka tsaya suka siye abinda zasu ci sannan suka ci  gaba da tafiyarsu sannu a hankali har suka shiga  garin Abuja,   inda ba tareda yayi shawara da ita ba kai tsaye gidanshi ya wuce da ita,    bayan ya biya shoprite yayi masu sayayyar abun buk'ata, kasancewarshi gwauro ba komai ne yake tanada a gidan ba.

Cike da fargaba tabi bayanshi da kallo har suka k'arasa babban falonshi, ta zauna yaraf akan kujera saboda gajiya shikam kai tsaye d'akinshi ya nufa da 'yar k'aramar jakarta da tazo da ita,   sannan ya fito ya bata Unurnin ta shiga tayi wanka, Munara ta mik'e zuciyarta tana d'ar d'ar ta shiga d'akin da ya nuna mata, ganin kayanshi da komai nashi yasa ta gano d'akinshi ne aranta tace,   _"WALLAHI duk wayonka sai ka barni"_

Sannan ta fad'a toilet ta fara wanka sai gashi yazo k'ofar toilet d'in yana fad'in   "Ga towel d'in na manta ban baki ba"   Munara ta d'an bud'e k'ofar k'adan ta zuro hannunta da nufin ya bata aiko ya YI kamar bai gane ba ya fara tura k'ofar da nufin shiga, aiko tayi zuruf ta k'wace towel d'in ta maida k'ofar ta dantse da k'arfi sai ga k'arar Dr ta jiyo yana fad'in wayyo hannunshi, ta CI gaba da wankanta tana yi mashi dariyar mugunta aranta tace

_"gobe ka k'ara don Ubanka"_

Sannan ya fice d'akin zuwa kitchen yana 'yar dariyar jin dad'in wai yau Fiance d'inshi ce a gidanshi, yarinyar da a kullum da begenta yake kwana yake tashi, yau gata a gidanshi a arha bagas zai ci banza.

A gaskiya idan har bazaka bani nawa d'aki dabam ba zan bar maka gidanka nikam, don ba karuwanci ya kawoni Abuja ba kaima ka sani."    Dr kam da tunda ta juya yabi bayanta da kallo, tudun d'uwawunta da yagani ya k'ara tado masa da zallar shaawarta, da sassarfa yaje bayanya ya tsaya cikin daukewar tunaninshi ya janyota jikinshi kamar wani magnet, ya zura hannunshi ta gabanta ya fisgo towel d'in, aiko ba shiri ta juyo tareda damk'e towel d'in ta k'arfin tsiya, shikam ganinta a gabanshi duk rabin breasts  d'inta a waje yasashi k'ara dulmiyawa, idonshi a lumshe yayo kanta da nufinshi na kaiwa saman k'irjinta kiss, aiko ba shiri yaji saukar wani gawurtaccen Mari akan fuskarshi, sai gashi da bud'e jajayen idanuwanshi ya dorasu akan kykkyawar fuskarta, Munara ta maka masa wata uwar harara tareda nunashi da d'an yatsa tace,,   "Zanyi maka abunda sai kasha mamakina matuk'ar ka k'ara zuwa kusa dani, sau nawa zan fad'a maka cewa ni ba 'yar iska bace?, koko kana tantama ne in nuna maka wacece Munara a aikace?"

Dr yana 'yar dariya yace,    "Tunda baki so na daina yanzu shirya kije ga abinci cen kici nima bari inje inyi wankan."

"Ga numbata nan na saka maki duk ranar da kika zata fita ki kirani ki sanar dani, haka ma yanzu kafinnan dole kiyi k'ok'arin d'auko mani pic's d'in hijabban da take sakawar har ma da yanayin nikafs d'in nan zuwa gobe, idan kika kuskure ni yankan rago zanyi maki ba allurar guba ba, kuma koda wasa kika kuskura wannan maganar ta fito bakinki ki d'auka cewa ke sunanki shekakka, don kuwa WALLAHI sai na d'auki mummunan Mataki akanki da iyayenki da zuri'arki gaba d'aya sai naga bayansu, saboda ki tseratar da kanki da duk wanda ya had'a dangantaka dake wajen kame bakinki kiyi shiru, kuma duk abunda muka saka ki kiyi k'ok'arin yi muna aiki cikin gaggawa mai had'e da takatsantsan, don wannan bak'in tsoron naki idan baki rage shi ba rayuwarki tana cikin had'ari ba k'adan ba, don abu k'adan zai sa ki fallasa kanki don haka ki kiyaye kuma k'i iya takunki."

Munara tayi shiru tana nazari sannan ta dawo ta zauna don bata son duk abunda zai hana mata cikar burinta,   amman fa tasa aranta bazata zauna gidan Dr ba kada ya mamayeta ya cutar da ita.

Munawwa kam a 'yan kwanakin tana fama da jikinta sosai, saboda cikin yana sakata kasala ga amaye amaye da tsirfar tsiya, shiyasa kowane lokaci tana kwance kamar ruwa idan Basarake baya gida Ladi ce mai d'an rage mata 'yan guntayen ayukanta, duk da bata sakar mata fuskar shiga d'akinta ko na Aliyunta iyakacinta Kitchen da ko falo,   Basarake kam kullum damuwarshi d'aya ce matsalarshi gashi yana son barin k'asar ga jikin Munawynshi a kullum da take faman shan wahala da d'an tayin cikinshi, idan har yace ya tafi ya barta hsnkalinshi bazai tab'a kwantawa ba, haka ma idan ya tafi da ita wannan tsirfe tsirfen da take so ta ina zata same su?,   Damuwa ta saka shi a gaba wadda har ta kasa b'oyuwa akan fuskarshi,   zuciyarshi kullum cikin k'unci yake da tunani don yanzu su Kakale har sun gano akwai abunda ke damunshi, amman ita Kakale tafi jingina abun da bak'in kishinshi da ya saka a rai, shiyasa kullum cikin yi masa nasiha da lurar dashi abunda ya dace take,   shi kuma ga zurfin cikinshi ya hana ya fad'awa koda Nuradden ne yaji sanyi,   amman duk da haka a kullum safiya yana fafutukar nemarwa kanshi magani, don ma sunyu magana da wani DR a india inda yace zai turo masa magani shine yake dakon zuwanshi, wanda shine ma ya hanashi yunk'urin barin k'asar da yake son yi, saboda yanayin da yaga  Munawynshi take ciki. 

Wannan dama da ladi ta samu itace ta k'ara mata karfin gwiwar da zata aiwatar da shirinta,   Munawwa tana kwance a falo tana maida numfashin wahala saboda aman da ta k'are yi ba da dad'ewa ba,   sai ga sak'on Ummi na alalen da tace tana so Ladi ta shigo mata da kular, cikin jin dad'in ganin kular ta yunk'ura ta tashi fuskarta cike da fara'a ta karb'i kular, tace da Ladi ta d'auko mata plate da spoon a kitchen, Ladi jikinta yana rawa ta d'auko ta kawo mata sannan ta fice, Munawwa ta zuba alalen yawunta har suna tsinkewa saboda kwad'ayi, ta fara ci tana lumshe ido zuciyarta cike da jin dad'in dace da tayi da suruka ta gari, wadda bata gajiya da dawainiyarta a kullum safiya,  komi tace tana so ba b'ata lokaci zata aiko mata duk da wani zubin kunyarta take sa ta danne abu koda tana sonshi, tayi k'ok'arin yin abun da kanta k'arshe kuma tazo taji bata sonshi, tafi son na wani idan ya yi mata da hannunshi, shiyasa take saka Ladi yi mata wani zubin sai dai kuma sam bata jin dad'inshi kamar na Ummi, shiyasa take b'oye kunyar ta kira Kakale ta sanar da ita tayi ta tsokanarta Munawwa tayi gum kawai saboda har lokacin tak'i sakin jikinta da Kakale suyi wasar jika da kakan da take yi mata, shiyasa a koda yaushe take k'ara girma a idonsu don sun tabbatarwa kansu Aliyu yayi dace da samun macen kirki, saboda kunyarta ba k'aramin burge Kakale take yi ba, saboda ganin yanda yanzu kunya tayi k'aranci a idanun Mutane. 

Munawwa taci alalen sosai sannan ta ajiye saura da nufin ta k'ara ci,    saboda taji dad'inta sosai kuma cinta ya haifar mata da kuzarin da ta dad'e bataji a jikinta ba,   ta mik'e ta nufi d'akin Aliyu da nufinta na gyareshi tsaf don 'yan kwanakin duk shi ke yin abunshi, ko tace zata yi hanata yakeyi saboda ganin yanda itama take fama da kanta,   aiko cike murnarta ta fara gyara masa d'akin lungu da sak'o har tazo kan wata drowa ta bud'a da nufin ajiye wasu kaya ne sai ga wasu ga wani file ya biyota ya fad'i takardun ciki sun watse,    ta daukosu tana harhadawa zata maidasu ne wata zuciya tace da ita ta bud'a tagani,   kasancewar bata da k'arfin jiki yasa ta d'an zauna gefen gadon ta fara dubawa, saboda sunan asibitin da tagani an rubuta a sama yaja hankalinta sosai,   musamman Sunan Aliyunta da ta gani jikin takardun a razane ta mik'e tsaye saboda abunda idanuwanta suka yi tozali dashi,  cikin tsananin fad'uwar gaba ta dafe k'irji wasu hawaye suka fara gangaro mata a fuska aranta tace

"H. I. V. Positive Aliyuna? innalillahi wa inna ilaihirrajiun"   idonta akan takardun ta k'urawa shekarar da aka yi masa test d'in aranta tace,   

"Shekara biyu kenan amman kuma a hakan yayi jarumtar  aurena  ba tareda ya sanar dani gaskiyar zance ba"     Hawaye wasu bayan wasu ta kasa d'auke idonta akan takardun sai kace Aliyun ne take kallo,   murdawar da taji cikinta yana yi ne yasa tayi saurin k'udundune takardun duka ta lallab'a ta fito d'akin ta nufi d'akinta, zuciyarta cike da bak'in cikin da misaltashi abu ne ba kad'an ba,   tana zuwa ta d'aga katifar gadonta ta jefa takardar sannan ta kwanta akan gadonta a wahalce saboda yanda take jin mararta ta rik'e tamau, tashin hankalinta bai k'aru ba sai lokacin da taga jini yana biyar k'afafunta, jikinta yana rawa ta ta durk'ushe wajen tana wani irin kuka gwanin ban tausayi,   saboda tausayin kanta da Aliyunta da ya mamaye mata zuciya uwa uba tausayin abun cikinta da zai zo duniya ya taradda wannan mugun labari, wanda shi kanshi rayuwarshi tana cikin ha'u'la'i shiyasa tak'i yunk'urin kiran kowa tafi son cikin ya zube gaba d'aya koda shi zai tsira,    tana nan durk'ushe tana murk'ususun wahala tana kuka saboda azabar da take ji a cikin zuciyarta da mararta, sai ga Aliyun ya shigo d'akin yanayin da ya ganta yasa shi fad'uwar gaba, aguje ya k'araso wurinta ya tallafota jikinshi yana kururuwar kiran sunanta,   danshin da yaji k'afarshi a ciki yasa ya kai kallonshi k'asanta aikuwa sai ga jini  malale a k'asa da kafafuwanta wanda har lokacin bai daina biyar k'afafunta ba,   a rikice yayi wata k'ara ya sunkuceta kamar wata 'yar yarinya ya fito da ita gidan zuwa motarshi , gaba d'aya 'yan aikinnata suka biyoshi suna kuka Ladi kam har da su shesheka saboda har ga ALLAH ta tausaya mata matuk'a gaya. 

Basarake kam a rud'e yaja motar ya nufi wata Asibitin dake kusa dasu saboda tsoron ya rasa abun cikin, Hankalinshi a tashe yayi parking bai jira azo a d'auketa ba ya fiddota motar suka shiga ciki, ganin halin da take cikine yasa akayi gaggawar karb'arta suka shiga da ita, ya biyosu baya yana rokonsu don ALLAH su ceci matarshi kada su bari cikinshi ya zube, gaba d'aya ya d'agawa nurse's da likitocin  hankali,  ganin yanda ya biyosu a rud'e yasa suka hanashi shiga saboda su samu suyi aikinsu a natse,   ya dawo yana ta safa da marwa saboda jin yakeyi kamar ya aza hannu akai yayita kuwwa,   saboda tsoron ya rasa Munawynshi ko abun cikinta don cikinsu ko guda ya rasa baisan ina zai jefa kanshi ba. 

Likitoci suka rufu kanta cikin k'warewa duk da asibitin ba wata babba ba ce amman tana da kwararrun likitoci da suka san aikinsu, shiyasa ALLAH ya taimakesu suka samu suka tsayar da  jinin har ma da cikin da suka yi nasarar aikinsu,   kasancewar jini ya zuba sosai a jikinta yasa aka nemi da a kwantar da ita ayi mata treatment sosai, wanda zai tado da jininta ya dawo ba tareda an k'ara mata wani jini ba,   duk da baiso ta kwanta asibitin ba Amman farin cikin cikin da akace ya tsaya ne yasa yayi saurin amincewa,   aka fito da ita zuwa wani d'aki na musamman sannan aka yi mata allurai sannan aka mak'ala mata ruwa, Sai lokacin hankalinshi ya dawo jikinshi ganin tana ta barcinta sharkaf tana ajiyar zuciya,   ya d'aga wayarshi ya kira Alhajinshi ya sanar dashi sannan ya kira Ummi ya fad'a mata, zuciyarshi cike da farin cikin nasarar tsayar da cikin da ALLAH yayi, don ya riga da ya k'wallafa ranshi sosai ga cikin saboda ganin halin da yake ciki, wanda idan har ba ALLAH ya kawo k'arshen abun ba shikenan shi da haifuwa haihata haihata.

Daddy kam a gurguje aka nufi wata Asibiti dashi wani bawan ALLAH yayi tsaye akanshi har aka gyara masa hannun aka yi masa dressing d'in ciwukan da yaji,  kasancewar a hankalinshi yake yasa da aka yi masa bill d'in kud'in yace da mutumin ya ara mashi wayarshi ya kira matarshi, aiko ba musu ya bashi ya saka numbar Bily da take hardace akanshi ya danna mata kira, tana ta ringing bata d'auka ba saboda tana cen suna watsewarsu da zuby, sai da yayi kiran da yafi biyar sannan ta d'aga da wata muryarta mai kama da ta bacci,   cike da d'okin jin muryarta yace 

"Ina asibiti Bily na sami accident naje biyar Baby da aka ce mani tayi Abuja, ki shiga d'akina ki d'auko ATM  d'ina kije ki fidda kud'i ki kawoman ganinan ina jira"

 Bakin yana rawa tace, "Ita wannan numbar ta wace ka kirani da ita halan?"   Daddy yana dariyar k'arfin hali yace,    "wayar Wani bawan ALLAH ne da ya taimaka mani sai kinzo kiyi masa godiya"

"Wai miye haka? Baki ganin abun alkhairin da ya samemu ne?"

   Asibiti kam Basarake yana manne da Munawynshi yana kallon yanda take ta maida numfashi a hankali,  tausayinta ya kamashi har cikin ranshi saboda yasan ba k'aramin hak'uri take dashi ba, tana iya k'ok'arinta akan gudun b'acin ransa duk yaji zafi sosai akan rashin kiranshi da batayi tun farkon fara jin ciyon ba, don kenan in banda ALLAH ya soshi da rahama ya dawo dashi lokacin  da tayi masa k'atuwar asarar da maidata abu ne ba kusa ba,  yana nan zaune yanata sak'awa da kwancewa sai ga su Kakale da Ummi har ma Alhajinshi, cike farin cikin ganinsu yaje ya ri'ko hannun Kakale suka k'araso jikin gadon, ya jawo masu kujeru suka zauna suna yi masa ya jikin Munawwa yana amsa masu saboda Munawwa har lokacin bacci take sharkaf, sannan suka yi mata fatar samun lafiya tareda rok'on ALLAH ya tsare gaba,sannan Alhajin yayi sallama dasu ya fice Basarake ya rakoshi suka fito, sai gashi yaci karo da wata mace da sauri yace "sorry" ba tareda ya kalli fuskar matar ba yaci gaba da firarshi da Alhajinshi,  Munara kam zuciyarta ba k'aramin bugawa tayi ba saboda ganinshi, ta sauke k'atuwar ajiyar zuciya saboda  kallonta da baiyi ba, duk da ba lalle bane ya ganeta don lik'abin dake sanye a fuskarta. 

   Basarake kam bayan sunyi sallama da Alhajinshi ya dawo d'akin da Munawwa take, suka CI gaba da firarsu da su Ummi har dare inda zuwa lokacin Munawwa ta falka sun gaggaisa da su Ummi sunyi mata ya jiki, Ummi ta zuba kunun ayar da ta had'o mata mai kabri yaji kayan had'i sannan ta zuba mata gurasar da tayo mata cike da kula, Munawwa taji dad'in aranta duk da zuciyarta bata sha'awar cin komai saboda damuwar dake ranta,  don har lokacin tak'i bari su had'a ido da Aliyunta, don ko shi fushi yake yi da ita akan rashin sanar dashi da batayi ba tun farkon fara jin ciyon a jikinta, Ummi ta tursasata sai da taci gurasar da tasha k'uli mai shegen dad'i da sauran tarkacen kayan had'in, sannan ta zuba mata  kunun ayar tasha da d'an yawa aka sake yi mata allurai da k'arin ruwa ta koma bacci.
