Nuradden yace.,    "Tabbas itace ko tantama banayi, kuma itacen ta jefaka a cikin wannan halin rashin k'arfin mazakutarka."   Basarake ya mik'e jikinshi yana rawa zai fita Nuradden ya rok'oshi yace,

"Ina zaka je?"

Basaraken yana huci saboda tsananin k'ufula da yayi yace,   

"Zanje in gani idan har na tabbatar itace to WALLAHI zanci Ubanta ne laada waje, don sai na koya mata hankali"    Nuradden ya dafa kafad'arshi yace,    "Cool Dawn My Man wannan ba itace hanyar da zaka bi wajen shawo kan matsalar ba, kabi komi daki daki cikin natsuwa har ka tantance gaskiya, sannan sai ka d'auki matakin da zaka d'auka, amman tukunna kafin cen kai da wace alama kake gane Munawwara ne?"

Basarake yayi masa wani kallo sannan yace,   "Bangane sheda kake nufi komi?"   Nuradden yace.  " Eh ina nufin akwai wata sheda da kake saurin gane Matarka?"   Basarake yayi shiru yana Nazari sannan yace,    "Akwai zoben da ke hannunta wanda dashi ne zanyi saurin ganota da inda take"    "Nuradden yayi 'yar dariya yace,    "A lokacin da take yi maka magana kaga zoben a hannunta". 

Basarake ya sauke ajiyar zuciya sannan yace

"Tabbas naga zoben a danyatsanta  hasali ma hannun da zoben yake shine na rik'eta dashi"   Nuradden yayi shiru sannan yace

     "zo muje nima inga in zan iya tantancesu"

Suka fito kowane zuciya sai sak'e sak'e takeyi sunata hasko fuskokin Munara da Munawwa a ido a k'ok'arinsu na son banbancesu, amman duk iya nacinsu basu gano komi ba haka suke zugum ba wata fira a tsakaninsu har suka isa Asibitin,inda kai tsaye d'akin suka nufa Basarake a gaba Nuradden a bayanshi, koda yaje ya taradda Munawwa sai baccinta take tana sauke numfashi a hankali,    Basarake yana zuwa ba b'ata lokaci ya wanketa da wani bahagon mari, a razane Munawwa ta falka saboda shak'ar da taji a wuyanta, ganin Aliyunta ne yake aikata wannan d'anyan hukunci akanta,  wasu hawaye masu zafi suka sirnano mata a fuska idonta akanshi tana yi masa wani kallo mai cike da tsantsar mamaki,   Aliyu kam idonshi a rufe yace,   "zakiyi bayanin wacece ke ko kuwa har sai na wulak'anta rayuwarki?" da sauri  Nuradden ya k'araso wurin yana k'ok'arin cire hannun Aliyun daga shak'ar da yayiwa Munawwara, saboda jikinshi ya bashi cewa Munawwa ce wannan ba Munara ba,  bakin Basaraken yana rawa saboda masifar da ke kanshi yace,   "Barni kawai in kashe shegiya kowa ma ya huta"     

Hargowar da yake yi wajen fad'in maganar yasa mutane shigo d'akin da sauri, cike da Mamaki nurse's d'in suke kallonshi, saboda ganin aika aikar da yake shirin aikatawa aiko dak'yar aka tab'i Munawaa a hannunshi yana ta surfa mata zagi cikin fitar hankalin da yayi na wuccin gadi. 

Munawwa Hawayenta basu daina sauka ba har lokacin idonta akanshi, ganin yanda yake ta huci yasa ta kalli Nurses d'in tace, 

"Me yasa kuka hanashi k'arasa nufinshi akaina? Wallahi na tsani duniyar da duk wani abu dake cikinta ni kaina buk'atar mutuwar nike yi ido rufe koda zan sami sauk'in rad'ad'in da ke cikin zuciyata"      ba Basarake kawai ba har kowa ma na d'akin ya tausaya mata matuk'ar tausayi akan abunda ta fad'a wanda ko tantama babu daga tsakiyar zuciyarta zancen yake fitowa,   Basarake cikin sabon tashin hankali ya kalli Nuradden, Nuradden yayi masa alama da ido akan ya tafi wurinta, aiko ya gano manufarshi cikin sanyin jiki ya k'arasa jikin gadon hawaye suna zuba akan fuskarshi ya ri'ko hannunta, da sauri ta fisge hannunta tareda nunashi da yatsa tace,   

"Kada kayi gangancin sake tab'ani matuk'ar ba kashe nin kazo da niyyar yi ba, tun kafin cutar da ke jikina ta karni in bar duniya a wulakance"   cike da tsoron lafuzzan da take ta furzowa Basarake yayi sa'ar ri'ko hannunta yace, 

"Kiyi hak'uri Matata WALLAHI ban dauki cewa ke bace shiyasa kika ga na aikata abunda nayi, kiyi hak'uri ki yafe mani don ALLAH Munawyta, ke kanki kinsan dagangan bazan yi hakan ba"

Munawwa ta bishi da kallo mai cike da tuhuma tace,    "Babu wani abu da zaka fad'a yayi tasiri akaina tunda har baka ji kunyar yi mani abunda kayi man ba, wanda ko a mafalki ban tab'a zata ba to gara kawai kayi nesa nesa dani,  don gudun watarana ka kaini kiyama ba shiri tun kafin masu cetona su zo"   ta k'are maganar cikin wani marayan kuka. 

Basarake kam kalamanta sun daureshi da jijiyoyi,kuma sun sunyi tasirin shiga jikinshi da kyau zuciyarshi ta tabbatar masa da  cewa lalle Munawynshi ce,  Nuradden kanshi kanshi ya k'ulle sosai don ya gasgata cewa wannan Munawwa ce, SAI dai kuma yana tantama idan har ba Munara ce tayiwa Basarake magana d'azu ba, ya dafe kanshi aranshi yace

Basarake kam kasa motsawa yayi saboda bala'in  jin nauyin abunda ya aikata ga masoyiyar matarshi wadda yake jin sonta  ko'ina lungu da sako na zuciyarshi. 

Idonshi akanta yana  kallon yanda take ta kuka gwanin ban tausayi,  zuciyar ta cika fam da jin kunyar abunda ya aikata yaja kafafunshi ya zauna gefen gadon cikin wata siririyar murya ya kira sunanta yace, 

"Munawwara bansan da wane baki zan baki hak'uri ba, amman kisani nayi nadamar abunda na aikata ba tareda na tantance gaskiya ba, sannan duk kalaman da kika fad'a akaina duka nagode, amman kisani yazama dole gareni akan in tashi in nemi lafiyata ko don gudun matsalar da kike k'ok'arin kunnuwa a tsakaninmu"    Munawwa tayi masa wani kallo aranta tace

_"Wato ma NI ke son kawo matsalar? Kai ka manta irin cin amanar da kayi mani, kasan kana da ciyon aids amman ka yaudareni ka aureni"_    
 
       Tana k'are zancen zucin ta fashe da wani sabon kuka,  Basaraken yayi saurin 
fad'in         "ki kwantar da hankalinki gobe gobennan zan bar kasarnan inje nemo lafiyata ko don in d'inke barakar da ta kunno muna a rayuwar aurenmu"

Munawwa kam kallonshi kawai takeyi zuciyarta cike fam da  tunanin irin zaluncin da yayi mata,  ya mik'e yana share hawayen dake fuskarshi ya fice, Nuradden ma yabi bayanshi har zuwa office d'in likita, kai tsaye ya nemi alfarmar abashi sallama zai koma gida da matarshi, ganin yanayinshi yasa aka basu takardar sallama ya d'aukota suka dawo gida,  zuciyoyinsu gaba d'aya ba dad'i inda tun cikin daren ya fara shirin barin k'asar,   Asubar farin ya d'auko jakarshi ya nufi d'akinta, koda yaje tana zaune akan sallaya kwance da tasbaha a hannunta tana lazimi hawaye sai kwaranya suke a idonta kamar fanfo, duk da ya damu sosai da yanayin da ya ganta amman hakan ya mazge ya tsaya kanta yace,    

"Ni zan tafi agadaz neman lafiyata da na rasa wadda akan hakan kika kasa hak'uri har kiketa jifata da k'azafi tareda miyagun kalaman da duk suka fito a bakinki, idan ALLAH yayi dawowata zamu d'ora inda muka tsaya idan kuma kanjamau ya kasheni a cen shikenan sai kiyi takaba ki auri wanda kike so mai yi maki abunda yafi wanda nayi maki a baya, haka ma wanda baya da ciyon kanjamau"

Yana k'are fad'ar maganar ya juya Munawwa dai ko motsin kirki kasa yi tayi saboda yanda zuciyarta take ta dakan lugude tunda taji ya ambaci wai k'azafin da tayi masa, wani kuka ya k'wace mata ba shiri wata zuciya tace da ita,   

_" kyaleshi kinji nad'e tabarmar kunyarshi ne yake yi da hauka,  watak'ila  saboda ya duba baiga takardun bane shiyasa ya biyo maki ta wannan hanyar"_

Munawwa ta k'ara sautin kukanta tana fad'in sunan ALLAH akan ya kawo mata agaji. 

Basarake kam yana k'ok'arin shiga motarshi ne sai ga Munawwa ya hango ta nufoshi sanye da hijabinta har k'asa,  sai dai fuskarta a murtuk'e take ba alamun sassauci a ciki, tana zuwa ta tsaya d'an nesa dashi tace, 

"Ba fa zan zauna ina kallo kana yiman abunda kaga dama BA, dole ne in sami hanyar da zan nemowa kaina waraka don bazan lamunci jiran sai ka dawo ba"

Tana k'are fad'ar maganar ta juya ta bar wajen yabi bayanta da kallo yana ta sak'a abunda take nufi da maganarta,  ranshi ya hasko masa ko gidansu zata tafiyarta take nufi?   cike da wata fad'uwar gaba ya BI bayanta da sassarfa ya haye sama kai tsaye d'akinta ya nufa, yana zuwa ya taradda ita inda ya barta tana ta kuka gwanin ban tausayi, Yaji kukan har tsakiyar zuciyarshi amman ya daure yace, 

"Ban lamunta ko harabar gidannan ki fita ba har sai na dawo, na fad'a maki kuma zan nanata ban amince da ki fita ko harabar gidannan ba dafatar kin jini?" 

Munawwa taci gaba da kukanta kawai ba tareda ta kula shi ba, jin haushin hakan yasa ya fice fuuu ya koma direba ya jashi aguje suka bar gidan, zuciyarshi cike fam da jin haushin Munawynshi saboda irin yanda halinta ya sauya cikin d'an k'ank'anin lokaci.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*

Saboda takaicin halin da yake ciki ko gidansu baije yayiwa su Ummi sallama ba,  har sai da yaje airport ne kafin su tashi ya kirata ya sanar da ita  zai yi tafiya, tayi masa fatar alkhairi amman ta nuna masa rashin alfanun tafiya ya bar matarshi cikin ciyo, ya kwantar da kai yayita nuna mata amfanin tafiyar tashi, don baya da matsala da Alhajinshi saboda tun jiya ya sanar dashi tafiyar duk da ya rufeshi bai fada masa ainahin Inda zai je ba da abunda zai kaishi,  Kakale dai ce yasan zata kawo masa cikas a tafiyar tashi amman idan ya dawo zai yi mata bayanin komi, don yasan wajibi ne ma said taga laifinshi tunda ita kullum ganin matsalarshi  take yi dangane da zamanshi da Munawynshi.


***

Bangarensu Daddy kuwa Ila cike da tsananin farin ciki ya wuni a ranar saboda murnar cikar burinshi, domin kuwa yayi alwashi tunda har aikinshi ya ci to ko ta hanyarta shi kam zai yi nashi arziki.

Bily kam anji maza ta baje tana kwanan wahala ta dau lokaci tana bacci, sanan ta tashi ta kalli Alhajinta da ya kasance cikin damuwar halin da take ciki, ta feshe shi da murmushi tace

"ka kwantar da hankalinka Alhajina kaga ma na ji sauki ni"

Cike da jin dad'i Daddy yace,    "Wallahi naji tsoro wannan canji na farad d'aya?".  

Bily tuno abunda ya faru ita da Ila taji haushin kanta har da su cin alwashin zata yi masa korar kare a gidan,  sai dai kuma gashi da marece sakaliya suna zaune ita da Daddy suna fira,  sai gashi ya shigo falon yana sanar da ita an aiko wani da sako ya kawo mata,  ta mike ta fito da nufin taga wane dan sako ne aiko tana fitowa yayi caraf da ita, Bily ta zabura ta buda baki da nufin tayi magana sai kuma tayi gum ta biyeshi suka yi dakinshi, nan ma ya samu abun banza yayi ta gurzarta ba kakkautawa,  bayan sun kammala ne ya riko hannunta yace

Ba kunya Ila yace 

"Dubu Dari zaki bani idan har zan samu"

Bily tazo zata fita dakin tace

Zata fita yayi wuf ya riko hannunta cikin tsantsar farin ciki yace,

 Tayi dan murmushi tace,      "kada ka damu bazan fadawa kowa ba".  

Tana fitowa dakinshi Zuby tana shigowa gidan,   da gudu Bily taje ta rungumeta cike da farin cikin ganinta,  Zuby tayi saurin dad'e hancinta tana kokarin yin amai, cikin dabara ta janye jikinta tana yi mata kallon mamaki tace,

"Me nake shirin gani ba dai da wancen bakin kazamin dan kauyen kika yi mu'amala ba? Cabdi lalle farashinki yayi kasa danger".  

Bily ta riko hannunta suna dariya suka yi cikin gidan,  ganin Daddy yana Bacci ne yasa suka yi sama zuby dai sai kallon mamaki take yi mata.

Bily ta zauna tana fadin   "wash Allah"

  Zuby ta yatsine fuska tace ,     "plss kije kiyi wanka mana sannan ki zo tunda har kin daukarwa kanki tarayya dashi to ki koya masa yanda ake tsaftar jiki sosai mana"

Bily ta mike ba tareda tace komi ba tayi toilet Zuby sai jinjina abun take a ranta ,   

 _wai ace kamar Bily danger tana kula wancen matsiyacin cabdi jam duniya tayi lafiya._

Bayan ta fito ne ta canza kaya ta dauki gyalenta da jaka tana kallon Zuby tace

"Zo muje ki rakani"

Zuby ta kalleta tace

"Ina zaki je ne yanzu? nifa Alhajinki na zo dubawa".    

Bily ta dauko key tace


"Muje ki dubashi sai mu wuce"

Suka fito suka taradda har lokacin Daddy baccinshi yake yi suka fice abunsu, kai tsaye banki Bily ta wuce ta Ciro dubu Dari a account din Daddy sannan ta cirowa Zuby ten thousand ta ajiyeta gida sannan ta dawo, cike da murna Ila ya nufi motarta tun kafin tayi parking,  ta fito cikin ko in kula ta mika masa kudin tayi cikin gida abunta,  ya bita yana ta yi mata kirari yana godiya bakinshi yaki rufuwa saboda wannan almara, wai shine da kudi irin haka? Lalle dole ma ne ya je ya kara biyan wasu kudin a kara mallake masa ita har ma da Daddyn kanshi.

Basarake kam ko minti goma bai yi  da barin gidan ba Munawwa ta fito, cikin shigarta ta kullum sanye da dogon hijab har kasa da Nikaf da socks hannu da kafa, a hankali take taku zuwa bakin gate ma'aikata sai gaidata suke yi, sai dai ta daga masu hannu har ta kai bakin gate din,  direba ya karaso da gudu da mota da nufin ya dauketa amman ko kallonshi bata yi ba ta fice, bai daddara ba ya biyota a baya da motar har ya fito wajen gidan yayi parking, sannan ya fito ya nufota cikin rawar jiki yace

"Ki yi hakuri hajiya ki zo muje in kai ki"

Ta kalleshi da kwayoyin idanuwanta da suka fito cikin Nikaf tace

"Je ka abunka kawai akwai motar da nike jira......".   Aikuwa kafin ta kai karshen maganarta sai ga wata mota ta Parker ta shige abunta, tana shiga ta dage labulen tana feshe mai motar da wani shuumin murmushi suka bar kofar gidan aguje.

Direban ya saki baki da hanci yana ganin ikon Allah a ranshi yace,      _"Ashe daman ita matar maigida haka halinta yake? Abun mamaki waton don taga baya garin shiyasa zata fara cin amanarshi, Lalle taci amanar Maigidanmu da ta lullubeshi da kykkyawan halaye a bayyane Ashe kura CE da fatar rago, lalle akwai daru a gaba duk ranar da ya ganoki don dai nikam ba za'a ji mutuwar sarki a bakina ba."_    

 Sannan ya ja motar ya koma ciki da ita zuciyarshi cike da mamakin abunda idanuwanshi suka ganar masa, a gefe d'aya kuma zuciyarshi cike da tausayin Ubangidanshi saboda yarda da yayi da ita 100%.

Munara kam suna tafe suna firarsu suna dariya da sabon abokin aikin da Dr ya hadata dashi, sai ga kira ya shigo wayarta tana dubawa taga sunan mai kiran da har ta mance tana da  numbar a wayarta,  taso karta dauka wata  zuciya tace da ita 

_dauki kiji da me yazo?_

Aiko ta bata fuska sosai sannan ta daga tareda saka hand's free  sai ga muryar Nuradden ta fito radam a cikin wayar yace

"Munara ko?" 

Ta yatsina fuska race

"In ka yarda da ita din kake magana ba" 

Nuradden ranshi ya fara baci da renin hankalinta amman ya dake yace,    "Gargadi na ke so in yi maki akan ki fita rayuwar Aliyu da matarshi ki barsu su zauna lafiya su ji dadin aurensu"

A cikin fushi Munara tace,    "idan ba haka ba fa?".  

Cikin sauri Nuradden yace

"Zaki ga nima irin matakin da zan dauka akanki"

Munara ta kyalkyale da dariya tace

"Woww sannu fa abokin gaske to ina maka albishir kafin ni ka dauki mataki kaina ni CE zan fara dauka akanka, saurin me kake yi ne? Daman cen ai ban manta da kai ba abokin Amana, INA jiran in kare da su ne sannan in dawo kanka karamin kwaro, amman tunda har kana bukatar ayi komi cikin sauri to ka jirani zan zo kaji?"

Nuradden yayi saurin fadin     "ni bana tsoron duk abunda zaki yiman amman INA son ki kyale Aliyu da matarshi ki tausayawa yar uwarki ko don abunda yake cikinta"

Munara ta kyalkyale da wata arniyar dariya tace,    

"Ya sheikh INA godiya fa sai anijma".   

Kitttt ta kashe wayar tana dariya zuciyarta cike da tunanin irin abunda ya dace tayi masa,   wani kiran ya sake shigowa taki dauka yayi ta kira amman tayi banza dashi sai cen taji shigowar text tayi saurin dauka ta duba ba shiri ta dafe kirji tareda zaro ido a waje saboda abunda taga ya rubuto mata kamar haka,

_Nasan kina cikin garin Abujarnan saboda haka kiyi gaggawar Barin garinnan ko insa a binciko Mani ke duk Indi kike a fadin garinnan._

Aiko ba shiri ta kira Dr tana sanar dashi yace ta kwantar da hankalinta ta karaso suyi shawarar abinda ya dace suyi.

Cikin tsoro da zullumi ta isa asibitinsu Dr kai tsaye ta nufi office dinshi,  cikin rawar Jiki ta zauna suka fara tattauna abunda ya dace suyi, suna cikin maganar ne sai gashi an kwankwasa kofar Munara ta gyara zamanta as zuwan ita patient CE ita, ya bada Umurni aka shigo ganin mai shigowar yasa yayi saurin mikewa ya mika masa hannu, Mutumin yana kai kallonshi ga Munara yace

"Wa nake gani kamar wifyn mutumin?"

Cikin faduwar gaba Dr yace,   "wa kenan?"

Mutumin yana karewa Munara kallo yace

"Aliyu mana ko ba matarsa bace da muka je Kd dauro aurenta?"

Dr ya fadada fara'arshi duk da bugawar da  kirjinshi yake yi yace

"Eh itace kasan ta samu juna biyu ne shiyasa tazo ganin likita" 

Mutumin yayi yar dariya yace

"Ashe yau sai anyi ruwa tunda har mutumina ya bar maka amanar matarshi, Namijin kishi kenan to ni zan wuce Madam ina Angon?"  Ya kare maganar yana yar dariya

Munara ta fara sunne kai tace    "Yayi tafiya". Yace   " Ok ki gaida man dashi kafin muyi waya Allah ya baki lafiya".  

Sannan ya kara mikawa Dr hannu suka yi musabuha sannan ya fice, sai dai zuciyarshi bata yi masa dadin ganin Matar Aliyu a office din  Dr Hafis ba,   amman ya kudurta zai kirashi ya ja masa kunne a fakaice akan ya hana sake faruwar hakan saboda gudun abunda zai je ya dawo a gaba.

Bai yi kasa a Gwiwa ba yana fita yayi ta kiran Basaraken bai samu ba,  dole ya hakura sai zuwa dare cikin natsuwa suyi maganar.

Munawwa kam tana kwance cikin dakinta inda ya barta ta kasa tashi daga wajen sai sharar hawayen bakin ciki take yi, zuciyarta cike da tunanin maganganun Aliyunta ,ta dauki lokaci tana ta juya abunda ya faru tsakaninsu tun jiya zuwa yau din,  tasa aranta in banda kashedin da yayi mata da ba abunda zai hanata zuwa Kaduna ko don taga Mummynta taji sanyi, saboda halin da take ciki tana bukatar ta rabu jikinta tayi kuka koda bazata fada mata abunda ke damunta ba, tasan hakan  zai rage mata kaso mafi rinjaye a cikin damuwartata, amman dole ta bi umurninshi ko tana so ko bata so saboda gudun sabawa Allah akan kin bin umurnin da Mijinta ya gindaya mata, duk ko da girman laifukan da take ganin yayi mata ita har lokacin bata ji wani sauyi ga zuciyarta ba akan son Aliyunta.

 Tana cikin tunane tunanen nata  ne  Ummi da  Kakale suka zo gidan duba jikin nata.

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*



*75*


Ladi ta shigo har d'akinta jikinta yana rawa tace,    "Aunty ga su Hajiya nan sun zo duba jikinki suna falon k'asa"

Munawwa ba tare da ta tashi daga kwancen ba tace,    "ki shigo da su falon sama gani nan zuwa.".  Ladi ta fita jikinta yana rawa ta shigo da su, Munawwa ta yunkura taje ta wanko fuskarta ta fito falon cikin sanyin jiki, Ummi da Kakale suka bi ta da kallo cikin tsantsar tausayinta, Munawwa taje ta zauna kasa ta fara gaidasu kanta a sunne don bata yarda ta bari sun hada ido ba, amman a take sai da suka gano tashin hankalin da take ciki uwa uba ramar da ta kara yi,   Ummi taje ta tada ita daga zaunen ta zaunar da ita akan kujera cikin damuwa tace,      "kiyi hakuri kinji kowa da tashi kalar kaddara ki dauki hakan a matsayin jarabawarki, sannan mu kara godewa Allah da har yasa abun cikin ya tsaya, Abu daya ya rage muna waton rokon Allah akan ya rabaki dashi lafiya sannu kinji? ALLAH UBANGIJI ya baki Lafiya".    Munawwa tana share hawaye tace,   "Ameen" cen cikin makoshi  Kakale tayi yar dariyar karfin hali tace,     "yau ba za ki ci alalen bane?".  

Munawwa ta kara sunne kanta a jikin Ummi tana yar dariya,  Ummi tayi murmushi tace,      "yau ni da kaina zan baiwa 'yata alalen ta ci a gabana inga yanda take guma girman lomarta."

Munawwa ta rufe fuskarta tana dariya Ummi ta mike da kanta ta jawo ledar da ta zo da ita,  sai ga Ladi ta shigo Ummi tace da ita ta dauko mata plate da spoon,  Jikinta yana rawa ta kawo mata har da su durk'usuwa a k'ok'arinta na son ta nuna kirkinta garesu, Ummi ta karb'i plate d'in ta zubawa Munawwa alalen da tasha manja da yaji mai d'an Karen dadi wanda yaji kayan k'amshi wad'anda suka boye kaifin yajin, ta ajiye a gabanta tace

"Saki jiki ki ci kinji 'yata don nasan yau ko abincin da wuya in kin ci".   Munawwa cikin jin nauyinsu sosai ta dauki spoon ta fara cin alalen a hankali tana sauke ajiyar zuciya,  Kakale ta kalli Ummi tace    

     "Ki saki jikinki ki ci da kyau don ni ina ganin ma dake zamu je gida ki zauna a cen har sai Mijinki ya dawo".  

Munawwa taji kirjinta ya buga saboda tuno kashedin da yayi mata amman ta daure tace,

"Ai ma naji sauki Kakale".   Ummi tayi dan murmushi tace

"Zancen hajiya gaskiya ne da ke zamu je don ba zai yiyu muyita zirga zirgar zuwa gidannan dubaki kullum ba, gara muje da ke cen har ya dawo don zaki fi samun kula fiye da nan d'in tunda baya nan, don haka ba zamu barki kiyita zama ke kad'ai cikin daki ba ciyo yana damunki.".    Munawwa tayi jarumtar furta cewa,   

  "Ummi yace dani kar in fita koina kafin yayi tafiya yanzu idan naje zai ga kamar nice na tsara hakan".    Kakale ta tabe baki tace
  "kira man Aliyun ni in fada masa da bakina don ba zamu barki anan ba kamar yanda shi ya tsallakeki ya tafiyarsa ba tare da yayi duba da lalurar da kike ciki ba".  

Munawwa ta dauko wayarta aka yi ta kiranshi baa samu ba, Ummi ma ta kirashi da wayarta nan ma baa sameshi ba,  Kakale tace da Munawwa idan ta kare ta je tayi wanka ta hado yan kayanta su tafi, duk lokacin da aka sameshi ita da kanta zata sanar dashi.

Cikin sanyin jiki Munawwa ta kammala cin abincin ta je tayi wanka zuciyarta cike da zullumin wannan tafiya da su Ummi suka tirsasata akan sai tayi, saboda har ga Allah bata son kin bin Umurnin Mijinta haka ma bata so su suga kamar ta renasu,  ta dan hada yan kayan da zata bukata tayi zaune a kan gadonta tana kukan rashin mafita,  Ummi jin shirun nata yayi yawa ne ta biyota ta shigo dakin da  sallamarta ganin halin da Munawwa take ciki yasa ta yi saurin karasowa ta zauna a gefen gadon tace,    "ALLAH yasa wannan kukan ba na rashin son biyarmu bane?"

Munawwa ta share hawayenta cikin muryar da tasha kuka ta koshi tace,    "Ummi yace dani kar in je koina har sai ya dawo ,hasali ma ko harabar gidannan cewa yayi bai yarda in fita ba har ya dawo garinnan".  Ummi tayi shiru tana girmama wannan girman biyayya da Munawwa tare yiwa Aliyu duk da taji haushin wannan dokar banza da ya shinfida mata, amman ta dake tace da ita 

"To bari in sanar da Hajiya sai mu kira har mu samesa a wayar sannan mu tafi don ba zamu tirsasaki ki ki bin dokarshi ba".  Ta fito dakin taje tace da Kakale ,     "Hajiya fa yarinyarnan bata son biyarmu sabida Aliyu yace da ita ko harabar gidannan bai amince ta taka ba har sai ya dawo".      Cikin fushi Kakale tace

"Zancen banza da na wofi kenan shikenan yanzu idan aka CE wani ciyo ya kamata bazaa je a kaita asibiti ba har sai an kira ya dawo tunda ana tsoronsa ko? To wallahi ba zamu bar yar mutane anan ba tunda baya nan, ya zama dole mu kula da lafiyar yar mutane saboda amanace a hannunmu,  je ki fito da ita ta zo mu tafi ni ba zaayi wannan sakarci dani ba."

Cikin ladabi Ummi ta fara baiwa Hajiya Kakale hakuri don ranta ya baci sosai da halin Aliyun na kishi don tasan duk baking kishinshi ne yasa ya aza mata wannan dokar banza.

Ummi ta koma tana baiwa Munawwa hakuri akan ta zo su je ran Hajiya ya bace,  Munawwa ta fashe da sabon kuka ganin haka ne yasa Ummi ta sake gwada kiran nashi amman bata samu ba, Hajiya Kakale ko tana falo sai zuba ruwan fada take yi inda ta shiga ba tanan take fita ba,  Ummi ta jawo mata jakar suka fito ta rufo dakin suka sauka kasa Ladi biye da su tana yi masu adduar ALLAH ya tsare hanya  Kakale ta kalli Ladi tace,    "kema je ki dauko kayanki muje ko da zaki taimaka mata da wani abun". 

Cike da tsantsar farin ciki Ladi taje ta dauko yan kayanta suka shige mota direba ya jasu sai katafaren gidan ALHAJI DIKKO MAI GWAL.

***

Munara kam mutumin yana fita ta kalli Dr cikin rudewa tace,  "anya ko ba mu tafka kuskure ba?".   Dr yayi yar dariya yana jujjuyawa akan kujerarshi yace   " kuskuren mi?"

Munara tace,   "Da wannan guy din ya ganmu baka ganin hakan zai kawo muna matsala a tafiyar tamu?".  Dr yana karkada Biro a bakinshi yace,   "Matsala daya CE da har ya ganni dake amman in ta bangarenki ne wa zai gano ba Munawwara bace ke?, sannan koda ma ya sanar da Aliyun ni zanyi kokarin zamewa in CE ita ta zo da kanta wurina ai tunda ba da karfi na jawota ba,kin ko ga laifinta a wurinshi zai fi nawa yawa hasali ma idan har yaji hakan zai kara jefa zarginta a ranshi, kinga mun doki tsuntsu biyu da dutse daya yanzu abunda ya rage muna daya ne , ya zama dole muyi abunda zai raba Aliyun da Nuradden idan ba haka ba kwabarmu tana GAF da yin ruwa".   Munara tayi shiru tana nazarin maganganun Dr sannan tayi guntun murmushi tace,   "Nice your welcome idea to yanzu ta ina zamu fara ne?".   Dr yace matso da kunnuwanki ki ji,  Munara ta maka masa harara tace,   "ba wani wayo fada kawai ina ji"    Dr ya mike ya je ya sanyawa kofar key ya dawo da munafukin murmushinshi ya rungumota a jikinshi yace,   "dole ne in rage zafin wahalata da kike bani" duk da tirjiyarta bai hanashi tsotse bakinta ba,  sannan ya saketa yana dariya yace,   "na rage zafi kafin time dina nima ya zo inyi abunda Nike so, don na gaji da hadiyar yawun da nike yi mu kwana gida daya mu tashi tare amman ko dan yatsanki ban isa in taba ba, ni wallahi na matsu sosai a kare wannan game din ayi komi a gama in mori kurciyata". Munara ta maka masa harara tace,   "ni dai muyi abunda zai fishemu ba wannan shirme ba......". Maganarta ta katse ne sabida kiran Ladi da ya shigo wayarta cikin sauri ta daga ladi tace,   "Aunty kar ki dawo gidannan saboda Hajiyar Aliyu ta zo da kakarshi".   Munara ta zaro ido tace,   "suna ina yanzu?". Tace "suna sama wajen ganin Auntyn" 

Munara tace,  "Ok" ta kashe wayar Dr ya kalleta yace,   "Ladin CE halan?" Tace,   "Eh ai yarinyar tasan aikinta".

Sun dauki lokaci suna  firar Ladin sannan tayi masa sallama ta fito Wanda ya daukota ya jata suka tafi,  zuciyarta cike da jin dadi musamman da ta tuno yanda direban Aliyu ya rude da abunda yaga tayi, don ta tabbata ta jefa masa shakkun Munawwa a ranshi kafin ta zo daukar fansarta akan Nuradden, tana tsaka da tunaninta ne text din ladi ya shigo wayarta tana sanar da ita abunda yake faruwa, cikin jin dadi tace   "good Ladi"  sai dai kuma tuno Munawwa bata gidan yasa taji haushin rashin cika ayukanta ,  amman dai tace ba komi ko a hakan ma ai kudi zasu biya kudin sabulu idan har ta koma kan Nuradden tasan komi zai zo karshe gayyar ta watse.

*****

Basarake kam bai samu kanshi ba har sai dare bayan ya samu nutsuwa ne ya kira Munawwa da ke dakin Kakale kwance tana nazarin abubuwan da suka faru,   cikin jin dadin ganin kiranshi tayi zumbur ta tashi zaune saboda doki sai dai tuno abunda ke tsakaninsu yasa taki daukar wayar har Kakale ta fahimta tace da ita,  "ba kiranki bane ake yi?". Munawwa tace

"Eh shine ya kira"

Cikin mamaki Kakale tace,  "to ki daga kiji mana".   Munawwa tayi rau rau da ido saboda zuwan wasu sababbin hawaye tace

"Bansan me zan fada masa ba Kakale". 

Kakale ta harareta tace,   "miko man wayar ni nasan me zan fada".   Ta mika mata wayar tayi saurin tashi ta shigewarta kuryar dakin kakale zuciyarta cike da fargaba,  Kakale ta bita da kallo tace

"Irinku ne maza suke renawa saboda sunga tsoronsu da kuke sai su dauka cewa komi suka yi dai dai ne". 

Wajen fadan har kiran ya tsinke wani ya kara shigowa,  sannan ta dauko jin muryarta yasa shi faduwar gaba yace,   "ina ita Munawwarar?". Kakale ta harareshi kamar tana ganinshi tace,  "ka damu da ita ne bale kasan a wane hali take?". Basaraken ya bata fuska yace,

"Plss Kakalena don Allah ina take? Kuma ya jikinnata?". 

Kakale kai tsaye tace

"Idan ka damu da kasan inda take da halin da take ciki to ka dawo ka kula da matarka, don banga amfanin wannan tafiya taka ba ka tsallake matarka da ciyo ka barta ita kadai cikin gida, to na dawo da ita wurina don bazamu taru mu yasar da yar mutane a banza ba."

Gabanshi ya kire ya fadi yace,   "Yanzu duk da nace kar ta je koina sai da ta fito kenan?"

   Cikin fada Kakale tace ,  "ta fito nace idan Baka son ganinta a wani wuri kazo ka dauki abarka Ku tafi mu bamu da damuwa ,damuwarmu daya CE abarta ita kadai ba wani babba a tare da ita alhali kuma kasan bata da isashiyar lafiya, da kasan bazaka iya ba ai sai ka barta cen asibitin aci gaba da kula da ita,  amman ka daukota ka jibge a gida ka tafiyarka to wa kake so ya kula da ita ne idan ba mu ba? Mu kuma kasan duk va zamu dinga reton zuwa gidanka a kullum ba".   

Ganin ranta ya baci yasa ya dinga bata hakuri sannan ya kashe wayar cikin kunar rai,  sai zancen zucinshi yake yi musamman da ya tuno kalaman da ta fada, duk da jikinshi ya bashi Munawynshi bazata iya aikata wani abun tirr ba amman dai dole ya tauna tsakuwa don aya taji tsoro.

Yana cikin tunanin zucin ne kiran Abokinshi KB ya shigo wayarshi, bayan sun gaisa ne yake cewa dashi,    "shine ba zaka fada Mani mun kusa zama Baba ba? Ai naga Madam dinka yau a asibiti office din Dr Hafis taje ya dubata".  

Cikin wata muguwar faduwar gaba Basaraken yace,    "ita matar tawa?" KB yace 

"Ita fa ai nayi mamaki da har ka barta taje wurinshi ita kadai, amman dai yana da kyau ka kiyaye sake faruwar hakan, don kasan mutumin namu ba ya wasa da damarshi."

Kit ya kashe wayar ba tare da ya jira Karin wani baasi bada bakin shi ba, don duk abunda zai fada kara bata masa rai ne kawai zai yi a banza,  jikinshi yana rawa ya kara kiran Munawwa cikin doki ta dauka saboda tsammaninta ko ya sauko ne shiyasa ya kirata yaji ya take, don taji duk maganganun da suka yi da Kakale duk da aranta bata ji dadin abunda tayi masa ba, shiyasa tayi hanzarin dauka domin ta bashi hakuri akan dokarshi da ta saba sai gashi taji sabanin tunaninta wanda ya sata birkicewar tunani, don kai tsaye yace da ita

"Wa ya baki izinin zuwa Asibiti office din Dr Hafis??????"

Ta zaro ido waje tace 

"Yaushe na je?"

Cikin tsananin fushi ganin zata Rena masa wayo yace,   "Kada ki kuskura ki kawo man renin hankali kinji? Au daman matakin da kika CE zaki dauka shine kika fara ko? To kisani Wallahi bazan lamunci abunda kike so ki zo man dashi ba, kin hadani da iyayena kuma abun bai maki ba har sai kinje kin zubar man da mutunci to kije ke da ALLAH don wallahi duk takun da kika yi daga nan gidansu Ummi zuwa wani wajen daban wuta bal bal kike takawa, kuma ban yafe hakikina ba mutukar kika sake zuwa wani wurin ba tare da izinina ba"

Yana kare maganar ya jefar da wayar, Munawwa kam wani jibine yake ta tsatsafo  mata a jiki, cikin tsananin tashin hankali ta dafe cikinta da ya murda mata, tare da toshe bakinta tana wani irin kuka mai raunata zuciyar mai sauraro, jikin Kakale yana bari ta shigo dakin saboda kukanta da ta jiyo, cikin tashin hankali ta fara tambayarta lafiya? jikin ne ya sake tashi ko kuwa wani abinne ya faru? Munawwa dai kasa magana tayi saboda tsananin kukan da take yi, cikin rudewa Kakale tace 

"Aliyun ne ko?". 

Munawwa tayi kokarin sassauta kukanta tace

"Mummyta nike son gani don Allah a kaini gida in ganota"

Cikin damuwa Kakale ta fita sai gata ta dawo tare da Ummi itama ganin halin da Munawwa take ciki ne yasa taji damuwa ta lullubeta, jikinta yana rawa ta janyota jikinta ta dora kanta saman cinyarta tana ta lallashinta,  kakale tace da Ummi " kashe wayarnan miko man ita".  Ummi tayi kamar yanda ta bukata Kakale taje ta boye wayar sannan tace,  

"Kiyi hakuri ki barni dashi zai zo ne ya sameni""



Princess Dija✍🏻

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*


*76*


Munawwa dai Kam kwata-kwata ta rasa natsuwar zucuyarta saboda matsaloli kala kala da take fuskanta a kwana biyunnan daga wannan sai wannan, tayi shiru tana nazarin maganganunshi tana aunasu a ma'aunin hankali, tun daga shak'ar da yayi mata a asibiti har ma da maganganunshi da ta rasa fahimtar inda suka dosa dalilin da ya kawo furtasu, musamman renin hankalin da yayi mata na cewa wai baisan ita ba ce, to idan ba ita bace wacece yake nufi? Don dai ai ba tare take da Munara ba balle yace ya kasa tantancesu, haka ma yanzu maganar da yayi na zuwa Office din Dr shima me yake nufi?.

Ta sauke k'atuwar ajiyar zuciya aranta wani tunani ya fad'o mata ba shiri a zabure ta Mike zaune tareda dafe saitin zuciyarta tace, 

_"Allah yasa dai Aliyu ba zargina yake yi ba shiyasa ya aza man dokar bai yarda in fita ba, cabd'ijam idan ko har haka dinne da gaske wallahi yaci amanar soyayyar da nake mashi, yanzu Ni duk irin rufa rufar da yayi Mani ya cutar da rayuwata bai gani ba sai Ni zai bi da wannan bak'in kullin sharrin?? Tafd'i lalle Namiji Dan k'anen ajali ne don dai nikam shine zai yi ajalin nawa ba ma cutar k'anjamau d'in da ya lik'a man ba"_. 

Tana k'are zancen zucinta ta fashe da wani marayan kuka, Kakale da ke k'ok'arin shigowa tayi saurin k'arasowa ranta a b'ace tace da ita,

"Ki daina d'agawa kanki hankali akan shirmenshi, ki natsu da kyau ki kula da lafiyarki da ta abunda ke cikin cikinki, ki barni dashi ai zai zo ne ya sameni zaki ga yanda zamu yi dashi, yanzu ki shirya ga wata likita nan zata zo ta dubaki ki kwantar da hankalinki komi yayi farko to da sannu za'a ga k'arshenshi idan an fauwalawa Allah komo, kuma insha Allahu idan ya dawo Ni da kaina zansa ya kaiki kije Kaduna ki gano iyayenki kinji?" 

Cikin jin dadi Munawwa tace, "Nagode Kakale".   Kakale tayi Yar dariya saboda kawai ta kwantar mata da hankali tace,.   "Daina godiya Ni biyana zaki yi tunda ke bakisan ta wace Hanya zaki rage masa wannan kishin haukar da yake yi ba"

Munawwa ta rufe idonta tace,. "Kishi fa gaskiya ne Kakale Ni banga laifinshi ba akan hakan."   Kakale ta rik'e hab'a tace,.  "Iyye um lalle kice to Ni in cire hannuna kenan?"    Munawwa tana Yar dariyar karfin hali tace,.  "Kar ki cire don Allah amman bana son kiga laifinshi"   Kakale zata yi magana kenan sai ga Ummi ta shigo da plate d'in da tayo mata d'anmalele yasha manja da tosshi da su kayan had'inshi, tana zuwa ta ajiye mata a gabanta cikin jin dad'in  ganin Munawwa ta saki jikinta har tana  dariya.

Munawwa Kam ganin Ummi ta rufe bakinta ruf duk tsokanar da Kakale ke yi mata bata mayar da amsa ba sai ma dariyarta da take a hankali, Ummi tace da ita ta ci abunta ta canye idan bata k'oshi ba sai a k'aro mata wani, Munawwa cike da jin dad'in kulawar da suke bata tayi wa Ummi godiya sosai. 

Ummi tana murmushin jin dad'in dace da sirukar kirki tace,    "Bari in tafi tunda naga kinfi sakewa da Hajiya kar in hanaki cin abincinki".     Kakale tayi mere tace.  "Da ma kin dawo don ni ban iya jinyar kishiya ba tunda ganin take yi kamar ta fini son Mijin".    Munawwa ta zaro ido tana rufe baki tace ,  "Rufani ki saya Kakalenmu".    Ummi ta fice tana dariya tace

"Ai Hajiya ba'a shiga tsakanin halshe da hak'ori"

Haka Kakale ta tasa Munawwa a gaba da fira har sai da ta canye d'anmalelenta, suna ta barkwancinsu har zuwa lokacin da likitar ta zo, cikin kwarewa ta dubata da kyau sannan ta d'orata akan wasu magungunan tare da shawarwarin da zata bi wajen kare lafiyarta da abunda ke cikinta.


******

Ila Kam tunda yaga wad'annan manyan kud'in a hannunshi ya bazama wurin bokanshi ya K'ara masa wasu kud'in aka shiga aiki gadan-gadan, sannan ya nufi kasuwa ya siyo tufafi da duk wani abunda yake buk'ata, cikin kwana biyu Ila ya Fara canzawa domin kuwa shigarshi yake yi mai kyau ga wanka yanayi ga abinci mai rai da lafiya da Bily take dafo masa ta cika kula ta kawo masa, don har da D'an k'aramin gado ta saka masa a d'aki don su ji dad'in holewa abunsu.

Daddy Kam cikin sati d'aya gaba d'aya ya rasa tantance ainahin a wace duniyar yake, domin kuwa duk abinda Bily tace shi ake yi a gidan saboda itama kanta umurnin Ila take bi, kudi ko nawa ne yace yana so da ta fad'awa Daddy zai ce a bashi, da kanta zata je ta ciro a account din Daddy ta bashi ya karb'e har ma ya bata tukuicin yagalgalata jikinta ya gurjeta son ranshi, don ko Zuby yace da Bily ta hanata zuwa gidan don gudun tonon asirinsa, sannan yaji dadin sharholiyarshi ba takura don tsakani da Allah Bily tana ciyuwa hannunshi.

Kwatsam sai gashi tsautsayi ya rutsa da Zuby tana shigowa Gate din gidan ta hango takalman Bily kofar d'akinshi, jikinta ya d'auki rawa saboda tsoron wani ya zo ya kamasu asirin kawarta ya tonu, ta d'auki talkamin za ta shiga gidan da nufin taje ciki ta jirata har su k'are tanbad'ewar, sai kuma wata zuciyar tace da ita taje ta D'an lek'a ta ga me suke, aiko ta dawo baya ta d'an lek'a kanta ta windown  d'akin aiko sai gashi tayi tozali da Ila kwance da Bily sai aiki yake mata yana Sambatun dadi, ta zo zata janye jikin windown da sauri don kar ya ganta, aiko sai d'ayan takalmin Bily dake hannunta ya fad'a cikin d'akin, a razane Ila ya Kai kallonshi ga windown ganin Zuby ce yasa ya d'an ji hankalinshi ya kwanta amman duk da haka nan ya Mik'e tsam ya saka kayanshi ya fito, Zuby Kam tana k'ok'arin shigewa cikin gidan ya Kira sunanta ba shiri ta waigo zuciyarta cike da tsanarshi saboda ganin yanda ya mayar da Big girl irin Bily k'aramar mace ya sauko mata da farashi k'asa yayi warwas, cikin gadara ta dawo wajenshi tana yi masa wani mugun kallo tace

"To Dan akuya lafiya kake kirana? Koko duk  cikin borin kunyar ne?"

Ila yana dariyar mugunta yace     "Ai Ni  har abada bana nadama akan abunda na aikata bale har inyi maki borin kunya musamman irin wannan romon dad'i da Nike sha ga kud'i ina hawa kuma dole yarinya ta d'aga Mani skirt d'inta in shiga inyi abunda nike so son Raina Allah ya kashe ya bani kema ai da sannu zaki zo hannuna k'aramar mara kunya"

Zuby ta zaro ido tare da nunashi tace, "duk sai in rasa wazan lalace aka shi sai Kai? Cab wallahi kama Rena man wayo, to bari kaji ko ita din da kaga kayi nasara a kanta don saboda yanzu bata samun yanda take Samu ada shiyasa, sabida auren wannan tsohon najadun Ubangidan naka ne, amman nikam kullum sharr nake ba abunda zai sakani tanbad'ewa in lalace a kanka"      Ila yayi dariya yace    "haka kike gani?"   cikin tabbatarwa tace    "haka ma din ne ai"  

Ila yana Yar gajeruwar  dariyar shakiyanci yace 

"Jirani in zo yanzunnan kinji k'aramar karuwa"       Zuby ta maka masa wata jar harara tace 

"Ina jira k'aramin Dan iska Dan akuya sai naga abunda zaka iya yiman"   ta kare maganar cikin wata dariya tana biyarshi da wani kallon reni don ita rashin kunyarshi da karfin halinshi dariya ma yake bata.

Aiko tana tsaye tana girgizar k'afafun jiran ko ta kwana sai ko gashi ya fito d'akin nashi hannuwanshi duka biyu cikin aljihunshi yana k'arasowa wurinta ya mamayeta ya tura mata wata laya  a Baki ya rufe da karfin gaske tana mutsu mutsun kwatar Kai sai da ta d'auki tsawon minti biyu sannan ya saketa ya jawo hannunta ta biyoshi kamar wata wawuya har zuwa d'akinshi inda Bily take kwance har lokacin don bai bata damar tashi ba, ganin Zuby yasa tayi saurin tashi zaune saboda tsoron ace asirinsu ne ya tonu,  sai dai ganin yanda ko uf bata ce ba har ya Ila rabata da kayan jikinta tass, sannan ya Fara sarrafata son ranshi cikin k'warewa, Malam Ila Kam da ma abun yayi masa dad'i hadasu yayi waje d'aya idan yayi da Bily ya dawo kan Zuby, sai ihunshi yake yi shi kadai yana gurnani kamar wani k'aton tsohon kare, saboda yasan daga shi sai su ne kawai a gidan Daddy ya fita kuma baya dawowa sai dare, saboda yasa anyi masa tsafin da baya jin dad'in zaman gidan, ya fi son ya fita yayita shawaginshi kan tituna ba tare da yayi tunanin zuwa wani waje ko daya ba, don ka'ida ne kullum sai yayi full tanck saboda zirga zirgar da yake yi ta rashin dalili balle sanin mafita, shiyasa idan dare yayi ya kwanta yayi ta baccin wahala don ko abinci ma baya samun zama ya ci yanda ya kamata, ya bi ya rame a tsaye ga karayar da yayi har lokacin bata k'arasa warkewa ba,  ga damuwar tafiyar da Munara tayi wadda a Kullum cikin tunanin halin da take ciki yake yi aranshi amman kuma jinshi yake yi kamar a d'aure ya rasa me ya hanashi zuwa ya dubo 'yarshi kullum da tunanin bin bayanta yake amman ya kasa.


******


Munara Kam Kai tsaye gidansu Taly ta nufa inda babbar matattarar karuwai take, don tun a cikin mota ta cire hijabin da Nikaf din ta jefar seat din baya tare da sauke k'atuwar ajiyar zuciya, saboda ba k'aramin karfin hali take yi ba wajen zama da shigar ba, tana yi ne kawai don biyan bukatarta da zarar ta biya zata yi cilli da komai ta koma Munararta ta ainahi, tun a bakin kofar gidansu Talyn ta Fara cin karo da manya manyan motoci zunduma zunduma na manyan mutane kuma yan iska ajin farko, ganin haka yasa tayi tsaki ba shiri ta janyo hijabin ta saka tare da d'aura Nikaf d'in sai dai ta d'ageshi sama ta fito, aiko gaba d'aya idanuwa suka yo kanta cike da mamaki wani matashin saurayi ya bita da kallo Baki bud'e har ta shige cikin gidan, ya biyo bayanta zuciyarshi cike da kokwanton abunda idanuwansa suka hango masa, cikin sauri ya sha gabanta tayi saurin Kai kallonta gareshi tare da maka masa wata uwar harara ta zagayeshi zata wuce ya K'ara Shan gabanta yace,

"Tambaya Nike yi don Allah". 

Munara ta galla masa wata hararar ta rik'e k'ugu tace,.   "Bashin nawa na ci naka da zaka saka Ni  a gaba haka?"   Matashin saurayin yayi Yar dariya yace

"Kiyi hakuri hajjaju wallahi kama kika yiman da wata wadda na sani"

Munara ta yi masa kallon tuhuma sannan tace,.  "A'ina nayi kama da wadda ka sanin? Sannan kuma ita din wacece miye sunanta?"   Ya gyara tsayuwarshi yace

"Yar Kaduna ce sai dai kuma anan Abuja take aure tana auren Dan gidan Dikko mai Gwal"

Munara ta zaro ido waje tareda dafe kirji saboda bugawar da taji zuciyarta tayi, cikin sauri ta zagayeshi ta yi cikin gidan da sassarfa hankalinta a tashe tana wuce mata da mazan da ke tsakiyar filin gidan suna ta sharholiyarsu, Kai tsaye ta fada dakin Taly, yanayin da Talyn ta ganta yasa ta gimtse dariyar da take yi da wani saurayinta, tayi hanzarin rik'o hannunta suka fito waje cikin rud'ewa ta Fara tambayarta 

"Lafiya na ganki haka? Allah dai yasa ba asirinku ne ya tonu ba?"

Munara tana k'ok'arin yin magana sai ga saurayin ya nufosu fuskarshi cike da mamakin ganin Munara tare da Taly , Munara Kam tana hangoshi ya nufosu tace da Taly 

"Wai shi wancen wanene shi halan naga fa yana so ya bata muna shiri fa"   

Kafin Taly ta bata amsar maganarta har ya iso wurinsu cikin mamakin da ke kwance kan fuskarshi yace,

"Taly Baby wannan mai zafin fa daga Ina kika samota?, 

Taly tana Yar dariya ta d'aga masa gira tace,  

"Ko kana ciki ne?"

Yayi saurin fadin  "no naga dai tayi man kama da Amaryar da Mutuminki yayi watannin baya ko itama daman cen Yar hannu ce baisani ba?"

Taly tayi Yar gajeruwar dariya sannan ta kalli Munara tace

"Wai Mazaje ba dai bin kwakkwafi ba ya aka yi kayi mata irin wannan sanin haka?"

Wanda aka Kira Mazaje ya shafa gemunshi yana k'arewa Munara kallo sama da k'asa yace,.  "Ya bazan ganeta ba? yarinyar da muka je d'aurin aurenta har pics munyi da ita fa lokacin"   Munara ta zaro ido Taly tayi saurin dafa kafad'arta  tace,.   "To itace dai yanzu atakaice me kake so a wurinta? don nasan wannan idon naka da wuya in ba abokiyar rage dare kake nema ba"

Mazaje ya sosa Kai yace ,   "Cab ai ko Ina nema ba a wurinta ba matar aure fa ai sai zunubin yayi yawa, amman gaskiya Ni dai nayi mamakin da har Aliyu ya aureta, anya ko dai yasan ainahin wacece ke kuwa?"

Ya k:are maganar yana kallon Munara, Munara ta maka masa harara zata bar wurin Taly ta rik'o hannunta tace,.  "Ba fushi zakiyi ba tsayawa zaki yi ku fuskanci juna ki nemeshi da ya rufa maki asiri kada ya fad'awa Mijinki ya ganki anan, don kuwa shi wannan da kike gani akwai zumunci sosai tsakaninshi da Mahaifin Aliyu waton Dikko Mai gwal"

Taly ta k'are maganar tare da kashewa Munara ido aiko take ta d'au hannu ta marairece kamar tayi kuka tace da Mazaje

"Don Allah ka rufaman asiri kada ka bari yaji wallahi nasan zafin kishinshi tsaf zai iya rabuwa dani"

Mazaje yayi shiru yana nazari sannan yace

"To amman ke bakya son yasani me yasa kika kasa daina sana'ar taki ne bayan kunyi aure? Me kike nema a wajen Namiji Wanda Aliyu bai mallaka ba? Kud'i ko kyau ko mulki duka cikinsu Babu Wanda Allah bai azurtashi da su ba, bakya gudun ki b'atawa gidansu suna da wannan mugun halin naki?, Kuma wallahi Ina jin tsoron ranar da dubunki zata cika da irin matakin da zai dauka a kanki? don nasan Aliyu Sarai da kishin tsiyarshi matukar ya kama ki dumu dumu akan wannan harkar wallahi tsaf zai iya kasheki, don gara Taly ita kasheshi taso ta yi yafi ganin saukin hakan da dai ke irin balai'n da kike Shirin jefashi a ciki, shawara daya zan Baki yanzu ki kame kanki ki natsu ki rungumi Mijinki da zuciya daya ki tsare masa mutuncinshi da na gidansu da kike k'ok'arin zubarwa a idon duniya, don wallahi matukar Baki daina ba to Ni da kaina zan sanar dashi wacece ke don ya hankalta ya gano kalar matar da yake aure tun kafin ki goga masa ciyon Aids bai ji ba bai gani ba"

*🔥BAK'ON YANAYI🔥*



*77*



MUNARA ta fashe da kukan munafucci tana rokonshi da yayi hakuri ya rufa mata asiri zata daina, Mazaje Kam ya hau sosai saboda har ga Allah yaji zafin abun abun a ranshi, saboda takaicin abun ma tsaki kawai ya  buga mata ya bar wurin zuciyarshi tana Kuna saboda ganin renin hankalinta ya ma yi yawa aranshi yace, 

_"bakya so yasani ubanwa ya hanaki dainawa don wajibi ne in kere na yawo  zabo na yawo watarana zasu hadu kuma Yana da tabbacin  karonbattar tsakaninta Aliyun ba zai yi kyau ba"_

Bayan tafiyar Mazaje ne MUNARA da Taly suka kyalkyale da dariya, Taly tana dafe ciki tace

"Kai bariki iyawa ce Ashe dai kema kin iya hada taki kirsar, wai harda su Kuka lalle naki salon ba wasa a ciki"         MUNARA ta gyara tsayuwarta tace,

MUNARA ta yi warai da ido tare da jin wani sabon bacin Rai saboda tuno kalaman Nuradden Masu dauke da k'aton  gard'ad'in da yake yi mata akan ta fita rayuwarsu Aliyu kuma ta bar garin cikin gaggawa,.  Ta furzar da wani iska a bakinta tace

"Abokin Aliyu ne ya sakoni gaba Taly yace lalle in bar garinnan tun kafin yasa a binciko duk inda Nike,,sannan yayi man gargadi sosai akan in fita rayuwarsu Munawwa, kaina ya k'ulle da yawa Taly duk  da Ni shi dinma a shirye Nike da d'aukar fansana akanshi"

"Ki tada wannan k'aton banzan ya bamu wuri plss"  Taly tayi Yar dariya tace

"Barman Babyna yayi baccinshi don nasan Yana tashi bazai barni muyi firar ba don b'ukata ce ta kawoshi kuma kin katse masa hanzari"

MUNARA ta tabe Baki tayi wani mere ta maka masa harara tace

Cikin jin zafin maganarta Taly tace

MUNARA da tunda Taly ta ambaci tsirara ta zaro ido waje dafe da k'irji tana kallonta har ta Kai karshen maganarta,  sannan tana dariyar wautar Talyn tace "WALLAHI bazan iya ba canza man wata shawarar dai"

Ta kaiwa hannunta naushi tace

MUNARA har ta Kai bakin titi batasan ta Kai ba saboda tsananin tunanin da take, don Taly ba k'aramin aiki ta bata ba saboda tunda take bayan Dr Babu wani Wanda zata iya tuno cewa ya tab'a ganinta ba Kaya ma balle har tayi irin wannan ta'asar dashi, a ranta tace

_"Yanzu ace duk Babu wata hanya mafi sauk'i da za'a bi sai wannan? Gaskiya Ni dai Kam bazan iya ba"_


*****

Basarake Kam Yana Agadas ya duk'ufa sai d'irkawa kanshi maganin warakar matsalarshi yake yi, don tun kafin ya zo ya Kira ya fayyace komi ga wani abokinshi D'an Sarkin Agadas d'in Mai ci a wannan lokaci, Wanda ya kasance matsayin Kawu a wurinshi saboda Mahaifinshi yayan Innayo ne na Uwa d'aya Uba d'aya.

Akwai jituwa sosai a tsakaninsu kuma basa b'oyewa junansu komi shiyasa ya sanar dashi sai gashi ko Yana zuwa suka tafi wajen wani babban Malami masani akan harkar karya sihiri da aljanu.

Aikuwa sai gashi Yana ganin Basarake ya Fara girgiza kai cikin bacin Rai yace dashi

"Wannan bak'in Aljani da ke kanka me kayiwa Wanda ya turo maka shi?"

Basarake ya zaro ido a waje yace, "Aljani?"

Dattijon mutumin yace

"Tabbas Aljani don gashi Ina hango bak'in hayaki Yana tashi a saman kanka"

Basarake ya zabura zai Mik'e tsaye ne mutumin yace,.    "Zauna insha Allahu matsalarka ta kau tunda har kazo wurinmu, zamu taimaka maka da wasu magungunan ka Fara amfani da su kafin mu shiga binciken matsalar taka gadan-gadan"

Sannan Basarake hankalinshi ya D'an kwanta Amman zuciyarshi sai wasi wasi take tana tambayar kanshi to wacece tayi masa wannan muguntar? Aiko sai gashi zancen Nuradden ya fad'o masa arai inda yake cewa MUNARA ce sanadin wannan lalurar tashi, cikin tsananin b'acin Rai yaci alwashin muddin ita d'ince da gaske to WALLAHI sai ta yi da tasanin zuwanta duniya, tunaninshi ya katse ne sabida dafashin da Moussa yayi Saboda maganar da dattijon yake yi masa Amman hankalinshi baya wurinshi.

Cikin sanyin jiki Dattijon yace,.   "Ka daina d'aga hankalinka insha Allahu komi zai zo cikin sauk'i har a Samu waraka da yardar Allah" 

Basarake ya jinjina kanshi cikin tabbatarwa ya Fara yi masa godiya, ya d'auko magungunan ya Mik'a masa yace dashi yaje ya Fara Sha nan da kwana Uku zuwa sati d'aya ya dawo kafin lokacin ya k'are bincikenshi akan cikakkiyar matsalar tashi.

Cikin jin dad'i ya karb'o maganin suka fito Moussa Yana ta k'arfafa masa gwiwa akan insha Allahu matsalarshi zata kau har ma ta zamo labari,cikin jin dadin fatan alkhairinshi gareshi suka Isa gida ba ba tare da wani jinkiri ba ya Fara Shan maganinshi, cikin ikon Allah tun kafin kwana ukun su cika ya Fara jinshi wasai saboda ko ciyon kan da yake ta fama dashi a kullum yaji kan ya saki sosai, cikin tsantsar farin ciki Sati dayan yana cika ya koma wurin dattijon don ko rakiyar Moussa bai nema ba, aiko sai gashi yaji abunda zuciyarshi ta kasa hasko masa tun farko, don ko dattijon ya tabbatar masa da matar Babanshi ce tayi masa wannan asirin kuma Yana cikin gidanshi da an tone layar da aka yi k'ulle kullen balain da ita zai dawo dai dai har ma yafi da samun k'arfin mazakuta saboda magungunan da yasha zasuyi tasiri a jikinshi sosai, cikin tsantsar farin ciki ya tambayi dattijon ta ya za'a gano inda layar take a cikin gidan sai yasa a tone tun kafin ya koma?, Dattijon yayi dan Murmushi yace

"Wannan aikin kuma Kaine zaka yi da hannunka sabida haka idan ka koma sai ka Kirani zan kwatanta maka ta inda Layar take sai ka toneta kayi mata fitsari akai insha Allahu shikenan ka karya duk wani sharrin da ke cikinta, don ko wannan bak'in Aljanin da aka turo maka Shima kanshi da kayi wa layar fitsari zai dimauce ya haukace yabi duniya saboda haka kayi gaggawar komawa gidan domin kawo k'arshen matsalar"

Cikin tsananin jin dad'i Basarake yayi ta yi masa godiya ya Ciro manyan kud'in da bai ma San ko nawa bane ya ajiye masa cikin murna tsananin farin ciki yayi sallama dashi a k'agauce ya Mik'e zai fita Dattijon ya kirashi Yana Yar dariya yace dashi

"Kar ka mance idan zaka tab'a layar ka Fara yin Basmalah"

Basarake yace 

"Insha Allah bazan manta ba nagode sosai Baba Allah ya K'ara basira ya ja da Nisan kwana"

Dattijon yace "Ameen" da Murmushi akan fuskarshi suka rabu Basaraken kam zuciyarshi sai jinjina lamarin yake yi aranshi Yana dokin ya ya koma don kawo k'arshen rikicinshi da Munawynshi, cikin tsantsar farin ciki da jin dadi ya Isa masaukinshi.

Yana cikin had'a kayanshi ne sak'on text ya shigo wayarshi har yaso ya share Amman farin cikin da ya ke  ciki yasashi jin k'arfin gwiwar d'aukar wayar ya duba text din sai gashi da kaiwa zaune bashiri dafe da zuciyarshi sabida sak'on da ya gani kamar haka,

_"Ka shiga WhatsApp na turo maka video kaga yanda matarka da abokinka suke cin amanarka"_

Cikin sauri ya bud'a Data jikinshi Yana rawa sai ga text's suna shigowa d'aya bayan d'aya har idonsa suka Kai kan wani text na wata sabuwar Number, cikin rawar jiki hannunshi Yana kyarma ya bud'a text din sai ko ga fad'uwar gaba ya dirar masa saboda ganin pic's da videon da tun kafin ya bud'asu gabanshi ya fad'i zuciyarshi sai lugude take yi,  tsananin tashin hankalin da yake ciki ne yasa ya jefar da wayar ya kasa bud'a Koda pic d'aya ne, ya kai kwance ba shiri tare da runtse idonshi Yana tunanin Anya ko hakan zata iya faruwa kuwa? Sabida nasan wanene Nuradden kuma nasan wacece Munawwara duk cikinsu Babu wannan shirmen a zuciyarsu har ma da rayuwarsu duka sai dai in wani tuggun sharrin ne kuma ake so a had'a masu.

Yana cikin wannan tunanin ne Kiran Mazaje ya shigo wayarshi tayi ta ringing yaki dauka shi kuma bai fasa Kiran ba damun da yake yi masan ce tasashi daga wayar cikin fushi ba tare da ma ya duba wanene Mai Kiran ba ya Kara a kunnenshi, aiko sai ga muryar Mazaje tar tar tana fitowa cikin wayar zuwa kunnenshi Yana fad'in cewa,

"Aliyu ka natsu da kyau ka fahimci wacece macen da kake aure don bana son kaji mutuwar Sarki a bakina".   Kit!!! ya kashe wayar ba tare da ya jira yaji abunda shi Basaraken zai fad'a ba,  cikin wata sabuwar fad'uwar gaba ya ajiye wayar ya fad'a zuzxurfan tunani ya Fara had'a maganganun da munawwa tayi masa a Asibiti har ma da wad'anda tayi masa lokacin da zai bar gidan da maganar da Kb ya fad'a masa akan ya ganta Office din Dr, sannan ga wannan text da aka turo masa yanzu ga kuma zancen da Mazaje ya k'are yi masa yanzunan, ya runtse idonshi Yana hasko Munawynshi tana yi masa murmushinta Mai sakashi cikin nishadi da mayen sonta, haka kawai yaji Zuciyarshi ta kasa amincewa da duk abunda yake faruwa, sai dai kuma  Wata zuciyar tace dashi 

_"yanzu fa ba'a shedar mutum duk yanda ka ganshi barshi kawai Kai dai shedi kanka, Amman idan har kana kokwanto ka bud'a videon ka gani don ka gasgata komi"_

Yayi shiru Yana nazarin abun Sannan ya jawo wayar ya Fara  bud'a pic's din sai gashi da tashi zaune ba shiri, saboda ganin Munawwarshi da yayi kwance saman jikin Nuradden dukansu ba Kaya a jikinsu tana dariya, hannunshi Yana kyarma ya bud'a videon sai gashi da mikewa tsaye cikin tsananin firgici domin kuwa Munawwa ce rike da jarumar Nuradden tana tsotsa sai lasa take tana wata dariyar shakiyanci.

Cikin firgici Mai cike da tsananin tashin hankali ya buga wayar a jikin bango da k'arfi gaske sai gata da tarwatsewa d'add'aya a tsakiyar d'akin, cikin karaji yace 

"No No No WALLAHI bazan tab'a yafe maku ba dukanku sai na kasheku sai kun bar duniya yau yau d'innan"


Ko ta kan kayanshi da yake had'awa a jaka  bai bi ba haka ma bai jira yayi sallama da kowa ba Kai tsaye ya fito gidan ya Fara cuku cukun barin Kasar Koda k'arfe biyar na marece tayi duk yana cikin Nigeria,  kasancewar ba waya hannunshi ya nufi wani telephone office ya Ari waya ya Kira Direbanshi minti biyar a tsakani sai gashi ya zo ya karb'i key din ya shiga motar ganin yanayin da Ubangidan nashi yake ciki ne yasa Kalla shiga motar cikin sauri kafin ma ya rufe murfin motar har  ya fisgeta da k'arfin gaske, Kalla ya hadiye miyaun wahala Yana muzurai saboda yanayin tukin na Ubangidan nashi yake yi  ya firgitashi matuka, idonshi a rufe Yana Salatin Shirin barin duniya yaji ya tsaida motar da k'arfin Bala'i yayi saurin bud'e ido ganinsu gidan Nuradden yasa hankalinshi ya Dan kwanta, sai dai kuma yanayin yanda Basaraken ke karajin Kiran sunan Nuradden  dinne abun yaso ya bashi tsoro, Yana tsaye Yana mazurai sai gashi ya fito ya sake shiga motar cikin sauri tun kafin Basaraken ya iso wurin, Aiko sai gashi ya shigo motar kamar wani zaki ya fisgeta da k'arfin gaske suka sake fita Kai tsaye gidan Dikko Mai Gwal ya nufa, tun daga nesa ya hango motar Nuradden a harabar gidan cikin zafin nama ya fito motar ko murfin bai tsaya rufewa ba ya nufi cikin gidan.

Nuradden Yana zaune babban falon gidan da su Ummi suna magana Wanda da alama ma kamar da Munawwa da kakale wani wuri suka fito saboda hijaban da ke sanye a jikin Munawwa har ma da Nikaf dinta sai dai  d'ageshi din da tayi a sama, Kakale ko da mayafinta ko cireshi bata yi ba, dukansu ba Wanda yaji Sallamar Basarake bale shigowarshi sai jin suka yi ya kaiwa Nuradden wani wahalallen Mari, dukansu suka Mike tsaye cikin firgici  saboda ganin abunda Basaraken yayi, mamakinsu ya k'aru ne lokacin da suka ga Basaraken ya Fara kaiwa Nuradden duka ta ko'ina kamar wani bajimin zaki, Ummi da Kakale suka Fara Salati suna fad'in ya daina Amman Ina bai masan suna yi ba, Cikin zafin nama Aliyu ya Ciro wata karamar bindiga ya saita Nuradden da ita Yana huci hawaye suna zuba akan fuskarshi, Munawwa a miliyan miliyan ta zo wurin aguje ta tsaya gaban Nuradden   Hawaye suna zuba akan fuskarta tace

"Ka Fara kasheni tukunna kafin shi"

Basarake ya Fara yi mata wani kallo Mai cike da tsantsar tsana saboda hakan da tayi ya K'ara tabbatar masa da lalle sunci amanar tashi, ya fisgo wata jaka dake kusa dashi da hannunshi d'aya wadda da alama ma Tata ce ya buga mata ita a fuska da k'arfin gaske har komi ma da ke cikin jakar ya watse sai ga wasu photuna suna tashi sama suna sauka k'asa.

Tarwatsewar  photunan ya ja hankalin kowa da ke falon cikin k'arfin gwiwa Basaraken ya duk'a ya d'auki wani photon da ke saman k'afarshi ya duba yayi wani Murmushin takaici ya saita bindigar saitin goshin Munawwa da tayi mutuwar tsaye hawaye suna kwaranya a idonshi ya ja halshen bindigar cikin balain zafin kunar da zuciyarshi take yi da nufinshi na aika Munawwa Lahira Koda zai rage zafin radadin da zuciyarshi take yi.
